Chapter 501: Fight Analysis (2)

“In the world of martial skill, nothing stands above speed. That is an eastern empire martial skill philosophy, a reasonable one at that.”

The dragonman youngster seeing it again, found that the origin of this qualitative change was not just the experience he absorbed from Spring Boxer, he had also greatly improved the Surge battle skill overnight.

There was one other reason, the Grand Arena Medal.

“This medal really suits duels. The more people paying attention to me and the longer they do so, the more the medal boosts me.”

“More wonderfully, the medal amplifies all attributes and won’t cause imbalances in me.”

The dragonman youngster subconsciously clutched it with his dragon claw.

His skin and flesh were aching and weary.

Because today’s fight had depleted him too much, even after resting until now, he was still in a weak state.

This hadn’t happened before.

Even the Battle of Two Eyes Islands hadn’t worn him out this much.

The moment the feeling of exhaustion spread through his body, the recording showed him fighting to his heart’s content, forming a distinct contrast.

The dragonman youngster inevitably felt a sense of frustration and yearning in his heart.

“My true strength was not recorded here.”

“This battle really felt refreshing.”

“I want to become stronger, until one day, I can rely on myself to achieve this level of power.”

“Getting stronger is the righteous path!”

The recording entered the final stage of the fight.

The Evil Eye attacked from Xiong Ju’s body.

A grey spirit ray quickly shot out, almost instantly hitting the dragonman youngster.

The dragonman youngster muttered to himself, “That eyeball is very weird, and there was no time to dodge its spiritual ray.”

Soon after, Xiong Ju decided to tear the Evil Eye out of his body and crushed it.

Xiong Ju looked very indignant.

When the dragonman youngster saw this scene again, he was more certain of his guess at the time.

“Xiong Ju wasn’t the one who used that artifact, it was probably outside interference.”

“Therefore, Xiong Ju was indignant about it and made his move.”

“Based on the intelligence reports, the Evil Eye has the aura of the Abyss and should belong to the demon hybrid ringleader.”

“He must have acted in the dark.”

“But to the vast majority of the people in the Ice Sculpture Kingdom, heroic duels are sacred and cannot be profaned. A silver level expert like Xiong Ju is like them.”

“That was what brought about such an outcome.”

After this incident, the dragonman youngster had a deeper understanding of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s customs.

“If Men Shi and Chi Lai actually interfered in the heroic duel, then I fear our end would be dreadful.”

The dragonman youngster pursed his brows in deep thought.

Since the Bayonet Gang decided to secretly meddle in this heroic duel, then could he exploit this flaw?

After a moment of pondering, the dragonman youngster bitterly laughed and shook his head.

He didn’t have any evidence.

It would be difficult for him to prove that the demon hybrid ringleader slyly moved to meddle in the heroic duel.

This was merely his conjecture.

Especially after Xiong Ju had squished the Evil Eye, destroying the biggest piece of evidence.

Snow Bird Dueling Arena didn’t detect anything either.

“It seems that it doesn’t matter whether the demon ringleader played a trick. Since the other side has the guts to do this, it proves that they are confident they were able to conceal themselves.”

“Even if I investigate now, I fear that the other party has already removed all of the clues.”

The survivors lacked expert investigators.

In addition, the dragonman youngster was considering the position of Snow Bird Harbor’s inhabitants.

Were the proletariat masses willing to believe an outsider like him?

The recording finally showed the end of the duel.

Both sides stopped fighting for a moment, doing their utmost to ingest medicine.

The dragonman youngster let out a wheeze.

“Taking medicine so crazily is not the most effective way to use them. Medicine has to be absorbed over time when it enters the stomach, and it would be best to take it in intervals. Such hasty ingestion of medicine will have medicinal effects offsetting each other. Sometimes different medicinal effects will cause highly toxic reactions and harm the body.”

“This also exposes another of my failings, my battle qi reserves.”

“Normally it is fine. But when using my strongest form, all my battle skills will consume large amounts of my battle qi. Even with the Flame Dragon King bloodline’s recovery speed, it can’t match the depletion rate.”

The dragonman youngster didn’t think that one day he’d have the problem of having a battle qi shortage.

The last scene of the fight played out before the dragonman youngster’s eyes.

He and Xiong Ju roared at each other, receiving each other’s blow.

But he recovered first and knocked Xiong Ju out.

The dragonman youngster coughed, summing up everything to himself, “This was a challenging victory. Xiong Ju is the strongest silver level I’ve encountered on this island. He himself is powerful and was also outfitted with a lot of quality equipment, he was exceptionally troublesome. He and I both fought especially hard.”

“The greatest reason I emerged victorious in this heroic duel was that I came out on top of this intense confrontation.”

“This included the time I was attacked by the eyeball and was unable to defend myself. But at the time when Xiong Ju saw I was in poor health, he was only able to squat on the ground gasping for air, simply unable to grab hold of that chance for victory.”

The dragonman youngster wasn’t satisfied with one viewing and watched through another time.

From another spectator’s viewpoint, he gained many more things.

Every time he watched it, he would think back to how he felt, both physically and mentally.

Why did I choose this tactic?

Why did I attack or defend? What result did it produce?

Was there a better choice?

The dragonman youngster was good at reflection.

Every review and reassessment could give him all new ways of thinking.

Through contrasting notions, the dragonman youngster found his many inadequacies and places where improvements could be made.

As for what those improvements would be, the dragonman youngster also had a practical plan mapped out.

He had lightning fast improvement.

This progress was not on the surface.

It was unlike the Grand Arena Medal’s attribute boost, which clearly improved his attack, defense, mobility, and speed.

This improvement was rather vague; it was the progression of his ideology.

He was constantly shoring up his foundation. Every minor shortcoming and every blemish that was improved and refined would make his skyrocketing strength less ephemeral and unstable.

And the battle with Xiong Ju was an excellent chance to learn.

The dragonman youngster seized this opportunity and exhausted his wisdom to unearth nutrients that could grow himself.

“I have never encountered an enemy as fearsome as Xiong Ju.”

“Although I have fought gold level enemies, those were just superficial exchanges before stopping, they didn’t last.”

“Fighting a heavyweight opponent like him was enough to expose my deficiencies.”

Chapter 502: Accidentally Learning Penetration Force

The dragonman youngster began to summarize today’s heroic duel.

First point: his attacks needed to be enhanced.

Normally, Demolition Fist was enough to handle the situation, however, when the dragonman youngster met a truly powerful foe, it was insufficient.

Second point: although his defense was formidable, and was entirely gold level, it still had flaws and could be broken through with Penetration Force.

Furthermore, a previous weakness still existed, his low spiritual defense.

Although Zi Di’s medicine slightly helped in that respect, it didn’t change things completely. When confronting a powerful enemy, it was an immense weak point. Thus, the dragonman youngster was constrained by Bear Roar and the Evil Eye.

Third point: when the dragonman youngster used his strongest form, his speed had a qualitative change.

This was the biggest factor that allowed him to prevail over Xiong Ju.

Speed was already the dragonman youngster’s strongest attribute, but it still had room to grow.

The fourth point was recovery rate: when the dragonman youngster was in his strongest form and attacking at his full strength, his battle qi rapidly depleted. His bloodline’s recovery rate could not keep up.

His stamina was also like that too.

During his battle with Xiong Ju, the dragonman youngster was forced to use medicine to make up for this deficiency.

After the dragonman youngster thought about it more, he came up with a way to resolve it.

For attacks, he could buy a gold level weapon.

At the same time the dragonman youngster also planned to meticulously choose some more battle skills. These battle skills had to be compatible with each other, form combos, so when he faced another silver level expert in the future, he would be able to hit them into the air at best.

Defense was comparatively difficult to handle.

It was extremely hard to make up for his weaknesses there.

Previously, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ improvement project in this was pretty good, and the dragonman youngster couldn’t think of a better solution.

But to realize this plan, they needed time and opportunity.

In terms of speed: it could still be improved.

But it required the dragonman youngster to precisely master the size and scale of every battle qi explosion. This needed diligent study, which would consume large amounts of his energy and time.

Of course, there were shortcuts. One would be looking for an opponent that was a master at the Surge battle skill and then using the Grand Arena Medal to absorb their battle experience.

In the recovery aspect: the dragonman youngster could only entrust his hopes into medicine, equipment, and other external help.

“My fighting strength is peak silver level already. Battle qi cultivation is incremental progress, a slow process that needs time for accumulation. Therefore, within a short time span, I had to rely on external things to improve my military power.”

“If the mercenary corps’ medicine manufacturing capability was improved by a level, then that would immediately solve many problems.”

Thinking of that, the dragonman youngster couldn’t help but shake his head, feeling that he shouldn’t make things difficult for Zi Di in this respect.

Zi Di was only a ghost girl who only reached the iron level in life. She also hadn’t cultivated as a necromancer for long. Therefore, she was only a bronze level magician right now.

It was rather difficult for a bronze level magician to make gold level medicine.

“After my duel with Xiong Ju, much of myself has been exposed. While the information on Teng Dong Lang’s military power is still vague, and in the future when I fight him, I’ll be at a disadvantage in that aspect.”

“So, during this resting period, I have to improve my strength and increase my trump cards.”

“The team has sufficient capital, perhaps I can imitate Xiong Ju and arm myself as much as possible!”

The dragonman youngster had a great longing for and a pressing need to improve his combat strength.

The survivors’ future was uncertain, and they couldn’t say at what moment imperial experts would come to arrest and chase them down.

Looking in the near term, the pressure on the dragonman youngster was coming from the Bayonet Gang’s current leader Teng Dong Lang.

Since he had declared that he wanted to challenge this gold level fighter, he naturally wouldn’t be getting cold feet.

If he decided to renounce his challenge, the prestige he just established would take a devastating blow.

“Actually, there is another way that can improve myself overall, and resolve all of my weaknesses and needs.”

“It is the Bloodcore!”

The dragonman youngster had absorbed the fat ocean dragon abdomen before his duel with Xiong Ju.

It gave him a Bloodcore transformation, wrapping his internal organs in a layer of thick and solid, yet pliable fat.

It was this layer of fat that prevented the Penetration Force from injuring him too much.

This then led to him completely strangling the last chance of victory Xiong Ju had.

It could be said that in this heroic duel, the Bloodcore too played an extremely significant role.

Because of this experience, the dragonman youngster didn’t dare to underestimate any of the Bloodcore’s transformations.

At the same time, his assessment of the Bloodcore’s practicality rose by another level.

“The more bloodlines I absorb, the more complex transformations I have. That can fill in the weak areas and flaws I find in offense, defense, recovery, and can improve all attributes overall.”

“Doing this not only has the advantage of being easy to pull off but is also very covert in nature.”

“When I purchase equipment in Snow Bird Harbor, information on it will definitely be leaked. As the local bully, the Bayonet Gang will be making discreet inquiries, thereby making their own responses and precautions.”

“If I can gather the right raw materials to gain the corresponding Bloodcore transformations, then from a production cost standpoint, this will cost less than buying gold level equipment.”

After analyzing his battle with Xiong Ju, the dragonman youngster then summarized it and formed plans.

He now had a clearer understanding and direction of the future.

The night wind slowly blew through the ship’s flag, its vibrating letting out noises.

Despite his endless exhaustion, the dragonman youngster didn’t rest, rather he struck while the iron was hot and clutched the Grand Arena Medal.

He began to absorb the ample battle experience inside the medal.

The accumulated battle experience inside the Grand Arena Medal was far more bountiful than last time!

The dragonman youngster spent more time and effort absorbing them.

He closed his eyes sand sat cross-legged on the floor, taking a good while to slowly recover.

When he finally opened his eyes again, his dragon eyes were dazzling, coming to a realization that was difficult to put into words.

It came from Xiong Ju’s vast battle experience.

The first part was his wrestling fundamentals.

Xiong Ju was a seasoned fighter!

From a young age, he had scuffled through the streets. Then after growing up, he joined the gang and matured in street blade fights.

In present times, he served as one of the Bayonet Gang’s six ringleaders and had experience in countless fights of all sizes.

Xiong Ju’s wrestling style sufficiently displayed his formidable physical strength. His attack frequency was low, but every strike was heavy and powerful. Because his body weighed a lot, he tended to fight in place and didn’t move much.

Apart from that, Xiong Ju also had some vicious dirty tricks he could use by surprise, often to wonderful effect.

He had some similarities and also some differences with the dragonman youngster.

The first similarity was their physique.

It can be envisioned that as the dragonman youngster absorbs dragon race bloodlines and constantly improves the Flame Dragon King bloodline’s concentration, his body would get taller, larger, and more robust. He would become as tall as Xiong Ju, then grow to be even larger than the latter.

The second similarity was that both were strength type users.

The most obvious difference was this: the dragonman youngster trended towards constant movement while fighting and rapid breaking of an opponent’s defense. Through running and dashing, he increased his firepower or attack frequency.

The dragonman youngster didn’t plan on changing his fighting style. Therefore, in this part of the battle experience, after absorbing the useful parts, he abandoned the rest that went contrary to himself.

He would say Xiong Ju’s fighting style was too cumbersome. Giving up his superiority in movement was very stupid.

After absorbing the wrestling experience, the dragonman youngster’s wrestling fundamentals became very shrewd and ruthless.

Xiong Ju’s battle experience was rich, and he could turn every part of his body into a weapon.

In this respect, the dragonman youngster was not his equal.

The gap between their ages is shown here.

Xiong Ju was also an adept weapon user. He was skillful enough to use the Gold Flower Hacksaw and treat it as an extension of his body.

During the heroic duel, Xiong Ju had cleverly used the Gold Flower Hacksaw’s weight to regain his center of gravity.

When it came to weaponry, the dragonman youngster was far from Xiong Ju’s level of mastery.

These experiences were very helpful to the dragonman youngster.

The second part of battle experience was about battle skills.

Xiong Ju knew many battle skills and displayed many of them during the heroic duel.

There was the two handed battle skill Hammer Fist.

The battle skill Barbarous Collision mixed firepower and movement.

During actual combat, Xiong Ju combined the battle skill Bear Pounce and Barbarous Collision together to form a small battle skill combo.

At the time, when the dragonman youngster faced that combination of battle skills from Xiong Ju, he wisely chose to avoid his attack.

However, it can be seen from this that Xiong Ju was not good at mobility. To get close to the dragonman youngster he had to rely on Barbarous Collision.

The battle skill Total Annihilation and Mountain Splitter matched Xiong Ju’s Gold Flower Hacksaw.

The dragonman youngster’s Gold Silk Chainmail was ravaged as a result.

The battle skill Black Armor helped Xiong Ju out of danger and allowed him to stabilize his footing.

If it weren’t for this battle skill activating, the dragonman youngster would have won the duel then and there.

This specialized defensive battle skill had a very good combat result.

But it also had its shortcomings.

Because the battle skill used a very wide circulation pattern, which caused the user to be unable to use any other battle skills at the same time.

When Xiong Ju used Black Armor, he couldn’t use any other battle skills, instead he used his racial magic to force the dragonman youngster back.

If the dragonman youngster wanted to adopt Black Armor, he could make the same choice as Xiong Ju – substituting Dragon Roar for Bear Roar.

During the fierce unarmed combat section of the fight between the two, Xiong Ju decided to deactivate Black Armor and instead use another defensive battle skill – Wreathed in Silver.

This battle skill could protect some parts of the body. It was clearly inferior to Black Armor in terms of defense.

But Xiong Ju could also use the battle skill Power Punch simultaneously.

The battle skill Wreathed in Silver and Power Punch worked together, allowing Xiong Ju to fight the dragonman youngster on even footing.

The two battle skills combined had a pretty good combat result.

Apart from those two battle skills, Xiong Ju used the racial magic Bear Roar a number of times.

This experience was also very helpful to the dragonman youngster. After all, Bear Roar was very similar to Dragon Roar.

The third portion of experience concerned equipment usage.

The main part was how Xiong Ju used the Gold Flower Hacksaw.

With this experience, the dragonman youngster had gone from a novice at using two handed hacksaw weapons to an experienced user.

But if he wanted to reach Xiong Ju’s level of expertise, one heroic duel’s worth of experience was insufficient.

Xiong Ju was very experienced with the Gold Flower Hacksaw.

Besides normal attacks, he also used the hacksaw’s flat edge as a shield.

He could throw the hacksaw to help adjust his center of gravity.

When Xiong Ju wielded the Gold Flower Hacksaw, he did so with his own fighting style. His attack frequency greatly decreased, but each strike’s power rose dramatically and were very threatening. Against a normal enemy, as long as Xiong Ju hit them with the Gold Flower Hacksaw, if they didn’t die outright, they would be seriously injured. Either way the aftermath was grim.

In other words, the dragonman youngster’s gold level defense diminished the charm of Xiong Ju’s main weapon.

Apart from the Gold Flower Hacksaw experience, the rest of the experience pertained to how to use the Flowing Wave Armor and the Small Thunder God Flying Hammer.

These experiences were of low practical worth to the dragonman youngster.

Because both the Flowing Wave Armor and the Small Thunder God Flying Hammer were equipment of unique design and weren’t often seen on the marketplace.

Furthermore, because Xiong Ju had borrowed this equipment, he was very inexperienced with them.

In real combat, the dragonman youngster had even exploited the Small Thunder God Flying Hammer to his advantage to launch his counterattack against Xiong Ju.

Xiong Ju had a lower level of control of these two pieces of equipment.

What was worth paying attention to was that the experience didn’t contain anything that indicated Xiong Ju used the Evil Eye.

This proved the dragonman youngster’s conjecture during the fight.

But unfortunately, this minute evidence could not be brought forth.

Xiong Ju had indignantly crushed the Evil Eye, and due to that unexpected turn of events, had annihilated the biggest piece of evidence.

The fourth part of battle experience was the part that most pleasantly surprised the dragonman youngster.

The dragonman youngster unconsciously stretched his limbs and stood up from the floor.

The Love Triangle’s training cabin had a humanoid puppet, a specialized boxing training dummy.

The dragonman youngster walked over to it, then mulled for a good while.

He mobilized his battle qi and used Demolition Fist.

Bang.

He punched the humanoid puppet’s chest.

After an explosion rang out, the humanoid puppet’s chest had a small hole in it.

The dragonman youngster immediately walked behind the humanoid puppet.

The humanoid puppet had a hole in its back, one far larger than the one in its chest.

The dragonman youngster’s Demolition Fist had penetrated into the humanoid puppet, carrying the explosion with it, leading to the resulting impact.

A rich smile emerged on the dragonman youngster’s face.

This was the offensive effect of Penetration Force!

“The medicine Xiong Ju took gave each of his attacks Penetration Force.”

“Through the battle experience I absorbed from him, I have learned Penetration Force.”

When Xiong Ju and the dragonman youngster were taking medicine throughout the battle, the former had taken all three of Teng Dong Lang’s pills one after the other,

This gave the dragonman youngster much more battle experience.

This resulted in the dragonman youngster understanding the secrets of Penetration Force.

“This really is Penetration Force!”

When it came to using battle skill techniques, three techniques stood as the most classical.

Penetration Force was the first among them and was very difficult to learn.

Due to unexpected circumstances, the dragonman youngster had gained it after a battle!

Chapter 503: Formation Construction Plan

The dawn’s light illuminated Snow Bird Harbor’s docks.

The color of the sky gradually brightened.

First to awaken was the food market, which was gradually filled with clamorous voices of transactions.

On the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ ships, mercenaries that had been on night watch sleepily yawned and began to change their shifts.

In the flagship’s training cabin, the dragonman youngster had an exciting and sleepless night.

He was constantly trying to use Penetration Force.

After absorbing Xiong Ju’s battle experience, the dragonman youngster had gained the ability to use Penetration Force.

This unexpected gain had given him an exceptionally pleasant surprise.

In any case, in practice, there were still some problems.

To ensure each strike had Penetration Force, the dragonman youngster had to focus for a moment.

This was obviously not practical in actual combat.

Like when he and Xiong Ju fought at a rapid tempo, it would have been impossible for him to have any chances to prepare himself.

Therefore, the dragonman youngster was constantly practicing.

Like with the other experience he absorbed, the dragonman youngster’s usage of Penetration Force swiftly improved as he trained, progress clearly visible with every session of practice.

He unwittingly trained through the night.

By the time dawn rolled around, he had ensured that when he used pure battle qi, he could bring forth Penetration Force.

When using Demolition Fist, his success rate of attacking with Penetration Force had reached 70%.

Machine Gun Fist’s success rate was somewhat lower than that.

But the dragonman youngster was confident that in the wake of more practice, his success rate would increase.

Zi Di stealthily returned from the Deep Sea Monster Fish.

She first showed her face publicly to deal with some affairs.

Normally, when she wasn’t present, an alchemy puppet pretended to be her.

Cang Xu was like that too.

These two alchemy puppets had been given Deceptive Disguise and the Anti-investigation and Prophecy spells, with no hints of flaws.

Therefore, to the other mercenaries it seemed that the magicians Lao Zhong and Hong Teng had always been on the ship and hadn’t gone off on their own.

Many of the tasks that Cang Xu would have dealt with now fell on Zi Di’s shoulders due to his absence.

When breakfast came around, Zi Di still hadn’t seen the dragonman youngster.

She went to the training cabin to see the dragonman youngster alone.

The dragonman youngster had been immersed in practicing Penetration Force and was startled to find that it was breakfast time when Zi Di came over.

The dragonman youngster paused his training.

The alchemy puppet had been ravaged beyond recognition, devastated beyond repair.

Zi Di’s gaze paused on the alchemy puppet, unable to make out the secret of Penetration Force.

She was more concerned about the dragonman youngster.

Seeing that her beloved was covered in sweat and hadn’t slept all night, Zi Di inevitably became distressed.

She knew that as their leader, the dragonman youngster had to face a gold level expert, the psychological pressure on him was massive.

“I have some good news.” Zi Di smiled, using a clever method to comfort the dragonman youngster.

She told the dragonman youngster what she had gained last night.

“I finally determined my bloodline; it is the gold level Gunpowder Flower bloodline!”

“In other news, Lan Zao’s bloodline hasn’t been determined yet. But last night, I made some headway in his bloodline archive paths.”

When the dragonman youngster heard it was the Gunpowder Flower bloodline, he faintly stared at her, soon feeling pity for Zi Di.

Previously, he had learned that Zi Di’s bloodline had four possibilities.

Of the four, two of them were saint level bloodlines.

After finding out the big guy had a legend level bloodline, it was hard for the dragonman youngster to not have high hopes for Zi Di’s bloodline.

But in the end it was not a saint level bloodline.

After thinking on it, the dragonman youngster comforted Zi Di, “The future is uncertain and anything is possible.”

“In the future, when we get stronger, and have a deeper understanding and control over the Bloodcore, we can use it to change our bloodlines. Like how I am now, able to adjust my bloodline at will.”

“At that time, a saint level, or even a legend level bloodline won’t be worth our attention.”

Zi Di gently chuckled.

Last night, she had already adjusted her attitude.

Now, she felt the full romantic happiness from the dragonman youngster’s consoling.

The ghost girl shook her head and replied, “It is so far off before we will be able to completely grasp the Bloodcore’s secrets. Right now, I am only looking forward to the Bloodline Purification formation.”

“I am actually quite satisfied with the bloodline testing results. At least my bloodline isn’t the Bitter Mouth bloodline. The Gunpowder Flower bloodline is gold level and has great potential. As I am now, it has plenty of upsides. By the time I unearth all of my potential, it won’t be too late to look at the Bloodcore again.”

“The issue at hand now is that we need to build the Bloodline Purification formation as soon as possible!”

“But we are a long way off from completing it.”

To be honest, Zi Di was unable to hold herself back now, especially after she had determined her bloodline.

Upon mentioning the Bloodline Purification formation, Zi Di was inevitably anxious, “Cang Xu and I had been constantly researching the Bloodline Purification formation’s alchemy blueprint.”

“To ensure the construction of this alchemy formation, we need a master alchemist’s help.”

“Based on the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ current capabilities, recruiting a master alchemist is as difficult as scaling the heavens.”

The dragonman youngster nodded, he understood how truly hard it was.

Master alchemist was a type of title, one of six ranks in the profession.

Ranked lowest to highest it was apprentice alchemist, alchemist, master alchemist, great master alchemist, grandmaster alchemist, and great grandmaster alchemist.

The grading rating of an alchemist was based on an individual’s alchemy ability. But as a matter of fact, one’s professional ranking and their magician level were strongly related.

Because many alchemy items needed a high level magician to refine them.

Only those with sufficient strength had the ability to guarantee the success rate.

Generally speaking, an apprentice alchemist was a bronze level magician.

An alchemist was iron level.

A master alchemist was silver level.

Finally, a great master alchemist was gold level.

Above that, a grandmaster alchemist was saint level.

A great grandmaster alchemist was legend level. War Merchant was a great grandmaster alchemist, and was himself a legend level magician.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps wanted to recruit a great master alchemist, which meant that they wanted to recruit a gold level expert, a gold level caster at that.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was only a small sized power, Long Fu and Shi Qi were just silver levels.

As a power of that scale, it would be nigh impossible for them to recruit a gold level caster.

Zi Di said with vexation, “Recruiting a gold level caster is obviously unrealistic. I am more inclined towards an alternative plan – using quantity to make up for quality.”

“We can recruit silver level master alchemists.”

“Through their combined efforts, the plan to build the Bloodline Purification formation has a high chance of success.”

Of course, the success rate would be lower than having a great master alchemist.

A great master alchemist could guarantee the Bloodline Purification formation’s success rate. In other words, they had a 100% chance of success.

The Bloodline Purification formation’s construction requirements were not too strict. Its most central and crucial component was the Bloodcore, which was provided by the dragonman youngster.

Recruiting silver level alchemists for a joint project.

The success rate was approximately 60%, that was entirely acceptable. If this plan was adopted, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had to prepare much more alchemy materials for the formation construction. With many attempts, one would eventually work.

If it were Zi Di and Cang Xu working together, the success rate would be too low, it would be less than 3%.

Such a success rate could not be made up for with stockpiles of materials. The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps would need to spend too high of a price for it. There was no need to be so stubborn.

The dragonman youngster suddenly had a thought, “Can we not recruit a silver level great master alchemist?”

When he said that, the dragonman youngster laughed involuntarily, knowing how stupid his question was.

If a magician attained the title of great master alchemist at the silver level, then that would prove their extraordinary gifts in the field of alchemy.

Such a talented person was positive to be treasured by a power; one they would focus on nurturing. How could the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps swipe them away?

The dragonman youngster thought about it, then asked, “If we cannot recruit alchemists, would it be feasible to hire them, using magic contracted to maintain secrecy?”

Chapter 504: Invitation Letters

Zi Di shook her head. “I considered that plan of action too. Naturally that has a chance of success and can allow us to more quickly make the Bloodline Purification formation.”

“But doing that is too risky!”

“Magic contracts seem reliable, but in actuality, there are many ways of breaking them.”

“Like how theft and anti-theft technology alternately advance, so too does the game of contract making and contract breaking play out perpetually.”

“Magic contracts will invariably be broken, and then more stringent magic contracts will be created. After more advanced methods are made, more advanced ways to break magic contracts will arise. This back and forth is an endless cycle.”

“Me and Cang Xu once explored this plan many times.”

“Compared to drafting magic contracts, we instead considered a more dependable plan.”

“That is to break the Bloodline Purification formation into many parts. We can employ outside alchemy masters to build a part of the formation.”

“We can hire different alchemy masters to obtain different parts of the formation. After collecting all of the parts, we can put them together.”

If that happens, we can obtain the Bloodline Purification formation with minimal information leaks.”

“But doing that is also quite risky.”

“First, despite being just a part of the formation, it will divulge the secret that is the Bloodline Purification formation.”

“Second, when it comes time to assemble it, we need excellent alchemy skill, from at least a master alchemist. In this aspect, neither I nor Cang Xu are qualified. If the assembly does not succeed and parts are damaged, we will have to re-refine them again.”

“Third, If the Bloodline Purification formation is broken up into parts, it will involve two steps. Step one is to break the blueprint up, something that is impossible for me and Cang Xu. Step two, we need to refine formation plates. These plates are divided into the main plate, the secondary plate, the distribution plate, the foundation plate, the base plate, and so on. This involves advances alchemy technology like formation disassembly, formation support, and formation plate refinement.”

“The tower spirit can break up the Bloodline Purification formation, but it will take a large amount of calculations.”

After the survivors had obtained the tower spirit, it had been silently performing an important function.

It could help a magician control formations and increase medicine refinement efficiency.”

It could also perform calculations that could improve recipes and formations and was also able to design some simple blueprints.

“The first step can barely be surmounted, but there is nothing we can do to cross the second step. It isn’t possible for us to refine formation plates. Leaving aside the rare and treasured alchemy materials needed, we will need at least a great master alchemist to make them.”

The dragonman youngster paused to rub his sore head.

After all this talk, the problem returned to square one.

The problem of talent!

The survivors needed a great master alchemist or a master alchemist.

Zi Di looked at the dragonman youngster, “I believe the most dependable plan is still recruitment. We need loyal and reliable people by our side.”

“The Bloodline Purification formation’s impact is too great.”

“If it is exposed, every power in the world will seek after it.”

“Our enemy will not just be the Sheng Ming Empire, but the entire world!”

“This significant secret must be defended to the death, and the optimal strategy is to pinch it off at the source! We now need to take the more conservative stratagem, so future risks will be smaller. If this secret is leaked, it will be too troublesome, so big that we will be unable to deal with it, it will undoubtably land us in a dire place.”

The dragonman youngster nodded repeatedly, approving of Zi Di’s viewpoint.

“But then again, there is a silver level alchemy great master in Snow Bird Harbor. She is not part of any power.” Zi Di had a somewhat inexplicable expression when she said that.

The dragonman youngster knew who she was talking about.

On the first day they arrived at Snow Bird Harbor, they had bought a lot of information from the Thieves Guild.

In these information reports, there was intel on the many talents of Snow Bird Harbor, focused mainly on those in the field of alchemy.

Zi Di was talking about a female snow elf named Bu Quan.

She wore black framed glasses and had many alchemy workshops in Snow Bird Harbor.

She was, without questions, one of Snow Bird Harbor’s lead figures.

The dragonman youngster sighed, “Recruiting her is extremely difficult, I don’t have any hopes of doing so.”

But Zi Di replied, “It isn’t impossible. As long as there is a thread of possibility, we can still try, right?”

“Lord Commander, you might not know about it yet. After yesterday’s heroic duel, your prestige now from what it was previous is as far as the distance between heaven and earth.”

When she said that, Zi Di took out a pile of letters.

These letters were all invitation letters without exception.

They universally came from Snow Bird Harbor’s great powers.

These powers were inviting the dragonman youngster in hopes of forming a closer connection with him.

After the dragonman youngster fought Xiong Ju, he had completely established his renown.

The people were realizing the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ strength.

From this pile of invitation letters, one could make out that the dragonman youngster’s goal in initiating heroic duels was half complete already.

This was the world of the strong. Only when one’s strength was acknowledged could they get respect and be qualified to have relationships.

The dragonman youngster quickly read through the invitation letters.

The six great merchant alliances of the Sheng Ming Empire all sent him a letter, with some containing a few small gifts.

The letter contained their cordial attitude, expressing that they wished to form a business relationship with the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

Merchant alliances and mercenary corps had a close relationship with each other.

Despite the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ star rating being a trifling one star, the dragonman youngster’s duel yesterday revealed his individual strength and style.

Therefore, the six merchant alliances tossed him an olive branch.

Snow Bird Harbor’s mercenary corps also sent letters.

In these letters, they explicitly stated or hinted that they wanted to buy some alchemy arrows.

During the battle between the dragonman youngster and Xiong Ju, the former had used alchemy crossbows. The alchemy arrows’ combat result then was pretty good and moved the hearts of many people.

Evidently, the hearsay that an arms merchant was the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ backer had spread like wildfire across Snow Bird Harbor in the wake of the dragonman youngster’s popularity.

There was also an invitation letter from the Seabird Church.

The Seabird Church’s bishop personally invited Long Fu to join them in the next prayer session.

The written intent was very clear.

The most luxuriously adorned letter came from Snow Bird Harbor’s City Lord.

The invitation letter was written on black paper, and its surface was covered in gold and silver lines that depicted the City Lord’s family name.

After opening the letter, many magic images flashed by inside it.

These images depicted the many iconic locations of Snow Bird Harbor.

The final last magic image was that of the City Lord himself.

He was a middle aged snow elf with slender eyes and gorgeous clothing. He sat upright with a hand on a brown scepter, putting on an air of nobility.

This invitation letter was a lower level alchemy item.

After the magic image dissipated, some words appeared.

Snow Bird Harbor’s City Lord was inviting the dragonman youngster to a one on one dinner with him, there was an important matter to discuss.

After beating Xiong Ju, the dragonman youngster had become Snow Bird Harbor’s most popular figure.

The people accepted that the dragonman youngster had the strength to threaten gold levels.

Xiong Ju was the head of the six ringleaders, and he had already been defeated by the dragonman youngster. The remaining three ringleaders were not Long Fu’s opponents.

What surprised the dragonman youngster was the last letter, it actually came from the great master alchemist Bu Quan!

In the letter, Bu Quan expressed that she wanted to give the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps a mercenary mission.

It was undoubtedly a way to get close to Bu Quan, get an understanding of her, and a great chance to recruit her.

“How does it feel to be famous?” Zi Di waited until the dragonman youngster thumbed through all of the invitation letters, tilting her head slightly when she teased him.

“Without question, it is glorious.” The dragonman youngster smiled, confessing his fine mood.

But soon after, he faintly pursed his brows.

Because he took note that all of these letters had come from large powers.

None from any small powers.

Large powers didn’t consider the Bayonet Gang’s attitude.

While small powers generally feared the Bayonet Gang. Therefore, at this time, they chose to maintain distance from the dragonman youngster.

Bu Quan was the only exception.

From her invitation letter, the dragonman youngster could see she didn’t fear the Bayonet Gang.

Be aware, she was only a silver level caster who had her property in Snow Bird Harbor.

From this, one could make out the aloof independence an alchemy great master had in their status and position.

Recruiting such a talent would be extraordinarily difficult for the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

Chapter 505: Dragon Rider

The dragonman youngster’s voice was tranquil, “The Bayonet Gang is undoubtably a colossus in Snow Bird Harbor.”

“I initiated a heroic duel to sway the Bayonet Gang’s position and prestige.”

“Most people enjoy where the crowds are, and many of them harbor unfathomable motives. They are looking forward to the Bayonet Gang and our mercenary corps to become deadlocked, exposing our weakness and vulnerabilities, then acting against us!”

“We are already standing in the heart of the struggle; we must be careful and prudent.”

“Based on how things proceeded today, we have displayed our strength and have achieved our original objective.”

“Next, we need to control the scale of the fight, we cannot allow it to expand or intensify the conflict.””

“After my duel with Teng Dong Lang, the outcome at an individual level doesn’t actually matter.”

“Of course, I maintain my desire for victory.”

When Zi Di heard the dragonman youngster say this, she couldn’t help but show her appreciation and loving admiration.

The dragonman youngster was not just a fighter, he was more so an excellent leader.

He could look at the big picture, recognize reality, analyze pros and cons, put himself second, and issue policy that most suited the collective.

He was a very reliable leader and could put the people at ease.

The dragonman youngster continued, “As for these invitations, I will tactfully reject them, even Bu Quan’s invitation.”

“I have thought of a good justification already.”

“During my fight with Xiong Ju, I was injured by Penetration Force, and I need a time to recuperate.”

“What, Penetration Force?” Zi Di suddenly grew worried.

“Of course it is a lie. It is merely an excuse.” The dragonman youngster smiled, then explained himself.

He had also told Zi Di his conjecture about the Evil Eye, deciding not to conceal it.

“In addition, I would like to tell you some good news. After I absorbed the experience from the Grand Arena Medal, I unexpectedly learned how to use Penetration Force.”

That information caused Zi Di’s eyes to widen.

The girl’s eyes flashed with heartfelt happiness for the dragonman youngster

“That is amazing! Comprehending Penetration Force is always challenging.”

“That is an immense gain!”

“I didn’t anticipate that the Grand Arena Medal was a way of learning how to use Penetration Force.”

“I look forward to what expression the Bayonet Gang’s higherups make when they hear about this. Hehe, Xiong Ju took three Penetration Force pills, but his chances of learning how to use Penetration Force is quite low. They wanted to use Penetration Force to deal with you, but as a result, they handed it over to their opponent.”

“Hold on, can this method be utilized again?”

“In the future, can we purchase Penetration Force pills, then use the Grand Arena Medal to cultivate some capable subordinates into grasping Penetration Force?”

When thinking of this, Zi Di looked vexed.

“What a pity, Penetration Force pills are rarely seen on the marketplace. They are only ever seen at auctions, and sold at a very high price.”

“Alas, War Merchant’s database doesn’t have the recipe for Penetration Force pills. The Eastern Temple tightly safeguards that recipe vigorously. If we want to buy Penetration Force pills, we need special channels and contacts. Just being wealthy is not enough.”

The dragonman youngster burst into laughter and gently flicked the bridge of Zi Di’s nose, “What are you thinking?”

“I already know how to use Penetration Force and also have the Grand Arena Medal.”

“We just need to give the Grand Arena Medal to another and have them fight me. During that fight, I will constantly use Penetration Force.

“If that happens, can’t the person grasp Penetration Force through the absorbed battle experience?”

“Huh?!” Zi Di looked endearingly silly.

She was a pharmacist, and her line of thinking was geared towards medicine, thus, she made a foolish mistake.

After reacting, Zi Di couldn’t help but sigh, “The Grand Arena Medal really is useful. In light of this method, we don’t need to make batches of Penetration Force pills for our fighters?”

But the dragonman youngster hesitated, “We can try, but such a thing might not be so simple!”

“It is reasonable to say that although the Penetration Force pills and the Grand Arena Medal are rare, as long as a large power is willing to pay the price, it is possible to possess both of them.”

“If this kind of method could work, a large power would have done it already. But I have never heard of an example of this working.”

Zi Di approved of this analysis, “Perhaps there are other obstacles we don’t know about in this, but it is worth trying.”

The dragonman youngster nodded, “I plan to attempt it with Zong Ge first.”

“If he can learn Penetration Force, it will greatly improve ourselves.”

Zi Di hesitated for a bit, but still nodded, not expressing any objections.

The dragonman youngster muttered to himself, “The battle against Xiong Ju caused me to realize that at my current strength, I fear I am more likely to lose against Teng Dong Lang.”

“Although losing against a gold level is something everyone can understand.”

“But if I can defeat Teng Dong Lang in a heroic duel, the gains will be immense! It will be the most favorable outcome for the mercenary corps.”

“Therefore, I want to use this excuse to rest and fight for more time. During that time, I will do everything I can to strengthen my combat strength.”

“What I need most is time!”

“The more time I have to prepare, the stronger I will be.”

“Externally, we must buy all the gold level equipment we can.”

“I also need to gather biomaterials that the Bloodcore can absorb.”

“Any bloodline transformation can give me unexpected assistance.”

“For example, when I was facing Penetration Force, the fat ocean dragon’s blubber helped protect me in a pinch.”

Zi Di nodded repeatedly, cutely complying, “My Lord, I will obey.”

“Using Penetration Force is a good excuse.”

“I believe that even the Bayonet Gang will also be deceived. Because Xiong Ju really did take Penetration Force pills and they don’t know about the Bloodcore’s existence.”

“They will believe that you barely endured the pain to defeat Xiong Ju, while in actuality, your internal organs have been gravely injured.”

“Such internal injuries cannot be discerned from one’s outward appearance.”

“The outside world is looking forward to the Lord Commander’s confrontation with Teng Dong Lang, but with this excuse, everyone will approve and accept a delay.”

“You were the one who incited the heroic duel, and so you control the initiative.”

“The Bayonet Gang is a colossus, but they can only passively wait for your challenge. In this aspect, they are in a completely passive position. As of now, their three remaining ringleaders and their gang leader must be feeling quite uncomfortable.”

Zi Di giggled with a charming smile.

The dragonman youngster looked solemn, “Don’t underestimate the enemy.”

“They are a gang and might use treacherous tricks.”

“Didn’t they interfere in the dueling arena? Snow Bird Dueling Arena is Snow Bird Harbor’s largest dueling arena, yet they detected nothing.”

Zi Di’s smile vanished, and she conscientiously replied, “I have made plans.”

“Next, we must gather alchemy materials on a wide scale.”

“The focus will be dragon corpses, gunpowder flowers, and other materials for magic blood testing medicine.”

“As we have an arms merchant backing us, long term and extensive alchemy material gathering is justifiable. There is no need to explain ourselves, outsiders will have thought of what our reasons are.”

“To deal with Teng Dong Lang, we need to procure as much gold level equipment as we can. However, this is very liable to be thwarted by the Bayonet Gang.”

“Finally, we need to peddle medicine and establish preliminary sources of steady income.”

“I have already chosen a good medicine; it is called dragon rider.”

This medicine is to be given to a mount. For a short time, it can give that mount the strength and stamina of a dragon.”

“We have already investigated the marketplace here, and found that there were many medicines for mounts, but none of them will be able to compete with the dragon rider potion.”

The dragonman youngster rapidly recalled what he heard and saw, as well as the great amount of intelligence reports he purchased.

He couldn’t help but applaud Zi Di’s choice.

The Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s alchemy technology was fairly developed, and it possessed numerous alchemists and master alchemists.

After all, the main race here was snow elves.

Elves had better magical gifts than humans.

Normally, when medicine made for a person’s consumption is put on Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s marketplace, the competitive pressure would be fierce.

Zi Di’s choice could even be said to be finding an alternative, having mounts be the target.

Crucially, the Ice Sculpture Kingdom had many mounts.

The most common was sled dogs.

There were giant sled dogs capable of hauling heavy goods by themselves and there were conventional sled dog packs good at working together.

Apart from sled dogs, there were also horses, deer, and chocobos.

On the coastal port cities, there were also dolphins, walruses, seals, sea horses, surfing starfish, and other aquatic mounts.

“Even though I am not a professional, I also think that the dragon rider medicine will have great sales here.” The dragonman youngster commended Zi Di, “In this field, you have always been astounding!”

Zi Di enjoyed her sweetheart’s compliment, laughing, “We should thank War Merchant most of all. His database contains so many medicinal recipes. I only needed to choose a suitable one, that’s all.”

Zi Di spoke modestly, but in actuality, most people couldn’t do this.

Her business talent was outstanding.

Only with such business talent and ability could she pick the best medicine out of a sea of choices.

“By the time we get stronger, we’ll be able to make much more medicine.”

“These medicines will all absolutely be market competitive!”

“We stand on the shoulders of a great grandmaster alchemist.”

“In addition, if in the future we can find a place to securely and secretly manufacture alchemy munitions, that would be even better.”

“Many people witnessed yesterday’s heroic duel, those are our potential customers.”

The ghost girl’s eyes glistened gold when she thought of the wonderful scene of her making especially large profits in the future.

The survivors had narrowly escaped Mystifying Monster Island.

With great dangers, there were also great opportunities.

But most of the things from Mystifying Monster Island could not be liquidated.

Only with sufficient strength could they make money out of them!

As president of the Wisteria Merchant Alliance, Zi Di had a profoundly deep understanding of this.

Chapter 506: Fighting Zong Ge

Ding.

A clenched dragon claw hit Zong Ge’s forearm.

At that moment, the dragonman youngster and Zong Ge were fighting in the Love Triangle’s training room

Zong Ge was a weapon great master, he was capable of using almost every type of weapon in a menacing fashion. Compared to his weapon mastery, his unarmed combat was undoubtedly a bit worse.

Even so, Zong Ge’s hand to hand skill was very solid he was steady and struck hard, simple and effective. Those traits matched each other and had a militaristic style.

The Gold Silk Chainmail and the Grand Arena Medal were being worn by Zong Ge.

Zong Ge was mainly defending against the dragonman youngster’s assault.

The dragonman youngster’s every attack had Penetration Force!

After some fighting, the dragonman youngster stopped attacking.

Zong Ge stared at him and asked, “What’s going on?”

The dragonman youngster pointed at his nose, “Your nose is bleeding.”

Zong Ge nonchalantly wiped it clean, replying boldly, “An insignificant injury not worth mentioning. Continue!”

The dragonman youngster shook his head, “Internal injuries are difficult to judge from the outside, you better rest for a bit first.”

Zong Ge didn’t insist on it.

He stood in place, drank a potion and smeared some other medicine across his body.

The medicine had decent effects.

Zong Ge felt his chest tightness and short breathing quickly dissipating after using the medicine.

His external superficial injuries also visibly recovered.

This medicine had been quickly refined by Zi Di this morning to protect against Penetration Force.

Before the dragonman youngster started sparring, Zong Ge had taken some medicine that helped protect his internal organs.

After Zong Ge took the medicine, he sighed, “I have taken at least three dosages. Penetration Force really is strong; the Gold Silk Chainmail is practically useless. It’s no wonder so many people rush like ducks for this battle qi technique and spend loads of energy and time pursuing it constantly.”

Zong Ge had little grasp of battle qi techniques.

The biggest reason was his age.

He was still very young.

He was already a weapon great master and had become an excellent general. His life level was close to the gold level, with his silver battle qi already having a trace of gold luster, he was tantamount to being a half step into the gold level.

These were already immense accomplishments.

Of course, Zong Ge’s bloodline also had a significant impact on this.

The vast majority of people in this world could not reach his pace, no matter how much time and effort they put in.

Bloodline talent decided one’s life.

Many things decided by birth could not be done through hard work.

In such a world, if things like the Bloodcore or the Bloodline Purification formation were exposed, it would stir up incomparably huge trouble.

Zong Ge was also a very hard working person.

He certainly knew the benefits of battle qi techniques. But he had to spend a lot of time and energy training to learn one.

Zong Ge chose to spend his time and energy in more productive areas.

His choice was very sensible.

Generally speaking, those pursuing battle qi techniques were those who had reached the limits of their individual strength, those who had unearthed all of their potential.

Under those circumstances, they pursued battle qi techniques because it was a possible way of getting stronger.

Zong Ge was nowhere close to pursuing battle qi techniques.

Of course, when he learned the dragonman youngster had quickly grasped Penetration Force through the Grand Arena Medal, he was moved by it.

Because the learning cost a fighter paid for this was extremely cheap!

If one only needed to pay a small price to learn Penetration Force, was there no reason not to?

After Zong Ge rested, the two continued sparring.

“The accumulated experience should be enough; you can use it now.” Not long after, the dragonman youngster stopped again.

According to his directions, Zong Ge sat cross-legged on the floor and began to use the Grand Arena Medal.

A moment later, he opened his eyes.

His eyes flashed with inspiration.

“Interesting, so this is how using the Grand Arena Medal feels…”

This was Zong Ge’s first time using the Grand Arena Medal, it felt rather novel.

“How is it?” The dragonman youngster asked.

Zong Ge pursed his brows, “There is a lot of experience, mainly made up of Penetration Force, but I cannot grasp it.”

The dragonman youngster smiled, unconcerned, “If the first time doesn’t work, then we can test it several more times.”

However, the following tries all failed.

“How strange, why was I able to succeed on the first try?” The dragonman youngster felt perplexed.

“It doesn’t matter how many times I fail, not even 10 times. As long as I try enough, I will succeed eventually.” Zong Ge greatly coveted Penetration Force.

Following this…

“Another try!” Zong Ge firmly wiped his bloody nose.

Zong Ge began to bleed from his eyes, ears, nostrils, and mouth.

“Another…cough, cough.” Zong Ge spat out blood.

Despite so many tries, he was still unable to control Penetration Force.

He certainly absorbed experience pertaining to Penetration Force, but for some unknown reason, he could never get it.

“We have tried so many times; it shouldn’t end like this.” The dragonman youngster’s brows creased.

“He continued to hypothesize, “Could it be because I didn’t use my full strength?”

The most obvious difference between their sparring now and the heroic duel was that Xiong Ju and the dragonman youngster were truly fighting with everything they had.

While during this sparring, the dragonman youngster held himself back, worried that he’d hurt Zong Ge.

“Maybe, the Grand Arena Medal is dependent on the intensity of the fight?” The dragonman youngster thought of a possibility.

“Makes sense.” Zong Ge muttered, “The god who made the Grand Arena Medal, Serene Fury, belongs to the demon race. He has battle clerics, and his sect’s doctrine always reveres fighting with your enemy with your full strength. Our spar was not that intense.”

From its nature, the Grand Arena Medal was no ordinary alchemy treasure, rather it was a sacred tool.

Sacred tools were similar to divine artifacts; they were the gods’ (or the clergy’s) and were made from divine power.

Of course, some included alchemy technology, but the core of them was divine power and divine spells.

In the vast majority of circumstances, only a clergy using divine power of their church could use a sacred tool.

Divine artifacts were more stringent with their usage. Normally, one needed a god to bestow their permission or otherwise be a pope. Red clothed archbishops or other types of high ranked clergymen could not use one as they wished.

The survivors were in a special situation.

They had two divine artifacts, the Mermaid Fairy Tale and the Greed Jade Gold Coffin. (The Bloodcore was uncertain.)

The gods behind these two divine artifacts were either fallen or were on the brink of doing so. Their control of these divine artifacts were at its lowest point.

The survivors had dug into this loophole and were able to utilize these two divine artifacts.

The reason why they could was due to War Merchant.

Without that legend level great grandmaster alchemist leaving behind his foundation, the survivors would have found it incredibly difficult to use these divine artifacts.

Even so, they were never able to display the divine artifacts’ true power.

Returning to the Grand Arena Medal.

It was a very special sacred tool. Its creator, the demon god Serene Fury wanted this sacred tool to be given to outsiders.

Zong Ge thought that since the Grand Arena Medal came from Serene Fury, it would reflect the demon god’s disposition, doctrine, and clergy, among other things.

Serene Fury encouraged fighting with everything one had and was certain to loath fraudulent fisticuffs.

Serene Fury would not have enjoyed the dragonman youngster’s and Zong Ge’s spar.

Perhaps, this was the reason for the Grand Arena Medal’s low efficiency.

His conjecture had logic to it.

“We can actually fight to validate that theory.” Said Zong Ge.

That sentence caught the dragonman youngster’s interest.

The two looked at each other, both seeing the other was eager to give it a try.

On Mystifying Monster Island, the two had competed with each other, but never had a real scrap against the other. Because survival was the main focus and beating each other up would cause stupid internal friction.

After escaping Mystifying Monster Island, despite constantly fighting, the dragonman youngster and Zong Ge never had an actual spar with each other.

They had a complex relationship.

They were comrades and also rivals.

They both held high positions and had to consider the team (despite Zong Ge not having good prospects for the survivors). They didn’t want to fight each other rashly, for no matter who won or lost, it would have a negative impact on the group.

The dragonman youngster took a deep breath, enduring the battle intent in his heart, “Speaking of which, we really didn’t use our full strength during this spar.”

The dragonman youngster’s progress was quick and violent, but Zong Ge’s arm had healed, and his battle qi cultivation was distinctly higher by a bit more. The latter’s battle qi had a gold luster within it.

The dragonman youngster did not know as many battle skills as Zong Ge.

The dragonman youngster had outstanding defense, and his current final form fundamentally changed his speed, while Zong Ge had that pair of mysterious short spears that were most certainly not ordinary.

Everyone, including the dragonman youngster guessed that those short spears were Zong Ge’s trump card, and using them had a high price. The short spears were filled with a mysterious character. Even in the most critical moments on Mystifying Monster Island, no one saw Zong Ge fighting with them.

“Yep.” Zong Ge gazed into the dragonman youngster’s eyes, not covering his own battle intent.

“Regrettably, there isn’t an appropriate place for us to have a real battle here.” Zong Ge looked around the training cabin with frustration.

Although the Love Triangle’s training cabin had carefully selected materials and was a hidden place, if the dragonman youngster and Zong Ge fought here, it would destroy it beyond recognition.

In fact, the entire Love Triangle would be unable to withstand it.

It was only an iron level magic ship.

“Snow Bird Harbor has many dueling arenas; we can contract one for a private battle.” But when Zong Ge said that he shook his head.

It wasn’t appropriate to do that in their current situation.

The Bayonet Gang was the local tyrant here, and they were surely paying very close attention to the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, and they were constantly making discreet inquiries on the important members of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

If the two really did have an all-out fight in a dueling arena, then the Bayonet Gang would almost certainly learn that the dragonman youngster could control Penetration Force.

Chapter 507: The Young Man’s Main Weapon

The dragonman youngster would be dueling Teng Dong Lang in the future.

Penetration Force was a great trump card, and if it was exposed like that, it would be such a pity.

Zong Ge sighed, “The ship can’t stand it. Even if we sail out and look for an uninhabited island, the conditions haven’t been met sufficiently.”

“I still need better medicine to resist Penetration Force.”

Zi Di supplied a lot of medicine, including those that were silver level.

But when Zong Ge used them, he noticed they was inadequate.

The dragonman youngster defended Zi Di, “She is just a bronze level magician, and wanting her to refine gold level medicine is making things too difficult for her.”

“We can buy some from the Secret Medicine store.”

“Even if they don’t have any now, we can place an order.”

Zong Ge nodded, “Next, I will teach you.”

After saying that, Zong Ge returned the Gold Silk Chainmail and the Grand Arena Medal to the dragonman youngster.

Zong Ge then asked, “Have you considered it yet? What will be your main weapon?”

The dragonman youngster spoke honestly, “As of now, I am considering a crossbow or a firearm.”

“By choosing a crossbow, I can improve my deficiency in long range attacks.”

“If Teng Dong Lang has a flying battle skill, I will be mainly relying on a crossbow or a firearm.”

Although the dragonman youngster could forcefully displace himself through the air, that was through Surge working in concert with the spurting smoke dragon ring mound transformation.

This kind of displacement could bully Xiong Ju, but it was far from being close to gold level flight.

After muttering to himself, Zong Ge shook his head, “You have the fundamentals of shooting, but that isn’t enough.”

“Your battle with Xiong Ju is proof of that: silver level crossbows with alchemy arrows was not enough to threaten him.”

“Therefore, you need to have at least a gold level crossbow or firearm.”

“But even doing that is insufficient.”

“If you want to sufficiently display a crossbow’s might, you need to pair it up with all kinds of archery battle skills.”

“Generally speaking, qualified archers need to have at least three archery battle skills.”

“Comparatively outstanding archers know even more of them, about 5-6.”

“I experienced a few archery experts myself; they knew at least 10 archery battle skills.”

“I know 4 archery battle skills and War Merchant’s database has many more.”

“The crucial problem is time. Even with the Grand Arena Medal, there is not enough time for you to learn those before you duel Teng Dong Lang.”

“Using Penetration Force as an excuse won’t give you enough time.”

Long ranged weapons like crossbows and firearms were quite easy to use, while simultaneously placing the user at a safe distance.

But the advantages of long ranged weapons also had their disadvantages too.

After arrows and bullets were shot, a fighter had low control over them.

Even an ordinary person, as long as they practice martial art techniques, could evade those arrows and bullets.

Therefore, one needed battle skills to change the flight trajectory of arrows and bullets.

Archery battle skills could also increase the might of ammunition, lessen interference, form combinations, and so on.

Only by doing this could monotonous fire become complex and be hard to defend against.

If one only knew one or two archery battle skills, they wouldn’t be very threatening.

A long ranged fighter not only needed to invest in increasing their battle skills, but they also needed to have the money to support their ammo consumption.

Arrows and bullets were expendable goods. The price of a single arrow or bullet wasn’t high, but during a battle, it was impossible to fire only a few arrows or bullets.

With many drops making an ocean, these arrows or bullets could become a gigantic sum of money.

The more one fought, the more one’s personal finances were tested.

The dragonman youngster understood what Zong Ge was saying, and was willing to accept his view, thus, he dispelled his thoughts of choosing a long ranged weapon as his main weapon.

“What do you suggest?” The dragonman youngster asked.

Zong Ge explicitly stated, “I suggest you choose a melee weapon.”

The dragonman youngster’s eyebrows wrinkled, “Then if Teng Dong Lang has a flying battle skill, I will be very passive.”

Zong Ge nodded, “Not just passive, you have no chance of winning.”

“Your opponent is a gold level.”

“There is a giant gap between silver levels and gold levels.”

“Your dashing through the air via explosions is very crude. It’s decent when dealing with silver levels, but against a flying gold level, it will expose many weak points.”

“Therefore, if Teng Dong Lang can fly, your best response is to find a way to concede with dignity.”

“There is no need to fight an impossible battle, right?”

The dragonman youngster’s face stagnated.

Zong Ge’s words were not pleasant to hear but were indeed reasonable.

Zong Ge continued, “Luckily, based on our current intelligence reports, Teng Dong Lang doesn’t possess a flying battle skill.”

“Envision engaging him on the ground, close ranged weapons are far more of a threat to him than long ranged weapons.”

The dragonman youngster asked, “In that case, what weapon should I choose, which one fits the best?”

Zong Ge had obviously thought on this question and blurted out, “The appropriate weapon is one that can restrain the enemy, and will best display your superiorities, you need to play to your strengths.”

“Teng Dong Lang’s bloodline is the Winter Blue bloodline, he has many ice attribute battle skills, and his main weapon is a long whip, a medium-ranged type weapon.

“While your current greatest advantage is your speed. Your high frequency of unarmed attacks can force the enemy to be caught up in your quick tempo.”

The dragonman youngster’s eyes suddenly lit up when he was told that, “Do you mean claw sheaths or boxing gloves?”

But Zong Ge shook his head.

He looked at the dragonman youngster’s dragon claws.

“Your hands are already natural weapons, and I believe they will get sharper in the future; it is unnecessary to draw legs on a snake.”

Zong Ge and Tripleblade knew the dragonman youngster had kept the Bloodcore when they left Mystifying Monster Island.

They surmised that the Bloodcore was crucial for the dragonman youngster’s various beast transformations.

The dragonman youngster had clearly gained the spurting smoke dragon transformation, among others, after they left that island.

Therefore, Zong Ge had guessed that in the future, the dragonman youngster’s beast transformations would develop further.

Zong Ge continued, “Axes and swords do suit you. To deal with Teng Dong Lang’s long whip, you need cutting damage.”

“In addition to that, short spears and hammers are also not so bad. The former can pierce through an opponent’s defense at a single point. The latter is compatible with your battle qi and can increase the power of your explosions.”

Zong Ge was worthy of being a weapon great master; he had a deep understanding of all types of weapons.

He carried on, “According to your current fighting style, short spears are the most suitable for you. Because your two offensive battle skills exert force similar to a short spear’s thrust. It will take a minimal amount of time and energy for you to grasp how to use a short spear.”

“With hammers, the round melon shaped hammer best suits you. When Xiong Ju was swinging a square hammer, he needed to pay attention to which direction he was moving it towards. Regardless of whether it is a square or round hammer, it will need to have a short shaft and not a long one.”

“Your speed advantage is very clear, and you don’t need to put time and effort into attack range. Because you can dash at an opponent with your rapid displacement and attack them directly.”

“A short weapon will make full use of your fighting style and won’t impair your superiority in high frequency of attacks and having a rapid tempo.”

“When choosing weapons like axes, knives, and swords, you can follow the aforementioned principles.”

“Those three are nearly identical to you.”

“If you want to choose one, see what you like.”

“Of course, the main thing we are looking for is what we can buy right now.”

“What the specific type of and of what quality the equipment is very crucial.”

Zong Ge gave his suggestion, it was voluminous and had many penetrating insights.

His suggestion was full of sincerity.

Like how the dragonman youngster treated him, Zong Ge also wanted to help the dragonman youngster from the bottom of his heart.

The two had quite a complicated relationship.

They were comrades and competitors.

They were open-minded types of people, they didn’t want to suppress the other, rather they wanted to support the other, then use the other’s strength to validate themselves, encourage themselves, and make themselves stronger.

After some reflection, the dragonman youngster decided to pick a short spear for the time being.

He thought of the short spears Zong Ge had on his back, perhaps that signified that out of all the weapons, Zong Ge was the best at using short spears.

From this angle, through absorbing Zong Ge’s battle experience, the dragonman youngster’s growth of using short spears would be more effective.

Although the tower spirit could not refine iron and silver level weapons, it could rush out a few ordinary short spears that were usable.

The dragonman youngster and Zong Ge both held a pair of short spears and began to train.

The dragonman youngster challenging Teng Dong Lang was not him acting alone, it was a major event for the entire mercenary corps.

To help the dragonman youngster, Zong Ge decided to give up training the Quick Knife and Backwater battle squads.

He had trained troops before, and now he was training the dragonman youngster.

While resting between bouts, he made every second count to talk about his insights as well as point out the dragonman youngster’s abnormalities. He was doing his utmost and wasn’t withholding anything back.

Time flew by.

The two were so immersed in it that they forgot lunch.

Zi Di came by with food, so the two stopped for a spell to wolf it down.

The ghost girl found out about how Zong Ge’s attempts went, as well as what the two hypothesized on why they failed. Then after a round of fighting, she took out a set of medicine.

She said to Zong Ge, “The medicine you wanted is right here. The main one is gold level and has a strong defensive effect against Penetration Force. The other three are silver level medicine with different helpful effects, plenty of them can increase your defense, and others heal you.”

The dragonman youngster and Zong Ge felt curious.

Zi Di told them, “Of course I didn’t refine these, rather at noon, someone gave this as a gift.”

“Using gold level medicine as a present?” Zong Ge grew more curious, “Who would have so much sincerity?”

Zi Di looked at the dragonman youngster, and the latter guessed, “Could it be Bu Quan?”

Chapter 508: Giving the Dragonman Youngster a Present

It was logical for the dragonman youngster to surmise it was Bu Quan.

He knew Bu Quan was an alchemy great master and had many alchemy workshops in Snow Bird Harbor. Someone with her status and strength would find it far easier to give gold level medicine than an ordinary person.

He also knew that Bu Quan had sent him an invitation letter, saying they had a mercenary mission she wanted to discuss, giving her a motive.

Both of them combined led the dragonman youngster to this conjecture.

But Zi Di shook her head and told him and Zong Ge, “The gold level medicine isn’t from Bu Quan, but Snow Bird Harbor’s City Lord.”

This morning, after Zi Di and the dragonman youngster had their discussion, she had told the outside world that the dragonman youngster needed to rest.

Snow Bird Harbor’s City Lord had learned that the dragonman youngster had been wounded by Penetration Force and decided to gift this batch of medicine.

Zong Ge responded, “Penetration Force is very rare, but Snow Bird Harbor’s City Lord was able to immediately provide medicine focused on it. Doing this is not so easy.”

Zi Di analyzed, “According to our intelligence reports, Snow Bird Harbor’s City Lord was born into one of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s great noble clans.”

“His contacts and channels obviously surpass the Bayonet Gang’s.”

The dragonman youngster nodded, showing his agreement.

The top powers in Snow Bird Harbor were the four gold levels. It could be said that the first amongst them was considered to be Snow Bird Harbor’s City Lord.

Zi Di continued on, “The City Lord’s people didn’t just send this gold level medicine, he also invited the Lord Commander to dinner again. The messenger specifically mentioned that it will be worthwhile for you to attend.”

“Anyways, I already tactfully refused on your behalf.”

The dragonman youngster replied, “The right decision, since we declared we needed a period of self-cultivation, that will be carried out. If we opened up for the City Lord here, it would be hard to explain to others.”

Both the dragonman youngster and Zong Ge were treating this as a brief interlude.

After lunch, they continued practicing.

The dragonman youngster was visibly improving!

Because this time, he not only had the Grand Arena Medal helping him, but Zong Ge too.

Zong Ge was absolutely a qualified teacher.

To be more precise, he was an excellent military instructor!

Zong Ge was very savvy at training troops, and when it came to training the dragonman youngster, he was just as effective.

“In just an afternoon, your short spear mastery has gone from nothing to an experienced user. Under normal circumstances, a soldier needs at least 5 years to get to your level of mastery.”

The dragonman youngster’s progress was fairly immense, and Zong Ge was deeply moved by this.

“Another thing.” When Zong Ge said that he looked at the dragonman youngster with somewhat complicated feelings.

“You are a natural fighter!”

“You are very good at learning, and your comprehension is quite high. You have the highest comprehension out of anyone I’ve met.”

The dragonman youngster was somewhat hesitant, “Really? The more I use the Grand Arena Medal, the more I can feel its value. During training, there are many things that are hard to convey with or cannot be articulated with words. However, when I use the pertinent experience from the Grand Arena Medal, and pour that profound meaning into myself, I am given a shortcut that can bypass the language barrier.”

But Zong Ge shook his head, “I didn’t mean that. Don’t forget, I also used the Grand Arena Medal and know how the battle experience it gives helps me.”

“Your learning talent is quite astounding, perhaps you haven’t realized it yet.”

“Before you started absorbing battle experience from the Grand Arena Medal, it had happened many times already. So long as you put in the hours, you can learn the essentials and deduce many things from a single instance during training.”

“The more significant phenomenon I noticed is after you learned many moves, you were able to fine tune them.”

“Is that so?” The dragonman youngster’s eyes widened slightly, feeling puzzled.

Zong Ge affirmed it, “You don’t feel it yourself, but it is what makes you so valuable.”

“Because your body self corrects and adjusts.”

“The movements I taught you came from me, and my body is different from yours.”

“These movements suit me and were the product of hard work and numerous revisions, while you, who just started to learn and practice them, have grasped them.”

“But so long as you know them a little, your body can adjust itself, the adjustments are very small, but very fine. After these adjustments, these things will become things that fit you.”

“Don’t look down on this.”

“Many people don’t have such improvements and will use another’s things for their entire lives.”
