“Through long periods of diligent training, battle, and the polish of fierce fights, experts are able to adjust themselves.”

“A fighter’s talent is not just about their mind, but also about their bodies too.”

“This is different from those scholars.”

“I can say for sure that your fighting talent is quite good, quite astounding.”

The dragonman youngster couldn’t help but lament in his thoughts, “The Flame Dragon King bloodline is worthy of being legend level!”

Zong Ge continued, “Now think back to when you learned Penetration Force.”

“Your fighting talent should have played an important role in that.”

Zong Ge had trained many soldiers and had been born into a great noble family. He had a wide horizon and from childhood had experienced all kinds of elite figures.

His assessment of the dragonman youngster’s talent was apropos.

When the sunset glow filled the sky, Zong Ge stopped training.

“Based on today’s rate of progress, in three days you will have a deep mastery of all of the techniques I have taught you.”

“They will be combat ready.”

“I have never seen such unprecedented improvement. Don’t divulge this wantonly, especially to our mercenaries.”

“It will negatively affect and heavily impact their daily training.”

Zong Ge intentionally reminded him before leaving.

If others knew about this, that someone did in three days what took them five years, it was sure to severely disturb their state of mind.

The dragonman youngster was relying on the Grand Arena Medal, Zong Ge, as well as his outstanding fighting gifts to make mind startling training results.

The dragonman youngster and Zong Ge entered the dining room together.

At this time, Zi Di came over to them.

Zi Di brought over a blanket and put it on the table.

This attracted the dragonman youngster’s and Zong Ge’s gaze, while simultaneously making them feel a bit puzzled.

The blanket was a reddish brown, it was made of soft fur, tight knitting, portrayed an exquisite design, and emitted a silver level aura.

Zi Di presented it, “This is the warm current sleeping blanket. It itself has no healing capabilities, but it can increase the medicinal effect of other things. It can spread the effects of magic medicine evenly across a person to every corner of their body or concentrate it on an injury.”

“By a conservative estimate, the warm current sleeping blanket can improve the healing effect of something by 30%.”

“Its practical qualities are not in doubt. It can help any medicine heal you.”

After the dragonman youngster heard this, he suddenly understood what was going on.

“This kind of practical equipment should be seldom seen on the market.”

“Let me guess, we didn’t buy this today.”

“Did it come from the City Lord again?”

“You guessed correctly.” Zi Di forced a smile, still looking worried, “Tonight, Snow Bird Harbor’s City Lord sent people over with this present, saying he was giving it to you, Lord Commander, so you could heal.”

For a moment, the dragonman youngster and Zong Ge pursed their brows.

At noon, Snow Bird Harbor’s City Lord had sent gold level medicine. Then in the evening, he had sent another very practical silver level treasure as a gift.

Snow Bird Harbor’s City Lord’s attitude was excessively cordial.

As the saying goes: respect for the wise will have people looking for it.

“What is this City Lord after?” Zong Ge questioned.

“That’s what I’m worried about. Said Zi Di, “When the envoy delivered this present, they also passed on a message. Snow Bird Harbor’s City Lord will be coming to the port tonight to check on how the Lord Commander is doing.”

The dragonman youngster stopped chewing his food, and his brows deeply wrinkled.

He sensed power in Snow Bird Harbor’s City Lord’s cordial attitude.

The dragonman youngster had refused Snow Bird Harbor’s City Lord’s invitation to dinner and didn’t expect them to come pay a visit.

They were a gold level fighter and also the most powerful local presence in Snow Bird Harbor, yet they treated an outsider like the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps so warmly.

This kind of attitude and action was abnormal.

What did Snow Bird Harbor’s City Lord actually want?

In this world, there was never love without reason and never hatred without cause.

What did he want from the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps?

After pondering it for a moment, he couldn’t sit still.

After all, his Penetration Force injuries were bogus.

Based on the cadence and way Snow Bird Harbor’s City Lord was acting, he feared that the next step the former would take would be to bring over a doctor to heal the dragonman youngster.

Of course, that was not the main point.

The main point was what was Snow Bird Harbor’s City Lord’s intention?

The dragonman youngster had to take it seriously.

Snow Bird Harbor’s City Lord was not just a strong individual, but he also controlled genuine authority.

His attitude and conduct would greatly affect the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

After a moment of contemplation, the dragonman youngster said, “No need for Snow Bird Harbor’s City Lord to come over, I will go attend his dinner.”

Allowing Snow Bird Harbor’s City Lord to pay a visit would have a greater impact.

Excessive attention from an authoritative figure would often provoke unforeseen consequences.

Rather than doing that, it was better for the dragonman youngster to sally out first.

Furthermore, after today, he would accept the invitations of other large powers and converse with them.

When this was done, others would realize the dragonman youngster’s wounds weren’t so serious.

This would reduce the amount of time the dragonman youngster had to prepare for battle.

But at the same time, it would minimize the negative effects as much as possible.

This was the more dependable move.

Zong Ge’s gaze deepened, “In his invitation letter, Snow Bird Harbor’s City Lord stated that he had a very important matter to discuss with the commander.”

“Regardless of what this matter is, I can smell trouble brewing.”

It was clear this trouble could not be avoided.

Chapter 509: The City Lord’s Intention

Due to the dragonman youngster challenging Teng Dong Lang, like the wind and the waves, his influence had already spread across all of Snow Bird Harbor.

As these winds and waves got bigger, it was faintly trending towards backfiring on the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

After the dragonman youngster made his decision, he dispatched a messenger to notify Snow Bird Harbor’s City Lord.

Night fell and starlight filled the sky.

The dragonman youngster publicly left the Love Triangle, then took a carriage to Snow Bird Harbor’s government prefecture.

He was led into the banquet hall.

The banquet hall had splendid lights, exquisite and luxurious decorations, and was full of gorgeous elven art.

Snow Bird Harbor’s City Lord was an unprepossessing snow elf.

All elves were generally beautiful.

This made Snow Bird Harbor’s City Lord somewhat unusual.

The City Lord enthusiastically entertained the dragonman youngster.

The banquet’s dishes were very fine; it was mostly made up of seafood, but there was some venison from the inland, dragon meat, and so on.

Ice Sculpture Island was very wide and had areas that could be counted as inland places.

The dragonman youngster had eaten dinner before coming, but he still couldn’t help himself from feasting in the banquet hall.

Because these dishes had come from a magic chef’s hands.

Not only was the taste exquisite, completely beyond any food the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had, but to a fighter, these magic materials also helped with their cultivation.

As he dined, the dragonman youngster inquired about the City Lord’s intentions.

After all, the City Lord had an important matter he wanted to discuss with the dragonman youngster.

But the City Lord didn’t give a direct answer, using clever methods to shift the topic.

At the end of the dinner, after the servants provided dessert, the City Lord waved his hand and had his two servants withdraw completely.

“Now we are only two here.” The City Lord smiled at the dragonman youngster.

The dragonman youngster couldn’t help but sit upright.

It seemed that the City Lord finally wanted to divulge his true intentions.

However, the City Lord’s next words stupefied the dragonman youngster.

“Commander Long Fu, did you know that during your duel with Xiong Ju, the Bayonet Gang secretly meddled to put you at a disadvantage?”

Seeing how the dragonman youngster reacted, Snow Bird Harbor’s City Lord laughed, “It was the Evil Eye.”

As he said that, a magic image appeared above the middle of the dining table.

The image showed the duel between the dragonman youngster and Xiong Ju.

A section of it was replaying itself over and over again.

It was when the Evil Eye in Xiong Ju’s chest attacked with its spiritual ray. After the dragonman youngster was struck, he sank into a fainting vertigo for a time.

Snow Bird Harbor’s City Lord pointed his finger at the Evil Eye in the magic image, “This equipment came from one of the Bayonet Gang’s ringleaders, it is an alchemy item that contains the power of the Abyss.”

“But at the time, Xiong Ju wasn’t the one who activated it.”

“It was the Evil Eye’s real master, he secretly casted in the viewing room, controlling it to attack you, Commander Long Fu.”

The image continued to be broadcast.

Next, Xiong Ju exposed a resentful expression, he pulled the Evil Eye out of his chest and crushed it between his fingers.

Snow Bird Harbor’s City Lord continued, “I know Xiong Ju well. He does not like anyone who desecrates a heroic duel.”

“Even though it is one of the Bayonet Gang’s six ringleaders who helped him.”

“Evidently, he was furious at the time, therefore, due to that anger, he destroyed the Evil Eye.”

“But when that happened, we lost our biggest piece of evidence.”

“After the Evil Eye attacked, the Snow Bird Dueling Arena also detected it.”

“The dueling arena’s master immediately dispatched investigators to look into it.”

“But the investigation was thwarted by the Bayonet Gang’s leader Teng Dong Lang.”

“At the time, you and Xiong Ju were reaching the end of your duel. Teng Dong Lang and the dueling arena master reached a secret agreement. I don’t know what price he paid, but in the end, the dueling arena master abandoned the investigation.”

“And so, the truth was covered up.”

“Commander Long Fu, the recordings of your duel with Xiong Ju has been selling very well right now.”

“When the public saw it, they universally believed that the Evil Eye went berserk, and Xiong Ju was forced to destroy it.”

“The Bayonet Gang’s crime has been concealed.”

“But I believe that as one of the people involved, the one who was in this duel, you surely sensed it to some extent.”

The dragonman youngster’s brows deeply pursed, and for a moment, he silently muttered to himself.

He had extrapolated this topic already.

Through the Grand Arena Medal’s battle experience, he had confirmed his inference.

Now, Snow Bird Harbor’s City Lord wanted to help him look into this matter, but the dragonman youngster didn’t feel the slightest bit happy, nor did he feel understood or supported.

He couldn’t help but feel his heart chill.

During the duel with Xiong Ju, the dragonman youngster gained a deep understanding of the customs of heroic duels.

Heroic duels were a social custom that were deeply ingrained in the hearts of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s populace and could not be tarnished.

If the Evil Eye was successfully investigated, then it would deal a heavy blow to the Bayonet Gang.

It could even be said that it could destroy them.

Even if Teng Dong Lang lost to the dragonman youngster in their heroic duel, that outcome couldn’t match the impact of this.

If Teng Dong Lang lost his duel, the Bayonet Gang’s prestige and interests would take a massive dip.

While if the Evil Eye issue was verified, the Bayonet Gang’s foundational existence would be strongly rattled.

The people would be disgusted with the Bayonet Gang and despise any Bayonet Gang member from the depths of their hearts. The vast majority of its members, and even ringleaders like Xiong Ju and Spring Boxer, would also feel deep shame.

Those on the righteous path are well supported, while those who stray from the proper course have scant aid.

Everyone in the Bayonet Gang would be spurned and wouldn’t have a shred of reputation left.

Perhaps ordinary people would still be intimidated by the Bayonet Gang’s military forces and continue to pay protection money, but their mentality while doing so would be completely different.

Equal powers would believe that the Bayonet Gang didn’t have the qualifications to hold its current power, position, and benefits.

The masses were bound to rise up and attack it.

The Bayonet Gang was just a gang; it was difficult for them to withstand attacks from all sides.

The most likely outcome was its defeat and even elimination.

It was precisely his awareness of how serious this issue was that the dragonman youngster didn’t perform an in-depth investigation into the Evil Eye and instead decided to give up on it.

Because if he looked into it, he was bound to trigger a fierce response from the Bayonet Gang.

When it came to that, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ and Bayonet Gang’s fight would escalate to irreconcilable adversaries.

This was extremely unfavorable for the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

The dragonman youngster didn’t want to investigate it, but Snow Bird Harbor’s City Lord wanted to grab hold of this weak point and verify it.

Snow Bird Harbor’s City Lord’s evil intentions for the Bayonet Gang was very clear.

The dragonman youngster contemplated for a good while, then with a deliberate tone, he carefully replied, “Perhaps it is just as you said City Lord, the Bayonet Gang secretly interfered in my heroic duel.”

“But as someone involved, I don’t have any deep feelings about it.”

“I also don’t have any proof that the Evil Eye was activated by someone else.”

“If I suspected it, I would have flared up at the time.”

“May I ask if you have any conclusive evidence in the Evil Eye matter, City Lord?”

Snow Bird Harbor’s City Lord looked deeply at the dragonman youngster for a while, then broke the silence, “Conclusive evidence? Of course it exists. I believe that as long as I am willing to invest the time, energy, manpower, and resources, I will be able to obtain it.”

“After it happens, there are facts. These facts will have traces.”

“The crucial bit is that if I want to open an investigation, I need to have a justification. As one involved in this, you can raise your doubts, and when you do, I will organize an investigation team.”

When the dragonman youngster heard that, he shook his head in refusal, “I don’t have those doubts.”

Snow Bird Harbor’s City Lord became silent again.

He let out a long sigh, then used a melancholy and aggrieved tone to say, “Commander Long Fu, you are an outsider, you don’t know the aggregate crimes the Bayonet Gang has done. It is Snow Bird Harbor’s biggest malignant tumor, bar none!”

Snow Bird Harbor’s City Lord seemed to recall something, “In the beginning, the Bayonet Gang was just a young thief organization.”

“Those thieves roamed everywhere in the city committing crimes, and through pickpocketing, they accumulated their starting capital from the stolen property of others.”

“With this capital, these thieves dedicated themselves to strengthening themselves, improving their equipment and recruiting more criminals.”

“Gradually, they were no longer satisfied with being small pickpockets and began to start doing assassinations and robberies.”

“Many innocent victims were murdered and robbed because they walked down an alley alone, then had their bodies thrown into the sewer, drainage channels, and off the coast.

“The Bayonet Gang was too rampant and eventually drew in an extermination campaign.”

“They faded away from Snow Bird Harbor for a time, but then their ashes burned once more afterwards.”

“After reorganizing, the Bayonet Gang became more treacherous and cunning, they decided to reduce their brutal robbery and murders, and changed to extortion and blackmail.”

Snow Bird Harbor’s City Lord’s finger tapped the dinner table lightly, changing the magic image in the middle of it.

A middle aged snow elf appeared before the young man’s eyes.

The City Lord introduced them, “This is a rich snow elf merchant who was set up by them, they lost a lot of money gambling and owed a high interest loan. After he was extorted for thousands of gold coins, his property was annexed by the Bayonet Gang.”

The magic image changed again, this time it showed a bandaged fat human.

“This was the owner of the Soaring Ox Butchery.”

“His butcher shop didn’t just sell raw meat, but also processed and cooked meat.”

“The shop owner had a secret recipe passed down by his forefathers, and his processed cooked beef could be regarded as a magic dish. Every beef slice was as thin as a cicada’s wing, and if they were blown on, they could flutter in the wind. The cooked beef not only tasted great, but it also helped cultivation to an exemplary extent. Many transcendents were long time customers and his business was prosperous.”

“Soaring Ox Butchery provoked the Bayonet Gang’s greed. They moved directly and violently, sending a dozen people with machetes to cut the shop owner down.”

“The shop owner was an iron level fighter, but due to being heavily outnumbered, he was seriously wounded while protecting his secret recipe and didn’t relent.”

“But the Bayonet Gang had plenty of methods.”

“They kidnapped the shop owner’s only child, and with them, they blackmailed and extorted the shop owner.”

“The shop owner asked the government for help.”

“But Snow Bird Harbor’s government at the time had been infiltrated by the Bayonet Gang and had a serious problem with bribes. Many officials were in the Bayonet Gang’s hands and had to follow them obediently.”

“The Soaring Ox Butchery owner’s pleas were only met with the Bayonet Gang’s admonishments.”

“They explicitly repeated every word the Soaring Ox Butchery owner said to the government.”

“In his endless despair, the shop owner was forced to bow his head to those criminals and handed over his secret recipe.”

“The next day, the entire family was found rotting in a gutter.”

Snow Bird Harbor’s City Lord deeply sighed, “The Bayonet Gang profits immensely from forced deals, blackmail, high interest loans, among other things.”

“Now they are Snow Bird Harbor’s foremost gang under the guise of innumerable casinos, restaurants, dueling arenas, and other assets.”

“These were the people’s blood, sweat and tears!”

The dragonman youngster gazed at the magic image, his look as deep as water.

He was a believer of the Justice God and wanted to walk the path of righteousness.

He believed these crimes had happened.

Because Snow Bird Harbor’s City Lord had provided detailed information that was completely verifiable.

His feeling of justice compelled the dragonman youngster to eradicate the Bayonet Gang.

But he forcefully restrained himself.

Because as the survivors’ leader and the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ commander, he shouldered a heavy responsibility and couldn’t let emotions affect his decisions.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had come to Snow Bird Harbor to contact the empire’s higher-ups and redeem themselves, while also establishing a footing here at the same time so they could grow and do their best to strengthen themselves against an unknown future.

Since they wanted to achieve triumphant progress, they also had to go steady and strike hard.

Therefore, the dragonman youngster decided to challenge Teng Dong Lang, while simultaneously tightly controlling the intensity of this fight to prevent the conflict from escalating.

Escalating the fight with the Bayonet Gang to irreconcilable adversity was obviously not wise.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps could not waste time like this.

After a moment of silence, the dragonman youngster tactfully refused the City Lord.

He stated that the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was only a small power and was unwilling to fight to the death over mere suspicions.

Snow Bird Harbor’s City Lord sincerely replied, “Commander Long Fu, I understand your decision. I can see from that that you are an outstanding commander.”

“I hope you can reconsider it.”

“No need to rush to give me a reply.”

“Outside of that, we can also discuss some other business. Such as this…”

As he said that, Snow Bird Harbor’s City Lord took out a scroll, pushing it across the dining table to the dragonman youngster.

The dragonman youngster unfurled it and noticed it was a list of items.

“I am a City Lord and have many channels. I can provide you with many resources that are not commonly seen on the market.”

This gave the dragonman youngster a feeling of déjà vu.

The dragonman youngster looked at it, unable to take his eyes off it.

Snow Bird Harbor’s City Lord was worthy of being a senior noble, his strength was robust, his foundation was deep, and his network and relationships were vast.

The dragonman youngster saw there were many alchemy treasures on the list.

He wanted to buy three gold level weapons, a longbow, a harpoon, and a set of darts.

There was also gold level armor, a round shield.

Apart from those, the list was a cornucopia of delights with its myriad alchemy materials.

Chapter 510: Armor Prohibition, Slavery Ban

The list of alchemy materials was the longest.

The Ice Sculpture Kingdom had a well-developed alchemy industry and possessed many outstanding alchemy talents. This meant that its alchemy marketplace was also massive.

The dragonman youngster’s gaze suddenly stopped when he saw some undead materials.

These materials were extinct on the marketplace, but Snow Bird Harbor’s City Lord could get them through his means.

Why was he selling them?

Wasn’t he worried about the risks?

The entire world hunted necromancers, and the Ice Sculpture Kingdom was no exception.

The dragonman youngster didn’t know what was going on, but Zi Di did.

As a matter of fact, before the undead school of magic had been established, undead materials had been sold all over the world.

Fire type alchemy materials weren’t just used to make fire magical items, and undead materials were the same.

Although the item list had a few undead materials on it, they had a wide usage in the field of daily alchemy.

From this, it could be seen how high end Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s alchemy marketplace was.

Despite the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps having a strong demand for undead materials, the dragonman youngster chose the more dependable option and immediately turned his gaze away.

He continued to be enthralled in his reading.

For a time, Snow Bird Harbor’s City Lord calmly sipped his wine very patiently.

The dragonman youngster read it through once, then reread it.

After two readings, he decided.

He suggested a transaction, and in the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ name, he first purchased a large amount of magic beast meat.

His battle qi cultivation needed him to eat a lot of path attribute magic beast meat. Zong Ge needed steel rhinoceros magic beast meat.

The mercenary corps added Chi Lai and Men Shi, two silver levels. They also need magic beast meat for their battle qi techniques.

There was also the Quick Knife and Backwater battle squads whose daily training was very arduous. If they were provided with plentiful magic beast meat, it would greatly improve their training efficiency. In addition, nutritious meals could also reduce complaints from the battle squad members to a certain extent.

It could be predicted that in the future, when the dragonman youngster’s and Zong Ge’s level improved and the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps expanded and recruited, along with the big guy and others suddenly distinguishing themselves, their demand for magic beast meat and other transcendent ingredients would grow bigger.

After the magic beast meat, the dragonman youngster purchased a large batch of alchemy materials.

This included all of the dragon corpses available.

It also included some undead materials.

Then, he bought all of the gold level equipment.

There were four, the longbow, harpoon, darts, and round shield.

The scale of this spending startled Snow Bird Harbor’s City Lord a bit, causing him to laugh, “It seems that after your corps captured the Iron Lump, you have quite the harvest.”

The dragonman youngster nodded, calmly admitted, “Indeed.”

One of the goals of the battle of Weeping Pine Island was to wash their gains from Two Eyes Island’s seafloor clean.

Seeing Snow Bird Harbor’s City Lord’s reaction now proved that the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ mission during that battle had been accomplished.

The dragonman youngster slightly explained the reason why he was buying gold level equipment.

“My showdown with Teng Dong Lang is coming. The better my equipment, the greater my chances of victory.”

Those words caused a smile to creep up Snow Bird Harbor’s City Lord’s face, he nodded in agreement, “Commander Long Fu, Xiong Ju has already been defeated by you, and the remaining ringleaders are not your match.”

“I await your fight with Teng Dong Lang.”

The City Lord paused, then added, “If you have any special needs, you can put them forth. Perhaps I can use some relationships to give you some help there.”

The dragonman youngster nodded, immediately saying in passing, “As a matter of fact, we need a store to sell some merchandise. Of course, we are just a small power and have some merchandise that can’t be put into the public purview.”

The merchandise out of the public purview was naturally pointing towards smuggled armaments.

The dragonman youngster said this to lay the groundwork for dragon rider, and also to give them a platform to sell alchemy arrows and other munitions in the future.

Snow Bird Harbor’s City Lord was the bigger local tyrant than Teng Dong Lang.

Every power craved outstanding armaments.

Snow Bird Harbor’s City Lord was no exception.

The City Lord nodded, “I can make the decision and sell a prosperous shop to your corps.”

“But forgive my bluntness, engaging in a business requires giving sufficient consideration to the environment around it.”

“At least wait until your duel with Teng Dong Lang is decided, Commander Long Fu, the environment will be considered stable then.”

The dragonman youngster thanked the City Lord for his advice.

After reflecting on it, he had another request for Snow Bird Harbor’s City Lord, he wanted to buy a gold level short spear.

Along with blank magic formation slabs.

“If you have gold level armor, that would be better.” The dragonman youngster added.

Snow Bird Harbor’s City Lord shook his head with a laugh, “Gold level armor is difficult to purchase. The list having that round shield is already a considerable rarity.”

“Don’t have much hope of buying a short spear in a short time span either.”

The dragonman youngster was puzzled, “As far as I know, the Ice Sculpture Kingdom has flourishing alchemy technology, and also has many alchemy talents. Since the market demands this kind of equipment, don’t tell me none of them want to make money?”

Snow Bird Harbor’s City Lord explained, “You are fresh off the boat and don’t know about this matter. The kingdom has explicit decrees on alchemy that all alchemy workshops in the kingdom have to strictly comply with. One of the ten decrees stipulates that the amount of gold level armor the Ice Sculpture Kingdom produces every year cannot exceed 27. We commonly refer to it as the armor prohibition.”

The dragonman youngster thought about it, somewhat understanding.

The armor prohibition was obviously a decree the Ice Sculpture Kingdom made to protect its rule.

Refining arrows, bullets, and other weapons took less materials and time than armor.

Many small weapons were far easier to transport and hide than armor.

Therefore, when regulating these, it was better to regulate armor.

Regulating armor was more cost efficient.

Apart from that reason, the Ice Sculpture Kingdom was also considering its people’s livelihood.

Regardless of whether it was a hunting group, fishing boat, mercenary corps, or so on, all of them needed weapons, and nowadays, pirates were getting more rampant by the day. All of them expended lots of arrows and bullets.

If they put a ban on weapons, then that would affect every profit everywhere, affect national defense, and impact the national economy.

The amount of 27 pieces of armor under the prohibition was decided after challenging negotiations and the weighing of the pros and cons between all parties.

As for the gold level armor that was made, the dragonman youngster could guess with his tail that those in positions of power would be the first to receive them.

“My corps has an alchemy magic ship and also a gold level cannon that needs repairs. If they aren’t fixed, they cannot be used.”

The City Lord nodded, “I can arrange for master alchemists to come do repairs for you. The repair cost will be 20% below market value.”

Of course, the dragonman youngster didn’t forget to mention alchemist talents.

Currently, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ greatest deficiency was of master alchemists and alchemists.

But strangely, the City Lord’s list didn’t have any slaves.

After the dragonman youngster asked, the City Lord answered that it was due to an Ice Sculpture Kingdom decree.

“My country strictly prohibits slaves on the marketplace.”

“All slaves were nationalized.”

“This is my country’s slave ban.”

Under those circumstances, the dragonman youngster regretfully abandoned the matter.

After ten minutes, the transaction was struck and both sides determined the time and place of delivery in detail.

The dragonman youngster looked out the window, and upon seeing the night was deeper, asked the City Lord if he could take his leave.

Snow Bird Harbor’s City Lord gave the dragonman youngster a warning before parting, “Commander Long Fu.”

“Please be careful of Teng Dong Lang.”

“According to my understanding of him, he will never resign himself to being in a passive situation.”

“While you are recuperating, you need to pay more attention.”

“He won’t let you calmly heal. Therefore, I sent you medicine and the blanket to lessen the possibility of attacks.”

“He will make a move.”

“Gangs often use dirty and despicable methods.”

“I’ll also reveal some secret information to you.”

“Teng Dong Lang and Hua Tang have a secret relationship.”

“Perhaps you don’t know who Hua Tang is.”

“He is an excellent thief who has been active all over the Ice Sculpture Kingdom. Many city governments have been robbed by his hands.”

“The magician tower cannot detect him either.”

“He has been wanted throughout the kingdom for a long time, but after a dozen years, Hua Tang has never been caught.”

The dragonman youngster memorized the name Hua Tang.

Previously, although he had purchased a lot of information from the Thieves Guild, this information was centered on Snow Bird Harbor.

This resulted in a big time thief like Hua Tang who committed crimes everywhere and didn’t have a fixed residence being overlooked.

The dragonman youngster decided on the spot that starting tomorrow, he would buy information on Hua Tang.

The night sky had many starry specks.

The lights along the way illuminated the streets.

The carriage’s wheels rolled across the flagstones, issuing a rumbling noise.

After the dragonman youngster left the government mansion, he specifically chose the road with the least pedestrians.

Even so, the streets were still clamorous with the sounds of people.

The more bustling a city was, the livelier the nights were.

Snow Bird Harbor was one of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s eight harbors, and so it was naturally very bustling.

Under this bustling, the dragonman youngster sensed the tumultuous and bubbling undercurrents.

When he recalled his conversation with Snow Bird Harbor’s City Lord, the dragonman youngster sighed to himself.

“I didn’t expect that the Snow Bird Harbor’s City Lord would have such a deep hostility to the Bayonet Gang.”

“The prior inquiries and information reports didn’t indicate that at all.”

“As expected, simple information purchases cannot ascertain this kind of secret relationship and the attitude of a high ranking person.”

“The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps needs a competent person that can scout out the truth and crucial facts.”

Snow Bird Harbor’s City Lord had invited the dragonman youngster to dinner to use the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps as reasonable grounds to attack the Bayonet Gang.

“Snow Bird Harbor’s City Lord has hidden his enmity well; this is the mind of a profound person. To deal with the Bayonet Gang, he must have already prepared many methods and only needs a sensible justification.”

“If I agreed, then Snow Bird Harbor would get caught up in a massive internal struggle.”

The dragonman youngster knew that Snow Bird Harbor’s City Lord wanted to exploit him.

But at the same time, the dragonman youngster knew this was a rare opportunity.

If the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps aligned themselves with Snow Bird Harbor’s government, and were victorious, after the Bayonet Gang was suppressed, a massive gap would appear.

They only needed to take a fraction of it for the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps to completely root themselves here.

This was something many people desired but couldn’t obtain.

Generally speaking, without such an opportunity, it would take at least two to three generations of hard work for someone to reach the upper layers of power in Snow Bird Harbor.

This rare stroke of luck also implied immense risks.

If the dragonman youngster had agreed, then the entire Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps would be on the foremost front lines of it.

The Bayonet Gang would want to eliminate the Dragon Lion Mercenary corps as quickly as possible.

While Snow Bird Harbor’s City Lord had to protect the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps to defend crucial witnesses and reasonable grounds for making war.

Two of Snow Bird Harbor’s big native powers were centered around the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, constantly tearing, tugging, tussling, and grappling against each other.

It would last until a winner was decided.

Regrettably, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was not a normal mercenary corps, nor was it the transportation power of an arms merchant.

Snow Bird Harbor’s City Lord’s error of judgement happened because of that.

“It seems I cannot put off my heroic duel with Teng Dong Lang.”

“Snow Bird Harbor might become a troubled city.”

“The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps needs to attain qualifications as soon as possible and enter into the Ice Sculpture Kingdom proper.”

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ destination was the Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s capital.

However, as foreigners, being able to rush to the capital was impossible.

The laws and institutions here did not permit newcomers to travel to important areas like the capital.

To go there, they needed to be audited.

Many things would be audited, such as how long they would be staying in the Ice Sculpture Kingdom, what they would be doing, if they had anyone vouching for them, what kind of property they had, and so on.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had registered with the Mercenary Guild and could rely on them to reduce the relevant indexes to travel to the capital at an earlier date.

But the lowest criterion was staying for 12 days, having a 3 star mercenary corps rank, along with the local Mercenary Guild’s recommendation letter and vouching.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had the Anti-investigation and Prophecy and Deceptive Disguise spells and were capable of ignoring the law and sneaking into the capital.

But after everyone in the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps deliberated, they felt that plan was too extreme.

On the one hand, the blackmail bizarrely vanished without a reply, and none of them knew what problem had arisen.

On the other hand, the survivors had to consider this: if their communication with imperial higherups failed, it was possible they would be hunted down for the treasures.

Therefore, the survivors had to keep a firm grip on time and every opportunity to increase their fighting strength.

After comprehensive consideration, the dragonman youngster and the others decided temporarily settling in Snow Bird Harbor and grow during this time.

During this time, they would absorb Snow Bird Harbor’s resources to develop, liquidating their riches and turning it into strength as much as possible.

If they could create a way out in Snow Bird Harbor, then that would be even better.

The Ice Sculpture Kingdom was vast, and completely different from Snake Rat Island and Two Eyes Island.

On the previous island, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps could flee with the Deep Sea Monster Fish.

But in the interior of Ice Sculpture Island, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps would be far from the Deep Sea Monster Fish. This way out was now suspended and temporarily lost.

Chapter 511: Stirring Up Trouble

Under the starry night sky, the dragonman youngster returned to the port.

At the same time, in a courtyard.

Teng Dong Lang was drinking ice crystal wine and enjoying sashimi alone.

“Your tastes haven’t changed over the many years.” A voice suddenly came from behind him.

Teng Dong Lang paused, then stood up and looked behind him with pleasant surprise.

He saw a slender silhouette standing under the shadows of the grapevine frames.

The silhouette wore a white robe with all kinds of exquisite and gaudy flowers threaded into it.

His face was wearing a silver fox mask.

In its eye slits, there were pink pupils.

In the dim light of night, they were especially striking.

“Hua Tang, why are you here?” Teng Dong Lang’s face blossomed with a smile.

“Naturally there is an important matter I have to do here. Do you remember the clues you gave me?” The silver white fox mask left the shadows and slowly walked to Teng Dong Lang.

“What clues? I have given you a lot of clues over the last few years.”

“Chicken Cluck Cave.” Hua Tang said the place’s name.

Teng Dong Lang immediately raised his brow, “So you found the Saint Thief’s relic? Don’t tell me it was a fake.”

Hua Tang shook his head, his tone serious, “This time it is unlikely, I am quite certain.”

Teng Dong Lang involuntarily laughed, “You said that several times before.”

“We aren’t talking about me, but you. It seems you’ve run into a bit of trouble.” Hua Tang stepped in front of Teng Dong Lang.

Teng Dong Lang waved his hand, “It isn’t really a problem. Just some small time mercenary corps, at most their backer is somewhat complex.”

“We rarely see each other. Don’t let that matter spoil things. Come, let’s drink together.”

Hua Tang laughed, and took a seat with Teng Dong Lang.

When he woke up, the dragonman youngster felt refreshed.

He had completely recovered.

The Flame Dragon King bloodline once again revealed its legend level quality.

At breakfast, a subordinate handed over a purchased intelligence report.

This information report was about Hua Tang.

It had a lot in it but was pretty cheap.

Hua Tang’s warrant for arrest was posted everywhere in the Ice Sculpture Kingdom, and stories about him circulated far and wide, thus information on him, relatively speaking, was not that valuable.

The dragonman youngster ate while thumbing through it.

Each case spoke of the beautiful skill of this gold level thief.

Even under impossibly tight security, he always miraculously stole all sorts of treasures.

Many times, Hua Tang would distribute the things he stole to the poor in a city.

Hua Tang had a bombastic style.

For example, his attire.

His clothes were iconic.

He wore a spacious white gown adorned with several flowers.

He wore a fox mask with a silver texture.

Whenever he moved through the night, his white gown was like a floating cloud with flowers swirling in it. The mask glittered with silvery light, as if its owner didn’t fear being conspicuous in the night.

Every time he committed a crime, he would leave behind a vibrant freshly plucked flower at the crime scene.

In addition, many other times, before he robbed someone, he would send a warning letter to the treasure owner.

The dragonman youngster analyzed it in his thoughts, “It seems he wasn’t satisfied with ordinary thievery and would raise the difficultly of his own accord. He constantly challenges himself and the world, and from this challenge, he feels joy.”

“Is he really just a gold level cultivator?’

“Despite weaving through the Ice Sculpture Kingdom for many years, he has remained uncaught.”

“Looking at these theft cases is like looking at a compilation of stories.”

The dragonman youngster’s gaze gradually congealed.

“Currently, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ preventative measures is mainly composed of patrolling mercenaries, along with Zong Ge and the others standing guard. Secretly, there are alchemy puppets scattered all over the place to keep watch. Then on the distant seafloor, there is also the Deep Sea Monster Fish.”

“But these defenses cannot stop Hua Tang.”

Places with far more defensive measures that were tighter and stronger than the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had been robbed by Hua Tang.

But they still couldn’t stop Hua Tang, to him, they existed in name only.

The dragonman youngster had no choice but to predict, “If Teng Dong Lang asks Hua Tang to act, how likely will it be for him to attack my mercenary corps?”

It couldn’t be predicted.

Because he didn’t know the relationship between Teng Dong Lang and Hua Tang.

He couldn’t help but feel how tight time was.

“I have to get stronger as soon as possible! I must carry out my heroic duel with Teng Dong Lang as soon as possible. If I put it off, I fear there will be many long nights.”

Despite the dragonman youngster holding the initiative in the heroic duel, it was difficult to change that it was the weak fighting against the strong.

The more the dragonman youngster knew, the more he could perceive the risks.

After hurriedly eating breakfast, he made his way to the training cabin to spar with Zong Ge.

Scarcely half an hour into training, noise came from outside the ship.

The Bayonet Gang ringleader Ban Lan Gen, bringing along Bing Kuai and a group of underlings, had openly come to the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ ships, loudly shouting a challenge.

Ever since the dragonman youngster had publicly challenged Teng Dong Lang, the two had been at the heart of the struggle and had their every movement being closely watched.

Ban Lan Gen and his people stormed in and raised a ruckus, afraid that someone wouldn’t hear.

Therefore, when he was publicizing his challenge, there was already a ring of people enjoying the show.

Ban Lan Gen didn’t challenge the dragonman youngster, to prevent gossip. Because the masses knew the dragonman youngster had been injured by Penetration Force and was in the process of training.

So, Ban Lan Gen challenged Zong Ge – the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ auxiliary commander.

The dragonman youngster and Zong Ge were startled and had to suspend their training.

Zong Ge naturally could not accept this challenge.

He had to teach the dragonman youngster, and with him there, the dragonman youngster’s military power would be improved more.

Zong Ge was too busy to get out.

But direct refusal was inappropriate.

That would cause the public to believe that Zong Ge was a coward who wouldn’t dare meet a challenge.

It would trouble the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ newly established prestige.

“This is easy to manage, we can rebuff Ban Lan Gen’s challenge the same way Teng Dong Long did mine.” The dragonman youngster proposed after some thought.

Zong Ge agreed with this method and quickly appeared on the side of the ship.

He looked down at Ban Lan Gen and stated, “You are just one of six ringleaders of the Bayonet Gang, while I am the number two person in the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.”

“Treeman who was defeated in a single punch, you must first prove your strength.”

When Ban Lan Gen’s old wound was ripped open, he immediately became ashamed and angry.

Men Shi and Chi Lai had received the dragonman youngster’s order and came out in a timely fashion, saying one after the other, “If you want to challenge our auxiliary commander, you have to get past me first.”

“Let me measure your strength first!”

Ban Lan Gen was forced to acknowledge this arrangement.

Men Shi went first and left with Ban Lan Gen to the nearest dueling arena.

The crowd of onlookers followed behind them, loudly departing the docks.

The dragonman youngster and Zong Ge returned to the cabin to continue their training.

A half an hour later, someone else paid a visit.

It was a dock worker.

He deliberately made things difficult, urging them to hand over their anchorage fee.

According to what he said, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had to pay double the fee due to incomplete reporting, polluting the docks, among other reasons.

Zi Di came out to hand over the entire sum in order to resolve this matter.

But the dock worker didn’t let the matter drop, “kindly” reminding them before leaving: the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps needs to go to the dock administration, submit a comprehensive report, resolve your ships’ environmental pollution, and the other listed problems. Otherwise, you will face fines, and if you have repeated offenses, you will face incremental fines that will become quite expensive.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had 60,000 gold coins, so naturally they wouldn’t care about such a small sum.

But this kind of method really made people sick.

It was very clear that the Bayonet Gang was already beginning to exert themselves, these acts exposing the foundation of this local bully.

In the morning, Zi Di had to deal with four more officials making things difficult so the dragonman youngster could keep his mind on training.

But at noon, Zi Di stopped blocking them.

Because the Bayonet Gang asked patrol fleet troops to come over.

The snow elf investigation team had a tough attitude, claiming that someone reported the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had concealed heavy weapons and didn’t report them, so they needed to reinspect the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ ships thoroughly again.

The snow elf investigation team came from the government.

Zi Di thought about it and found that it wouldn’t be good to obstruct them. If they did, it was bound to lead to charges of resisting investigation, which would confirm their pretexts that the mercenary corps was hiding something.

Zi Di was forced to allow the investigation team to carry out their survey.

And this time, the snow elf team was far nastier than normal.

They did not do a survey, but a search.

Wherever they went, there was nothing not turned over and in disarray.

The mercenaries were indignant and many of them raised their fists, wanting to teach these arrogant snow elves a lesson.

But Zi Di strictly ordered them to stop.

If a conflict happened between then and the snow elf investigation squad, they would undoubtedly fall into the Bayonet Gang’s plot. The latter was eager for the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps to get caught up in this conflict and were positive to have prepared many follow up tricks and traps they were waiting for the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps to fall into.

Seeing the mercenaries’ persistent forbearance, the snow elf investigators became more aggressive.

The inspectors reached the water pigmen, causing the team leader to pinch his nose, saying with disgust, “That strange odor, it turned out to be a group of pigs.”

“What are you looking at! Calling you pigs is speaking highly of you.”

“A group of bumpkins…”

“Are you still staring? Hehehe.”

“Come, stand over there, I suspect you are carrying and hiding hazardous materials on you, I need to do a body search!”

The investigation team leader dragged out a female water pigman from the group.

The investigation team leader circled around the female water pig a few times, extended an examination rod, then started to poke the water pigman’s body, followed by lifting the hem of her clothes.

The female water pigman indignantly growled.

The surrounding water pigmen also were enraged and began to close in.

The investigation team leader burst into laughter and glared at the water pigmen around him, sharply reprimanding, “You dirty and contemptible beastmen are still thinking of resisting investigation?”

“Come and fight then!”

“You looking to fight, to beat up an Ice Sculpture Kingdom snow elf investigator and violently resist the law?’

“Hm?!”

The investigation team leader chided, causing the water pigmen to hesitate.

The female pigman’s eyes were bloodshot and also glistening with teardrops.

She shuddered, clenched her fists, and steeled herself.

The investigation team leader’s mouth twitched up, slowly raised the hem of her clothes to expose her private parts when suddenly, a figure charged at him from behind.

The investigation team leader felt a strong wind from behind, and one of his companions shouted, “Captain, be careful.”

He quickly turned around to see a fist fill his perspective.

Ding!

The staunch fist ruthlessly knocked the investigation captain to the ground.

He had been hit with so much force that the investigation captain’s head smashed into the floor.

He coughed out blood and also some teeth.

The beaten investigation team leader wasn’t angry, instead he laughed like one whose scheme had prevailed, “Hehehe, I knew you stupid beastmen couldn’t stand being provoked.”

“Idiots, you group of idiots! You actually dared to beat up an investigator!”

“Men, arrest them!!”

Chapter 512: Your Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps is Done For

Hearing their leader’s command, the snow elf investigation team all took out shackles and fetters.

The investigation captain looked malevolent, but in actuality, he was quite proud of himself.

He had been corrupted by the Bayonet Gang long ago.

The Bayonet Gang used him to make things difficult for the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

The investigation team leader didn’t act alone, rather he had been given a special mission from his superior.

This mission was to pick a quarrel as much as possible, then detain some of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ members. By taking them away and detaining them, they could threaten the mercenary corps.

The investigation team checked a few ships and didn’t find any suitable targets. At least until the team leader saw the water pigmen.

The second the investigation captain saw the water pigmen, he was delighted, “This Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps is indeed chaotic, it even has water pigmen! This isn’t my fault.”

After purposely provoking these water pigmen, as expected, they fell into the trap.

The investigation captain’s mission was finally accomplished.

He immediately relaxed.

The investigation team approached the water pigmen.

The water pigman showed their teeth subconsciously wanting to resist the chains and shackles and began to get restless.

A fight could happen at any moment.

Thump, thump, thump.

The water pigman leader charged into the cabin with heavy steps.

When he saw the investigation captain’s miserable condition, his heart thudded, knowing what terrible event had occurred.

“Don’t touch him!” He yelled at the water pigmen, “Are you insane? Do you know what the consequences are? Don’t cause trouble for the mercenary corps!”

Gang Lie growled, “I hit that guy. Arrest me alone, the others had nothing to do with this!”

“Shut up. You idiot, you’re rushing to calamity!” The water pigman leader angrily stamped his foot.

After scolding Gang Lie, he forced a smile and stooped before the investigation captain, “Lord Captain, everything is a misunderstanding.”

“Misunderstanding?!” The investigation captain shouted. He pointed finger at his red cheek, then at the broken teeth on the ship deck, “You call this a misunderstanding?!”

The investigation captain angrily extended his palm and slapped the water pigman’s cheek with a sharp crack.

At the same time, he questioned, “If I hit you like that, would you consider it a misunderstanding?”

“It is a misunderstanding, of course it is a misunderstanding!” The water pigman leader emphasized.

An evil grin floated onto the investigation captain’s face, and he suddenly slapped again.

Pow!

A crisp ring.

He ruthlessly slapped the water pigman leader.

“Leader!” Gang Lie’s eyes widened in a glare, his urge to hit the investigation captain suddenly rising again.

The investigation captain cowered a little, but soon after straightened his chest and yelled at Gang Lie, “Come, you still dare to hit me? I’ll let you do it!”

The water pigman leader staunchly yelled at Gang Lie, “You will stay your hand for me! Have you not caused enough upheaval? Gang Lie, you moron!”

The water pigman leader looked at the other water pigmen, his anger rising, “Stop right there! Can you bear the consequences of attacking?”

The water pigmen all stopped at his shout.

Upon seeing this, the investigation captain grew more aggressive.

He used a pair of gloomy eyes to stare at the water pigman leader, grinning with a bloodstained and mangled jaw, “I didn’t know you were so interesting.”

The water pigman leader continued to stoop and smile, “If your Lordship wants to vent his anger on me, then give me a good beating.”

The investigation captain laughed heartily, “As you say!”

Pap, pap, pap!

The snow elf swung his hand, each slap ferociously hitting the water pigman’s face.

To ensure each slap was powerful, he even used his iron level battle qi.

The water pigman leader’s cheeks quickly swelled, and blood spilled from the corner of his mouth.

But he tenaciously stood in place, not saying a word. He was wholeheartedly serving as the object of the snow elf’s ire.

The water pigmen around him were sad and indignant, but at the same time, they were trapped in a deep helplessness.

This was a scene they were familiar with.

It seemed like they returned to their life of slavery.

As slaves, they had to swallow their anger, and submit meekly to bullying, as daring to rebel would lead to severe punishment and even death!

“Thing…” The investigation captain grew tired of hitting, looking at the wordless pigman leader with boredom and vapidness.

He gave an order to the investigation team, “Take these water pigmen away.”

When the water pigman heard this, he suddenly became worried.

He rushed in front of the investigation team captain and implored loudly, “Lord Captain, you have already vented your anger!”

The investigation team captain cackled.

“Right, I have vented my anger.”

“You did wonderfully.”

“But my work still needs to be finished. These water pigmen violently resisted, and as investigation captain, I have to justly enforce the law! I don’t want to take them away; it is the kingdom’s laws that do. I will remind you of something important, this is Snow Bird Harbor! This is the Ice Sculpture Kingdom!”

“Why do you think you can violently defy the law?”

The investigation captain glared at the water pigman leader, blue veins pulsing in his forehead.

How could he so easily let off these water pigmen?

If he didn’t bring back prisoners, he was sure to be criticized harshly by his superior.

The water pigman leader was incomparably impetuous, but he was unable to do anything.

Then at that moment, Zong Ge, Tripleblade, and the others walked into the cabin.

“Who is arresting my troops?” Zong Ge faintly said.

The snow elf investigation captain slightly raised his head to size up Zong Ge.

“You are the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ auxiliary commander? Our investigation team has been here for a long time, and finally the higherups are willing to appear. I will inform you now that your water pigmen underlings violently resisted the law! I will arrest them all, and your Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps is in big trouble!”

Tripleblade questioned, “How did they violently resist the law?”

The snow elf investigation captain glared and pointed at his cheek, “It seems you aren’t just short, but your eyes don’t work properly. Can you see? I was struck by them; this is the best proof!”

Even when facing the silver level Zong Ge and the others, he still remained arrogant. Even surrounded by the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, he was secure in the knowledge he had backing.

Because the investigation captain had seen many outsiders, and even if they were strong, when they came to the Ice Sculpture Kingdom, they had to bow before him.

Zong Ge glanced at the investigation captain’s check, “Whose hand did that?”

“Lord Shi Qi, it was me!” Gang Lie knelt down and hastily admitted his mistake, “Everything was my mistake, it doesn’t involve anyone else. But the investigation team wants to take everyone away!”

Zong Ge held out a hand, halting Gang Lie’s words, he then shifted his eyes to the water pigman leader’s face.

“Whose hand did that?” He pointed at the water pigman leader’s injured face.

The investigation captain didn’t say anything, merely sneering.

The water pigman leader quickly stammered, “It was me, Lord Shi Qi, I did it to myself.”

When the investigation captain heard this, he couldn’t help but let out a laugh.

Zong Ge inevitably raised a brow, “Is that so, did you hit yourself?”

“Yes I did, your Lordship.” The water pigman leader swiftly nodded.

“Hehehe.” This time, it wasn’t just the snow elf investigation captain, the entire investigation team laughed.

Their laughter was full of ridicule and disdain.

Zong Ge didn’t seem to notice and simply nodded.

The investigation captain impatiently waved his hand, “Shi Qi, did you hear it too? It is indeed as your person said. Hehe. Now then, get out of the way, I need to take these water pigmen away. If you want to redeem them, you had best come back with me, and we’ll have a nice chat.”

“Take them away!” The investigation captain raised his voice.

The investigation team was just about to act when Zong Ge suddenly interrupted them, “Wait.”

“What do you want this time?” The investigation captain wrinkled his eyebrows.

Zong Ge looked at Gang Lie and asked, “How did you beat the investigation captain?”

“

Gang Lie was confused by the question, and he subconsciously raised his right fist and replied, “I, I only hit him with his hand.”

“Did you hit them like this?” Just as Zong Ge said that he strode over to the investigation captain.

He raised his left hand, then suddenly swiped out a slap.

Pow!

A crunchy ring.

Zong Ge firmly gave the investigation captain a real slap to the face.

This slap had immense strength behind it, to the extent that the investigation captain actually flew into the air.

Then, he fell to the floor.

Plop.

He spurted out blood, along with a few broken teeth.

The investigation captain was shocked, and he unconsciously covered his mouth as he rigidly stared at Zong Ge, shrieking, “What did you do?”

“You attacked me! You actually attacked me?!”

When he said that, he finally reacted.

He broke out into strange laughter.

“You’re finished.”

“Shi Qi, you actually raised a hand and violently defied the law! You, a mere silver level, dared to defy the law, you’re finished.”

“You are the auxiliary commander; your Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps is also finished.”

“Hehe, all finished.”

“The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps having you as their auxiliary commander is indeed bad luck!”

Zong Ge was calm.

The water pigmen and the investigation team were both stunned too.

The investigation captain saw Zong Ge, and he hesitated a little as a misunderstanding appeared in his heart.

He continued to hoot, “We have alchemy devices on us, and everything that just happened has been recorded into magic images.”

“This is ironclad evidence! Denial will do nothing.”

“Hehehe!”

The investigation captain’s finger pointed at a button on his collar.

“Is that it?” Zong Ge walked over to him and stooped down to pluck out the button.

He looked at it, carefully scrutinizing it, and discerned that the button really was an alchemy device.

But what were its specific functions?

Zong Ge was not an alchemist and wouldn’t figure it out quickly.

The investigation captain allowed Zong Ge to take away his button.

His face was filled with a victorious smile.

“Hehe, are you still thinking of destroying evidence? Useless! We all have those devices on us!”

The other investigation team members slightly shifted at those words.

Chapter 513: Fighters Won’t be Easily Humiliated!

The Investigation Captain continued as he laughed: "Even if you destroy all of our alchemy devices, it is useless!"

"The mage tower at the port already has a copy of the magic video."

The water pigmen all showed expressions of uncertainty and fear on their faces.

He bent down and grabbed the Investigation Captain's collar.

After exerting slightly, he easily lifted the lightweighted Investigation Captain.

"Calm down," Zong Ge helped him up and kindly patted the dust off his clothes.

Next, he took a step back and waved his palm again.

Slap!

This time, the sound was much louder than before.

The pitiful Investigation Captain not only flew in the air from the impact, but he even turned one whole round before falling on the ground.

The intense feeling of dizziness even made him forget to cough out the blood in his throat.

"Gang Lie, did he slap him like this?" Zong Ge asked loudly.

The Investigation Captain was dizzy, and his vision blurred as if the sky was spinning around him.

He could not hear what Zong Ge was asking, so he nodded subconsciously.

Gang Lie shook his head subtly. Zong Ge's slap was far harsher than what he had experienced.

Seeing the Investigation Captain's response, Zong Ge couldn't help but laugh.

He hoisted the Investigation Captain again.

At this point, the latter was already unable to stand up on his own.

Zong Ge could only grab his collar "caringly" with one hand and slap at his face with his other hand.

Slap, slap, slap!

A series of slaps rained down quickly, forming after shadows in the eyes of the others.

The Investigation Captain's head shook left and right as he moved vigorously along with the slaps.

He opened his mouth and blood spewed out endlessly, along with his teeth at times.

His neck cracked along as if it was about to break at any time.

The Investigation Captain was merely at the iron level while Zong Ge was at the silver level.

To protect himself, the Investigation Captain, urged his iron level battle qi instinctively.

It was useless.

He was not a fighter like Xiong Ju, so the intense pain and humiliation made him unable to use any of his battle skills.

Soon, both his eyes rolled till they turned white, and his mouth opened wide. He had fainted.

Zong Ge stopped slapping after seeing this.

He let go of his left hand and the Investigation Captain fell on the ground motionlessly.

Under everyone's dumbfounded gaze, Zong Ge slowly walked to Gang Lie's side.

Gang Lie had been holding the alchemy button the entire time, being still as a statue.

He looked at Zong Ge with admiration in his eyes.

Zong Ge took back the alchemy button and flicked it towards the body of the Investigation Captain.

Next, he looked at the surrounding investigation team members who didn't dare to move and said: "Aren't you going to wake your captain up?"

The Investigation Captain was filled with fear and anger towards this man.

He shouted shrilly: "Do you think I'm a fool? Looking at how you treated me earlier, can that not be considered violence?"

Upon hearing this, Zong Ge moved towards the Investigation Captain.

The surrounding members flustered and scattered away.

The Investigation Captain who was held up by them lost his support in an instant and plopped to the ground.

He ignored the pain on his butt and held the ground with his hand as he shuffled back, shouting: "D-don't come towards me!"

The Investigation Captain took a deep breath as his expression turned livid.

In this situation, he had no choice but to give in. He relented: "No, that was not considered violent resistance."

"So, is it a misunderstanding? Am I right to say that?" Zong Ge asked softly.

The Investigation Captain nodded and lowered his head to hide his shameful and furious gaze: "Yes, this is all a misunderstanding!"

He felt that Zong Ge was too unreasonable.

There was no need to argue with him.

After all, a wise man would not fight when the odds were against him.

After the investigation team members heard this, they ran towards the ship door like they had just been spared.

They no longer talked about capturing the water pigmen.

The Investigation Captain looked at where Zong Ge pointed and found that it was the alchemy button from his shirt.

This was the alchemy device that recorded the process of his investigation.

The video was not only stored in the alchemy device but also transmitted to the mage tower as a copy.

Because of the copy's existence, the Investigation Captain did not think too much of this button.

The Investigation Captain's body turned stiff as a rock as if he had been paralyzed.

"You humiliated me, but you still want my gratitude?" The Investigation Captain was so upset he could feel his organs hurting inside. He wished he could bite this muscular human to death.

In the end, the other party was stronger, so he had no choice but to give in...

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' ship had already entered the dock, yet they were still conducting an investigation at this time.

He immediately saw through the underlying scheme.

The Investigation Captain before him had undoubtedly become a lackey of the Bayonet Gang.

As the city Lord's confidant, he despised such people from the bottom of his heart.

They also informed us that our ships are polluting the environment and must be repaired immediately; otherwise, we will face additional fines."

Additionally, he's made his private shipyard available to you. If any issues arise during repairs, your group's vessels may directly enter his private shipyard. There are large-scale repair facilities there, and no mooring fees will be charged. I can guarantee this last point!"

What met his eyes was the Investigation Captain's wide-eyed, pale-faced expression of panic.

The alchemist leader was satisfied.

The investigation team members instinctively lowered their heads to avoid the alchemists' icy stares.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord had actually aided the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, openly displaying such an attitude. Could they still cause trouble for the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps?

After all, the one who managed them was the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord, not the Bayonet Gang.

The Bayonet Gang had bribed them precisely because of their work and positions.

All of that had been granted to them by the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord.

The Investigation Captain felt like his world was collapsing.

The hatred that had filled his heart was now completely replaced by panic.

Zong Ge led the water pigmen onto the deck as well.

Remember, you are fighters, members of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps. You're no longer slaves.”

The water pigmen trembled at these words, some with reddened eyes, others choked with emotion.

He had to go to the training room to continue sparring with the dragonman youngster.

“Yes, Lord Shi Qi.” Gang Lie did not stand up; he kept his head bowed, his tiger-like eyes brimming with tears.

He was easily embarrassed and did not want others to see him crying.

For a moment, he could only maintain his half-kneeling posture, remaining motionless for a long time.

The deck directly below him was already covered with a tiny puddle of tears.

Chapter 514: Men Shi vs Ban Lan Gen

Snow Bird Harbor.

In a small arena closest to the dock.

The grueling battle had already lasted half a day.

"Puff, puff, puff..."

The dwarf Men Shi was panting and drenched in sweat.

He looked helplessly at Ban Lan Gen, feeling powerless.

Ban Lan Gen was a treeman, and he had already taken root in the sand of the arena.

His body was three times larger than normal, and there were dozens of times the branches on his tree body, swaying around as the wind blew.

"Come, I'm standing right here."

"Come attack me!"

"Consider it my loss if I take even a step back."

Ban Lan Gen taunted the dwarf Men Shi arrogantly.

His face could be seen on the upper half of the tree trunk.

A large number of branches and vines intertwined into two huge wooden palms by the sides of the tree.

One hand was holding the Gold Flower Hacksaw.

The other hand was holding a silver level rectangular tower shield.

At the same time, there was a epaulette hidden in his tree crown. It was the Flowing Wave Armor.

Other than the Flowing Wave Armor, his Battle Qi Syringe was concealed somewhere too.

The Small Thunder God Flying Hammer flew around him, landing on the tree crown at times like a flying bird.

In order to guarantee his victory, Ban Lan Gen had borrowed equipment from the other leaders before beginning the challenge.

He was fully armed!

In terms of equipment, he far surpassed Men Shi.

Men Shi only had his two-handed war hammer in his hands.

This war hammer was only at the silver level.

The dragonman youngster wanted to lend equipment too.

However, he had very few to begin with.

The Grand Arena Medal and Golden Silk Chainmail could not be lent out.

The Grand Arena Medal was constantly in use while the Golden Silk Chainmail needed repairing.

There was also another important reason: these two pieces of equipment were under the effect of both the Anti-investigation and Prophecy spell and Deceptive Disguise spell.

If he lent them to Men Shi, the secret would be exposed.

To be fair, Men Shi himself did not think that Ban Lan Gen would be this well-equipped either.

Xiong Ju had already done this before and now Ban Lan Gen did the same again.

"Both battle qi and stamina are nearly depleted."

"I can't fight anymore..."

Realizing this, Men Shi sighed deeply.

Then, he shouted: "I surrender."

He walked down the arena with dignity, but he could not conceal the frustration on his face.

He had been completely suppressed the entire fight. Other than the initial stage, he could not even threaten his opponent at all.

When they fought, Ban Lan Gen had thrown out the Small Thunder God Flying Hammer at the first moment.

It caused a great disruption to Men Shi.

Men Shi fought like a typical dwarf; he had outstanding defense but not enough offense.

While Men Shi was distracted, Ban Lan Gen quickly retreated and took root in the sand after pulling some distance.

Men Shi knew the situation was bad when he saw the treeman taking root, so he quickly expended his battle qi to launch a fierce assault.

When he arrived before Ban Lan Gen, he stood firm and prepared to engage in close combat.

The distraction level and threat of the Small Thunder God Flying Hammer quickly decreased.

Ban Lan Gen had only received this equipment for a short time, so he could not control it accurately.

However, this was within his expectations.

He had once observed the dragonman youngster and Xiong Ju's fight. The dragonman youngster had used this weakness to cause trouble for Xiong Ju instead.

Ban Lan Gen gave up the Small Thunder God Flying Hammer and used battle skills in a frenzy, disregarding the battle qi usage.

The audience thought it was unwise to engage in this style of fighting.

In a clash of battle qi expenditure, it was obvious that Men Shi had the advantage.

Even though Ban Lan Gen had taken root, it only gave him a slight boost in recovery speed and some minor effects.

In terms of battle qi consumption, his remaining reserves could not hold against the relentless attacks by Men Shi.

Ban Lan Gen kept to this combat tactic.

After paying the price of an enormous amount of battle qi, he finally took root in the sand of the arena.

Men Shi was full of vigor thinking that he had the advantage now.

But in the next moment, Ban Lan Gen took out the Battle Qi Syringe with a loud laugh.

He stabbed it fiercely into his arm.

The Battle Qi Syringe contains the battle qi he has accumulated over time, which is of the same origin as him.

After the jab, his battle qi was quickly replenished to its peak state.

Men Shi was stunned on the spot.

When Xiong Ju and the dragonman youngster fought, he had forgotten to use the Battle Qi Syringe.

This allowed Ban Lan Gen to successfully catch Men Shi unguarded.

This tactic succeeded brilliantly!

After taking root, his defense and recovery abilities gained the advantage.

His battle qi reserves also surpassed Men Shi.

This Battle Qi Syringe easily turned the tide in his favor.

The audience was shocked and discussed among themselves fervently.

The heroic duel did not ban equipment, potions, or other external-use items.

However, the audience gave greater recognition to the battle between the dragonman youngster and Xiong Ju.

It was able to show the determination and fighting spirit of the duelists.

Ban Lan Gen had such an advantage in equipment, but he still fought so carefully and reservedly. It was hard for the audience to cheer him on.

The rest of the battle had no surprise.

Men Shi continued to attack while Ban Lan Gen guarded himself.

He extended his roots into the ground and recovered more stamina and battle qi. Even though there were injuries sustained on his branches, he quickly recovered.

His self-healing speed was only slightly inferior to healing spells.

Ban Lan Gen's greatest weakness was that he could not move.

However, Men Shi could not take advantage of this weakness now.

This was because he lacked long-range attack methods.

His strongest long-range attack was a battle skill. This battle skill allowed one to throw their war hammer at the enemy.

It could not be used easily.

Ban Lan Gen was in a good condition now, so if Men Shi threw his hammer, it was likely he'd lose possession of it.

By then, Men Shi would be in an even more embarrassing spot without a weapon.

Men Shi's only choice was to engage in close combat with Ban Lan Gen.

However, he lacked an attack strong enough to decide the battle.

He was not only facing the fully armed Ban Lan Gen, but also his massive wooden palms and the vines and branches that could strike from any angle at any moment.

Ban Lan Gen had transformed into a tree-shaped fortress with no blind spots.

After several such exchanges, Ban Lan Gen burst into laughter and began to persuade Men Shi to surrender.

“Give up. My advantage is too great.”

"Can't you see it? The winner of this duel can only be me."

"You're just a clown. You can't threaten me at all. Why waste any more time? Just admit defeat gracefully."

"Once my tactic succeeds, this battle can be considered over. I've already secured victory."

Men Shi acknowledged that Ban Lan Gen had a point.

But he didn't give up.

Because he thought of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps and also of the dragonman youngster.

The Bayonet Gang had issued the challenge to disrupt the dragonman youngster’s recovery. They sought to use various underhanded means to force the dragonman youngster to face reality and engage in a heroic duel with Teng Dong Lang as soon as possible.

Men Shi knew this was the Bayonet Gang's scheme.

He wouldn't let Ban Lan Gen succeed so easily.

Thus, Men Shi adopted a stalling tactic.

Every time he launched a close combat attack, he quickly retreated to a safe area to rest and prepare.

During this time, he resisted the interference of the Small Thunder God Flying Hammer while consuming potions.

This left Ban Lan Gen speechless.

Remember, he could not move.

Even if he gained the upper hand in close combat and even injured Men Shi, it was still difficult to press the advantage.

Because once he pursued, he had to leave the ground and release his rooted state.

Once the rooted state was released, it would become extremely difficult to do it again.

This tactic succeeded because it caught Ban Lan Gen off guard.

If the Ban Lan Gen attempts to root again, Men Shi will certainly not agree.

The more critical point was the Battle Qi Syringe.

All of the battle qi stored had been used. It would take more than a few days to reaccumulate it.

Without this crucial equipment, Ban Lan Gen would find it difficult to execute the rooting tactic again.

"I cannot easily release the rooting state."

“Otherwise, all my careful preparations and efforts would be in vain.”

“Who would voluntarily give up their advantage?”

"Moreover, what if this is the opponent's trick, with Men Shi acting openly while setting a trap secretly? If I rush to victory, actively pursue, abandon the rooted state, and ultimately lose, wouldn't I become the biggest laughingstock in Snow Bird Harbor this year?"

Ban Lan Gen felt a heavy psychological burden.

Earlier, in his duel with the dragonman youngster, he had been careless and was successfully ambushed, knocked unconscious with a single punch.

Due to this humiliating outcome, he had endured mockery and ridicule in the days that followed.

On the surface, there might not be much open mockery directed at him, but the members of the Bayonet Gang privately gossiped about Ban Lan Gen.

After all, Ban Lan Gen was one of the six leaders of the Bayonet Gang, a high-ranking figure who needed to maintain his dignity.

Those in power needed reputation.

“This time, I must be cautious.”

“Losing to Commander Long Fu is one thing, after all, Xiong Ju was no match for him.”

“But losing to an obscure silver level member of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps would be too embarrassing.”

“What face would I have left to continue serving as the leader of the gang?”

Ban Lan Gen dared not be so arrogant anymore.

This time, he acted with extreme caution.

To provoke Men Shi into attacking and engaging in prolonged close combat, he constantly taunted and insulted Men Shi, aiming to stir up his anger.

If it were someone like Gang Lie, they might have fallen for it.

But Men Shi was an honest man.

No matter how vile Ban Lan Gen's insults were, he endured them in silence and steadfastly executed his delaying tactic.

Gradually, Men Shi spent more and more time resting and less and less time attacking.

In close combat, the situation was clearly unfavorable for Men Shi.

Whether it was the Flowing Wave Armor or the Gold Flower Hacksaw, both gave Ban Lan Gen a significant close combat advantage.

Meanwhile, the best equipment Men Shi had was his silver level war hammer.

The gap was too obvious.

However, once Men Shi retreated and created a safe distance, Ban Lan Gen also lacked effective long-range attack methods.

He was, after all, a close combat battle qi cultivator.

His most reliable long-range attack method was the Small Thunder God Flying Hammer he just borrowed.

However, while this flying hammer could cause disruption and threat, the level of threat it posed was insufficient to create a window of opportunity for victory.

In fact, the Small Thunder God Flying Hammer itself was not yet fully crafted and remained an unfinished product.

Men Shi relies on his long-handled two-handed war hammer to block the attacks of the Small Thunder God Flying Hammer. During the period when the Small Thunder God Flying Hammer was spinning around, he could calmly swallow potions.

In this situation, the Evil Eye was different.

The mental beams emitted by the Evil Eye were extremely powerful, almost always hitting their target, and extremely difficult to defend against.

Unfortunately, this precious artifact imbued with the power of the abyss had already been destroyed by Xiong Ju.

As a result, although Ban Lan Gen held a significant advantage, the initiative remained firmly in the hands of Men Shi.

The scene of the duel grew increasingly grim.
