At first, the spectators were enthusiastic, nearly filling every seat in the duel arena.

But soon, the battle became monotonous and tedious.

Some spectators grumbled and left.

Those who remained still held out hope that Men Shi might have other tricks up his sleeve. Would some unexpected change suddenly occur?

But as time dragged on, hour after hour passed.

Those who persisted in watching could see clearly: Men Shi had no other tricks; he was merely stalling for time.

More and more spectators left the small duel arena.

What had once been a packed arena now had only a few scattered spectators, with large empty sections.

Even the vendors had disappeared.

A group of children were laughing and chasing each other between the seats.

A few elderly people were lying on the seats, sound asleep. They had grown bored from watching and dozed off, falling asleep like that.

A few couples were chatting in the corners, whispering to each other, filling the air with the sweet stench of love.

Some new spectators entered the arena, hoping to catch the excitement.

But they usually couldn't last more than ten minutes before leaving in disappointment.

“I should have brought more potions,” Men Shi, who was in the midst of battle, regretted more and more.

He did not bring enough potions!

Earlier, he had not anticipated such a battle scenario.

Normally, in intense combat, there was rarely an opportunity for one to consume potions.

Men Shi had thought he had brought enough potions.

Now, he deeply regretted it but there was nothing he could do.

Without potions to replenish him quickly, his stamina and battle qi had plummeted to rock bottom, and his combat effectiveness had declined severely.

Men Shi found it difficult to engage in close combat again.

Meanwhile, Ban Lan Gen remained in peak condition.

In this rooted state, the treeman continuously accumulated advantages.

Ultimately, Men Shi decided it was unnecessary to risk close combat.

If he were injured, it would not just cause trouble for the mercenary corps or worry the Commander Long Fu, but it would also allow the Bayonet Gang’s plot to succeed.

Men Shi finally shouted out loud and admitted defeat.

The referee had already fallen asleep and was woken up by a staff member nearby.

“Ah, it's over?” The referee looked up at the sky.

It was already dark.

Chapter 515: Gold Level Equipment Comes

The referee had no surprises regarding the outcome of this duel.

He walked onto the stage with resentment and announced the result of the duel loudly to the empty stands.

"I won; I finally won."

"It was not easy, not easy at all!"

After securing this victory, Ban Lan Gen felt a sense of relief.

He could finally relax and exit his rooted state.

His size quickly shrank, returning to his previous mobile state.

However, the joy on his face did not last long.

As he was leaving, he overheard the audience's discussions and conversations.

"Have you heard? The Bayonet Gang started causing trouble for the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps early this morning. They firs sent in Ban Lan Gen to issue a challenge, then had the port officials and an investigation team pick a fight."

“They're just bullying outsiders.”

“The Bayonet Gang is too despicable!”

“This puts the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps in a dire situation.”

“Fortunately, the city Lord promptly dispatched a team of alchemists to assist the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps in repairing their magic ship and their gold level cannon.”

“Thanks to the city Lord's intervention, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was able to turn the situation around.”

“The city Lord is truly wise!”

“The Bayonet Gang is despicable; they deserve to be dealt with.”

“It turns out that last night, Commander Long Fu, despite his injuries, held a secret meeting with the city Lord and secured his strong support.”

"It seems Commander Long Fu is not only a formidable fighter but also a capable leader. He had already anticipated this."

“According to reliable sources, Long Fu has purchased a large quantity of gold level equipment from the city Lord.”

"What? This is going to be interesting!"

"Commander Long Fu, almost unarmed, defeated the fully armed Xiong Ju and emerged victorious. If he had been fully armed as well, Xiong Ju would have been no match for him."

“I can't wait to see the showdown between Commander Long Fu and Teng Dong Lang.”

"I wonder what kind of gold equipment? If he's acquired powerful and unique treasures, Teng Dong Lang won't have it easy."

“Not necessarily! If Commander Long Fu is only skilled in hand-to-hand combat, using unfamiliar weapons recklessly could become a huge weakness.”

"Teng Dong Lang is very powerful. Though he hasn’t fought much in recent years, he's not just an ordinary gold level fighter. As the leader of the Bayonet Gang, how could he be simple?"

Upon hearing the various discussions, Ban Lan Gen’s joy at victory vanished instantly.

“What’s the difference between this and the city Lord helping the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps openly?”

“Is the city Lord using the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps as a pawn to challenge our Bayonet Gang?”

“No, that’s unlikely. Since he became the city Lord, he's always coexisted peacefully with our Bayonet Gang. Back when he first became city Lord, he even specially invited Lord Teng Dong Lang to a banquet to discuss governance policies."

“So, he's just admiring Long Fu, purely out of appreciation, and wants to befriend them?”

Ban Lan Gen speculated on the city Lord's intentions.

His thoughts reflected the current thinking of the Bayonet Gang.

No one had anticipated that the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord would suddenly act this way.

This had a significant impact on the Bayonet Gang.

With the city Lord’s intervention, many of their tactics were no longer viable.

Without the City Lord’s protection, the Bayonet Gang had meticulously prepared further obstacles for the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, including a third wave, a fourth wave, and more.

They would then search the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' ships under various pretexts, humiliating them.

During the searches, they would have made every effort to gather intelligence on the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps and probe into their background. If given the opportunity, they might even plant a few pieces of evidence to frame the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

This was something that could not be helped.

The storm the dragonman youngster had stirred up had already engulfed him and endangered the entire mercenary corps.

In fact, once he had forcibly purchased that giant-type intelligence potion, the power struggle between the Bayonet Gang and the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had already begun.

Only by determining the true winner and loser could the rivalry between the two sides come to an end.

After training with Zong Ge for nearly an entire day, the dragonman youngster was completely exhausted.

However, at the same time, he felt deeply satisfied. A day of training had yielded immense gains, with his progress clearly visible.

Before dinner.

The city government palace sent another group of people.

This group was led by four silver level individuals, with the rest being the city Lord's elite guard troops. They escorted the shipment, bringing seven large boxes.

Along the way, they attracted countless curious glances.

The group arrived smoothly at the dock and completed the handover with the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

They exchanged goods and money at the same time.

The boxes contained the supplies the dragonman youngster had purchased from the city Lord the previous night.

Two of the boxes were filled with magic beast meat and other items.

The remaining four boxes were packed with various alchemy materials.

The remaining boxes contained gold level items, the most valuable part of this shipment.

The dragonman youngster opened the final box and personally inspected these gold level items.

A massive gold level longbow, without matching arrows.

Five three-pronged darts, with sharp edges and a cold gleam.

The gold level harpoon was a long-handled weapon, roughly the same height as the dragonman youngster.

Additionally, there was a set of gold level formation diagram panels.

These were specifically requested by the dragonman youngster from the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord. He had not anticipated receiving them during their first handover.

When assembled, these formation diagram panels could form a complete portable magic formation.

Of course, the panels were currently blank.

To construct any type of formation, an alchemist must first build the formation on each plate before assembling them.

Regarding the construction of the Bloodline Purification formation, the dragonman youngster had discussed it with Zi Di, but no formal plan had been finalized.

If external alchemists were hired to build portions of the formation on the plates, there was still a risk of information leakage.

If the Bloodline Purification formation was exposed, it would be unbearable for both the dragonman youngster and the entire Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

However, these preparations must still be made.

Preparing in advance was better than being caught off guard.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps held a large amount of funds, so investing in this area in advance was the correct strategy.

This was the first handover.

According to the agreement between the dragonman youngster and the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord, there would be two more handover sessions.

It was because the dragonman youngster's procurement volume was extremely large.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord could not immediately provide all the goods directly.

The items on the list were not exclusively his own.

Rather, they were sold by the entire network of connections and channels behind him.

The same list was also in the hands of other forces' leaders.

The resources the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord sold externally were only a portion of the list.

Therefore, it took time to gather them.

After sending off the city Lord's entourage, the dragonman youngster found himself in a blissful dilemma.

He had purchased a lot of gold level equipment, but storage had become an issue.

“We need a spatial storage device, and this storage device must be at least gold level.”

These supplies and equipment were collective property.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had a corresponding military equipment system.

For example, the alchemy crossbows and firearms used by the mercenaries were considered collective property and were stored in the armory during peacetime.

They were only distributed urgently when combat arose.

Previously, the higher-ups of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had also promised the mercenaries: as long as their merits were sufficient, these alchemy crossbows and firearms would be transferred to private ownership. This undoubtedly sparked immense combat enthusiasm among the mercenaries.

The large quantities of equipment and supplies that the dragonman youngster procured from the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord this time were also collectively owned.

The treasures they purchased at the Two Eyes Island auction were also of the same nature.

As for how to use them, the priority of distribution went to the extent of urgency and necessity.

Therefore, the substitute death puppet was first given to the dragonman youngster. He was the most critical figure in the entire team.

Then, when Cang Xu had to venture out to attend the undead gathering, the dragonman youngster voluntarily gave it to Cang Xu. Because under the circumstances at the time, Cang Xu needed it more.

In fact, the outside world has always believed that the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps possessed a gold level storage device.

This misunderstanding stemmed from the battle between the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps and Rou Zang.

However, in reality, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps did not yet possess such high-end equipment.

Zi Di sighed, "It's very difficult to purchase a gold level spatial storage device. Such treasures are almost extinct on the market and can only be found at auctions."

"The list provided by the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord already exceeds the limits of the normal market. However, there are no spatial storage devices on the list either."

Zi Di explained the market situation to the dragonman youngster.

Low-end storage equipment was actually quite common.

For example, the human skin storage bag that Cang Xu once made himself.

But once the grade reached gold level or higher, it became extremely rare.

High-level spatial devices were not only difficult to craft and required premium materials, but there was also a crucial external factor.

That was the strict control exercised by major factions!

It was because high-level spatial devices had a significant impact. If used properly, they could alter the outcome of a major battle. Whether it was transporting war supplies or troops, they were critical factors that greatly influenced the course of a war.

Zi Di believed: Rather than relying on purchasing gold level spatial storage equipment on the market, it would be better to enhance one's alchemy abilities. It would be more reliable to create it themselves in the future.

After dinner, the dragonman youngster left the dock again.

The carriage carried him along the road.

This time, his destination was the Treasure Merchant Alliance..

The Treasure Merchant Alliance. was one of the six major merchant alliances of the Sheng Ming Empire and naturally had a branch in Snow Bird Harbor.

Such merchant alliances typically maintained a neutral stance. They did not openly support others or other factions like the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord did.

However, in reality, the relationship between merchant alliances and mercenaries was closer.

Gangs like the Bayonet Gang often stood in opposition to the merchant alliances. They extort protection fees and even blackmail the merchant alliances.

The dragonman youngster believed that this inclination alone was sufficient to secure some support from the merchant alliance.

He did not require much support.

His primary objective was to acquire gold level items through the merchant alliance's channels.

After all, he had the funds to do so!

The primary business of the Treasure Merchant Alliance. was the sale of various items crafted through alchemy.

Chapter 516: A Thief Visits

A mysterious figure has been hiding by the window.

He watched as the city Lord's men carried seven large boxes to the dock, boarded the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ ship, and then returned with the empty boxes.

Then, he watched quietly as the carriage carrying the dragonman youngster departed from the pier.

Glancing at the sky, he began his action.

He reached into his robe and pulled out a tomato.

Of course, this was not a real tomato, but an alchemy artifact.

The artifact was of gold level quality!

The mysterious man pressed his thumb against the top of the tomato.

In the next moment, the tomato opened like a pocket watch, revealing a smooth, flat surface inside.

The mysterious man infused it with battle qi.

After being filled with a large amount of battle qi, the tomato's surface transformed into a clock face with clear markings.

The dial also had three hands, representing hours, minutes, and seconds.

The mysterious figure pressed the top of the tomato again with his thumb, and the clock hands immediately began to move.

A few seconds later, the mysterious figure's figure quickly faded, becoming a vague outline.

Soon, the outline disappeared as well, and the mysterious figure vanished completely.

The mysterious figure closed the tomato clock and tucked it into his chest.

He gently pushed open the window and leaped out.

He ran across the roof, swiftly approaching the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' ship.

Upon reaching a certain distance, he landed lightly on the empty ground of the pier.

There were few people on the pier.

The mysterious figure quickly made his way to the underside of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' flagship.

Without hesitation, he leaped lightly. He landed on the side of the Love Triangle.

He extended his hands, spreading his fingers apart.

He gently touched the surface of the Love Triangle's hull with the pads of his fingers.

At the moment of contact, his golden battle qi created ripples on the hull, providing him with a solid foothold.

The mysterious figure hung in mid-air, relying solely on his hands to alternate and support himself with his fingers, effortlessly climbing upward.

The entire process was as light as a dragonfly skimming the water, drawing no attention.

In about 10 seconds, he had scaled the ship's rail and truly set foot on the deck of the Love Triangle.

Numerous scout-type alchemy puppets were stationed around the dock.

They monitored all the ships of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, with a particular focus on the Love Triangle.

However, the mysterious man's entire operation did not trigger any detection from the alchemy puppets.

At the same time, magic images were projected inside the Deep Sea Monster Fish.

Similarly, there was no trace of the mysterious man in the images.

The mysterious man walked across the deck.

His walking posture was different from that of an ordinary person.

He only touched the ground with the tips of his toes, walking on his heels.

Every time his toes touched the deck, a small golden battle qi rippled out for an instant.

The aura, which was originally visible, became invisible and odorless under the influence of the tomato clock.

A group of patrol mercenaries approached from the corner.

The mysterious figure sidestepped, pressing against the wall, and easily avoided them.

The mercenaries crossed paths with him narrowly.

The mysterious figure's shoulder was only a fist's length away from one of the mercenaries at the most dangerous moment.

Without waiting for the mercenaries to move further away, the mysterious figure followed them and continued on his way.

As he passed the forecastle, the mysterious figure's ears twitched.

He heard unusual sounds coming from inside the forecastle.

He approached the door of the forecastle.

First, he spread his left hand and gently pressed the pads of his fingers against the door.

Golden battle qi ripples reappeared, swiftly spreading across the entire door surface before penetrating into the forecastle.

The door to the forecastle was originally locked.

Under the influence of the golden battle qi, the lock opened silently.

At that moment, there were two patrol teams on the deck, and the mercenaries were walking around while searching the area.

The door to the forecastle was right under their noses, and their gaze often swept over it.

The mysterious man, bold and daring, decisively pushed the door open a crack.

The next moment, he unleashed his battle skill.

His entire body moved like water, swiftly sliding through the crack.

The interior of the forecastle was spacious and sparsely furnished.

There was only one person inside—the big guy.

“So, the sounds I heard earlier were just snoring?”

The mysterious man's gaze was immediately drawn to the big guy.

He immediately saw through the trick.

Although the big guy was asleep, he was also cultivating battle qi.

"Cultivating battle qi while sleeping. What kind of battle qi technique is this? It's interesting."

“No wonder the snoring sounds so strange, with a hint of extraordinary power leaking out.”

“I thought it was those treasures.”

The big guy’s cultivation technique, the Mindless Sleeping Posture Technique, was carefully selected for him by the dragonman youngster from the War Merchant’s database.

The mysterious figure did not recognize the origin of this battle qi cultivation technique; in fact, it was quite difficult to determine its source from just observation.

“This little giant is on the verge of a breakthrough.”

The mysterious man's expression was somewhat subtle.

He sensed that the big guy's life aura was approaching the threshold of transcending.

The next step was to transcend the ordinary, shed the identity of a normal person, and elevate his life level to bronze level!

The mysterious figure narrowed his eyes, contemplating the idea of striking him down with a single blow.

But the next moment, he dismissed the thought.

He had more important matters to attend to.

If he killed the big guy, others might notice, and it would thus implicate his entire operation.

“Even if I kill him, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps would only lose a bronze level member.”

“Moreover, they might not even realize that this big guy was on the verge of breaking through.”

“The operation has only just begun; there's no need to unnecessarily increase the risks.”

The mysterious figure spared the big guy.

If he knew the big guy's bloodline quality, he would definitely change his mind.

He had no idea what he had missed.

The mysterious figure slipped through the narrow gap in the door, channeled his battle qi once more, and left the forecastle.

As he departed, he gently closed the door completely and relocked it.

By then, the deck had already been reinforced with an additional patrol unit.

The mercenaries from the three patrol teams hadn’t noticed that the forecastle had already been visited by an enemy.

The door’s opening and closing had been too subtle to detect.

The big guy was still sound asleep and had no idea that he had narrowly escaped death.

After leaving the forecastle, the mysterious figure headed straight for the aft cabin, knowing that was where the captain's cabin were.

The captain's cabin were empty.

The dragonman youngster had already gone to the Treasure Merchant Alliance..

The mysterious figure stood in the center of the captain's cabin, looked around, then spread his hands wide, fingers splayed.

Golden battle qi ripples emanated from him in waves.

The golden battle qi ripples spread rapidly, sweeping over the surface of every object in the captain's cabin.

Some magic traps and small mechanisms were instantly disarmed, without a single alarm sounding.

Then, some drawers and cabinets opened automatically, revealing their contents.

The mysterious figure discovered the nautical charts, paper, pen, ruler, and other measurement tools used daily by the dragonman youngster.

He found the interactive map and immediately unfolded it for examination.

Chapter 517: Sacred Artifact — Golden Finger

These daily activity maps often recorded the recent routes taken by the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps and might also contain valuable intelligence about future travel plans.

After the mysterious figure opened it, they took a look but found it was less detailed than the intelligence description of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

This activity map was purchased by the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps on the Two Eyes Island, and the previous route was not recorded within it.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps were not true arms smugglers; their destination is the capital city of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom.

The next steps would depend on how the situation developed.

The mysterious man was not satisfied and examined the activity map more carefully.

He hoped that the map contained more hidden information which was usually concealed.

In fact, such nautical charts were not uncommon.

This was especially for those used by pirates. The surface layer contained information, while the hidden layers held more genuine and secret intelligence.

In the end, after studying the map, the mysterious figure was left with only disappointment. He was certain that it was just an ordinary nautical chart.

In the drawer of the dragonman youngster's desk, the mysterious figure stumbled upon a pile of invitation letters.

He glanced at the invitation letters and took note of all the parties involved in sending them.

“There are no secret compartments or treasure chests here,” the mysterious man said disappointedly, instinctively looking down at his feet. “It seems that today's cargo is hidden in the lower hold.”

One of the main purposes of the mysterious man's journey was to steal the gold level equipment that the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had just purchased.

After finding nothing in the captain's cabin, the mysterious figure left immediately.

He came to a ship ladder and was about to descend it.

At that moment, a patrol of mercenaries happened to be ascending the ladder.

The mysterious figure quickly stepped aside.

Although he was in an invisible state, he could still bump into them physically. He was not like a ghost or a spirit that could ignore physical laws and pass through objects.

“There's really a lot of people patrolling here.”

“Their patrol routes are very strict,” the mysterious person thought to himself.

While stepping aside, the mysterious person took out the tomato clock from his pocket again.

He opened it and quietly noted how much time was left.

Very soon, the patrol team walked away.

They were completely unaware of the mysterious person right next to them.

The mysterious person climbed the ship's ladder and smoothly reached the second deck.

Following the same actions and procedures as before, he quickly checked the ship's cabins on both sides along the way.

His movements did not make a sound.

The tomato clock perfectly concealed his presence.

As time passed, the mysterious figure still hadn't found his target, and he couldn't help but feel anxious.

A patrol team passed by him again.

The leader, Lan Zao, suddenly stopped in his tracks and cast a puzzled glance toward the mysterious figure's location.

The mercenary behind him stopped and asked, “Lord Lan Zao, what's wrong?”

Lan Zao shook his head and laughed, “Nothing.”

He had just felt something was off, but after looking closely, he confirmed there was nothing.

“It was probably just an illusion,” Lan Zao thought to himself.

The mysterious man remained motionless.

He stared intently at Lan Zao's back until he disappeared around the corner of the corridor, then finally averted his gaze.

The mysterious man's eyes flickered with icy intent to kill.

He recalled the scene from earlier.

Since boarding the Love Triangle, this was the first time he had faced the danger of being discovered.

“I haven't found the treasure storage cabin yet, and my nerves are getting frayed, causing my movements to become more noticeable.”

“Did that mercenary detect me through the fluctuations in the air?”

The mysterious figure had a physical presence, so even slight movements caused the air to flow differently.

At that moment, he almost attacked!

Lan Zao was merely a bronze level individual, and the mercenaries behind him were all ordinary people.

With the mysterious figure's gold level strength, he could eliminate them all within two seconds.

Throughout the process, he could do so without making a single sound.

Furthermore, within the next 10 seconds, the mysterious figure could swiftly dispose of their bodies, leaving no trace at the scene.

However, the mysterious figure suppressed his killing intent and ultimately did not act.

“Such a tightly organized patrol route, with so many patrol teams, it's almost like a military camp.”

“There must be a connection between them, a secret code for transmitting emergency information.”

"If any patrol team delays even slightly, the entire patrol system will detect it. Killing them would be easy, but if this team goes missing, it will trigger the entire system's alarm!"

It was precisely because of this consideration that the mysterious figure did not act.

“Half the time has already been consumed.” The mysterious figure opened the tomato clock again, furrowing his brows.

There wasn't much time left.

The mysterious figure decided to use his battle skill to search for the gold equipment.

After the battle skill was unleashed, there was no effect.

This battle qi was specifically designed for investigation. Within a certain range centered on the mysterious figure, detection of any extraordinary aura could be captured by the battle skill and fed back into the mysterious figure's mind.

Gold level equipment naturally had a gold level aura.

However, those who collected such equipment would typically store them in treasure chests.

As a result, the auras were completely blocked and concealed.

The mysterious person's search attempt failed, but it did not surprise him.

“In this situation, I can only consume a potion,” the mysterious person sighed deeply.

Then, with a constipated expression on his face, he pulled out a vial from his robe.

He removed the stopper, tilted his head back, and poured the entire contents of the vial into his mouth, then swallowed it in one gulp.

The next moment, a look of pain appeared on his face.

He opened his mouth wide, stuck out his tongue, and leaned against the wall, vomiting repeatedly.

His tongue turned an eerie purple in an instant from the potion.

As he vomited, the mysterious man shed copious amounts of snot and tears.

But no matter how loud he screamed, his cries were drowned out by the tomato clock.

Furthermore, the snot and tears he shed were carefully collected by himself, lest they become a weakness in his infiltration.

After about half a minute, the mysterious figure finally regained his composure.

He twitched his nostrils and immediately detected a peculiar scent.

Following the scent, he descended another deck to the lower level of the Love Triangle.

“This must be the place,” he said, precisely locating the cargo hold containing the gold equipment.

The mysterious figure extended both hands, spreading his ten fingers, with all fingertips gently pressed against the surface of the hatch.

Golden-colored battle qi ripples spread outward from the contact point.

Soon, the mysterious figure detected numerous traps behind the door.

His expression grew serious and focused.

This was because he sensed numerous magic traps and intricate mechanisms inside.

These mechanisms and magic traps were intertwined, forming a series of tight security barriers.

If the mysterious figure made even the slightest mistake, it would trigger the traps and catch him off guard.

More critically, it would activate the alarm, alerting the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

Disarming these traps and mechanisms was not difficult for the mysterious figure.

The survivors did not have advanced equipment in this regard.

In addition, Zi Di, who had set up these magic traps, was greatly limited by her magician level.

But for the mysterious figure, he could not afford to waste precious time dismantling these traps and mechanisms.

The buff granted by the tomato clock has a time limit.

The mysterious figure then pulled out a sacred artifact from his robe.

This artifact was quite unique, resembling a goblin's finger. The tip of the finger had a long, sharp nail.

The finger was as golden as if cast in gold, radiating a brilliant golden glow, and felt heavy in the hand.

This was the sacred artifact—Golden Finger!

The mysterious man gently touched the surface of the hatch with the Golden Finger.

The moment he activated it, a burst of golden light flashed briefly.

The commotion was still suppressed by the tomato clock.

In an instant, all the magic traps and mechanisms around the hatch were perfectly disarmed without triggering a single alarm.

The mysterious figure glanced at the Golden Finger, which surface had grown dim, and carefully placed it back into his robe.

He pushed open the hatch, slipped inside, and then gently closed it behind him.

The entire process took less than a second.

The ship's cabin was empty.

The mysterious figure paused for a moment.

Just then, another patrol team arrived nearby.

The mysterious figure heard the footsteps of the patrol members.

The footsteps grew closer, seemingly heading straight for this cabin.

He realized something was wrong.

He had just disabled all the magic traps and mechanisms, so there was no way he could rebuild them in an instant.

This was a huge flaw!

Even if the patrol team couldn't detect his presence, they would certainly notice that the magic traps and mechanisms had been disabled.

Realizing this, the mysterious figure narrowed his eyes slightly and drew his dagger.

Chapter 518: Dispelling the Formation

The patrol team's footsteps gradually faded away until they had completely left the ship's cabin.

The mysterious man remained calm as he quietly sheathed his dagger.

He exhaled silently, unable to help but marvel once again at the strict discipline of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

Generally speaking, patrol duty is a tedious task. Patrol members typically engage in casual conversation during their rounds to combat boredom and pass the time.

When the mysterious man inspected many barracks, it was common to see patrol members chatting with each other.

However, when he arrived on the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' ship and encountered several patrol teams, he never saw any of them chatting idly with each other. Each patrol team focused their attention solely on their work.

Because once they started chatting, it would distract them from their surroundings. Sometimes, even if an intruder made a slight noise, it would be drowned out by the patrol members' conversations.

“Mercenary Corps rarely have such discipline; even military camps might not reach this level.”

The mysterious man couldn't help but think of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ other identity—arms smugglers—and suddenly understood.

Arms smuggling was an illegal activity that could cost one their life.

It was far more dangerous than regular mercenary work.

Only by cultivating such discipline could one enforce orders and keep secrets. This would raise the success rate of smuggling.

“When it comes to arms smuggling, these people are undoubtedly very professional.”

The mysterious figure's attention returned to what was before him.

The ship's hold was empty, but the mysterious figure knew not to trust what his eyes saw.

Earlier, when he had used his battle skill to inspect the area outside the door, he had discovered that gold equipment and various alchemy materials were stored within this hold. However, they had been concealed by a magic formation, which had erased all traces and suppressed their aura.

As a result, even if an outsider stood right in front of these treasures, they would be completely undetectable to the naked eye.

The mysterious figure approached the formation and once again used his battle qi to create ripples of golden battle qi.

Ripples spread rapidly, covering the entire invisible magic formation without triggering any alarms.

After investigating, the mysterious figure quickly confirmed that the magic formation before him was primarily used for camouflage and concealing energy.

Its grade was not particularly high.

"The magicians of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps are Lao Zhong and Hong Teng. These two are iron level and bronze level, respectively."

"This iron level formation shows clear signs of recent construction. I wonder which one of them constructed it?"

Although the mysterious figure immediately saw through the formation, he could not immediately set about breaking it.

This was because, in addition to the main magic formation itself, four smaller magic formations had been set up in the hidden corners of the ship's cabin.

These four smaller magic formations were secretly connected to the main magic formation.

Once the main magic formation was broken, or any one of the four smaller magic formations was damaged, an alarm would be triggered.

The mysterious figure's sacred artifact, the Golden Finger, could break this arrangement.

But he did not wish to use it as it was too wasteful.

The Golden Finger could be reused, but each use consumed the divine power stored within it.

Obtaining divine power was extremely difficult.

The mysterious figure was not a devout believer. Accumulating divine power through prayer would require numerous attempts to gather enough for a single use.

The mysterious figure decided to take action himself.

He retrieved a set of tools.

They were kept in a small leather pouch.

He untied the rope binding the pouch, unfolding the entire leather strip completely and suspending it in mid-air before him.

The leather pouch had numerous clasps, each securing a variety of precision tools.

Without exception, these tools were all gold level, their auras faintly resonating with one another.

Even the leather pouch housing them was of the same quality.

This was a relatively rare set of gold level tools, and the mysterious man's primary tools of the trade.

The mysterious man first used his battle skill, causing his eyes to glow with a golden light.

After using his battle skill, a vague image of a magic formation appeared in his field of vision.

This main magic formation was three-dimensional, nearly cubic in shape, and nearly filled the entire ship's cabin.

Every detail of the formation and the ripples caused by the flow of magic power were clearly visible to the mysterious figure, who memorized them all.

The mysterious man observed for a moment before taking out several long needles.

Under the infusion of his battle qi, the slender needles rapidly elongated, growing significantly longer.

From their original length, which was only as long as a finger, they stretched to the length of an adult's forearm.

The mysterious man carefully selected the needles and swiftly embedded them into the three-dimensional magic formation.

The formation continued to operate normally, seemingly unaffected by the needles.

The mysterious figure then took out a magic ink pen.

Using the pen, he first drew new alchemy pathways in the blank spaces of the formation.

This was no easy feat.

The mysterious figure was clearly a battle qi cultivator with exceptional alchemy skills.

After outlining at least six alchemy pathways, the mysterious figure returned the magic ink pen to its original place in the leather case and withdrew a carving knife.

He hovered the carving knife over the position he intended to alter.

Taking a deep breath, he struck swiftly.

His movements were lightning-fast, making over a dozen cuts within a single second. Each strike was precise beyond belief, striking exactly where he intended.

The magic formation was damaged, and its stable operational state began to show signs of collapse.

But in the next instant, the several thin needles inserted by the mysterious figure activated simultaneously, emitting a variety of colorful lights.

The needles temporarily stabilized the magic formation, barely keeping it functioning.

Subsequently, the magic power flowing through the magic formation was deliberately guided by the mysterious figure. The magic power entered the alchemy pathways he had previously outlined.

Soon, these alchemy pathways were all infused with magic power.

After passing through these pathways, the magic power quickly returned to the original parts of the alchemy formation.

The original parts that had been severed by the mysterious figure were thus abandoned.

In silence, the alchemy formation underwent a transformation.

After abandoning the original sections, it merged with the new sections deliberately drawn by the mysterious figure, forming a new formation.

This formation retained the original functions of storing materials and gathering energy, but its control had fallen into the hands of the mysterious figure.

Most critically, throughout the transformation, the formation continued to function without triggering any alarms from the four smaller formations.

The mysterious figure casually snapped his fingers, and the formation moved at his will.

The scene before his eyes suddenly changed.

A large quantity of alchemy materials were neatly arranged.

Three gold level pieces of equipment also came into view.

The mysterious figure raised his eyebrows slightly, revealing a hint of surprise.

Chapter 519: Trigging the Alarm

The mysterious man was surprised to see a large amount of alchemy materials and gold level equipment just lying around, stored haphazardly.

Previously, he had assumed there would be at least one treasure chest, serving as his final obstacle.

But there wasn’t any.

Not even the Balloon Knapsack mentioned in the intelligence report could be found.

The mysterious figure’s expression briefly relaxed. Without the final storage device, he could skip the trouble of cracking it.

But his gaze quickly grew solemn.

“According to the information we’ve gathered, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps revealed during their naval battle with Rou Zang that they possess at least one gold level storage device.”

“And the gold level Balloon Knapsack was acquired from the auction on the Two Eyes Island.”

“Which means to say they have at least two such spatial storage devices.”

"This defies logic. They are so careless, storing these precious alchemy materials and gold level equipment like this. Why?"

“Could it be that all their spatial storage devices are already filled with other items?”

The mysterious figure couldn't help but think of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ other identity once more.

An answer immediately came to mind.

“In that case, these arms smugglers must be carrying a large quantity of arms.”

“At least two of the storage devices are being used to carry arms.”

“Where?”

“If I can find those arms, it will be even better!”

The mysterious man looked at the materials and equipment within arm's reach but did not act immediately.

He used his battle skill again to confirm safety.

He was extremely cautious, knowing that many renowned thieves often fell at the final step.

After the battle skill inspection, he used a tool.

In the end, he found nothing suspicious.

"There really are no traps. These people are really bold… no, perhaps they don’t even care about these things?”

“Perhaps they only traded with the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord to establish a cooperative relationship, deepen their connections, and pave the way for future arms sales in Snow Bird Harbor?”

The mysterious man thought to himself as he walked forward.

He first picked up the gold level longbow, then glanced at the long-handled harpoon.

Finally, he picked up the set of darts.

He ran his fingers over the surface of the darts, then swung his forearm in a throwing motion.

From this throwing motion alone, it was clear that he possessed a mastery of darts as a combat technique. He must have put in a great deal of practice.

Among the three gold level equipment pieces, the mysterious man favored this set of darts the most.

He held onto them lovingly, examining them for over ten seconds before tucking them into his robe.

Afterward, he took out two silver level storage bags and stuffed them with various alchemy materials.

Even after filling them, there was still plenty left in the ship's hold.

“I can't take it all,” the mysterious man thought regretfully.

“It's too difficult to buy a gold level storage item.”

“Sigh, if they used that Balloon Knapsack to store these things, it would be perfect!”

If the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps used the Balloon Knapsack to store these supplies and treasures, it would be very convenient for the mysterious man.

He could simply pick up the bag and go.

The Balloon Knapsack is merely a storage item and lacks anti-theft measures.

Of course, the mysterious figure was aware of the Balloon Knapsack's properties: when the value of the stored items exceeds a certain threshold, the Balloon Knapsack expands significantly.

At this point, the two silver level storage bags he carried with him came in handy.

Hopeful expectations often diverge from reality.

Silver level storage bags could not hold gold level equipment.

This meant the mysterious person had to carry the three gold level items himself.

However, there were still gold level items here.

Specifically, the blank formation diagram panels.

These were too large—even the smallest one was equivalent to a large shield nearly two meters in diameter.

Not only were they massive, but they were also extremely heavy.

The mysterious figure retrieved several bundles of alchemy bombs, set the countdown timers, and placed them at the center of the magic formation.

He held a longbow in one hand, a harpoon in the other, and darts in his pocket. Finally, he forcefully clamped a formation diagram panel under his arm.

He walked to the hatch, his face showing a look of distress and resignation.

He knew that in this heavily burdened state, if he encountered combat, it would be disastrous.

Moreover, when he picked up the longbow, harpoon, and other gold level equipment, their power was obscured by the tomato clock's influence.

This accelerated the depletion of the tomato clock's concealment power, causing the hands to move faster.

The mysterious man already had little time left, and now he had even less.

After thinking it over, the mysterious man had no choice but to turn back and reluctantly put the formation diagram panel back in its original place.

After doing this, he turned and left.

Using his golden battle qi to open the door, he avoided the two patrol teams using the same method as before and arrived at a ship's cabin.

This was a storage cabin for miscellaneous items.

During his previous explorations, the mysterious figure had already marked its location in his mind, intending to use it as a temporary hiding place for his treasures.

Here, he set down the longbow and harpoon.

Then, he pulled out a vial of potion from his robe.

After removing the stopper, he evenly sprinkled the mustard-yellow powder over the surfaces of the longbow and harpoon.

The powder quickly took effect, concealing the shape and aura of the longbow and harpoon.

Clearly, this was a gold level potion.

However, the effect was only temporary.

Once the time limit expired, the longbow and harpoon would reveal their true form and emit a gold level aura, making them easily detectable.

After gaining a critical window of time with the powder, the mysterious figure once again emerged from the storeroom.

His mission had a second objective besides treasure theft—to assassinate a key figure within the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

The dragonman youngster was not part of his plan.

The defensive prowess the dragonman youngster demonstrated in the heroic duel had even left the gold level mysterious figure feeling exasperated.

Moreover, the dragonman youngster had already left the dock.

According to the intelligence gathered by the mysterious figure, the second-in-command of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was undoubtedly their deputy commander, Shi Qi.

However, Shi Qi himself was a peak silver level battle qi cultivator, with golden-hued battle qi.

Such a figure would be difficult to assassinate in a short period of time.

More critically, the mysterious figure feared that if he made a sound while assassinating Shi Qi, it would trigger an alarm.

This would cause significant complications for his subsequent actions.

For him, the more key figures he could assassinate, the better.

In the mysterious figure's plan, the assassination targets had already been listed, and there was a strict order to follow.

The mysterious figure continued to explore the cabins of the Love Triangle.

He first discovered the cabin of the magician Lao Zhong.

The mysterious figure stood before Lao Zhong's cabin door, pressing his fingertips lightly against the door, using gold level battle qi ripples to probe Lao Zhong's condition.

Lao Zhong was seated in meditation, his eyes tightly closed, with intense elemental fluctuations surrounding him.

He was clearly meditating.

This made the mysterious figure contemplate.

After thinking it over, he did not act immediately.

He continued his search.

Soon, he found the cabin of the magician Hong Teng.

Hong Teng was preparing potions, completely focused on her task.

This time, the mysterious figure began to act.

He first used the sacred artifact Golden Finger to bypass the cabin door's defensive measures.

He did not rush inside but instead opened a small crack in the door and pulled out a bamboo blow dart from his robe.

Though only silver level, it was more than sufficient to deal with a mere bronze level magician.

The mysterious figure paused for a few seconds before blowing with all his might.

The slender needle, as thin as a cow hair, struck Hong Teng's neck directly.

Hong Teng furrowed her brows slightly.

It felt like being bitten by a mosquito.

But at that moment, she was busy brewing potions and couldn't spare her hands to swat the mosquito.

The mysterious figure smiled darkly and closed the hatch again.

The needle was coated with gold level poison, and killing a bronze level magician was well within the poison's effectiveness range.

The mysterious figure had used this tactic to assassinate numerous targets, including several silver level targets.

In the mysterious figure's mind, Hong Teng was already as good as dead!

After completing this assassination attempt, the mysterious figure's movements clearly accelerated, rushing toward the cabin where Lao Zhong was located with a sense of urgency.

He once again activated the sacred artifact Golden Finger, breaking through the cabin door's defensive measures, then gently pushed open a small crack.

He retrieved the silver level bamboo blow dart once more.

He placed his lips at the tip of the blow dart, aiming it at the exposed neck of the magician Lao Zhong.

Just as he was about to blow, Lao Zhong suddenly opened his eyes wide and glared at him angrily.

At the same moment, a piercing alarm sounded on the Love Triangle!

Chapter 520: Successful Assassination

The alarm suddenly sounded, and the mysterious man's heart skipped a beat.

“I've been discovered!”

“How did I get discovered?”

In an instant, his mind flashed with suspicion.

“Was it because the ship's hold where I planted the alchemy bombs after stealing the treasure was discovered?”

He couldn’t have replicated the mechanisms and magic traps at the hatch, which was a glaring flaw.

“Or did Magician Hong Teng realize she was poisoned?”

Though the mysterious figure had coated the blow dart with lethal poison, there was still time before the target would succumb to it.

This time was what the mysterious figure had bought for himself, but it also gave the enemy an opportunity.

Never underestimate a magician’s capabilities, even if she was only a low-level magician.

Then, in the next moment, the mysterious figure completely cleared his mind of all distractions.

If he was discovered… So be it!

The strongest members of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps were only silver level. Moreover, the group’s leader, Long Fu, had already left the dock long ago.

Earlier, from the heroic duel between Men Shi and Ban Lan Gen, it was clear that Men Shi's strength was nothing special.

The only one who caught the mysterious person's attention was the auxiliary commander, Shi Qi.

“I am a gold level battle qi cultivator!” The mysterious person's eyes flashed with murderous intent.

Gold level battle skill—Instant Flash.

The mysterious person vanished from his original position in an instant.

The next moment, he suddenly appeared behind Lao Zhong.

It all happened too quickly!

Lao Zhong was still glaring angrily at the mysterious man, but the next moment, the mysterious man had vanished from his field of vision.

Gold level battle skill—Slicing Song.

The mysterious man drew his dagger and slashed across the magician Lao Zhong's neck.

A brilliant flash of blade light flickered and vanished.

The sharp dagger combined with the gold level battle skill severed Lao Zhong's entire head.

In the mysterious figure's perception, bright red blood gushed out from Lao Zhong's neck, propelling his head into the air before it rolled onto the floor.

The fountain of blood quickly subsided.

The mysterious figure slowly rose to his feet and gently kicked with the tip of his toe.

Lao Zhong's headless corpse fell to the floor.

Blood quickly stained the ship's floor red.

Even in death, Lao Zhong's eyes remained wide open in anger.

Clearly, he died with unresolved grievances.

The mysterious figure exhaled a breath of stale air and coldly glanced at Lao Zhong's severed body.

He was a gold level battle qi cultivator, while Lao Zhong was merely iron level.

The gap between them was not just one level; more importantly, the mysterious figure had complete initiative.

Lao Zhong was instantly killed without any chance to fight back, and the mysterious figure was not the least bit surprised by this outcome.

But the next moment. He heard the shouts and footsteps of a large group of people rushing toward this location.

“Follow me and charge!”

“Hurry, hurry!”

The dwarf Men Shi led the way, growling constantly.

Bang!

Men Shi charged forward with his shoulder, smashing through the ship’s hatch.

He was the first to rush into the ship’s hold, followed by the mercenaries swarming in.

The next moment, Men Shi’s eyes widened, and his pupils contracted to the size of needles.

He saw a bundle of alchemy bombs stuck to the ship’s hull.

The fuse of the bombs had already burned to the end.

“Get out of the way, no, get down!” Men Shi roared, warning the mercenaries behind him.

Some mercenaries saw the alchemy bombs, some retreated, and some hurriedly dropped to the ground.

Those who retreated collided with those rushing into the ship's hold from behind, plunging the scene into chaos.

“It's too late!”

Men Shi's face twisted into a ferocious expression as he boldly activated his defensive battle skill and charged toward the alchemy bombs.

Boom!

The next moment, the bombs exploded.

Flames and explosions erupted simultaneously, but most of the blast's destructive force was absorbed by Men Shi.

Men Shi suffered severe injuries, his body swaying, blood spilling from his mouth, his ears ringing, and his head spinning.

The mysterious figure struck silently from the side of the hatch!

It turned out he had been hiding on the side of the hatch all along, his body completely invisible.

Men Shi did not see him; after crashing into the ship's cabin, the first thing he noticed was the alchemy bomb.

All his attention was immediately drawn to the bomb, causing him to overlook the mysterious figure.

The situation was too urgent.

He had no time to think, instinctively unleashing his defensive battle skill to withstand the bomb’s explosion.

He protected most of the mercenaries behind him, but also inadvertently shielded the mysterious figure.

Everything was within the mysterious figure’s calculations.

He drew a dagger and stabbed toward Men Shi’s lower back.

Gold level battle skill—Kidney Strike.

Men Shi was struck, and one of his kidneys was pierced through by the dagger.

The power of the gold level battle qi was immense, piercing directly through his lower back.

Men Shi let out a muffled groan, the pain jolting him back to his senses.

He didn’t dodge but instead reached out with his free hand.

He grabbed the mysterious figure’s arm with both hands!

The mysterious figure tried to break free but found that Men Shi was using all his strength, his large hands clamping down on the man’s arm, preventing him from escaping.

The mysterious figure sneered coldly, unhurried.

In the next moment, he used his battle skill—Slippery Body.

The mysterious figure's entire body became oily and slippery, as if it were covered in grease.

The harder Men Shi tried, the more he was unable to restrain the mysterious figure.

The mysterious figure gently pulled his hand away and broke free from Men Shi's grasp.

He pulled out his dagger again and was about to slit Men Shi's throat to deliver the fatal blow.

But in the next moment, Zong Ge pounced.

The mysterious figure didn't need to turn around to feel a strong gust of wind behind him.

He chose not to assassinate Men Shi and let him go for the time being.

He pulled a small spherical alchemy item from his sleeve.

He flicked his wrist and threw the spherical alchemy item onto the floor.

With a dull bang, the spherical item exploded into a large cloud of thick smoke.

The white smoke quickly filled the entire cabin, and the pungent smell forced the mercenaries to close their eyes and mouths.

Zong Ge also held his breath for a moment.

After distracting all of his enemies, the mysterious figure disappeared again.

Zong Ge's weapon missed its target, but it saved Men Shi.

Thick smoke billowed out of the hole in the ship's hull.

The mysterious figure emerged from the smoke and flew out like an arrow.

Boom!

The sound of a cannon resounded!

The cannonball hit the mysterious figure with precision.

When the alarm sounded, Chi Lai did not follow the others.

He understood his strengths, and his excellent military training allowed him to know how to cooperate with his comrades.

He immediately took up a position at a cannon and waited in readiness.

When the mysterious figure leaped into the air, he immediately fired the cannon.

Chi Lai was a professional gunner with specialized battle skills. Not only could he increase the power of the cannonball, but he could also increase its accuracy.

After being hit by the cannon, the mysterious figure immediately fell into the sea.

“Follow me!”

The water pigman roared fiercely and took the lead in jumping into the sea.

The rest of the water pigmen followed suit.

Water pigmen were naturally good swimmers and had always been one of the best sources of soldiers for naval warfare.

“Gang Lie, get back!” The water pigmen's leader arrived first and dragged Gang Lie behind him.

The water pigman leader was at the iron level, much stronger than Gang Lie.

His outward display of weakness was just a strategy for survival.

He was also a responsible leader.

He knew that the enemy was mysterious and powerful, so he preferred to charge ahead himself to protect his people behind him.

Zong Ge ran to the breach created by the alchemy bomb and saw waves rolling on the sea.

The water pigmen were fighting a fierce battle with the mysterious figure.

Zong Ge snorted heavily, his face grave, his heart filled with anxiety.

He knew that this newly formed Backwater Battle Squad was no match for the mysterious figure.

The enemy was truly gold level.

The Backwater Battle Squad going into battle was tantamount to sending them to their deaths.

However, just as Zong Ge was about to give the order for the water pigmen to retreat, he saw the leader of the water pigmen excitedly raise a corpse and emerge from the water.

Gang Lie also emerged from the water and shouted excitedly, “We killed him!”

There was no surprise on Zong Ge's face.

He quickly shouted, “This is not his real body, be careful!”

In the next moment, the leader of the water pigmen was surprised to see the corpse in his hands gradually revert to a humanoid alchemy puppet.

The mysterious figure took advantage of the thick fog obscuring everyone's vision to release the alchemy puppet and disguise himself.

He tricked them with the classic art of diversion.

His true form was nowhere to be found.

Zong Ge held his weapon, fully alert, his face pale.

He would rather fight this mysterious enemy.

A worse situation had already arisen—the other party had quietly slipped away.

Zong Ge had no idea what the enemy's next move would be, or what his purpose was.

He could only defend passively.

But what effect would such a defense have?

The enemy had already secretly boarded the flagship of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps and attempted to assassinate the two magicians, Hong Teng and Lao Zhong.

Such actions had fully demonstrated his infiltration capabilities.

Given the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ current defensive measures, they truly could not guard against such a skilled individual.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had been completely forced into a defensive position.

Under Zi Di's control, the two scout alchemy puppets charged into the storage compartment.

“Damn it!” Zi Di's expression soured.

She realized that many of the items they had just obtained were missing.

A large amount of alchemy materials were gone.

Most importantly, the three gold equipment were gone.

Only the formation diagram panels remained.

Even more terrifying was that there were several bundles of alchemy bombs in the ship's hold.

Without time to think, Zi Di immediately controlled the scout-type alchemy puppet, instructing it to grab the alchemy bombs and fly out of the ship at full speed.

The scout-type golem used all its speed, but it couldn't outpace the countdown.

“It's too late!”

“Everyone, pay attention! It's going to explode!”

Through the communication device, Zi Di hurriedly notified Zong Ge and the other survivors.

Just as the alchemy bombs were about to explode, a beam of light suddenly shot out from the mage tower on the pier.

The beam hit the alchemy bomb right in the middle.

In an instant, the bomb was decomposed and reduced to countless particles of powder.

“Hmph.” The mysterious figure had returned to the pier and was hiding in the shadows, watching the scene of the alchemy bomb being destroyed.

The next moment, the mage tower sounded an alarm.

The people nearby let out panicked cries.

A large number of city guards quickly gathered and rushed to the pier.

It quickly became bustling with activity.

Meanwhile, the mysterious figure, carrying a harpoon in one hand and a longbow in the other, calmly left the pier, following the shadows.

Chapter 521: Lan Zao’s Bloodline

The Deep Sea Monster Fish.

The spectral Zi Di was currently cultivating.

She held a soul crystal in each hand, continuously drawing power from them.

The soul crystals appeared to be black, elongated crystals, but at their core, they were the essence of souls.

Necromancers utilized necromantic magic to slaughter beings of various races, extracting their souls, grinding them, pressing them, and purifying them, ultimately producing soul crystals of varying sizes and qualities.

With the two soul crystals in her hands, Zi Di's soul foundation was advancing at a visible pace. As her soul foundation deepened, her spirit strength also continued to rise.

Soon, the soul crystals in Zi Di's hands completely dissipated.

Zi Di looked at the few soul crystal shards in her hand with regret, feeling a sense of loss.

"Soul crystals are really useful! It's a pity there aren't many of them."

“In fact, Cang Xu was very lucky to be able to purchase them.”

Cang Xu acquired a large amount of undead resources from the undead gathering on Weeping Pine Island.

He stored half of the soul crystals and half of the undead materials on the Iron Lump.

Additionally, he obtained replicas of all the undead inheritances at the auction.

Zi Di benefited from this.

The soul crystals she now used for cultivation came from Cang Xu.

In fact, it wasn't just these gains; Cang Xu also gained a lot of insight at the auction, learning about the current state and situation of necromancers.

Zi Di also learned that necromancers capable of creating soul crystals were extremely rare. Moreover, even those who could create them rarely sold them externally. Because once exposed, it would provoke an imperial crackdown or even a scramble among necromancers themselves!

It was a relatively uncommon situation for Jian Gui to sell soul crystals externally.

Soul crystals could not be abused; they were like food for the soul and had to be used at intervals rather than concentrated in a short period of time.

Using them in the latter manner could cause explosive growth in the soul, leading to indigestion or even soul deformity.

After finishing her soul crystal training, Zi Di began to chant a spell.

Necromancy—Burden Alleviation.

After successfully casting the spell, the power of the Burden Alleviation spell enveloped her.

The mental burden she had incurred from enslaving other souls became lighter.

After casting the Burden Alleviation spell three times in succession, Zi Di began chanting the Karma Removal spell.

The name of the Karma Removal spell has an imperial eastern style, and its primary function is to reduce the erosion of negative energy on the necromancer herself.

Both the Burden Alleviation spell and Karma Removal spell were extremely precious core spells.

Zi Di has obtained these two spells for some time now.

The effect of the Burden Alleviation spell on her has begun to weaken significantly.

The Karma Removal spell, however, was still effective.

Zi Di had only recently transformed into a spectral lifeform, but the erosion of negative energy on her was constant and gradually accumulating and deepening.

"Now, I can fully control the silver level ghost Lan Lu. Adding another silver level ghost is also possible."

This was primarily due to the absorption of soul crystals and the use of the Burden Alleviation spell.

“At this rate of progress, my promotion to iron level necromancer is just around the corner.”

Zi Di was an iron level magician in her lifetime, and after her death, she was transformed into an iron level ghostly girl. But after switching to necromancy, she had to start from scratch.

Therefore, her necromancer level was cultivated from the start, and now she was at the bronze level.

Although she had never ceased her related cultivation, the effects of her progress were not significant.

This situation persisted until she obtained soul crystals, the Burden Alleviation spell, the Karma Removal spell, and so on.

Therefore, a magician's cultivation heavily relies on resources. If resources were abundant, the effects of training could be greatly improved, saving a lot of time.

Zi Di had a strong premonition.

She could feel that she would be able to be promoted to an iron level magician, and that day was not far off.

This was because she had already gone through the process of being promoted to an iron level magician once before in her lifetime.

The feeling between the two was very similar.

“I just don't know what the qualifications of my ghost body are, and whether the iron level is the upper limit.”

Although Zi Di detected that her bloodline was the Gunpowder Flower bloodline, this answer was related to her body before she died, not her current ghost body.

Zi Di currently did not have the ability to test the bloodline of an undead life.

This was beyond her capabilities.

She had to wait for Cang Xu's return and obtain more comprehensive undead data before she could perform a magic blood test on an undead life form.

“I hope Cang Xu's mission is a great success.”

"If he can obtain information on the usage of memory crystals, that would be perfect. It would be a huge piece of good news for all the survivors."

Zi Di thought to herself and silently wished Cang Xu well.

Whether Cang Xu's mission succeeded or failed, the impact on all the survivors would be enormous.

The day's undead training came to an end.

Zi Di left the training cabin and returned to the alchemy cabin.

The alchemy formation in the alchemy cabin emitted a brilliant glow.

Before the training, Zi Di had already conducted a magic blood test here.

Since she had obtained the key materials from the alchemy materials sent by the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord, Lan Zao's blood test could continue.

However, the reaction time for this magic potion blood test was too long, so Zi Di, valuing her time, had the tower spirit control and oversee the alchemy formation.

She herself went ahead to perform her necromancy cultivation.

When the alchemy cabin door opened, Zi Di immediately saw the results of the magic potion blood test within the alchemy formation.

The small sample of Lan Zao's blood had expanded over a hundredfold in volume, forming a massive blood bubble.

The blood bubble floated above the alchemy formation, slowly rising and falling vertically, constantly fluctuating.

A flash of light shimmered in Zi Di's eyes.

“So, Lan Zao's bloodline is the Fish Bladder bloodline.”

“This bloodline can give its owner a strong perception of airflow.”

"If the bloodline concentration reaches 30%, the owner of the Fish Bladder bloodline can consciously control the gas concentration in their body. For example, by absorbing a large amount of air and infusing it into the bloodstream, they can enhance physical performance."

“If the bloodline concentration reaches 60%, the possessor of the Fish Bladder bloodline can freely float up and down in water, demonstrating exceptional aquatic prowess.”

"If the bloodline concentration exceeds 95%, the possessor of the Fish Bladder bloodline can even float mid-air. However, the height at which they can float is limited."

Various pieces of information about the Fish Bladder bloodline flashed through Zi Di's mind.

This information came from the War Merchant's database.

“Obviously, the concentration of this extraordinary bloodline in Lan Zao is very low, definitely less than

"The Fish Bladder bloodline is a silver level bloodline. If Lan Zao can purify it, his strength will break through bronze, cross iron, and reach silver level. This will be a tremendous change to his life trajectory!"

Just as Zi Di was silently thinking, the tower spirit suddenly issued a warning.

Zi Di immediately realized that the alchemy puppet impersonating her on the Love Triangle had come under attack.

The alchemy puppet impersonating Zi Di had its own behavioral programs and was also remotely controlled by the tower spirit.

The mysterious assassin's blow dart was coated with poison. The poison reacted violently to the surface of the alchemy puppet, causing it to be detected.

“Enemy attack!”

Zi Di immediately reacted and remotely controlled the alchemy device in the Love Triangle, emitting a loud and shrill alarm.

Then Zong Ge and the others rushed to catch the thief. Zi Di found the alchemy bomb and controlled the scout-type alchemy puppet to take out the explosives. In the end, with the unexpected help of the mage tower, the explosion was stopped in advance.

The mysterious assassin disappeared with the many treasures he had stolen.

The tower spirit then analyzed the poison on the blow dart.

"The cost of manufacturing this highly toxic poison is very high. In order to deal with Hong Teng, a bronze level magician, this gold level assassin was willing to spend a fortune." After seeing the test results, Zi Di felt flattered.

She did not feel in danger.

Even for her, blow darts and poison had no effect on her ghostly body.

Although Zong Ge and the others had scared away the mysterious assassin, everyone's faces were grave.

Where did this mysterious assassin come from?

What exactly is his identity?

What is his primary objective?

Will he return again?

How did he manage to infiltrate the premises?

These questions were of urgent concern to the entire Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps!

If appropriate countermeasures were not taken in a timely manner, then the mysterious assassin who came the first time would naturally come again the second time.

This was a huge psychological burden for all the mercenaries of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

If such assassins were allowed to come and go as they pleased, they might even be beheaded in their sleep.

The carriage carrying the dragonman youngster was slowly making its way down the street.

He was carefully studying a list in his hands.

Tonight, he accepted an invitation from the Treasure Merchant Alliance. to discuss matters with its leader.

This list came from them.

The Treasure Merchant Alliance. was one of the six major merchant alliances of the Sheng Ming Empire, specializing in the sale of alchemy treasures.

The dragonman youngster wanted to obtain more gold level equipment from this channel.

However, after carefully studying the list in his hand, he found that the equipment provided by the Treasure Merchant Alliance. was inferior to that provided by the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord, with only a few items standing out.

This showed the depth of the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's resources.

"Commander Long Fu!" Suddenly, a mercenary rushed over to report.

The Love Triangle had been robbed.

Not only had a large amount of alchemy materials and gold level equipment been lost, but the magicians Lao Zhong and Hong Teng had also been assassinated!

The dragonman youngster's face suddenly changed, and he immediately jumped out of the carriage and displayed his strongest form.

Battle skill—Surge!

With the cooperation of spurting smoke ring, he increased his speed to the current limit in just a few seconds.

The commotion caused by the dragonman youngster immediately drew the attention of many people on the street.

When he returned to the dock, the city guard had already secured the area. A squad of snow elf magicians had boarded the Love Triangle and conducted an on-site investigation.

Upon seeing the bodies of Lao Zhong and Hong Teng, the dragonman youngster erupted in anger. His face contorted with fury as he growled, “Investigate! Find out the truth! I will make the murderer pay a heavy price."

The snow elves who came to investigate had already confirmed the deaths of Lao Zhong and Hong Teng.

Both of these alchemy puppets were equipped with the Anti-investigation and Prophecy spell and Deceptive Disguise spell. Not only did they deceive the mysterious assassin, but they also managed to keep almost everyone else in the dark.

The snow elf magician in charge of the investigation even comforted the dragonman youngster.

He expressed sympathy for the situation.

According to intelligence, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had only two magicians, both of whom had perished in this assassination. This was undoubtedly a massive loss for them, even if only one bronze level and one iron level magician died.

However, when the dragonman youngster returned to the captain's cabin, the resentment on his face quickly disappeared.

Zi Di had secretly rushed back.

The two met in the captain's cabin.

When Zi Di saw the dragonman youngster, she smiled and spread her hands, "I just tested Lan Zao's bloodline, and in the blink of an eye, I found myself dead."

A look of joy flashed across the dragonman youngster's face, and he was about to ask a question.

Zi Di answered him in advance, “Lan Zao's bloodline is the Fish Bladder bloodline, which is consistent with what you predicted earlier.”

“A silver level bloodline... Very good!” The dragonman youngster was happy for Lan Zao.

He then asked, “Can the location of the alchemy equipment be determined?”

Zi Di nodded: "Although the positioning device was blocked for a period of time, it has now been restored. These gold level pieces of equipment are not scattered but concentrated in one place. Looking at the map of Snow Bird Harbor, it should be somewhere in the slums. It is very likely that the mysterious assassin is also there!"

The dragonman youngster nodded: “Next, let's implement the plan we discussed earlier.”

Chapter 522: Two Heads Are Better Than One (1)

It turned out that the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had already taken precautions against the assassins' invasion.

Earlier, the dragonman youngster had accepted the city Lord's warm invitation and learned from him about the existence of Hua Tang and his secret connection to Teng Dong Lang, the leader of the Bayonet Gang.

The dragonman youngster immediately took note of this crucial information and began to gather information about Hua Tang.

After learning about Hua Tang, the survivors began to prepare for the worst.

Zong Ge looked up from the information in his hands and looked at Zi Di: "Obviously, Hua Tang is not an ordinary gold level thief. With such a track record, his bloodline must be extraordinary. Gold level is not his limit."

“It's difficult for a fighter like me to guard against him.”

“I think magic and alchemy are more useful.”

Zi Di smiled bitterly: "I'm just a bronze level magician. It's basically impossible to rely on magic traps to guard against a legendary figure like Hua Tang."

"As for other alchemy methods... we are currently struggling to even make silver level alchemy equipment. It was only after we obtained the magic medicine bath tub that our medicine-making abilities greatly improved. I can refine some specific potions as a prevention measure."

"But you need to understand that only gold level potions can be effective against such an opponent. Gold level... that exceeds our current pharmaceutical capabilities. We have no lack of formulas, but there will definitely be a shortage of alchemy materials. I can't estimate how long it will take to gather all the medicinal materials we need in such a short period of time."

At this point, Zi Di changed the subject.

"Compared to this Hua Tang, I'm more concerned about the attitude of the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord."

“According to our intelligence, this Lord has never had any conflicts with the Bayonet Gang since taking office.”

“Does he truly intend to confront the Bayonet Gang?”

"Is there a possibility that he has already established a secret alliance with the Bayonet Gang? Could he be the Bayonet Gang's greatest ally in Snow Bird Harbor?"

"According to the policies of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom, the position of Lord is rotated. Snow Bird Harbor is one of the eight major ports of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom and is a very important city. Calculating the time, the city Lord's tenure is already short. Would he really be willing to take the risk of targeting the Bayonet Gang, such a local powerhouse?"

The dragonman youngster nodded solemnly: “If that's the case, then the city Lord's invitation to me is the prelude to the Bayonet Gang targeting us—it's a trap they've set.”

Zi Di continued, “If the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord truly intends to confront the Bayonet Gang, we must not grow complacent.”

“Since he has been able to endure for so many years, he is clearly a man of great schemes.”

“What are his plans for outsiders like us?”

“Will he strike us down ruthlessly after we have made great efforts and sacrifices to achieve ultimate victory?”

“Is his stance against the Bayonet Gang truly resolute?”

"If the situation changes, will he stab us in the back and abandon us? Will he use us to curry favor with the Bayonet Gang?"

Although Zi Di was young, she had a wealth of experience.

In particular, she had recently learned some secrets about her father and the difficult times the Wisteria Merchant Alliance had gone through.

She had a deeper understanding of and guarded against the deceitfulness of human nature.

The dragonman youngster agreed with Zi Di's analysis.

This was also his hidden concern.

Although the youngster was a believer in justice, that did not mean he would trust outsiders unconditionally. Especially since he had only just met the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord for the first time.

The dragonman youngster looked at the survivors around him and sought their opinions: “Let's hear what you all think.”

After everyone looked at each other for a moment, Tripleblade spoke first.

“As you all know, I used to be the leader of a Mercenary Corps.”

“From my previous position, this is a very rare opportunity.”

"It's difficult for a Mercenary Corps to establish itself in a city. Especially for outsiders like us arriving in such a city."

“Snow Bird Harbor has been developing for a long time, and every industry is already crowded with people.”

“Even if the commander and Teng Dong Lang's heroic duel was impressive, it only secured a foothold for the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.”

“If it were me, I'd take the risk!”

“Such an opportunity might never come again for a normal Mercenary Corps.”

“Besides, as we discussed earlier, we might have to stay in the Ice Sculpture Kingdom for a while.”

“Even if the ransom note has been sent, it will still take some time to negotiate and exchange hostages.”
