"The Ice Sculpture Kingdom is a neutral nation. Even if our secret on Mystifying Monster Island is discovered, the imperial pursuit force would have to consider the Ice Sculpture Kingdom's stance before attempting to capture us here."

"Regardless of the final outcome of the Redemption Plan, we must act swiftly to convert our gains along the way into our strength. Only a place like the Ice Sculpture Kingdom has sufficient resources to help us achieve this transformation."

The dragonman youngster nodded in agreement with Tripleblade's words.

Giant islands like the Ice Sculpture Kingdom are rare.

It was not to say that giant islands were not rare. It's that the Ice Sculpture Kingdom had developed over thousands of years and had abundant resources.

It was an independent kingdom in the vast sea, capable of self-sufficiency, and many of the alchemy products it refined were widely sold all over the world.

After Tripleblade finished speaking, Lan Zao said, “Regardless of the attitude and intentions of the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord, providing us with a large amount of resources is a real benefit.”

"We can definitely drag it out. Through his channels, we can acquire a large amount of alchemy materials and gold level equipment."

Seeing that the conversation had reached this point, Zi Di interjected, "There is one thing I need to remind everyone. No matter what you buy, there are risks involved."

“Even gold equipment.”

“There are countless cases of gold level equipment being counterfeited on the market in order to sell at high prices and obtain illegal profits.”

"We need a way to detect this equipment. Unfortunately, we don't have that ability at the moment. We desperately need a qualified alchemist."

"Buying counterfeit goods is still somewhat acceptable. But if the equipment we purchase has been tampered with or has surveillance devices installed, our situation will be very disadvantageous."

Zi Di paused for a moment before continuing, “Currently, purchasing alchemy materials is relatively safe.”

"This is because we purchase a very large quantity of alchemy materials. If someone with ulterior motives were to tamper with them, it would be difficult for them to choose the right target."

“Inspecting alchemy materials has much lower requirements than inspecting equipment.”

"The consequences of tampering with alchemy materials are also less severe. Even if we fail to detect it, the worst that could happen is that we fail when refining potions or crafting equipment. Because the other party doesn't know exactly what we're trying to refine, they can't target us specifically."

“But gold equipment is a different matter.”

"If we purchase gold equipment, it is clearly targeting Teng Dong Lang. If the city Lord is colluding with them, they might tamper with these items."

“If the gold equipment malfunctions during the captain's duel with Teng Dong Lang, the consequences are easy to imagine.”

“So even if we manage to purchase a large quantity of gold equipment, we must remain vigilant when using it.”

After Zi Di finished speaking, everyone fell silent for a moment.

Bai Ya gathered his courage and spoke up: “If that's the case, I... I have an idea.”

"We have the Anti-investigation and Prophecy spell and Deceptive Disguise spell; we can fully utilize them. We can add these two spells to those scout-type alchemy puppets."

“We can also add them to those trigger-type spell traps.”

"If someone tries to sneak in, they won't be able to detect the traps. On the contrary, we'll detect them, or they'll trigger the traps and set off an alarm."

“That way, we'll be able to detect intruders.”

Bai Ya was a hunter by trade, and setting traps was one of his habits of mind.

He kept his head down as he spoke, and when he finished, he looked up anxiously to observe the reactions of the others.

Then he saw the dragonman youngster and Zi Di smiling at him.

Their smiles were full of encouragement.

Chapter 523: Two Heads Are Better Than One (2)

Zi Di nodded and said, "That is indeed a good idea. We can do that."

“But I have some bad news to share with everyone.”

"The efficiency of the Mermaid Fairy Tale divine artifact in producing pearl bubbles has been decreasing. It is clear that we have consumed a significant amount of the divine power stored within the artifact."

“We need to use it sparingly and avoid waste.”

“Of course, I agree with using it in the current situation.”

“However, this method is not foolproof.”

“Hua Tang might not notice the scout-type golems, but his stealth skills are so advanced that he won't be detected by our scout alchemy puppets either.”

“Setting the trap is acceptable. If Hua Tang comes, we can catch him off guard.”

Most people agreed with this plan, but Fei She, who had always been a staunch supporter of Zi Di, shook his head.

He offered a different opinion.

“If we find Hua Tang sneaking in, are we really going to expose him?”

"He is a gold level assassin with a legendary track record in the Ice Sculpture Kingdom. Even if we find him, what can we do to him?"

"Can we really keep him here? The city Lords and so many powerful people couldn't make him stay, so how can we?"

“My idea is: if Hua Tang is only here to steal some equipment and alchemy materials, then he is acting on Teng Dong Lang’s request to give us a profound lesson.”

“We should just let him have it. From the start, we should pretend we never discovered him.”

“This doesn’t mean we’re giving up on dealing with him! This is just a strategy. We need time to develop and grow, right?"

Everyone fell silent.

Fei She's seemingly cowardly suggestion was unexpectedly approved by Zong Ge.

He then revealed his true thoughts: “If we expose the existence of the gold level assassin, then our flagship and the pier we are in will become a battlefield.”

“Regardless of the final outcome, our losses will be immense.”

“Perhaps none of our ships will survive, and the mercenaries will suffer heavy casualties.”

The crowd fell silent once more.

Fei She’s words made sense.

Zong Ge continued, “Actually, we can design the trap from a different angle.”

“We can't allow a battle to take place on our ship, and we can't treat this place as a battlefield.”

“Based on our current defensive measures, only an expert can break through.”

"Trigger-type magic traps are actually a weakness for us. They will provoke conflict and cause a battle to break out here."

“Why not be bold and not set up any such traps at all?”

"We can use a tracking device. Imbue it with the Anti-investigation and Prophecy spell and Deceptive Disguise spell."

“We can deliberately place some valuables in the cabin to attract Hua Tang to steal them.”

“There's a high probability he'll steal the tracking device.”

“According to the intelligence we've gathered, Hua Tang always steals something during each of his crimes.”

“With these two spells, even he would find it hard to notice.”

"Once he leaves, we can track him down using the locator. We'll find his exact location and launch an assault."

“This way, the battlefield won't be at the pier.”

“Most importantly, we have allies.”

“If the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord is serious about dealing with the Bayonet Gang, he will take action.”

After Zong Ge finished speaking, Tripleblade immediately applauded and agreed, “As expected of Lord Zong Ge!”

Zi Di also nodded, "Good plan! We can even use this to judge the city Lord's true attitude."

The dragonman youngster fell into deep thought.

He was surprised to hear Zong Ge agree with Fei She.

After listening to Zong Ge's entire opinion, he couldn't help but feel emotional: “This is the Zong Ge I know.”

Zong Ge's plan was just like his personality, with the iron-blooded style of the military.

Seeing the dragonman youngster's indecision, Zong Ge saw through his thoughts at a glance and said to him, “You have to understand one thing.”

"We can't guard against a gold level thief. Especially someone like Hua Tang."

“I know what you're worried about.”

“You're worried that he'll come to assassinate us, and without trigger-based magic traps, we'll have a hard time detecting him.”

“If he's not just here to steal treasures but to assassinate us, then let him do it!”

"Even if we use trigger-based magic traps to detect him in advance, what can we do? He can just retreat temporarily."

“We can detect him once, block him twice, but can we stop him a third or fourth time?”

“According to the intelligence we've gathered, even if the traps are triggered, given Hua Tang's skills, he can overpower most people.”

"He has encountered hidden traps before; he didn't notice them and triggered the alarms. That's how he operates—if one attempt fails, he tries again and again. Long term defense will eventually fail; the highest number of attempts he’s tried is three. So far, there’s no target he can’t assassinate.”

“Our strength is far weaker than the powerful figures and faction leaders mentioned in the intelligence.”

"Since we’re attacking the strong as the weak, how can we avoid taking risks?"

"If anyone here, including myself, unfortunately sacrifices their life, it will be a worthy death. The rest of us can avenge them, right?"

Finally, Zong Ge stared at the dragonman youngster and asked, "As a leader, you are very responsible, but don't exaggerate this responsibility. Can you protect the life of every single person? Can you make such a guarantee? You must recognize the limits of your own abilities."

“Therefore, you must be mentally prepared—on this path we seek redemption, some among us will sacrifice themselves.”

“Who can guarantee that we will all survive to see that moment of success?”

“Sacrifice is not the issue; what matters is that the sacrifice must have value.”

“They cannot die in vain!”

Zong Ge's words were sharp and piercing, exposing the biggest burden in the dragonman youngster's heart.

The dragonman youngster fell into a long silence.

He reflected on himself: could he really make such a guarantee? Could he guarantee the safety of every member's life?

Of course not.

He was only a silver level battle qi cultivator.

This environment was much more complicated than the Mystifying Monster Island, and the opponents he had to face were much more powerful than those artificial monsters.

Because of this psychological burden, did he become timid in his decision-making and actions?

Yes.

The dragonman youngster had to admit this.

He knew that Zong Ge's plan was the right one.

It could truly solve the problem.

Using two concealment spells to set off traps could not solve a difficult problem like Hua Tang's infiltration.

Zong Ge's plan had more possibilities.

Finally, the dragonman youngster looked up and sighed deeply, “I am too weak!”

Then, he turned his head to look at the people around him.

“We are all too weak!”

“Sure enough, pursuing one's own strength is the path of righteousness.”

The dragonman youngster agreed with Zong Ge's plan, but he still asked the others for their opinions on the plan.

Lan Zao was the first to speak up: “I will follow you to the death!”

The big guy was the second to speak: “I'll listen to my dad.”

Tripleblade looked at Zong Ge: “Lord Zong Ge's plan is the best; I am willing to carry it out.”

Zi Di glanced at the dragonman youngster.

Now that Cang Xu was gone, she was the one who understood the dragonman youngster best and knew his long-term plans for the entire team.

The dragonman youngster wanted to share the secret of the Bloodcore, so the more allies he could establish, the better.

The survivors in front of him were natural allies.

Now it was even more important to let them fight the enemy together and deepen their mutual recognition through repeated life-and-death cooperation.

Zi Di said, “I have no objections.”

Seeing Zi Di's stance, Fei She hesitated. He was more concerned about Zi Di than himself.

If possible, Fei She was willing to take on a riskier position himself, even acting as bait, as long as Zi Di remained safe.

However, Fei She knew it was impossible, and he also knew that such moments of declaration were always critical.

He then expressed his approval.

Bai Ya unconsciously clenched his fists. As a young man, he was stirred by the atmosphere and exclaimed, "Isn't this how we've always pushed forward? Does leaving the Mystifying Monster Island mean we've become cowards? That's clearly impossible."

Seeing that most agreed, Mu Ban nodded in agreement.

Qiao Shan's face paled slightly as she was the last to speak.

The dragonman youngster saw her expression and comforted her, “If you don't want to take the risk, you can find an opportunity to return to the Deep Sea Monster Fish, where it should be safe.”

“Everyone don't force yourselves. This choice applies to everyone here.”

When the dragonman youngster said this, he tried hard not to look at Zi Di.

Zi Di, however, felt something in her heart and looked at the side of the dragonman youngster with a smile on her lips.

These words made Qiao Shan firm in her resolve, and she agreed to the plan.

Two heads were better than one.

In the end, everyone agreed to Zong Ge's strategy.

Chapter 524: City Lord, Please Help Me

Because he had already made plans, when the dragonman youngster heard the mercenary report in the carriage on his way home that the Love Triangle had been attacked by assassins, he was not shocked. His first thought was, “The other party actually took action tonight!”

When the city government palace handed over the goods to the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, it caused quite a stir.

In fact, when the transport team was halfway there, the news had already spread throughout Snow Bird Harbor.

This was the first time that both sides had completed the handover and settled the payment.

The survivors had already discussed this during their deliberations—this was the first dangerous period, and it was very likely to attract the enemy to infiltrate!

Because everyone knew that the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was buying up gold level equipment on a large scale, obviously to help their commander, Long Fu, prepare for the heroic duel that would take place in the near future.

As Long Fu's opponent, would Teng Dong Lang sit idly by and watch his opponent grow stronger?

Even if he was proud of his identity, his subordinates would inevitably take the initiative to be the "bad guys", tripping up the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps and playing dirty tricks.

Because the duel between the two had a very wide-ranging impact, it was not only about the two parties involved, but also about the future activities and profits of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps and the Bayonet Gang in Snow Bird Harbor.

It could be said that the survivors were already on guard against the mysterious intruder and had taken as many defensive and investigative measures as possible.

But the mysterious intruder was indeed skilled and daring. That night, he struck and “successfully” assassinated the only two magicians of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, then disappeared with a large amount of treasure.

After receiving this news, the dragonman youngster immediately rushed out of the carriage and rushed to the pier at full speed, looking extremely anxious and urgent.

Of course, he was extremely vigilant on the way.

This was because the survivors had also analyzed in advance that it was very likely that the other party would ambush the reinforcements.

Fortunately, the dragonman youngster ran all the way back without any accidents.

When the dragonman youngster returned to his flagship and saw the corpses of Hong Teng and Lao Zhong, he immediately put all his worries aside.

They were just two alchemy puppets.

As for the lost supplies and equipment, the dragonman youngster didn't care.

The lost gold level equipment was risky and unsuitable for the dragonman youngster to use in the first place. Using it as a vessel for the positioning device was the best use for it at the moment.

The lost alchemy materials could be bought again. The alchemy materials delivered in the first batch were naturally more common and ordinary than the latter two batches. The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had sufficient funds and the confidence to do so.

The dragonman youngster even felt that these alchemy materials had already served their purpose by distracting some of the mysterious assassin's energy and time.

The magician leader of the investigation team talked with the dragonman youngster, his words revealing a hint of comfort and sympathy.

However, he did not know the true thoughts in the other party's mind.

Such a “loss” was good news for the survivors!

The dragonman youngster perfunctorily replied to the magician leader with a few words, then returned to the captain's cabin to meet secretly with Zi Di.

Good things came in pairs.

He learned another piece of good news from Zi Di—

What exactly was Lan Zao's bloodline?

The answer to this question has been found.

The Fish Bladder bloodline was a silver level bloodline, which meant that Lan Zao would have more room for growth. He would no longer be limited to the bronze level for the rest of his life.

Of course, in order to enhance the Fish Bladder bloodline in his body, the survivors had to build a Bloodline Purification formation.

This brought them back to the old problem.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps urgently needed an alchemist!

The dragonman youngster and Zi Di didn't stay long. The former left the captain's cabin.

He came before Zong Ge, the magician leader, and the city guard leader, and instructed, "Shi Qi, take care of the two leaders and continue to command the flagship."

“I'll go ask our city Lord for help!”

The magician leader's eyes lit up when he heard this. He ran a few steps to catch up with the dragonman youngster and whispered a few words to him, implying that he had received orders from the city Lord to favor the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps before taking action!

After hearing this, the dragonman youngster immediately thanked the magician leader.

The implied meaning was very clear: the city Lord was on the side of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, and there was a high possibility of cooperation between the two sides!

The dragonman youngster jumped directly off the ship and stepped onto the pier.

His every move immediately attracted a large number of hidden eyes.

The pier was very noisy, attracting a large number of intelligence workers to come and spy. However, the city guard and the magician investigation team were one step ahead of them, preventing them from getting close to the Love Triangle.

Under the gaze of everyone, the dragonman youngster, with an angry look on his face, did not take a carriage, but rushed directly to the city government palace.

He was much faster than a carriage.

Soon, he arrived at the gate of the city government palace.

An attendant had been waiting for a long time. When he saw the dragonman youngster, he immediately greeted him and whispered, “The city Lord had already anticipated your arrival, Lord Long Fu. He has given orders that you should go directly to his study.”

In the study, the dragonman youngster met the city Lord.

The city Lord was very concerned about the vicious incident that had occurred at the pier. As soon as they met, he asked directly, “Commander Long Fu, is the mysterious assassin who committed this crime really Hua Tang?”

The dragonman youngster bowed his head and said, “I am ashamed to say that our group was completely fooled by this despicable mysterious assassin. From beginning to end, we were unable to determine his true identity.”

“All we can say now is that he may be Hua Tang.”

The city Lord looked up and sighed, "Ah, if it really is him, it's understandable that your group couldn't defend against him. Not to mention the pier, even my castle is probably just a place that Hua Tang can come and go as he pleases."

The city Lord was very eloquent.

He was both comforting the dragonman youngster and excusing himself for his poor supervision of the pier.

The dragonman youngster bowed slightly and said bluntly, “I came here to ask for your help in investigating the truth.”

The city Lord looked solemn and said, "The criminal committed a heinous crime, and it is my duty to investigate and pursue the murderer. What's more, we are close trading partners."

"Commander Long Fu, you asked me for help, which shows that you trust me. I naturally don't want to betray that trust. However, I can't guarantee the success of the investigation. Especially if the perpetrator is really Hua Tang."

The dragonman youngster nodded, "If it really is Hua Tang, I want revenge! He slaughtered the only two magicians in our group. This is a deep hatred that I will never be able to get over."

The city Lord patted the dragonman youngster on the shoulder: “Let's go, we have to hurry, many clues will gradually disappear with the passage of time.”

The city Lord personally led the dragonman youngster into the magician tower on the pier.

It was the first time the dragonman youngster had entered such a core location.

The magician tower lived up to its reputation indeed!

Although it was just a quick tour, it still made the dragonman youngster envious.

Chapter 525: Silver Fox Mask

Soon, the city Lord took the dragonman youngster to the third floor of the magician tower.

This was the core floor of the magician tower.

Each magician tower had a different structure, so the number of core floors varied. Generally speaking, the top floor of a magician tower was the observation and detection floor, and the basement was the energy floor.

However, there are also examples of magician towers that place the core layer in the basement to avoid enemy targeting.

On the third floor, the city Lord introduced the dragonman youngster and the magician tower master to each other and reiterated their intention to investigate the identity of the mysterious assassin.

On the way there, the city Lord first communicated with the magician tower master to express his intention.

When they approached the magician tower, the city Lord and the dragonman youngster were both subjected to detection by the magician tower. During this process, they were surrounded by several alchemy puppets and had to remain where they were.

After passing this test, they entered the gate and arrived at the first floor.

Here, there was a special device for detecting lies, and the city Lord communicated with the magician tower master and asked key questions.

Only after answering correctly could they proceed further.

The procedure for entering the magician tower was extremely strict.

This was to prevent enemies skilled in disguise from first imprisoning the city Lord, then impersonating him to enter the magician tower and breach the defensive core of Snow Bird Harbor from within.

“I have prepared the relevant video, City Lord. Please take a look.” The magician tower master summoned the magic image through mental projection.

In the next moment, a three-dimensional floating magic image appeared before the dragonman youngster's eyes, showing the scene at the pier earlier.

In the image, there were patrol teams on all the ships of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, with the most patrol personnel on the Love Triangle.

Scout-type alchemy puppets were distributed on the masts, gunwales, and buildings near the pier.

The dragonman youngster quickly saw himself walking down the gangway, getting into a carriage, and leaving the pier.

“Look, the assassin has begun his operation. He started from this building.” The magician tower master stopped the magic image and gestured to the city Lord and the dragonman youngster.

The two followed the direction of his finger and saw a faint, transparent human figure jumping out of the window upstairs.

The dragonman youngster immediately asked, “Since you have already discovered his whereabouts, why didn't you take immediate action when he first made his move?”

The magician tower master shook his head with a cold expression on his face and said, "This is a video that I have processed preliminarily. At that time, the magician tower did not find anything unusual. As for how we were able to deduce the assassin's current movements, it involves professional alchemy techniques. I won't go into detail here; please continue watching."

The footage continued to play.

The semi-transparent human-like silhouette representing the mysterious figure effortlessly crossed the pier, climbed the ship's hull, leaped over the ship's rail, and arrived on the deck of the Love Triangle.

After brushing past a patrol team, he entered the forecastle.

Seeing this, the dragonman youngster's pupils contracted imperceptibly.

But soon after, the mysterious assassin came out of the forecastle and went to the captain's cabin at the stern.

Then, he left the captain's cabin, was stopped by the patrol team at the ship's ladder, waited for a moment, and descended the ship's ladder to the next deck.

"Once the other party is inside the ship, the magician tower cannot detect anything. I'll speed things up a little." The magician tower master sped up the playback speed of the magic image.

Soon, the dragonman youngster saw a sharp alarm sound from the Love Triangle, and the mercenaries sprang into action.

Zong Ge and Men Shi led their men and rushed into the cabin.

In a short while, the hull was blown apart by an explosion. Amidst the explosions, flying debris, and smoke, a hole appeared in the ship's hull.

A figure leaped out of the hole through the thick smoke.

But just as it reached mid-air, it was struck down by a cannonball.

The one who fired the cannon was Chi Lai.

Gang Lie and the other water pigmen jumped into the sea together, launching a pursuit of the fallen assassin, causing the sea surface to churn violently.

The leader of the water pigmen held up a corpse, floated to the surface, and shouted loudly.

Zong Ge gave the order to continue searching for the killer.

Several scout-type alchemy puppets carrying alchemy bombs had just flown out of the Love Triangle. At this moment, the magician tower suddenly fired a disintegration beam, successfully destroying the alchemy bombs.

“Damn that guy, I'll make him pay!” The dragonman youngster saw the mysterious assassin's actions with his own eyes, and couldn't help but snort, clenching his fists in anger.

The city Lord patted him on the shoulder to comfort him.

The dragonman youngster took a deep breath and bowed slightly to the city Lord to express his gratitude for the magician tower's help.

The magic image finally froze on the mysterious assassin reappearing, lingering at the corner of the pier, looking back at the moment the alchemy bomb was disintegrated.

The dragonman youngster still couldn't see the assassin's true appearance, only a translucent human-shaped outline.

However, unlike before, the assassin was now holding a longbow and a harpoon in each hand.

Even the gold level darts he was holding in his arms were clearly visible.

The magician tower master explained in advance, “This is also the result of post-processing. We didn't discover anything at the time.”

"The relevant alchemy technique is called the ‘Aura Algorithm’.

"We all know that everything has its own aura. Life has its own aura, battle qi has battle qi aura, and weapons and equipment also have their own auras."

"There are no two identical leaves in this world, so every aura is unique. Even if two leaves are infinitely similar, their auras will have extremely minor differences."

“By analyzing these auras to infer other information about the owner, that is the essence of the Aura Algorithm.”

“The above is only the basic principle of this alchemy technique. The actual details are too technical and vast, so I won't explain them.”

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord nodded and looked at the dragonman youngster, explaining, "The gold level equipment I sold you did not belong to me. They came from many sources and had long been registered in the Ice Sculpture Kingdom."

“Therefore, their auras were all recorded.”

“After analyzing this incident, the magician tower will be able to restore their specific locations through these auras.”

The dragonman youngster frowned and keenly grasped the key point: “So, the magician tower can analyze the outline and shape of the murderer because of his life aura?”

“If that's the case, can his true appearance be revealed?”

The magician tower master nodded: “Hua Tang has committed many crimes in the Ice Sculpture Kingdom.”

“Therefore, a significant amount of his aura remains.”

“The tower spirit is currently comparing these auras.”

“This semi-transparent human-shaped outline is the result of that process.”

"While I was receiving the two of you earlier, the tower spirit was continuously performing aura calculations. I believe it has now produced new results."

As he spoke, the magic image in front of everyone gradually changed.

Under the gaze of the dragonman youngster, the face of the translucent human-shaped outline gradually turned into...

A silver fox mask.

Chapter 526: Leaving the Harbor

Seeing the silver fox mask on the mysterious figure's face, the dragonman youngster couldn't help but take a deep breath.

He had previously collected information about Hua Tang, which included details about the silver fox mask.

Hua Tang always wore a silver fox mask when committing crimes.

According to the information, the silver fox mask was a very effective disguise.

It had also become one of Hua Tang's trademarks.

The dragonman youngster continued watching, but the magic image stopped.

The youngster immediately shifted his gaze to the magician tower master and silently asked a question.

The magician tower master shook his head: “It seems that this is all the tower spirit has calculated.”

The dragonman youngster said disappointedly: “The result is just a silver fox mask?”

The magician tower master explained, "Hua Tang is extremely cunning. He knows the Aura Algorithm, so every time he goes out, he uses methods to conceal or confuse his own aura. This is also one of the reasons why the Ice Sculpture Kingdom's nationwide manhunt for him has been ineffective."

“Therefore, using the Aura Algorithm to predict his whereabouts has significant limitations.”

“On many occasions, official calculations using this method have only yielded a silver fox mask.”

“Because this mask is Hua Tang's outer layer of disguise, its aura cannot be further concealed for it to function normally.”

“If it were concealed, it would be superfluous and counterproductive, actually impairing the mask's effectiveness.”

The dragonman youngster’s brows pursed, and he looked at the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord and asked, “Since that is so, can we confirm if he really is Hua Tang?”

"With this evidence, can we ask the Ice Sculpture Kingdom for help? Can we get powerful reinforcements?"

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord sighed deeply and said helplessly, “This evidence is too weak, it's difficult to get help from the upper echelons.”

“This might be Hua Tang's cunning strategy.”

“Similar incidents have occurred before, where someone wore a silver fox mask and impersonated Hua Tang to commit crimes.”

“You've gathered intelligence on Hua Tang, so you should know that such impersonations have happened multiple times.”

"The authorities even believed on several occasions that they had already captured Hua Tang. In the end, Hua Tang committed another crime, greatly embarrassing the authorities."

The dragonman youngster fell silent.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord continued, "According to intelligence, when Hua Tang is in action, he likes to wear a white robe with colorful flowers blooming on it. His eyes are bright pink. He acts very conspicuously, which is different from the mysterious person who attacked your group this time."

“The existence of all these doubts makes it even more impossible for us to get support.”

The dragonman youngster frowned even more deeply: "If I were Hua Tang and had a secret connection with Teng Dong Lang, I would of course make some changes when taking action against my group. In that case, even if discovered, it wouldn't expose the secret connection between Teng Dong Lang and Hua Tang."

“In my opinion, these differences are actually further proof of his identity!”

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord spoke sincerely: “Commander Long Fu, to be honest, from my personal perspective, I also find it difficult to immediately request assistance.”

“Before the suspect Hua Tang causes more harm, if I rashly ask for help, it will be a stain on my political career.”

“My governance ability will also be questioned by the upper echelons.”

The dragonman youngster fell silent again.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord spoke quite frankly.

The dragonman youngster also heard the subtext of his words.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was just a group of outsiders. Even though they had lost two magicians, it was not a big deal for the Ice Sculpture Kingdom.

It was just a trivial matter!

Until their vital interests were affected, they had no sufficient motivation to help the outsiders and deal with “Hua Tang”.

Especially since the identity of this “Hua Tang” was still in doubt.

The dragonman youngster gritted his teeth, subconsciously clenched his fists, then helplessly relaxed them.

He looked at the mage tower master, his eyes revealing a look of expectation: “Since we have discovered this mysterious person, can we track his current location?”

The magician tower master shook his head and spread his hands: "We failed. We have tried many times, but the other party was obviously prepared and there is no way to track him! "

The magician tower master pointed at the magic image before them: “This is the limit of what we can currently achieve.”

The city Lord added: "In fact, if the mysterious assassin left enough clues for us to track him down, we should be even more vigilant. This is likely a trap set by the other party."

“If it's Hua Tang, he's not alone in Snow Bird Harbor. Behind him is the entire Bayonet Gang!”

Upon hearing this, the dragonman youngster finally looked up and sighed deeply, saying with confusion and bewilderment, "What should we do? Our defenses are completely ineffective against a powerful man like Hua Tang. He can freely sneak onto our ship anytime, anywhere."

“All of our lives are in danger.”

“Against a powerful assassin, even if he doesn't act, he exerts immense pressure on us!”

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord mused, “Commander Long Fu, your group has already performed exceptionally well.”

“If the opponent is Hua Tang, this is already an extraordinary achievement.”

“You must know that the entire Ice Sculpture Kingdom has no effective countermeasure against Hua Tang.”

“However,” the city Lord shifted his tone, “under the current circumstances, we do have countermeasures.”

“Do you remember the invitation I once extended to you?”

“Hua Tang targeted you to protect the Bayonet Gang led by Teng Dong Lang.”

“There must be deep ties of interest between them. I suspect the Bayonet Gang has provided Hua Tang with significant intelligence.”

"We can't catch Hua Tang, nor can we defend against him. But his weakness is right under our noses, isn't it?"

The Lord looked deeply at the dragonman youngster: “Blindly defending has no future. Sometimes, offense is the best defense!”

The dragonman youngster looked hesitant.

The magician tower master beside him spoke with a serious expression: “Commander Long Fu, please trust our sincerity!”

“All along, the City Lord has been biding his time, secretly gathering evidence of the Bayonet Gang’s crimes.”

“Earlier, they dared to interfere in the heroic duel—this is the biggest flaw they’ve exposed, and it’s the best opportunity we’ve been waiting for all these years!”

"The Bayonet Gang is definitely a cancer tumor in Snow Bird Harbor. Eliminating them will be good for everyone! It is an act of absolute justice!"

“The mysterious assassin suspected to be Hua Tang attacked your corps and killed two of your magicians. This was revenge by the Bayonet Gang and a stern warning to the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps!”

“But such retaliation also proves their weakness.”

“We must join forces and fight terror with courage. Only by taking up arms against such vicious enemies can we make them understand that evil cannot prevail in this world!”

The dragonman youngster looked even more hesitant.

After a long pause, he said, “This matter is too important. I need to consider it carefully!”

"It's about time, and I need to return to my ship. It's best for me to personally command the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps right now."

The city Lord patted the dragonman youngster on the shoulder and said, "Think it over carefully. I'll accompany you to the pier, and my presence will also serve as a deterrent to the assassins."

“But I can't guarantee how effective it will ultimately be.”

The dragonman youngster hurriedly thanked him.

In the carriage rushing back to the pier, the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord once again recounted the various evils and crimes of the Bayonet Gang to the dragonman youngster, persuading him to cooperate with him.

The dragonman youngster only made excuses, saying he needed to think about it.

By the time they returned to the pier, the senior members of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps were ready to welcome the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord.

The dragonman youngster led the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord onto the Love Triangle and into the captain's cabin.

While serving the Lord tea, the dragonman youngster suddenly said, “I've made up my mind, City Lord.”

“I'm sorry, but I may have to disappoint your expectations.”

Upon hearing this, the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's expression suddenly darkened.

The dragonman youngster said sincerely, "The Bayonet Gang is the local powerhouse in Snow Bird Harbor, while we are just a small mercenary group. We cannot possibly be a match for Teng Dong Lang."

"Whether or not the person who came this time was Hua Tang, this assassination attempt alone shows how weak we are. Facing such a powerful assassin, we had almost no chance to fight back."

“The two magicians in our group both lost their lives because of this.”

"Such a loss is too devastating!"

“This has also made me deeply realize the gap in strength between our sides.”

“Continuing to confront the Bayonet Gang head-on would be extremely unwise.”

“Since the city Lord is here, I'll be straightforward: our group wishes to apply to leave the port immediately.”

“Of course, our transactions will continue and will not end because of this.”

“However, the locations and times for the next two transactions will need to be arranged by us.”

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord had a stern expression.

He had a deep background and a strong personality.

This could be seen from the way he invited the dragonman youngster.

First, he sent an invitation letter, and then when he heard that the dragonman youngster was pretending to be sick, he sent a set of gold level medicine directly. After that, he even threatened to visit the dragonman youngster himself.

The dragonman youngster knew the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's determination and had no choice but to accept the invitation and go to the Lord's mansion ahead of time.

Although the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord was enthusiastic at the dinner party and reached an agreement with the dragonman youngster, his main purpose was to obtain the most legitimate reason to deal with the Bayonet Gang and make the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps the sharpest weapon against them.

He never gave up on this goal from beginning to end.

When he heard that the mysterious assassin had attacked the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, he immediately took the dragonman youngster to the magician tower to investigate the magic image after receiving the request for help.

This action was still self-serving, as he had been trying to persuade the dragonman youngster to cooperate closely with him in dealing with the Bayonet Gang.

Chapter 527: List Your Requests, We Can Discuss

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord had put in so much effort, but in the end, in the captain's cabin of the Love Triangle, he heard the dragonman youngster refuse his offer.

As a domineering leader, his mood was understandable. His face immediately turned grim, expressing his displeasure.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord stared at the dragonman youngster, slowly shook his head, and said slowly, “This is not a good decision, Commander Long Fu.”

“You took the initiative to propose a heroic duel to Teng Dong Lang. You are the initiator!”

“If you leave Snow Bird Harbor now, what difference is there between you and a deserter?”

"You have gained a high reputation in Snow Bird Harbor, which is rare. However, once you flee, this reputation will turn into a massive force that will backfire on you. The higher you climb now, the harder you will fall."

“Anyone who dares to back down in a heroic duel will face the deepest contempt and widespread scorn in the Ice Sculpture Kingdom.”

“Essentially, you will be treated as dead in this society.”

“Everyone will despise you. You will live under the gaze of contempt and disdain until the end of your life. This is a very serious consequence!"

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's eyes flashed with a fierce look.

The dragonman youngster stared back at him without flinching, revealing his determination.

"Death in society is only a symbolic death, which is better than losing my life."

"I am only a silver level, so I basically have no hope of defeating a gold level like Teng Dong Lang."

“But what I'm more worried about is that before I fight Teng Dong Lang, I'll be killed by that mysterious assassin.”

"Only when people are alive can they have more possibilities. Leaving the port and avoiding the enemy's threat is a wiser approach and choice."

At this point, the dragonman youngster paused, then continued.

“The city Lord must be aware of our mercenary group's background. We came to the Ice Sculpture Kingdom this time solely to expand our arms business.”

“From the very beginning, we never intended to provoke the Bayonet Gang, let alone engage in a life-or-death confrontation.”

“We merely sought to demonstrate our strength and secure the right to conduct business here.”

“The current situation has exceeded our original plans and is not what we desired.”

"The Ice Sculpture Kingdom is so big. If Snow Bird Harbor doesn't work, there are other ports. If the other ports don't work, there are inland cities. We have many options."

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord spoke in a low voice, shook his index finger at the dragonman youngster, and said, “No, in fact, you have no choice.”

“Information is shared, and it spreads quickly.”

“Believe me, before you even reach the next port, the news of your cowardly escape will have spread throughout the entire Ice Sculpture Kingdom.”

“You will be boycotted everywhere, and no one will buy from you anymore.”

“Everyone will think you are weak, and it will be much more difficult for you to regain the right to do business.”

But the dragonman youngster had long anticipated that the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord would say this.

“We already anticipated this.” The dragonman youngster smiled slightly and said, “Lord, you seem to have misunderstood our plan of action.”

“The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps is just one of our identities.”

“If its reputation is ruined, we can just discard it.”

"If my reputation is ruined, I can just stop operating in the Ice Sculpture Kingdom."

"Besides me, there are other people in my corps. My deputy commander, Shi Qi, is no weaker than me in combat."

"Even if it is inconvenient for all of them to continue operating in the Ice Sculpture Kingdom. Believe me, there will be people even better than us to replace us. By then, even you won't know their true identities."

The dragonman youngster's words were full of meaning and hinted at a sense of strength.

What he meant was to tell the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord in front of him that they were not just a mercenary group!

They had powerful backing.

Those who could engage in arms smuggling were naturally very powerful.

Sure enough, after hearing the dragonman youngster's words, the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord fell silent for the first time.

The dragonman youngster's bluffing tactic was successful.

Although there was a gap in strength between the two sides in the negotiations, and the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps could not compare to the Snow Bird Harbor, the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord had been fooled by the dragonman youngster's bluff.

Information about the mysterious background of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had been circulating for a long time.

The more detailed Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's investigation was, the more he believed in this identity!

This time, Snow Bird Harbor City Lord carefully considered for a moment before speaking solemnly.

“Commander Long Fu, I believe you can see that I really need your help!”

Unlike before, this time his tone and attitude were much more conciliatory, and he appeared more sincere.

The city Lord looked at the dragonman youngster and said, "I'll be honest with you! In order to deal with the Bayonet Gang, I have prepared for a very long time and expended a great deal of energy. Now, I am very confident."

“I can say that we will defeat the Bayonet Gang without fail!”

“I just need an opportunity—a formal opportunity to declare war!”

“Now, that opportunity is right before us.”

“This time, the Bayonet Gang made a mistake, exposing a massive weakness. This is truly rare.”

“Missing such an opportunity would leave me with lifelong regret.”

"As you know, my term is nearly over. What else can I do for Snow Bird Harbor?”

“Am I just going to let it slide? Are we going to let the Bayonet Gang, this cancer, continue to harm this beautiful port? Am I going to leave this mess for the next city Lord to deal with?”

“That would be betraying the royal family’s trust in me and an irresponsible act toward the people here.”

"I hope to have such a bright spot in my political career. I hope that when I return to this port in my old age, I will be highly praised by the citizens here. They will say that I was an excellent city Lord who brought tremendous improvements to Snow Bird Harbor during my tenure!"

When the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord said this, he stroked his chest and revealed a lot of emotion.

This was the first time the dragonman youngster had seen him being so emotional.

As a believer in justice, the youngster's impression of the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord changed greatly at this moment!

If he were alone, he would probably have agreed.

However, the youngster always kept in mind his responsibility as a leader. Therefore, he sat steadily in his seat, his expression calm.

Such a calm gaze put even more pressure on the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord.

The city Lord slowly composed his expression, slowly stood up, and paced back and forth in front of the dragonman youngster.

His feet stepped on the floor, occasionally making a creaking sound, while he himself bowed his head and fell into deep thought.

After pacing back and forth a few times, the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord stopped, turned around to face the dragonman youngster again, and offered new conditions.

In view of the dragonman youngster's stated purpose for coming to the port, the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord proposed that he could use his connections to take the initiative to open up upper level channels for the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps in the Ice Sculpture Kingdom. This would allow the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps to sell their weapons on a large scale throughout the country!

This level of support was completely different from before.

Previously, he had only been willing to promise to sell the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps a prosperous shop. He would allow them to sell their weapons in Snow Bird Harbor to a limited extent.

“If your weapons are of high quality, like the alchemy arrows that you, Commander Long Fu, used in the Snow Bird Dueling Arena...”

“I can even help you establish connections with important members of the royal family in the Ice Sculpture Kingdom!”

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord offered very generous terms.

If the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps really had a background as arms dealers, this condition would undoubtedly be very tempting.

But the dragonman youngster did not.

The secrets he and the survivors carried were far beyond the imagination of the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord.

Therefore, the dragonman youngster remained calm, and the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord secretly admired his composure.

The dragonman youngster said, "City Lord, in fact... Well... we're not just interested in selling some alchemy products. We also hope to purchase some unique resources within the Ice Sculpture Kingdom."

“Such as the Ice Heart.”

The Ice Heart was a specialty of Ice Sculpture Island.

It could be consumed directly by people.

After consumption, one would become extremely rational, with all emotions frozen, and mental strength and willpower greatly enhanced.

Of course, if the dragonman youngster got his hands on the Ice Heart, he wouldn't use it that way.

Extreme rationality with all emotions frozen—at first glance, it sounded very powerful, but in fact, it had a big flaw. The dragonman youngster didn't want to be heartless towards Zi Di.

The Ice Heart was a very precious and important alchemy material.

It could be used to make alchemy tools.

The most successful product was the Ice Heart Headband.

Wearing it allowed magicians to remain calm at all times, enhancing their mental power, and accelerating their spellcasting speed. Of course, it also served to protect their willpower.

So far, the entire survivor team urgently needed to strengthen their mental and willpower defenses.

They had already suffered losses in this regard on multiple occasions.

However, conventional solutions are not suitable for the survivors.

Before arriving at the Ice Sculpture Kingdom, the Ice Heart was one of their primary objectives.

Obtaining a sufficient quantity of Ice Hearts and crafting enough Ice Heart Headbands would resolve their severe issues with weak mental defenses.

When the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord heard the request made by the dragonman youngster, he frowned deeply.

“Ice Heart...” He shook his head and smiled bitterly, “Commander Long Fu, you are an outsider and don't understand the situation.”

“Let's put it this way, obtaining an Ice Heart is much more difficult than obtaining a gold level armor!”

The dragonman youngster already knew that the Ice Sculpture Kingdom had a ban on armor.

Basically, it was very difficult to buy gold level armor in the market here.

Even though there were many alchemists here, and it had the largest alchemy market in the nearby sea area and the most advantageous alchemy production environment, alchemists were strictly restricted by law when it came to forging gold level armor.

The number of gold level armor produced by the entire Ice Sculpture Kingdom each year was fixed.

Even if it was made, it was given priority to the upper echelons and powerful figures of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom.

The dragonman youngster heard the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's answer and felt puzzled: “The Ice Heart is just an alchemy material, it can't be compared to gold level armor, right?”

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord sighed and shook his head slightly: “In fact, the production of Ice Hearts is actually very high.”

He revealed a secret.

Rumors in the market said that the production of Ice Hearts was very low.

This was actually false information deliberately released by the Ice Sculpture Kingdom.

"Ice Hearts are secretly controlled by the royal family. The control is much stricter than that of gold level armor."

“Ice Hearts are a very important alchemy material and are the main ingredient in many alchemy items.”

“They are rare all over the world.”

“Back then, one of the main reasons our snow elf ancestors chose to migrate to Ice Sculpture Island was the Ice Hearts.”

“In fact, the quality of the Ice Hearts currently used for alchemy potions and items is quite low.”

“The truly high-quality Ice Hearts are all in the royal family's possession and have never been released.”

"Even the inferior-quality Ice Hearts are strictly regulated every time they circulate. Even for me, it is very troublesome to obtain them for my own use, as I have to go through layers of reporting procedures. Under these circumstances, it is even more impossible for me to sell them to you."

“It's not that no one has ever done this, but in the end, they were all secretly dealt with for treason.”

"Treason?!" The dragonman youngster was secretly surprised.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord was silent for a moment, staring at the dragonman youngster, his tone and expression very serious: “Commander Long Fu, if you have any other conditions, feel free to bring them up, and we can discuss them.”

Of course, the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord was not easy to fool.

When the dragonman youngster brought up the Ice Heart, he immediately saw through the ploy.

The dragonman youngster's previous threat to leave the port was just a tactic to gain the upper hand in negotiations.

The dragonman youngster was silent for a moment. Since the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord had made such a statement, he didn't hide anything anymore.

He directly took out a list that he had prepared in advance.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord immediately took it and examined it carefully.

There were many items on the list.

After reading through it, the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord found that there were only three important items.

The first item was the purchase of a silver level magic ship.

The Ice Sculpture Kingdom had advanced alchemy technology and was skilled at manufacturing a type of magic ship.

Although the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had obtained the Love Triangle, Iron Lump, and White Bandage, it still had a huge demand for magic ships.

This was because even the flagship, Love Triangle, was only iron level.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps needed at least one silver level magic ship to barely protect their gold level cannon.

The second item was the white dragon and all alchemy materials related to them.

This was mainly for the dragonman youngster's Bloodcore.

Absorbing the bloodline of the white dragon could quickly enhance the dragonman youngster's resistance to frost.

This would also be of great help to him in dealing with Teng Dong Lang.

Chapter 528: Solving the Most Difficult Problem

The third item was some alchemy blueprints and alchemy techniques unique to the Ice Sculpture Kingdom.

Although the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had the War Merchant's database, it was incomplete.

Secondly, the Ice Sculpture Kingdom did have its own unique features and possessed alchemy techniques that the War Merchant did not have.

For example, some potion recipes related to the Ice Heart. Among them, the ice head flower potion was not found in the War Merchant's data.

The survivors collected these, on the one hand to supplement the database, and on the other hand to deepen their understanding of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom, so that when they sold potions here in the future, they could greatly enhance their market competitiveness.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord took the list and looked at it a second time.

After that, he began to walk alone, thinking rapidly.

He didn't think for long. Soon, he stopped and stared at the dragonman youngster.

He put the list on the table and pointed to the contents on it.

“Regarding the magic ship, Commander Long Fu, you must understand that there is no way you can buy a brand new magic ship.”

"The situation now is completely different from a few years ago. Pirates are rampant, and the emergence of the Pirate Throne has plunged the world’s oceans into chaos.”

“You should know that even on Gear Island, orders for magic ships are already booked years in advance.”

“Although our Ice Sculpture Kingdom has the capability to produce magic ships independently, our production capacity still has its limits.”

“Several years ago, our country began to devote all its efforts to the production of magic ships, and every year, new magic ships are launched.”

"But these ships, just like on Gear Island, have long been reserved. Even I can't get one."

Seeing the dragonman youngster frown, the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord changed the subject.

“However, although we can't buy new magic ships, we can buy old ones!”

“Every warship has a limited service life.”

"Especially in recent years, with the abundance of newly produced magic-powered ships, the navy takes priority. As a result, the kingdom's navy retires a significant number of warships each year."

“Though the alchemy technology used on these warships dates back several or even over a decade, they remain highly sought after in the lower level market.”

“A large number of mercenary corps, hunting groups, and merchant alliances are all eager to get their hands on these decommissioned warships!”

“If you are willing to accept old warships, I can still deliver them.”

After hearing this, the dragonman youngster stated, “My group needs silver level magic ships.”

“Right.” The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord nodded affirmatively, “Gold level is difficult, but silver level old warships are still within my capabilities.”

Hearing this reply, the dragonman youngster did not hesitate much and immediately nodded, “Of course, that's fine.”

Having solved a difficult problem, the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord perked up and continued the discussion.

“Regarding the white dragon and related alchemy materials, it is also very difficult for us to sell them.”

“Although there are a large number of white dragons living in the interior of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom, the royal family and the white dragon clan have long had a treaty of non-aggression and mutual exchange.”

“The highest snow peak in the Ice Sculpture Kingdom is called White Dragon Peak, which is the gathering place of the largest white dragon clan.”

“The current White Dragon King is a saint level existence, and there are also a large number of gold level white dragons in the white dragon clan, who are very powerful.”

“Even the royal family finds it difficult to obtain the bodies of pure-blooded white dragons, let alone us.”

"The bodies of white dragons that circulate in the market are extremely rare and are mostly obtained by tomb raiders. They can only be traded on the black market. Alternatively, they are processed through alchemy to disguise them as other materials, then traded in a private transaction where both parties are in on the secret."

"One of the prohibitions in our country's laws is the ban on hunting white dragons. If I were to trade with you, it would first violate the kingdom's laws, and secondly, I simply have no goods to sell."

“However, the sale of wyverns and dragon beasts are permissible.”

Wyverns were no longer pure dragons, but their dragon bloodline still dominated.

Dragon beasts had even weaker dragon bloodlines, which were no longer the primary bloodline, with other racial bloodlines being more concentrated. Dragon beasts only possess individual dragon characteristics. Sometimes, these characteristics were not very obvious.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord continued, “The sale of wyverns and dragon beasts is not prohibited by the royal family.”

“I wonder if these can be used as substitutes?”

Of course they could!

The dragonman youngster mainly wanted to use the Bloodcore to absorb the white dragon's bloodline and enhance his resistance to frost.

By absorbing the bloodlines of related wyverns and dragon beasts, the dragonman youngster could also achieve this goal after accumulating a small amount.

However, the dragonman youngster did not immediately agree.

Instead, after thinking about it for a while, he looked hesitant.

Finally, he said, “If there really are no white dragons, then we can barely accept the related wyverns and dragon beasts.”

“But in that case, the number needs to be greatly increased.”

A flash of joy appeared on the face of the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord, and without any hesitation, he nodded and agreed, “That's no problem at all!”

White dragons were relatively rare, and each one was cherished by the White Dragon King.

However, the number of related wyverns and dragon beasts is indeed quite large.

Every year, the Ice Sculpture Kingdom organizes hunting parties and mercenary corps to conduct large-scale sweeps around White Dragon Mountain.

Sometimes, royal family members even participate personally, organizing royal hunting activities.

These actions were strictly enforced to prevent the numbers of these wyverns and dragon beasts from becoming too numerous and posing a threat to the kingdom's inland security.

Two of the three difficult problems had been solved, and the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord pointed to the last one.

“I cannot provide you with anything related to ice sculpture technology either.”

"This also violates the kingdom's prohibition. Once discovered, it will be treated as treason."

The dragonman youngster's expression remained unchanged. He was already somewhat familiar with the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's conversational habits, so he said, "So, there is room for compromise on this issue, right?"

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord smiled slightly and nodded, “That's right.”

“These alchemy techniques are taken extremely seriously by the royal family. Even alchemy from more than ten years ago has been under strict control.”

“This is because these are one of the foundations of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom.”

"Although alchemy techniques can be leaked, they are strictly regulated. But even the royal family cannot prohibit the movement of alchemy talent."

"My opinion on this matter is: your group might as well spend a lot of money to hire some alchemy talent from outside. These talents happen to have mastered some alchemy techniques related to ice sculpture."

“Spend a lot of money to hire from outside?” The dragonman youngster looked hesitant.

"We are only a silver level mercenary corps, and although we are willing to pay the price to hire them, if they are only alchemy apprentices or alchemists, it is not necessary."

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord shook his head slightly and continued to explain, "Your group can definitely hire extremely talented silver level Alchemy Masters. Although the cost is relatively high, believe me, it is definitely worth it. You can sign strict and even harsh contracts with them, which will basically bind these Alchemy Masters for life."

After hearing this, the dragonman youngster couldn't help but appear even more hesitant.

This was because the suggestion put forward by the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord was in stark contrast to what he had said before.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord clearly understood the dragonman youngster's doubts, so he explained at the right moment, "I told you before: The Ice Sculpture Kingdom has an anti-slavery law, prohibiting the buying and selling of slaves in the market."

"However, the reality is: pirates are rampant, and naval battles are constant. Wherever there is war, there are winners and losers; where there are winners and losers, there are prisoners of war, and these prisoners of war become slaves. As long as wars continue, slaves will keep coming."

“These externally hired alchemists are essentially slaves.”

"However, in our country, only the royal family can own slaves. Therefore, we use strict contracts to control these slaves in the form of external hiring."

The dragonman youngster suddenly understood.

In short, there were policies from the top and countermeasures at the bottom.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord continued, “In order to comply with the national conditions of our country, the cost of hiring these alchemists is not cheap.”

"The remuneration paid must at least meet the average salary in our country. The details must be recorded in the external contract and notarized. Every once in a while, it even needs to be inspected."

"Our Ice Sculpture Kingdom is famous for alchemy. It can be said to be the kingdom of alchemists. In our country, alchemists usually have a high status and freedom."

“I can use my connections to squeeze out a few silver level alchemists for you.”

“But Commander Long Fu, you have to be prepared for a large expenditure.”

The dragonman youngster nodded and said, "No problem! If they are excellent high level slaves, their masters will improve their treatment to ensure their motivation to work, right?"

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord clapped his hands and smiled, “That's right.”

The dragonman youngster's eyes lit up slightly as he suppressed the excitement in his heart.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had always been looking for alchemists.

The demand for alchemists was extremely high!

Because they needed to build a Bloodline Purification formation.

This formation was of utmost importance to all survivors, as it concerned their present and future.

The sooner it was built, the better!

However, they were currently stuck on the alchemist issue, as they lack the necessary talent.

Zi Di and Cang Xu did have some expertise in alchemy. However, specialization was key, and their abilities were insufficient for constructing such an alchemy formation.

The dragonman youngster had thought about using alchemist slaves.

But when he communicated with the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord before, he learned about the Ice Sculpture Kingdom's ban on slavery and had to abandon the idea.

After communicating with them now, the dragonman youngster realized that he was still naive.

Although the Ice Sculpture Kingdom had a ban on slavery, the demand for slaves could not be prohibited. In fact, the more slavery was banned, the more vibrant and massive the demand became.

Hiring external magicians was a strategy to address this demand. It significantly addressed the demand for high-quality slaves among the upper echelons of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom.

Chapter 529: Having a Whole Level of New Respect

The dragonman youngster looked at the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord and thought to himself that this round of negotiations had indeed been very fruitful!

The last time the dragonman youngster and the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord had a pleasant conversation, they had reached a big deal.

But after all, it was their first meeting, and the degree of cooperation between the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord and the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was limited.

Now that the dragonman youngster was threatening to leave the port, the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord had no choice but to show greater sincerity.

From this perspective, the mysterious assassin's attack on the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was actually a good thing for the dragonman youngster and the survivors, as the benefits outweighed the disadvantages.

Without this unexpected event as a catalyst, the level of cooperation between them and the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord would never have progressed to this depth.

The dragonman youngster said, "Our corps needs many alchemists. We need at least three silver level Alchemy Masters and one gold level Alchemy Great Master."

Upon hearing this, the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord hurriedly raised his hand and said, "Wait a minute! A gold level Alchemy Great Master is completely impossible."

“Gold level slaves are very rare, and even a single battle qi cultivator is strictly controlled by the kingdom.”

"An Alchemy Great Master is even more impossible. He represents not only a spellcaster, but also the productivity to continuously produce various alchemy items in the future, which means long-term and huge profits."

“A gold level Alchemy Great Master slave will basically stay in the hands of his first owner and will rarely be traded, unless it is a special situation where there is no other choice.”

“In fact, even if they become slaves, people of this caliber will quickly receive better treatment due to their contributions and abilities and will eventually be freed from slavery.”

The dragonman youngster fell silent.

He had known about this situation for a long time and had only mentioned it as a test balloon.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's decisive refusal was also within the dragonman youngster's expectations.

He continued, "I can provide a few silver level Alchemy Masters at most."

“The specific number will depend on the situation after I make contact.”

“At least, I don't have such resources at my disposal at the moment.”

“However, I can provide your corps with a large number of alchemy apprentices and some alchemists.”

“Your group wants to sell arms on a large scale in Snow Bird Harbor and even the entire Ice Sculpture Kingdom, and building a corresponding alchemy factory is also part of your plan, right?”

“At that time, these alchemy apprentices and alchemists will have a place to use their skills.”

The dragonman youngster fell silent again.

Such a large-scale investment was not yet part of the survivors' plans.

However, if things continued to develop in this manner, constructing alchemy factories in the Ice Sculpture Kingdom would be a natural progression.

This was because the cost of transporting military supplies across the vast ocean was extremely high.

Especially with pirates rampant at present, the transportation risks were many times higher than before.

Building an arms factory in the Ice Sculpture Kingdom offered numerous advantages.

On one hand, it reduced transportation costs and significantly minimized transportation risks.

On the other hand, the Ice Sculpture Kingdom possessed abundant alchemy resources. It was very convenient to build an alchemy factory. In terms of the entire sea area, the Ice Sculpture Kingdom had the best environment for building factories.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's proposal made the dragonman youngsters think about the distant future.

It was not that they hadn't thought about this before.

But the fact was that the current situation of the survivors was quite complicated.

The specific progress of atoning to the Sheng Ming Empire was related to their current survival situation.

In many cases, they had to take things one step at a time and assess the situation before acting.

If they rashly made large-scale investments and the secret of Mystifying Monster Island was discovered, they would have to flee into exile if they made no progress in atoning for their sins. Then, all their previous investments would be wasted.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord was, of course, unaware of the complex situation the dragonman youngsters were currently in.

Seeing the dragonman youngster remain silent, he thought he had guessed his thoughts and plans correctly.

The city Lord became even more confident.

Of the three items on the list given by the dragonman youngster that he considered most difficult, he had revised them during the negotiations, and both sides had successfully reached an agreement.

Finally, he brought up the topic again and took the initiative to add the trading options of alchemy apprentices and alchemists.

Looking at the dragonman youngster's expression, the city Lord judged that the chances of success in this negotiation were very high!

But just then, the dragonman youngster changed the subject.

He first thanked the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord.

Then he said bluntly that the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord was still not sincere enough in terms of cooperation!

These words surprised the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord and made him feel angry.

He believed that his side had already expressed sufficient sincerity and made significant concessions. Commander Long Fu still wanted to ask for more, which was too greedy.

For a moment, the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord did not question, but stared at the dragonman youngster, his eyes revealing a silent inquiry.

The dragonman youngster was fearless, looking back at him with an open and sincere gaze.

He said slowly, "City Lord, the deal needs time."

“Don't forget, we still have two more exchanges to complete from the deal we made before.”

“Our new negotiation is meaningless until that is completed.”

“Time is precisely the key.”

“Now, our group has been attacked by a mysterious assassin, and two important members have already been killed!”

"It can be said that every moment from now on is dangerous for our entire group. After all, we cannot prevent the mysterious assassin from attacking again."

“Especially since there is evidence suggesting that this mysterious assassin is likely Hua Tang!”

"Therefore, before we proceed with the transaction, you must first demonstrate sufficient sincerity. Let us believe that you are truly willing to cooperate with us."

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord couldn't help but laugh in anger.

The deal was a condition of cooperation, but the city Lord never expected that the dragonman youngster would have conditions on top of conditions.

This was a bit too much!

The city Lord took a deep breath, slowly turned around, walked a few steps, and sat back down in his original position.

He tilted his head slightly, looked down at the dragonman youngster, and added a hint of coldness to his tone: "Go ahead and say it. I may not necessarily agree."

The dragonman youngster could sense the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's anger, but in this situation, he became even more assertive.

Leaning back in his chair, he shook his head slowly, his gaze unwavering, his tone absolute: “This is the most fundamental requirement. If the city Lord cannot meet this condition, there can be no cooperation between us.”

“Everything discussed earlier is null and void!”

“We will depart the port tonight, even if we have to force our way through the gates.”

Seeing the dragonman youngster so assertive, the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's heart stirred slightly, and the coldness on his face softened a little.

He narrowed his eyes slightly and carefully examined Commander Long Fu in front of him.

At this moment, the city Lord suddenly realized that Long Fu's previous threat to leave the port was not just a negotiating tactic. He really seemed to be determined to do so!

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord considered his tone carefully: “Commander Long Fu, relax, I am not your enemy.”

“We can cooperate, and we have already begun to cooperate.”

"We have already completed the first handover of our transaction. Regardless of whether this negotiation is successful or not, we will complete the next two handovers. Because I keep my promises!"

“Please tell me the specific conditions.”

The dragonman youngster said, "Our group is currently facing a severe survival crisis. Therefore, we must eliminate that mysterious assassin! Only then can we have the confidence to cooperate with you, the city Lord, to deal with the Bayonet Gang."

"Only when we no longer have such worries can we devote our energy to dealing with external enemies. Otherwise, we will be on edge every day and every night during the upcoming operation."

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord spread his hands helplessly and said, “Commander Long Fu, you must understand that you are asking too much!”

“Just a short while ago, you came to me for help, and I led you into the magician tower, such a sensitive location. You witnessed the results of our investigation with your own eyes.”

"Don’t even mention eliminating the mysterious assassin—we’ve had great difficulty even locating him. The prospects of eradicating him are extremely slim!"

He took a sip of tea first, then looked at the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord and said calmly, “We already know the killer's exact location.”

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord: “???”

After hearing the dragonman youngster's words, he was stunned for several seconds, his face full of surprise.

After that, his face showed surprise.

This emotion was so strong that he couldn't help but stand up subconsciously, staring intently at the dragonman youngster in front of him.

It was as if they were meeting for the first time!

It was also as if he was shouting: I didn't expect you, Commander Long Fu, with your thick eyebrows and big eyes, to be so calculating! You actually have such deep thoughts!

In those few seconds, the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord suddenly realized that all of this was Commander Long Fu's plan and arrangement!

First, he pretended to ask for help.

Then, in the magician tower, he learned that the city Lord was unable to track down the mysterious murderer.

If the city Lord hadn't taken the initiative to follow him to the pier this time, Commander Long Fu would have invited him to this negotiation later.

“The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had long known the killer's location.”

“But they don't want to do it themselves!”

“Because the assassin is at the gold level, acted mysteriously, and is ruthless, dealing with him is extremely risky.”

“With the help of my power to capture and kill the assassin, the risk is minimal, and the reward is the highest for the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.”

"After all, I am the city Lord and this is my duty." It's also my fault for repeatedly expressing my willingness to cooperate with the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps to deal with the Bayonet Gang."

After figuring this out, the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's attitude towards the dragonman youngster changed subtly once again.

He deeply sighed, “Commander Long Fu, I have a whole new level of respect for you!”

“It’s no wonder you were made commander of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps and was entrusted with the duty of breaking into Ice Sculpture Kingdom’s market.”

“I underestimated you. In fact, the Bayonet Gang underestimated you, and almost everyone in Snow Bird Harbor underestimated you!”

“But this is a matter of great importance.” The city Lord asked the dragonman youngster again, “Can your group really determine the location of the murderer?”

“Of course!” The dragonman youngster replied very decisively and confidently.

Chapter 530: The Assassin Flees

Snow Bird Harbor, slums.

The mysterious assassin is having a meal.

He was currently in a private house.

On the surface, this humble dwelling appeared no different from the surrounding houses, utterly unremarkable. However, in reality, it was heavily fortified.

After all, this was one of the hideouts of the mysterious assassins.

Setting aside the intricate traps and mechanisms, the defenses here were primarily divided into three layers.

The first layer of defense was the magic potion-created illusory maze.

The potion coated the interior walls of the house.

Most people who entered this shantytown, even if they wandered around searching, would find it to be an ordinary house with no valuables at all, literally bare walls, plain as day.

Even if the mysterious assassin was right nearby, they would not notice.

The second layer was a candle lit on the dining table.

This candle was pure white in color, as thick as an infant's arm, and stood 10 centimeters tall, burning quietly.

The flame of the candle was pure white.

Its glow illuminated the surroundings, faintly forming patches of mirror-like surfaces.

This was the Mirror Light Candle, a sacred artifact.

It originated from the Mirror God.

The Mirror God was a deity of the dwarf race, and the mysterious assassin was also one of his devotees. Through the church, he obtained this sacred artifact.

When the Mirror Light Candle burns, it emitted reflective light.

Within the range of this reflective light, all external attacks were reflected away.

This power reflected all external attacks, regardless of their nature. It was effective against battle skills, spells, and even divine magic. Of course, the actual effectiveness depended on the specific situation.
