The third layer of defense was a defensive magic formation buried underground by the mysterious assassin.

Although the mysterious assassin was not a magician, he had a certain foundation in alchemy.

Of course, this defensive magic formation was not laid out by him personally but was built on a set of formation diagram panels.

This set of formation diagram panels was only silver level, much smaller and lighter than the one the dragonman youngster had obtained from the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord, and even more portable.

The third layer of defense was mainly to guard against surprise attacks from underground.

Of course, if enemies breach the first two layers of defense, the mysterious assassin can fully activate this defensive formation to provide ground-based defensive support.

Under the candlelight, the mysterious assassin enjoyed a delicious dinner with peace of mind.

The number of dishes was limited, but they were exquisitely prepared.

It was clear that the magic chef who created them possessed extraordinary culinary skills.

The mysterious assassin felt at ease.

The defenses in the room gave him a sense of security.

But the main source of his confidence was his own strength.

It was not that he was confident he could fight a thousand enemies, but if a strong enemy attacked, the mysterious assassin would immediately retreat and abandon this hiding place.

This was just a place for him to stay, so it would not be a pity to abandon it.

According to his gold level strength, there were only four people in the entire Snow Bird Harbor who were his equal.

Moreover, he was not alone in Snow Bird Harbor.

The mysterious assassin took a small sip of wine, then put down his glass.

He leaned back in his chair and took three gold level darts out of his bosom.

This set of darts was what he had stolen from the Love Triangle.

The three darts flipped between his fingers, landing obediently between the assassin's fingers.

The sharp blades of the darts caused him no harm. The mysterious assassin's movements were smooth and fluid, as if the three darts were not menacing weapons but coins or marbles.

The mysterious assassin suddenly flicked his finger.

One dart whizzed out.

After flying half a meter, a centrifugal force caused it to return to its starting point.

The mysterious assassin continued to play with the remaining two darts in his hand, making no extra movements to intercept it.

The returning dart landed precisely between his little finger and ring finger.

In the next instant, it joined the other two darts, spinning continuously between the assassin's fingers.

From this single move alone, it was clear that the mysterious assassin's mastery of throwing weapons was extraordinary.

Because throughout this process, the assassin did not use any battle qi or mental control techniques. It was purely a matter of his throwing technique!

Among the three gold level items stolen by the mysterious assassin, his favorite was this set of darts.

The darts twirled in his hand.

The other two gold level items, the longbow and the harpoon, were placed directly at his feet.

The contrast between the two was very obvious.

The mysterious assassin played with the darts while thinking back on his recent actions.

Regarding this theft and assassination operation at the pier, the mysterious assassin was fairly satisfied.

Killing two magicians was nothing to boast about for him.

As a gold level assassin, killing one or two magicians who weren't even silver level was a foregone conclusion.

The mysterious assassin's best record was far beyond that.

The main reason he was satisfied was the generous loot he had gained this time.

The storage bag he carried with him was already filled with various alchemy materials.

The three gold level equipment items could not fit in the storage bag, so he had to carry them with him. They were the most valuable part of his haul this time.

Even with the mysterious assassin's experience, he could not steal so many things at once during his usual operations.

“Their gold level equipment was not stored in a treasure chest.”

“If all my future opponents were like the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, how wonderful would my life be?!”

“They must be living in constant fear now, haha.”

The mysterious assassin chuckled to himself.

He was about to take another sip of wine when a sudden sense of foreboding gripped him, causing his pupils to contract to the size of needle tips.

A powerful flame spell descended from the sky, striking the civilian house where he was hiding with pinpoint accuracy.

The flames were intense, quickly burning through the walls.

The raging heat spread, filling the mysterious assassin's field of vision.

The magic potion serving as the first line of defense melted and evaporated under the flames, unable to provide any protection.

The flames surrounded the mysterious assassin from all sides and closed in, launching a relentless assault.

This offensive triggered the effect of the Mirror Light Candle.

Reflected light formed mirror-like surfaces within the flames.

Countless mirrors interlocked, creating a vast protective barrier.

However, this barrier held for just over a second before collapsing completely.

The white candle, which was originally as thick as a baby's arm and 10 centimeters high, also burned out in that second.

The mysterious assassin's face was grave: "A gold level fire spell! It exceeds the limits of the Mirror Light Candle."

This sacred object was only silver level.

However, it had already played a very powerful role.

Under the reflected light, part of the gold level fire magic was reflected, causing internal friction. After canceling each other out, its power was greatly reduced, and its momentum plummeted, becoming a spent force.

However, in the next moment, the color of the flames changed from orange red to white.

The temperature of the air dropped suddenly.

In an instant, the mysterious assassin seemed to have been transported from the side of an erupting volcano to a thousand-year-old ice cave.

Crack, crack...

Extremely cold air gushed out, quickly covering the ground with a layer of frost.

The fire spell had actually turned into an ice spell.

The latter was equally powerful, still at the gold level!

The mysterious assassin hesitated not a moment, immediately activating the formation diagram panels buried in the shallow layers of the ground.

The next instant, the defensive formation activated, forming a spherical shield that enveloped the mysterious assassin.

Half of the spherical shield was above ground, half below, providing him with all-around protection.

The freezing air engulfed the spherical shield, raging unchecked.

After a few seconds, the spherical shield began to fade in and out, showing signs of collapsing.

But the mysterious assassin had already gained the most precious time he needed.

He even had time to clean up the scene and remove his traces and clues as much as possible.

He carried the gold level longbow and a harpoon, stirred up his battle qi, and used his battle skill. Like a sharp arrow, he shot out the moment the spherical shield collapsed.

The mysterious assassin successfully escaped from his landing point, stepped on the roofs of the surrounding houses, and quickly retreated.

While leaping and fleeing, he also quickly observed and gathered intelligence.

He soon discovered the three groups of magicians who had cast spells and attacked him.

One group of magicians emitted a strong aura of fire elements, another group was surrounded by icy coldness, and the middle group of elf magicians was the smallest in number and had the deepest aura.

The mysterious assassin immediately recognized them.

These were the three magic teams of the city government palace!

They were the Fire Meteor Team, the Face Frost Team, and the Coordinator Team.

The elves were a race with strong magic talents.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps only had a handful of magicians, but in the Ice Sculpture Kingdom, there were so many magicians that they had to organize teams to accommodate them.

The mysterious assassin was greatly relieved.

He had originally thought that it was a gold level magician casting a spell, but he didn't expect that it was just three teams with nearly ten silver level magicians.

For the mysterious assassin, the former was much more threatening than the latter.

“These people can't stop me!” The mysterious assassin ran all the way, and in a short time, he had already pulled away hundreds of meters from his hiding place.

But just then, a clump of bird droppings fell from the sky, landing on his head.

The mysterious assassin was stunned.

The droppings appeared out of thin air, falling from the sky at such speed that he didn't even have time to react.

He was hit!

Divine spell—Bird Poop!

The mysterious assassin's heart sank, and he cast a sharp glance straight ahead.

The clergyman who had used the divine spell had also revealed his exact location with this move.

He was the bishop of the Sea Bird Church in Snow Bird Harbor.

One of the four gold level high-ranking officials in Snow Bird Harbor!

"You've been hit by my divine spell, you can't escape. Surrender, Hua Tang." The Sea Bird Bishop announced loudly with a solemn expression.

"How is that possible?!" The mysterious assassin sneered and used his battle skill to increase his speed.

He did not sprint for long before suddenly changing direction and pouncing on the nearest magic team like a hawk diving for its prey.

He did not want to fight the bishop standing in front of him.

The other party was also gold level, and once the battle began, it would be difficult to determine the winner in a short period of time.

Moreover, he was currently holding two gold level weapons in his hands.

The mysterious assassin did not want to be entangled, so he made a quick decision and charged towards a magic team.

He wanted to use this as a breakthrough and escape!

Chapter 531: Death

There were three magic teams surrounding the mysterious assassin.

The mysterious assassin chose the team with the most members.

This group of magicians was full of energy and heat, and it was the Fire Meteor Team.

The mysterious assassin, who was well-traveled and experienced, chose the largest team not because he wanted to deal a heavy blow to Snow Bird Harbor, but because this team was the weakest and easiest to break through.

Among the three magic teams, the Coordinator Team was the strongest. Although they had the fewest members, they had the most silver level magicians.

The Coordinator Team only played a supporting role, but their standards for recruiting members were the highest.

Only magicians who had mastered the art of spell fusion could join this team.

It was their role that allowed the previous fire spells and ice spells to be coordinated and fused.

After the fire-based spells' attack power was significantly reduced, they smoothly transitioned into ice-based spells.

The transition between the two was seamless, causing no internal conflicts or energy loss. The remaining energy from the fire-based spells was even passed on to the next ice-based spell, creating nearly perfect coordination.

Spellcasting techniques were similar to battle skill techniques in that they were both icing on the cake.

Only when their own level, battle skills, and spells had reached the limit of what could be achieved would transcendents begin to study these techniques in order to effectively improve their own strength.

Those who mastered spellcasting techniques were rarely young. Though their natural talent might be limited, their extensive spellcasting experience made them formidable opponents.

Upon closer observation, it became evident that the members of the Coordinator Team appear significantly older than those of the other two teams.

At first glance, the mysterious assassin realized that the Coordinator Team was the most formidable of the three.

Of the remaining two teams, one specialized in frost magic, while the other focused on fire magic.

Fire magic was the most powerful, while frost magic excelled at controlling the battlefield.

The members of both magic teams were relatively young, with no obvious differences in appearance.

Which team should be targeted as the breakthrough point?

Of course, the fire-based team!

Because this was the Ice Sculpture Kingdom.

The members of the magic teams were almost all snow elves.

The bloodlines of snow elves clearly leaned toward the ice attribute.

When they cast ice-based spells, their bloodlines provided a boost. The magicians of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom were most skilled at ice magic, and after years of research, they had achieved many unique results.

However, when they cast fire magic, they were negatively affected by their bloodline.

It could be said that among the three magic teams, the Fire Meteor Team was the weakest overall.

The reason why successive city Lords had maintained the Fire Meteor Team was only to ensure the diversity of troop types.

If they went to the battlefield, only the ice-type magicians on their side would be at a disadvantage, easily targeted by the enemy and thus falling into a disadvantageous position.

Seeing the gold level battle qi cultivators rushing towards them, the Fire Meteor Team immediately fell into chaos.

The snow elves who were selected to form the fire-type magic team were basically those with weaker qualifications.

Those who were better than them were selected for the Face Frost Team.

Of course, the most talented individuals were the independent and free magicians.

The Fire Meteor Team's chaotic reaction also proved that the mysterious assassin's choice was correct.

Based on the mysterious assassin's strength, as long as he closed the distance, the Fire Meteor Team would inevitably suffer heavy losses.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord would certainly not allow this to happen right before his eyes.

Spell—Short-distance Teleportation.

The spell's light scattered, and the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord suddenly appeared halfway there.

Seeing an ordinary-looking snow elf suddenly appear right in front of him, the mysterious assassin's expression suddenly became serious.

Obviously, he recognized the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord held a short staff in his hand and successfully blocked the mysterious assassin.

The short staff in his hand was also ordinary in appearance, resembling a rolling pin used by a housewife.

The mysterious assassin's dagger and the short staff collided repeatedly, clashing over fifty times within a dozen seconds.

Both sides fought with lightning speed.

The mysterious assassin moved with incredible agility, swinging his dagger continuously, creating a series of illusory shadows.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord was equally fast, with a wide range of short staff techniques, blocking, parrying, slashing, and stabbing, combining offense and defense with astonishing skills.

The mysterious assassin was exceptionally powerful, but he was entangled by the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord and couldn't escape for a while.

From time to time, lightning or freezing air burst forth from the short stick, causing the mysterious assassin a lot of trouble.

It turned out that the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord practiced both martial arts and magic.

He relied mainly on his own battle skills, but in between battles, he used various small and micro spells to assist himself and create advantages.

Even if the advantages were extremely small each time, after many times, they accumulated to the point of qualitative change, and he was able to turn the advantage into a winning position.

The short staff in his hand was not only his melee weapon, but also his spellcasting tool. It had good conductivity for both battle qi and magic power.

Spell—Clone!

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord suddenly swayed, transforming into three bodies and surrounding the mysterious assassin.

Battle skill—Stab.

Spell—Electric Shock.

Spell—Slow.

Battle skill—Flying Kick.

The three Snow Bird Harbor City Lords were indistinguishable from each other, each simultaneously casting different battle skills and spells.

In an instant, the three city Lords formed a fierce offensive.

The mysterious assassin was stunned for a moment.

Because his opponent's moves were so varied and never repeated, he didn't know how to deal with them at first.

The combination of battle skills and spells created a very complicated situation, and it was difficult to think of a solution in an instant.

The mysterious assassin could only dodge and block, retreating continuously.

He made a series of mistakes, which were seized upon by his opponents.

The three Snow Bird Harbor City Lords scored consecutive hits, adding multiple wounds to the mysterious assassin's body.

The originally evenly matched scene suddenly changed dramatically, with the Snow Bird Harbor City Lords gaining a clear advantage.

Seeing that the situation was not looking good, the mysterious assassin was just about to use a smoke bomb to try to escape.

The two Snow Bird Harbor City Lords suddenly disappeared, leaving only his true form to rush towards the mysterious assassin.

The short staff in the City Lord's hand emitted a metallic glow.

Battle skill—Battle Qi Weapon!

Battle skill—Stab.

Spell—Cat's Agility.

Spell—Acceleration.

The short sword pierced the mysterious assassin's heart with lightning speed.

The mysterious assassin stared at Snow Bird Harbor City Lord with blood flowing from his eyes.

A few seconds later, he fell to the ground, his life force gone.

However, in the next moment, his entire body transformed into an alchemy puppet.

Seeing this, the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's expression changed greatly, and he shouted, “Be careful, this is not his real body.”

The mysterious assassin had a substitute puppet.

He had used one during his infiltration of the Love Triangle.

The one he was using now was clearly far superior in specifications and quality to the previous one.

The Sea Bird Bishop hurriedly cast a spell.

Divine spell—Bird Swarm Tracking!

The sky suddenly flickered with a mix of blue and white light.

Within the light, one by one, birds took shape.

A flock of blue and white birds circled and danced above the battlefield.

Then, with a loud explosion, the birds scattered across the battlefield.

Soon, one of the birds suddenly exploded.

The light that spread out was like crushed diamond powder.

Amidst the dazzling light powder, the mysterious assassin appeared.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord held a short staff and rushed towards the mysterious assassin again.

The mysterious assassin glanced at the enemy but had no intention of fighting the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord. He sprinted with all his might and attacked the Sea Bird Bishop.

Obviously, the Sea Bird Bishop's divine spells were a huge obstacle to his escape.

The Sea Bird Bishop fought while retreating.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord arrived, and soon the three were fighting together.

The mysterious assassin's situation became increasingly difficult.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord engaged the mysterious assassin in close combat. The Sea Bird Bishop continued to cast spells from behind to assist in the attack.

As for the three magic teams, they had retreated a distance and were stationed at the edge of the battlefield.

Once the mysterious assassin found an opportunity to escape, they would seize the opportunity and block him.

Even if they sacrificed many team members at that time, as long as they gained a little time, they could allow the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord or the Sea Bird Bishop to catch up with the mysterious assassin again.

The three magic teams all had silver level magicians who could match gold level opponents in a short period of time.

The spells cast by the teams earlier all had gold level power.

Bang, bang, bang...

The clash between the three gold level magicians ravaged the slums beyond recognition. Countless houses collapsed, and large numbers of civilians screamed and ran around in panic.

The scene became increasingly chaotic.

Amidst the rolling smoke and explosions, the alchemy puppet that had been stabbed in the heart by the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord and had fallen to the ground suddenly got up and shot out like lightning.

This unexpected change stunned everyone.

“Which one is the real assassin?” Everyone wondered.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord was unable to reach the alchemy puppet that had suddenly escaped, so he could only do his best to engage the “mysterious assassin” in front of him.

The Sea Bird Bishop hurriedly cast a spell.

The divine spell fell from the sky, but it missed the escaping alchemy puppet by a hair's breadth.

The alchemy puppet burst into laughter, its face gradually transforming into a silver fox mask.

“The one that escaped is the true form!” Realizing this, the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord turned around immediately, intending to pursue.

The “mysterious assassin” who had been engaged in close combat with him acted swiftly.

It let out a piercing scream, its disguise tearing apart to reveal the alchemy puppet's true form.

Two round holes appeared under its armpits, revealing two brand-new metal arms.

Each of the four hands held a dagger, and they frantically attacked Snow Bird Harbor City Lord.

The attack was like a raging storm, completely chaotic, completely disregarding the intense consumption of the magic crystal inside its body. It had only one goal: to cling to the city Lord.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord could only defend himself and was unable to pursue the mysterious assassin.

The three magic teams had originally focused their attention on the center of the battlefield, never imagining that the fallen alchemy puppet was actually the mysterious assassin's true form.

No one knew how he had managed to deceive the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's senses.

His skill at feigning death was simply unparalleled!

The mysterious assassin, who had been overlooked, had been secretly controlling the real alchemy puppet.

Therefore, the alchemy puppet's battle skill techniques remained above average, and its intelligence was normal, so it did not arouse suspicion.

In this way, the mysterious assassin not only influenced the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord and Sea Bird Bishop, two gold level opponents, but also indirectly controlled the battlefield positions of the three magic teams.

When he felt the time was right, he was already lying on the edge of the battlefield.

The three magic teams were unable to surround the entire battlefield. There was a huge gap between them.

The mysterious assassin had been preparing for a long time and easily penetrated this gap, about to walk away.

Suddenly, a bright green light pierced the sky and shot at him.

It turned out to be an attack from the magician tower in the distance.

The mysterious assassin was already prepared.

Earlier, it was the magician tower's disintegration ray that destroyed his alchemy bomb.

He had already taken this into consideration when he broke out.

The mysterious assassin twisted his waist violently, about to use his battle skill to dodge.

Suddenly, his whole body went limp, and his head buzzed.

His battle skill failed!

His hair stood on end, and he wanted to use his alchemy tool.

But it was too late.

The disintegration ray hit the mysterious assassin directly.

The protective tools on the mysterious assassin's body quickly split and disintegrated in the pale green light.

He was pierced by the ray.

A huge hole appeared in his abdomen.

The edges of the hole were bathed in thick green light, resembling flames burning.

Within the green light, his flesh and blood disintegrated and collapsed, like ashes formed after paper burns, swiftly dissipating in the wind.

Within a mere second, the mysterious assassin was completely disintegrated within the green light.

Even the silver fox mask he wore did not remain.

The upper half of the silver fox mask fell to the ground and completely dissolved in the glowing green light.

The mysterious assassin had died!

Chapter 532: Watching From the Sidelines

As dawn broke, Snow Bird Harbor seemed to be shrouded in a layer of silver-gray gauze.

The surface of the sea was calm.

However, the inside of Snow Bird Harbor was not. Sounds of combat resounded continuously.

When it came to gold level battles, the commotion was never small.

At this time, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' ships had already left Snow Bird Harbor.

They had not left completely yet.

After the dragonman youngster and the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord reached an agreement, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was given permission to leave the port.

At this time, their ships maintained a distance from the Snow Bird Harbor pier.

Near the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' fleet, there was also a Snow Bird Harbor patrol fleet.

When the agreement was reached, the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord specifically proposed to dispatch a fleet to protect the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

Nominally, this was for protection, but in reality, the dragonman youngster and the others knew very well that this was sent by the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord specifically to supervise the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

The dragonman youngster accepted this proposal. This was because both parties in the cooperation had to earn each other's trust.

Trust was the foundation of cooperation.

At this moment, the core members of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps gathered secretly.

Everyone was intently watching the magic images before them.

Numerous scout-type alchemy puppets were scattered across the chaotic battlefield.

The situation on the battlefield was being transmitted in real-time to everyone's eyes.

Moreover, the Deep Sea Monster Fish, stationed near the Snow Bird Harbor pier, was also conducting full-scale reconnaissance, recording everything that occurred on the battlefield.

The first thing the survivors saw was the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord preparing for battle.

Fearing that he would alert the enemy, the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord did not disperse the people in the slums at the beginning.

He dispatched three magic teams.

Before entering the battlefield, these three magic teams had already used magic to conceal themselves.

The dragonman youngsters and the others could only vaguely see some smoke and dust, sensing some small clues.

After the magic teams were in position, the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord secretly dispatched his subordinates to quietly disperse the surrounding crowd.

At the same time, he appeared on top of a house and began to cast a spell.

He did not chant but took out a gold level scroll.

Spell—Silent Accumulation.

It was a fairly common spell.

The spell's effect was to slightly increase the power of the magician's spells. The key point was that when the spell was cast, the caster remained silent throughout the entire process, and the spell's aura was completely concealed. It was only when the spell was released that its aura would completely erupt.

This spell often appeared in the history of world wars and was often used by magicians in surprise attacks and ambushes.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord used three identical Silent Accumulation scrolls in a row.

He invested a large sum of money at the beginning of the battle.

The Silent Accumulation spell was area-of-effect.

If the three magic teams were gathered together, perhaps only one scroll would have been needed.

However, under the command of the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord, the three magic teams were spread out, so three scrolls had to be used to cover everyone.

After the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord successfully cast the spell, he immediately gave the order.

The magic teams began to cast spells one after another.

Accompanied by the silent chanting of the snow elves and the use of spell materials, their figures gradually materialized.

The civilians being dispersed witnessed this scene and felt a sense of dread.

Many were reluctant to leave and showed signs of resistance.

However, upon seeing the magic teams, the majority realized something significant was about to happen and fled the area without further persuasion.

The surrounding area gradually became more and more noisy.

“So many magicians...” It was the first time the dragonman youngster had seen such a scene, and he couldn't help but feel envious.

For the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, magicians had always been a scarce resource.

But here, in the Ice Sculpture Kingdom, the elves' racial talents had brought them a large number of magicians, to the point where they could form teams.

The dragonman youngster glanced around and roughly counted that there were nearly 10 silver level magicians in the three teams!

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps currently did not have a single magician of this level.

Whether it was Cang Xu or Zi Di, their magician levels were below silver.

Qing Xin was a silver level wind magician.

But her loyalty still needed to be assessed.

Moreover, it would be difficult to get her to join the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

In Qing Xin's understanding, Zi Di, Cang Xu, and the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had no relationship. Strictly speaking, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps could even be considered their enemy. They had clashed on the Two Eyes Island and the Weeping Pine Naval Battle.

“The Fire Meteor Team, the Face Frost Team, and the Coordinator Team.” Zong Ge softly said the names of these three teams.

They had obtained this information long ago.

The existence of these three magic teams had never been a secret. The people of Snow Bird Harbor were well aware of this information.

Although the Coordinator Team only played a supporting role, it was clear that they were in a leadership position.

This team was the first to begin casting spells.

No survivor could identify the specific spells they were casting.

Immediately afterward, the members of the Fire Meteor Team began chanting in unison.

Immediately afterward, the members of the Fire Meteor Team began chanting in unison.

While chanting, they all retrieved their spellcasting materials.

Most of the spellcasting materials were consistent, with only a few differences.

The quantity of spellcasting materials was also roughly the same for everyone.

Members with weaker abilities naturally used more spellcasting materials.

These spellcasting materials dissipated in their hands, transforming into a large amount of fire elements.

The fire elements swirled around the team members, their concentration rapidly increasing to form a large number of sparks.

The sparks flashed continuously above the team's heads.

The aura of all the members of the Fire Meteor Team condensed into one, indistinguishable from each other. Their momentum continued to rise, and the magicians' robes moved without wind, creating an imposing presence.

The survivors had mixed feelings.

They were delighted by the teams' extraordinary strength but worried that their overwhelming aura might reveal their identity and alert the mysterious assassin.

The Silent Accumulation spell was doing its job, and the mysterious assassin showed no signs of action.

When the Fire Meteor Team's spellcasting was halfway through, the Face Frost Team also began their spellcasting.

They retrieved a large amount of ice-elemental spellcasting materials and began chanting in unison.

The survivors still couldn't hear anything.

In fact, even if they could hear it, most of them wouldn't understand it.

This was because the snow elves used the elven language.

Among the survivors, only Zi Di and Cang Xu were proficient in the elven language and could use it for daily communication.

Fei She and Zong Ge knew some of it; the former used it to identify elven merchandise, and the latter used it to read elven war history.

The dragonman youngster did not know the elf language at all.

When he communicated with the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord, it was the latter who took the initiative to use the human language.

The quality of the members of the Face Frost Team was obviously a cut above that of the Fire Meteor Team.

They cast spells more quickly, and the ice elements accumulated very rapidly, soon filling the surroundings with cold air.

The snow elves were slender in build, but at this moment, their auras coalesced into a single, surging force, resembling majestic snow-capped peaks, exerting an overwhelming sense of pressure on those around them.

The Fire Meteor Squad unleashed their fire-based spells.

The intense flames erupted like a monstrous beast opening its jaws, engulfing the mysterious assassin's dwelling in a single gulp.

The flames raged fiercely.

The survivors watched the magic images and seemed to be able to feel the terrifying heat.

“This is a gold level spell. They cast a spell beyond their level, and the key is that they succeeded!” When Tripleblade saw this scene, he was moved.

He subconsciously compared the mercenary's squads with these three magic teams.

Whether it was the Quick Knife Squad or the Backwater Battle Squad, if they encountered any of the magic teams, they would only suffer defeat and be slaughtered.

The gap between the two sides was enormous!

A gold level spell was similar to a gold level cannon, with the power of a gold level cannonball.

“If such a spell fell on our heads, how could the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps resist?”

When Tripleblade thought of this, his face turned a little pale.

Zong Ge, on the other hand, looked longingly at them.

He had once served in the imperial army and had seen the magic teams of the Sheng Ming Empire.

At this moment, Zong Ge compared the magic teams in front of him with those in his memory.

He found that in terms of spellcasting skills, the snow elves was obviously more proficient and experienced.

Of course, the army Zong Ge served in was just an ordinary regular army.

But the problem was that the three magic teams here were of a lower standard. They were only part of the Snow Bird Harbor's city defense system.

At best, they were just garrison troops.

“In the Ice Sculpture Kingdom, what is the standard of the magic teams in the regular army?”

Zong Ge thought of himself again.

“When will I be able to form a magic team in the future?” Zong Ge smiled bitterly to himself.

This goal was too long-term. For him, it was simply a distant dream.

So far, he had formed two teams, the Quick Knife Squad and Backwater Battle Squad. Both teams have few members, and the death of even one member would cause Zong Ge great pain.

It was difficult to replenish the team members.

Only those with the right bloodline could join the team.

Only countries and large aristocratic families had enough people to select from to build a team of sufficient size.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was too small, and even if it tried its best to recruit new members, it would only be a small group. Luck played a big part.

To be honest, the Quick Blade and Backwater squads were mostly products of good luck.

The flames burned out, and before they were extinguished, they turned into countless frosts that raged.

The transition between the fire and ice spells was seamless and natural.

“What beautiful spells,” Bai Ya exclaimed in admiration.

The spells cast by the elves seemed to possess an extraordinary sense of beauty.

Bai Ya had also experienced major battles.

The Snake Rat Island, the Two Eyes Island, and the Weeping Pine Naval Battle.

But upon reflection, he had never seen spells like this before.

They looked both magnificent and dazzling yet also contained terror and danger.

Zi Di glanced at Bai Ya with a solemn expression: "It's not just beautiful. These three magic battle teams are exceptionally powerful, condensing battlefield-class spells into tactical-class spells. Perhaps that is the secret to their ability to cast spells beyond their level."

Chapter 533: Seabird Church

Spells could be classified into tactical, battlefield, and strategic classes according to their effectiveness.

Tactical-class spells could influence individual combat.

Battlefield-class spells could determine the outcome of an entire battlefield.

Strategic-class spells were mostly used for deterrence. Once released, they had an enormous impact and were difficult to contain.

Battlefield-class spells were almost always large in scope, but the magic team's spellcasting was confined to a very small space.

This was not something ordinary people could achieve.

The dazzling fire spells moved the survivors.

From these three magic teams, they sensed the power of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom.

Tactical-class fire spells were perfectly suited for the current battlefield.

The mysterious assassin's hiding place seemed ordinary and unremarkable, but even a fool could guess that this civilian house must have all kinds of defensive measures.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord did not send an assault team to trigger the traps but instead burned it down with fire.

Soon, most of the defensive measures were destroyed by the flames.

Amid the flames, fragments of translucent mirrors began to emerge.

A second later, these mirrors shattered one after another.

The fire spells were neutralized, and their power was greatly reduced.

But in the next instant, the fire spells transformed into ice spells, with the magic power of the former seamlessly transferring to the latter.

The power of ice spells was even stronger than that of fire spells, emitting a large amount of energy and a bone-chilling cold.

Zi Di narrowed her eyes and her heart trembled slightly.

“The ice spells are also gold level!”

“It seems that casting spells above their level is a normal operation for them.”

“Any magic team can give the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps a hard time.”

“The Ice Sculpture Kingdom is truly powerful.”

“But if you compare them to the Sheng Ming Empire, they are incomparable.”

The Sheng Ming Empire was the world's leading power today, and its national strength completely surpassed that of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom.

Compared to the Ice Sculpture Kingdom, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was like an ant.

Compared to the Sheng Ming Empire, it was not even an ant.

“If we are hunted down by the Sheng Ming Empire, we're truly doomed.”

“Only by acting preemptively and successfully atoning for our sins can we secure a brighter future.”

With this in mind, Zi Di's resolve grew even stronger.

Amidst the biting cold air, a hemispherical light barrier suddenly appeared.

"This is a defensive magic formation. The formation diagram should be laid out in the shallow underground layers."

Just as Zi Di noticed the clue, the defensive magic formation collapsed.

A figure shot out like lightning.

It was the mysterious assassin!

“Ah, he's escaping, escaping!” Fei She let out a low cry, feeling disappointed.

The best case scenario was that the mysterious assassin would not be able to withstand the surprise attack and would die directly under two rounds of magic attacks.

But that was not the case.

As a gold level battle qi cultivator, the mysterious assassin was quite difficult to handle and could not be dealt with that easily.

The dragonman youngster and Zong Ge were not very surprised.

Regardless of whether the mysterious assassin was Hua Tang, judging from his assassination attempt on the Love Triangle, it was clear that he was quite skilled.

The assassin's escape made the survivors' hearts sink.

But in the next moment, the Sea Bird Bishop appeared and unleashed his divine spell.

Until then, he had been hiding his presence.

It was only when he suddenly made his move that the survivors discovered him.

Divine spell—Bird Poop!

The big guy saw a pile of bird poop fall on the mysterious assassin's head and couldn't help but laugh loudly, “Look, poop fell on his head!”

Unlike before, this time the dragonman youngster also brought the big guy in.

The main purpose was to broaden his horizons.

At the same time, the dragonman youngster also had some other concerns.

If the big guy was left out every time important matters were discussed, it would create a barrier between him and the group.

The old captain entrusted his son to the dragonman youngster, who also did his best for the big guy and fulfilled his responsibilities as a brother and father.

The dragonman youngster pointed at the magic image and said to the big guy, “Xiao Guai, don't underestimate this divine spell.”

“This is one of the core divine spells of the Sea Bird Church.”

"It has many uses. In addition to marking enemies, it can also be used to declare sovereignty, occupy territory, and increase soil fertility."

The deity worshiped by the Sea Bird Church was unique.

Though it was the native deity of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom, it was not a snow elf.

Long, long ago, it was merely a sea bird.

According to legend, the sea bird was in distress and was saved by a young fisherman from the Ice Sculpture Kingdom.

After recovering from its injuries, the sea bird regained its strength. It attempted to assist the young fisherman to repay the debt of gratitude for saving its life.

At first, the sea bird guided the young fisherman in finding directions, successfully locating large schools of fish each time, ensuring the young fisherman reaped abundant catches every time he went fishing.

Before storms arrived, the sea bird would issue early warnings, allowing the young fisherman to evacuate in advance and avoid disaster.

The young fisherman was ostracized and betrayed by others, becoming a farmer but only receiving a plot of extremely barren land.

The sea bird led its flock to frequently fly over this land, dropping countless bird droppings.

The bird droppings, rich in nutrients, seeped into the soil, transforming the once barren land into fertile ground.

The sea bird's actions earned the faith of the young fisherman. They also inspired the surrounding fishermen to begin worshipping the Sea Bird.

The sea bird began assisting others, gaining even more devotees.

The young fisherman grew into a transcendent and, in turn, aided the sea bird.

The sea bird unexpectedly received fragments of divinity and successfully ascended to godhood.

The young fisherman also became the pope of the Sea Bird Church.

This was the origin of the Sea Bird Church.

The dragonman youngster originally wanted to tell the big guy the story of the Sea Bird Church.

But in the next moment, his attention was drawn to the new battle situation.

The mysterious assassin rushed out of his hiding place and immediately jumped to a high place to observe the battlefield.

Without the slightest hesitation, he made a split-second decision and chose the Fire Meteor Team, which had the largest number of members, as his breakthrough point.

Seeing his choice, even the dragonman youngster, who was his opponent, couldn't help but admire him in his heart.

This choice was quite correct.

The dragonman youngster couldn't help but ask himself: Could he make such a quick decision?

The dragonman youngster was open-minded and saw the assassin's strengths, secretly learning from him.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord naturally would not sit idly by and watch his team be slaughtered.

He took the initiative to intercept the mysterious assassin.

The battle between the two gold level battle qi cultivators attracted the full attention of the survivors.

The dragonman youngster watched with the most fascination.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord was undoubtedly a veteran gold level battle qi cultivator.

The dragon youngster had also seen several gold level battle qi cultivators.

But unlike Hun Tong, Er Gua Zi, and others, the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord was a snow elf and a high-ranking member of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom.

If the dragonman youngster wanted to have a heroic duel with Teng Dong Lang, the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord was a more valuable reference.

“The Ice Sculpture Kingdom has a large number of alchemist magicians.”

"The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord is also highly influential. I wonder how many alchemy tools does he have? Will they be endless?"

The survivors were all looking forward to it.

However, the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord only used a short staff as a weapon.

This was unexpected.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's subsequent display of combat prowess also impressed the survivors.

He used both battle qi and magic!

This was a true dual cultivator. The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord had perfectly combined battle skills and magic to form his own unique fighting style.

Watching the two gold level battle qi cultivators move at lightning speed, their figures flickering as they shuttled between the slum houses, the dragonman youngster and Zong Ge's expressions gradually became serious.

Because they discovered that if they fought the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord or the mysterious assassin alone, they would be at a disadvantage and would not be their match.

The speed of the two gold level battle qi cultivators was astonishing.

When pushed to his limits, the dragonman youngster could match them in speed. However, he was far inferior in terms of agility. Both the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord and the mysterious assassin were superior to him, being more agile and flexible.

This was because the dragonman youngster's movements were mostly thrust type. Similar to catapulting, his direction was straight, and his movements were obvious. Such a monotonous way of moving is itself an obvious flaw.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord and the mysterious assassin continued to fight, jumping and turning, often condensing their golden battle qi to borrow power from thin air.

This scene caught the eye of the dragonman youngster, making him wary.

The moves of the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord and the mysterious assassin were very precise, not the kind that were wide and sweeping.

The dragonman youngster also noticed that neither of them seemed to possess flying battle skills, or at least they were not used yet.

After thinking about their situation for a moment, the dragonman youngster understood.

If the mysterious assassin could fly, then he would become a living target if he flew recklessly. At the edge of the battlefield, the three magic teams that had not yet intervened were already on high alert.

Since the mysterious assassin did not fly, the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord naturally did not take to the skies either.

The current situation was favorable to him, so if he had flying battle skills, it would certainly be safer to reserve them as a backup to deal with unexpected situations.

The mysterious assassin tried to escape several times but was held back by the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord.

Compared to each other, the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's moves were undoubtedly more varied and even spectacular.

He was extremely skilled at using both battle skills and magic at the same time, and his coordination was exquisite, dazzling his enemies and making him difficult to deal with.

The dragonman youngster put himself in the mysterious assassin's position. Faced with such an attack, he could only retreat or fight back.

Retreating was difficult.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's attacks were relentless and frequent. This fighting style was actually quite similar to that of the dragonman youngster.

The mysterious assassin's flexibility surpassed that of the dragonman youngster, but he was also entangled by the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord and unable to escape.

What about resisting?

That was not easy either!

In order to block the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's attacks, the dragonman youngster had to have extremely strong physical and magic defenses.

The Golden Silk Chainmail primarily defended against physical attacks, while the dragon scales offered protection against both.

However, the Golden Silk Chainmail was severely damaged and urgently needed repair.

As for the dragon scales, they would also gradually sustain damage from these attacks. Once the damage accumulated, the defenses would be breached, making it difficult to continue defending.

Chapter 534: In Position

“The situation is in our favor. If things continue like this, the culprit will be brought to justice!” Lan Zao watched the battle for a long time and saw that the mysterious assassin was unable to escape, so he couldn't help but show a look of joy on his face.

But Zong Ge slowly shook his head, his face calm: "Although the battle between the two is fierce, neither of them has used powerful techniques. The outcome of the battle is still unclear, and it is far from over."

As soon as he finished speaking, he saw a shocking scene unfold in the magic image.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's short staff pierced the mysterious assassin's chest.

The mysterious assassin's heart was crushed, and he died on the spot.

The dragonman youngster and Zong Ge both frowned at the same time, sensing that something was wrong.

In the next moment, the mysterious assassin's body reverted to an alchemy puppet.

Obviously, this was a gold level alchemy puppet, whose quality and grade far exceeded the materials used in the Love Triangle.

The survivors' hearts trembled, and they all shared the same doubt—

“Where is the real assassin?”

Fortunately, the city Lord's side made the right response.

The Sea Bird Bishop unleashed his divine spell and saw through the mysterious assassin's true identity.

“Kill him!” The big guy couldn't help but shout.

He knew that the mysterious assassin was an enemy of the mercenary group.

When the mysterious assassin was in action, he was still asleep. After waking up, he learned everything that had happened from the others.

With the three gold level fighters fighting together, the mysterious assassin's situation became even more disadvantageous.

Suddenly, another unexpected change occurred.

The damaged alchemy puppet suddenly sprang up and jumped toward the edge of the battlefield.

Then, the mysterious assassin's “true identity” was revealed. He tore off his disguise and fought with all his might, disregarding his energy consumption, desperately entangling the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord.

“I didn't expect the assassin's true identity to be the alchemy puppet from earlier?”

"What kind of trick is this? It's one thing to disguise himself as an alchemy puppet and fool us, but he actually fooled the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord and the Sea Bird Bishop too!"

“This assassin is really something.”

The mysterious assassin's tactics greatly surprised the survivors.

It was a plan within a plan!

This move alone showed that the mysterious assassin was no ordinary person.

“We can't let him get away!” Seeing that the assassin was about to break through, the dragonman youngster subconsciously stood up, looking anxious.

"It's too late to stop him. Everyone's attention is focused on the battle with the three magic teams, and their position is not ideal." Zong Ge sighed heavily.

Zi Di hurriedly controlled some scout alchemy puppets, attempting to intercept the mysterious assassin's escape route.

However, it was evident that the distance was too great.

The mysterious assassin's tactics were astonishing, and his intellect was also remarkable, deceiving everyone.

Just as he was about to succeed, a bright green beam of light suddenly shot toward him from a distant location outside the battlefield.

The magician tower!

Hope rose again in everyone's eyes.

The mysterious assassin was already on guard, his aura rising, but in the next moment, his battle skill failed.

The splitting beam hit the mysterious assassin squarely.

His defenses were completely split in an instant, and he fell dead on the spot!

Only the fragments of the silver fox mask remained, falling to the ground, and then quickly disintegrating completely.

The big guy raised his arms and shouted, "He's dead, he's dead. The villain has been defeated!"

Qiao Shan exhaled a breath of stale air, patted her chest, and smiled broadly, “Finally, we no longer have to live in fear.”

Zi Di sneered, “This is the fate of those who steal our property.”

The dragonman youngster and Zong Ge fell silent for a moment, saying nothing.

Bai Ya sighed and spoke the thoughts of many at that moment, “This is the magician tower, how powerful!”

A gold level magician tower was far more powerful than a magician of the same level.

It was the absolute core of the city's defense system.

Don't be fooled by its location on the dock which was far away from the battlefield.

Its attack range was very wide and could completely cover the slums.

Territory with a magician tower had an entirely different level of defense.

Tripleblade frowned as he looked at the magic image, his face showing hesitation: “Is the culprit really dead?”

The survivors all had the same concern.

The mysterious assassin was highly skilled in using alchemy puppets, employing them to achieve tactical objectives during the previous dock assassination and the recent fierce battle.

“It must be his true body!” Zi Di replayed the magic image, allowing everyone to clearly see the details of the mysterious assassin's flesh dissolving in the light.

Both the scout alchemy puppet and the Deep Sea Monster Fish conveyed the result of the assassin's death.

Soon, the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord sent a message while cleaning up the battlefield.

He had proof from the magician tower.

The Aura Algorithm and other means confirmed that the culprit had been brought to justice.

“A gold level character has fallen just like that!” Qiao Shan sighed, covering her chest with her hand, feeling somewhat unreal.

Zong Ge nodded: “That's reasonable.”

“The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord and the Sea Bird Bishop are both gold level, and the two of them together are already superior to the mysterious assassin.”

“In this battle, they launched a successful surprise attack, were well prepared, and completely took the initiative.”

“The three magic teams can cast gold level spells beyond their level, which can be regarded as two gold level combat forces.”

“The magician tower's final blow was successful, and its strength far exceeded that of a normal gold level magician.”

“A simple calculation shows that at least five gold level combat forces surrounded and killed the mysterious assassin. If the latter couldn't escape, his demise was inevitable.”

"However, the most important thing is that the magician tower's blow was decisive, and the killer failed to use his battle skills at the critical moment. If this hadn't happened, he could still put up a fight. The murderer should have powerful means but didn't get to use them."

“But this is real combat, and not everyone has a chance to fully exert their strength in battle.”

“This is the charm of war.” Zong Ge was very moved.

“This is also the charm of strategy.” The dragonman youngster was silent, with many insights in his heart.

His perspective was different from Zong Ge's.

This time, as the leader of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, the dragonman youngster could only seek outside help when faced with an unbeatable enemy.

The result of seeking help was very good!

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps dealt with the mystery assassin without losing a single soldier.

The key point was that during this process, almost all the risks were borne by the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps only needed to stand by and watch.

The post-battle gains were also guaranteed.

At least, the gold level longbows and harpoons were left behind in the ruins of the slums.

When the mysterious assassin broke through, he didn't have time to take them with him.

“In the future, it won't necessarily be necessary to deal with enemies personally.”

“Sometimes, using diplomacy appropriately can save time and effort and reduce risks.”

“The reason why the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord was able to help this time was mainly because of an exchange of interests.”

“Next, it's my turn to help him deal with the Bayonet Gang.”

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord eliminated the mysterious assassin, which earned him the basic trust of the dragonman youngster.

“At least the relationship between the two is not that of allies, but enemies.”

“This also means that the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord probably really wants to deal with the Bayonet Gang.”

"The Sea Bird Bishop was also present and participated in the entire battle. The position of Snow Bird Harbor City Lord is rotational, but the Sea Bird Bishop has been in office for a long time."

“Moreover, our side and the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord are bound by a magic contract.”

The three magic teams were still cleaning up the battlefield when the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord arrived at the sea with two gold level weapons.

On the Love Triangle, he received a warm welcome from the dragonman youngster.

The dragonman youngster took the two gold level weapons from the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord apologized, "Unfortunately, we only recovered these two. We searched his residence and the underground. The rest of the stolen property should have been destroyed by the disintegration ray."

The dragonman youngster shook his head, "According to the magic contract we made before the operation, City Lord, you have done your best. Your sincerity is obvious to all."

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord took a deep look at the longbow and harpoon.

He was very curious about how the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had located the mysterious assassin.

Before coming, he had used his methods to inspect the longbow and harpoon but found nothing.

“Could it be that they tampered with that set of gold level darts?” The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord did not know that the three pieces of gold level equipment all had tracking devices.

But the combination of the Anti-investigation and Prophecy spell and Deceptive Disguise spell was extremely effective.

Even under his very nose, he was unable to detect it.

The mysterious and effective reconnaissance methods made the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord look up to the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps even more.

The dragonman youngster asked him: Could the identity of the mysterious assassin be confirmed as Hua Tang?

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord shook his head. Based on the existing evidence, it was impossible to confirm. Perhaps it was Hua Tang, but he didn't want to risk reporting it that way.

If it wasn't, the negative impact would be even greater.

The sky was already completely bright.

The dock was crowded with people, and the morning market was bustling. Many people were discussing last night's battle.

After a thrilling night, the situation in Snow Bird Harbor had changed dramatically.

But just like in a play, the climax was about to unfold.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' ships slowly sailed into the dock again.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord stood side by side with him at the bow of the ship, and the two of them attracted a lot of attention.

The key actors were in place!

Chapter 535: Commotion at the Port

The entire Snow Bird Harbor was discussing last night's battle.

Who was the enemy? They were so powerful that they forced the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord and the bishop to join forces.

The deployment of three magic teams and the fatal blow from the magician tower were both grand gestures, causing people from all walks of life in Snow Bird Harbor to actively investigate.

“That mysterious gold level assassin has been killed in battle!”

“Some people speculate that he is Hua Tang?”

"Hua Tang! That's unlikely, right?"

“What's going on with all this?”

“It has something to do with the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps...”

Because of his previous heroic duel, the dragonman youngster had become the center of public opinion.

Although he did not participate in this gold level ambush battle, he was very involved before and after the battle.

Before the battle, he took the initiative to ask the city Lord for help.

After that, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps quietly left the port in the middle of the night, obviously with the permission of the city Lord. Snow Bird Harbor even sent a patrol fleet to protect their fleet.

After the battle began, the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord took the initiative to come to the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' fleet and bring them two pieces of gold level equipment.

After that, many people saw the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' fleet sailing into the port again. Commander Long Fu and the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord stood side by side at the bow of the ship, looking quite close with each other.

Snow Bird Harbor, Clam Cloth Branch.

“Haven't you found out the inside story yet?” The Clam Cloth Branch Leader questioned his subordinates.

The three subordinates broke out in a cold sweat and hurriedly reported.

“We are already investigating with all our might.”

“The gold level who died in battle assassinated two magicians of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps during the previous operation and stole a large amount of property.”

“And this property was precisely the first transaction completed between the city government palace and the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps yesterday evening.”

"My guess is that although the mysterious assassin was targeting the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, he undoubtedly also caused the city government palace to lose face. That's why there was such a fierce battle last night..."

The subordinate who was reporting hadn't finished speaking when he was hit on the head by a vase.

The vase shattered, but the subordinate, being a transcendent, remained unharmed.

He immediately knelt on one knee and apologized to the branch leader of the Clam Cloth Merchant Alliance, repeatedly saying, “I deserve to die.”

“You are all incompetent!” the Clam Cloth Branch Leader growled, scolding them repeatedly.

“Just because it's embarrassing, the city Lord teamed up with the bishop to risk their lives and attack an enemy of the same level?”

"Clearly, a lot has happened here. There must be a huge conflict and entanglement of interests!"

“I want the inside scoop, the real key information!”

"What did you find out? Who doesn't know this information? The whole of Snow Bird Harbor is already abuzz with it."

The Clam Cloth Branch Leader was extremely powerful.

He was one of the four gold level experts in Snow Bird Harbor and one of the most authoritative figures in the city.

But he had not received any advance warning of last night's fierce battle.

This made the Clam Cloth Branch Leader angry, but he also felt a vague sense of fear.

He had been operating here for a long time and had always believed that Snow Bird Harbor was his territory.

But even in such a place, such a major conflict had occurred, and he was the last to know.

Did this prove that his control over Snow Bird Harbor was far less than he had imagined?

Had all the effort and time he had invested here been nothing but a dream, a very illusory thing?

“Get out of here and investigate thoroughly!”

“Understood, Lord!”

“I will do my best!”

The Clam Cloth Branch Leader dismissed his three subordinates, his brows furrowed in deep thought.

“Even if we don't uncover any crucial information, the sudden changes in Snow Bird Harbor indicate that something major is about to happen.”

“Although the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps is only a small mercenary group, based on various clues, this force must play a very important role.”

“It is very likely that it is related to the previous heroic duel and the Bayonet Gang.”

"What puzzles me most is the city Lord. The mysterious assassin targeted the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, so why did he fight so desperately and bring the Sea Bird Bishop with him? What was the deal between him and the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps? What was it that made it worth his while to do this?"

Just as he thought of this, there was a knock on the door.

A subordinate reported excitedly from outside the door, “Branch Leader, there's new intelligence!”

“Oh?” The Clam Cloth Branch Leader raised his eyebrows, "Come in.”

He took the intelligence and quickly reviewed it.

The next moment, he jumped up from his seat.

"What?

“Commander Long Fu filed a complaint against the Bayonet Gang. During his previous battle with Xiong Ju, the Bayonet Gang secretly interfered, committing sacrilege!”

“And just after Commander Long Fu reported this crime to the city Lord, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps suffered an assassination and theft by a mysterious gold level assassin.”

“A large amount of property was stolen, and the mysterious assassin took away three gold level equipment items that had just been purchased.”

“Furthermore, the only two magicians of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps were assassinated!”

Something big had happened!

The Clam Cloth Branch Leader couldn't tell if Long Fu's accusation against the Bayonet Gang was true.

But he knew that the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord had taken such drastic measures, so his attitude was very clear.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord was on the side of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, and a standoff had formed between the city government palace and the Bayonet Gang!

The Clam Cloth Branch Leader immediately ordered his men to gather and guard the shops, warehouses, and other important industries in Snow Bird Harbor.

He was a human from the Sheng Ming Continent.

If a violent conflict broke out between the city government palace and the Bayonet Gang, his first priority was to ensure that the interests of the Clam Cloth Merchant Alliance were not harmed.

Teng Dong Lang's face was gloomy.

He had learned of the dragonman youngster's accusation that the Bayonet Gang had desecrated the duel a little earlier than the Clam Cloth Branch Leader.

When he first saw the information, his heart skipped a beat.

The demon hybrid ringleader had indeed committed the act.

As the gang leader, Teng Dong Lang personally covered up the crime for his subordinates and promptly reached an understanding with the master of the Snow Bird Dueling Arena.

The exposure of the crime was not what weighed heavily on Teng Dong Lang's mind.

The Bayonet Gang was a gang, and many of the things they did were illegal. In the past, their crimes had been exposed many times.

Teng Dong Lang's main concern was the attitude of the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord.

“He helped the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps in this way and even personally took part in the fight. How many benefits did the former promise him?”

There was one more thing that weighed heavily on Teng Dong Lang's mind.

"A mysterious gold level assassin was encircled and killed. Someone saw a broken silver fox mask, which eventually melted and disintegrated."

“Was it Hua Tang?”

Teng Dong Lang met with Hua Tang, and after eating and drinking together, the latter left.

Hua Tang had a motive to deal with the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

“It shouldn't be him.”

“The other characteristics don't match him.”

“With his skills, how could he easily be defeated in Snow Bird Harbor?”

Teng Dong Lang comforted himself in this way.

But until it was confirmed, he felt uneasy inside.

He was uncertain.

Putting himself in Hua Tang's shoes, if Hua Tang had secretly helped him, he should have actively concealed his own features, but the silver fox mask could not be concealed.

Although Hua Tang was highly skilled, facing such an overwhelming force last night, his defeat and death were not entirely impossible.

At that moment, a subordinate reported, “Leader, Lord Bing Xiao has arrived.”

Bing Xiao had arrived.

Last night's fierce battle and this morning's testimony from Long Fu had left the old leader restless. Upon receiving the news, he had immediately rushed to the gang's headquarters to meet with Teng Dong Lang.

Teng Dong Lang dismissed those around him.

In the room, only he and Bing Xiao remained.

Bing Xiao impatiently asked, “Is what Long Fu said true?”

Teng Dong Lang's expression grew somber, and he did not answer the question directly.

He said, “Discussing this now is pointless.”

Bing Xiao's heart sank instantly.

He knew Teng Dong Lang well and immediately understood the implication of his words. He paused, holding his walking stick: "You're too foolish. Why interfere in the heroic duel? Don't you have the confidence to deal with a mere silver... no, wait... someone acted without authorization. Who was it?"

Teng Dong Lang's gaze sharpened as he stared at Bing Xiao: “I said, discussing this now is pointless.”

Bing Xiao tilted his head slightly; he knew Teng Dong Lang was determined to protect his errant subordinate.

Bing Xiao fell silent for a moment before nodding: “You're right; discussing this now would indeed be of no help.”

Under the current circumstances, the Bayonet Gang absolutely cannot admit to it. Even if they did do it!

Once they admitted it, their reputation would be completely ruined, despised by everyone. Even beggars or children would look down on the Bayonet Gang from the bottom of their hearts, despising them deeply. The gang itself would also face internal conflicts, with many demanding the purging of the traitors.

For an organization like a gang, if no one feared them, internal turmoil ensued, and they were not the sole dominant force—the consequences were obvious.

Bing Xiao asked again, “Was it the Hua Tang who carried out the assassination operation at the dock? Is it related to us?”

Teng Dong Lang sighed and shook his head, admitting that he couldn't confirm this either.

Bing Xiao gritted his teeth: “We have to find a way to prove our innocence!”

“Right now, public opinion in Snow Bird Harbor is running high. As per tradition, the city government palace will definitely send an investigation team.”

“We can use this investigation team to help us prove our innocence.”

The Bayonet Gang has been corrupting the officialdom of Snow Bird Harbor.

Teng Dong Lang nodded: “The key question now is, what is the city Lord's attitude?”

As he spoke, memories flooded his mind—the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord smiling and raising his cup, drinking with him and enjoying the conversation.

Teng Dong Lang also thought of last night, when the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord personally went into battle and surrounded the mysterious assassin.

“What price did the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps pay to get the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord to help them like this?” Teng Dong Lang asked Bing Xiao.

Bing Xiao also frowned and tried to analyze the situation: “A gold level assassin who sneaked into Snow Bird Harbor without reporting it basically has bad intentions.”

“Moreover, he assassinated members of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps. Even if the latter was not a local of Snow Bird Harbor, this illegal act was a provocation to the city Lord.”

“What's more, the city Lord had just handed over the money and goods to the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps when they were stolen by a mysterious person.”

“Of course, I think the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps must have paid a heavy price to get the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord to help them.”

“Now, Long Fu has publicly accused my gang of crimes, and he is also coercing the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord and forcing him to uphold justice.”

“This time, we have to get the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord to stand on our side, but I'm afraid it will cost us dearly!”

Chapter 536: We are Victims

Teng Dong Lang nodded lightly, agreeing with Bing Xiao's analysis.

Bing Xiao added: "The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord is our top priority, while the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps is the second. All our troubles were caused by this external force."

Teng Dong Lang shook his head: "The mysterious culprit was killed yesterday. It is impossible for us to take action against them now."

"Once we do, it'll confirm their accusations towards us, and everyone will think we're trying to silence the victims."

Bing Xiao added: "The dock is already swarmed with city guards. This is the city Lord's arrangement."

"Now is not a good time to get rid of them."

"But we have many ways to deal with this external force. For example, we can tempt them with benefits. If we can get Long Fu to withdraw his accusation, we will be able to ease our predicament to a great extent."

Teng Dong Lang stared at Bing Xiao coldly: "The chance of successfully bribing him is not high."

"He is the victim, and the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps lost their only two magicians."

"At this crucial time, if he withdraws the accusation, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps will develop a negative opinion towards this commander."

"If I were Long Fu, I would not easily withdraw my earlier accusation. Otherwise, my reputation in Snow Bird Harbor will be ruined, and nobody will trust me anymore."

Bing Xiao retorted: Gang Leader, this does seem unlikely, but after close consideration, I have a different perspective."

"Look."

"The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps is not an ordinary silver level organization. They are arms merchants. They smuggle illegal goods."

"This is not a normal force. Do you think they really have just two magicians? In fact, their only two magicians weren’t even silver level. Is that possible?"

Teng Dong Lang contemplated.

Bing Xiao continued: "Gang Leader, did you notice?"

"Although Long Fu exposed our actions in the heroic duel, he only publicly declared it and did not take out evidence!"

"Does he actually have evidence?"

Teng Dong Lang thought about what he did that day. He had already cleaned up the place and removed all traces of magic power.

The most useful evidence now was the demon hybrid ringleader.

However, how could he accuse Teng Dong Lang of being the culprit?

After thinking, Teng Dong Lang shook his head: "I think Long Fu does not have any evidence."

"He only publicly announced it to show his attitude."

"After all, two magicians in his Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps were assassinated and killed. Seeing such a commotion yesterday, it is obvious he paid a huge price to get the help of the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord."

"If he had proof, he would have taken it out already!"

Bing Xiao nodded: "I think so too."

"He has no evidence, and the mysterious assassin has melted under the disintegration ray. They are far from being able to ascertain our crime."

"In any case, the worst scenario is that he has some evidence of our wrongdoing. Then, why is he not showing it?"

Teng Dong Lang had an idea: "The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps does not want to fight to the death with us."

"We are the local tyrant here!"

"Long Fu has a mission to accomplish. They want to spread their smuggling business in the Ice Sculpture Kingdom."

"How can he go all out against us, a local force, the moment they arrive?"

"That is illogical!"

Bing Xiao nodded: "This is the same reason why we didn't go all out to eliminate this external force when they first arrived."

"Any sort of conflict carries risks. One loss can mean the end for us!"

"Of course, there is another possibility."

"Long Fu is keeping the evidence and waiting for us to go negotiate with him."

"He wants to extort us using this opportunity. I can already imagine his request. He definitely wants us to relent and cooperate with their arms smuggling business!"

"So, no matter what the situation is, we need to have a good talk with Long Fu."

"If we can convince him to withdraw the accusation, we will cross one hurdle. In the future, if he accuses us again, we can use this bribing incident to our advantage. As long as the people see his true nature, their trust in him will fall drastically!"

Teng Dong Lang thought about it silently for a while before nodding.

"You are right."

"Then, who should we send?"

Teng Dong Lang looked at Bing Xiao and said with deep meaning: "Ban Lan Gen has the brain of a wooden block. He messed up earlier and was defeated by Long Fu in one punch."

"Xiong Ju is rash and simple. He is suitable for combat but not negotiations. I'm afraid they'll start fighting if they meet."

"But if I let Spring Boxer go..."

At this point, a voice spoke out from outside.

"Let me in!" It was Spring Boxer who spoke.

The guard stopped him: "The Gang Leader instructed that nobody is to interrupt him while he's in a meeting."

"Let me in!" Spring Boxer shouted.

The guard held onto the door handle firmly, obstructing him.

"No, I must get an answer on this matter!" Spring Boxer was obstructed so he could only shout loudly, "Gang Leader, are the rumors true?"

"Did we really make a move on Commander Long Fu in the heroic duel?"

"This fellow..." Teng Dong Lang was speechless.

He knew that if he did not respond, Spring Boxer would not stop making a scene outside until he got an answer.

Thus, Teng Dong Lang shouted: "Of course not."

"Commander Long Fu has misunderstood."

"Don't worry, this misunderstanding will be resolved in some time."

"This is not something you should be concerned about. Go back and do your job now!"

Although Spring Boxer was scolded, he got the answer he wanted. He smiled sheepishly and replied: "I knew it was like this."

"Gang Leader, I'll leave now. Hehe!"

The outside became quiet again.

Teng Dong Lang looked at Bing Xiao and sighed helplessly: "I've spoiled this brat too much."

Bing Xiao laughed: "He has a pure heart. It is a rare thing."

"He is the successor you nurtured. How enviable. Don't act like you're not happy about it!"

Teng Dong Lang had a solemn expression most of the time, but now he showed a slight smile.

But soon, this smile disappeared and was replaced by seriousness.

Teng Dong Lang said: "Even a ringleader like him is uncertain. We can imagine the situation in our gang now."

"Although the Bayonet Gang is huge and has many members, we can't find a proper person to go negotiate with."

Bing Xiao laughed as he knew Teng Dong Lang's intentions in saying so.

At the next moment, Bing Xiao took on the duty: "Then let me go."

"This..." Teng Dong Lang pretended to be hesitant.

Bing Xiao said: "The person who is going to negotiate has to have status. Right now, the six ringleaders have status, but their personality and abilities are not meant for this."

"As the gang leader, you cannot go personally. You are the pride and dignity of our gang. We must make sure you can deal with future situations that occur."

"Only I, a silver level and also the previous gang leader, can go. Most importantly, the reason why Long Fu had a conflict with us was due to me."

Saying this, Bing Xiao sighed as he looked up, sighing deeply: "I did not expect it to turn out like this."

"Had I known this, I would've politely given that intelligence potion to Long Fu."

Teng Dong Lang shook his head and consoled: "It is not your fault."

"The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps has a heavy responsibility. As newcomers, they will definitely choose a target to flex their muscles and show their strength. This is the only way to pave the way for their arms business."

“Old gang leader, you just happened to run into them by chance.”

“I don't think the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps really wants to clash with our Bayonet Gang.”

"If Long Fu had evidence, why didn't he point it out during the duel with Xiong Ju? Even if he didn't notice it during the duel, he should have publicly accused us after reflecting on the battle."

"He originally wanted to control the scale of their conflict with us. But last night’s events happened too suddenly. He was caught off guard!”

Teng Dong Lang was pleased and grateful that Bing Xiao had voluntarily taken on the matter.

Bing Xiao was indeed the best choice for negotiations.

But Teng Dong Lang couldn’t directly order him to go.

On one hand, Bing Xiao was the former gang leader, and both were military men. On the other hand, if Bing Xiao went to negotiate, he might be insulted.

Only if Bing Xiao volunteered to go would he not harbor resentment toward Teng Dong Lang after being mocked or humiliated.
