Based on Teng Dong Lang's understanding of Bing Xiao, he was confident that Bing Xiao would volunteer.

The situation unfolded exactly as he had anticipated.

Bing Xiao stood up: “There's no time to waste. I'll prepare and depart as soon as possible.”

“I'll explain to Long Fu that last night's assassination had nothing to do with us.”

“He won't believe it, but this isn't an explanation for him—it's our explanation to the people of Snow Bird Harbor!”

Teng Dong Lang stood up to see him off.

“I will have the other three ringleaders surrender voluntarily and urge Long Fu to engage in a heroic duel with me as soon as possible!”

“At that time, in the arena, I will personally take Long Fu's life.”

“This dragonman has caused us great trouble, plunging the Bayonet Gang into a crisis of survival. I cannot ease my hatred unless I kill him!”

"Of course, if circumstances permit, such as if he has already secretly reached an agreement with us. Then I will spare his life to demonstrate the magnanimity of the Bayonet Gang."

Bing Xiao nodded: “It's time to teach this scoundrel a lesson.”

“This plan to resolve the issue is well-thought-out.”

"Teng Dong Lang, you are a more suitable leader than I am. You have always put the interests of the gang first. You've done better than me."

“For the sake of the Bayonet Gang, what does it matter if I sacrifice my reputation and suffer some sarcasm?”

Teng Dong Lang sighed, “The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps took the initiative to frame us, and the outside world misunderstands us too much.”

“We are the real victims.”

At the same time, on the Love Triangle.

There were only two people in the cabin.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord: “Commander Long Fu, I carefully selected these pieces of equipment for you.”

"With them, your chances of winning against Teng Dong Lang will greatly increase!"

Chapter 537: Borrowing the City Lord’s Treasures

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord made a secret visit this time, mainly to bring powerful equipment to the dragonman youngster.

Under the current circumstances, powerful equipment could quickly increase individual strength.

Equipping the dragonman youngster in advance would also give him more time to familiarize himself with the use of the equipment, allowing him to make better use of it.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord took a small bag out of his bosom.

This bag was obviously a gold level treasure, capable of concealing the aura of its contents.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord unfolded the small bag completely.

In the next moment, all the equipment was visible to the dragonman youngster.

The small bundle clearly did not have the ability to shrink space, and all the equipment was actual size.

The equipment was very small and delicate, with many large and small rings.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord did not prioritize introducing these rings but first picked up a scale with his finger.

It was a white, translucent dragon scale, only the size of an adult's thumb.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord introduced, “Commander Long Fu, this is a badge from the Dragon's Reverse Scale series, specifically the Sword Return Reverse Scale.”

The dragonman youngster immediately nodded, “I know this series of alchemy treasures.”

The Dragon's Reverse Scale has a series of badges, ranging in quality from bronze to saint level.

The one that the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord took out was naturally gold level.

All the equipment he was lending to the dragonman youngster this time was of this level.

The main material used in the Dragon's Reverse Scale series of alchemy badges was dragon scales. The dragon scales used were also extraordinary, as only the unique reverse scale from among all the scales on a dragon's body was used.

The bodies of dragons were covered in resilient dragon scales, which offered exceptional defense.

However, among all dragon scales, there was typically one special scale.

Almost all dragon scales grew in the same direction, but this special scale grew in the opposite direction. Due to this characteristic, it is commonly referred to as the reverse scale.

The reverse scale was the weakest point in terms of defense among dragon scales.

In many stories, dragon slayers exploited this defensive weakness by targeting the reverse scale, inflicting severe or even fatal injuries on the dragon.

The dragonman youngster had some knowledge of this type of alchemy equipment, which he had gained from expanding his reading while memorizing knowledge about dragons in his daily life.

For the youngster, once the bloodline concentration of the Flame Dragon King reached a certain level in the future, he would also develop a reverse scale.

Among the Dragon's Reverse Scale series, the most classic product was Dragon Reverse: Rage. When a battle qi cultivator wore it, every time they were attacked, they would accumulate Rage.

Once the Rage reached a certain level, it would erupt, granting the battle qi cultivator enhancements in strength, attack power, speed, and many other aspects.

While the variety of the enhancements did not match that of the Grand Arena Medal, the extent of the enhancements was very high.

Once Rage erupted, it often gave the battle qi cultivator several times the combat power in a short period of time, thereby changing the situation and securing victory.

·

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord did not lend out the most classic Dragon's Reverse Scale, but the relatively rare Sword Return Dragon Reverse Scale.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord explained in detail, "This alchemy badge uses the reverse scale of the Back Sword Dragon. Every time a battle qi cultivator takes a hit, whether it's melee or ranged, whether it's battle skills, magic, or divine spells, sword qi will accumulate in the badge."

“When the accumulation reaches its limit, it will erupt, forming a terrifying wave of sword qi.”

"Of course, if you can actively control it, you can also release it actively. During the process of accumulating sword qi, you can shoot it out one by one. It is very accurate and convenient, as long as you lock onto the enemy."

The dragonman youngster nodded, a hint of joy appearing on his face: “In this way, my long-range attack ability will be greatly supplemented.”

The dragonman youngster was only silver level, and silver level battle qi could not be condensed out of thin air like gold level. Therefore, the vast majority of battle skills were close-range, with almost no long-range battle skills.

Looking at his battles with Xiong Ju, Spring Boxer, and others, the silver level battle skills were all close-range attacks.

If his strength was not enough, his equipment could make up for it.

The Sword Return Badge directly made up for the dragonman youngster's long-range combat deficiency.

Although he could also use the alchemy crossbow, the Sword Return Badge allowed him to directly use it in close combat through mental mobilization.

It was convenient and fast, and very compatible with his fighting style.

The actual combat effect was much better than the crossbow!

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord put down the Sword Return Badge and took out the largest gold level item from the small bag.

It was a spherical glass reagent bottle, about the size of a kiwi fruit.

When Zi Di prepared the potion, she had glass reagent bottles of various sizes.

The one in front of the dragonman youngster was very high-end, reaching the gold level.

“This is the Automatic Blood Bottle,” said the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord, "You can swallow it temporarily or hold it in your mouth. When you want to speak, you can press it under your tongue, and you will be able to barely speak."

“It has the function of storing potions.”

“Don't let its compact size fool you; it has a large internal space capable of storing a significant amount of potion.”

“As the name suggests, its primary function is automatic medication administration.”

“Once your condition deteriorates, the potion will be automatically released and infused into your body.”

"The drawback is that it can only store healing potions, and the potions must be of the same type. Different healing potions cannot be mixed together in the bottle, as that would cause immeasurable damage to the bottle."

The dragonman youngster immediately praised, “Very practical.”

He thought of his fierce battle with Xiong Ju.

In the final stage, both he and Xiong Ju were exhausted and had to temporarily stop fighting. The two sides were far apart, each desperately swallowing potions to improve their condition.

With this Automatic Blood Bottle, he wouldn't have to be so desperate.

The blood bottle also had a hidden function, which was that he could secretly take medicine without his opponent noticing.

This would cause his opponent to misjudge the situation.

In a short period of time, that was all the dragonman youngster could think of.

The further practical value of the blood bottle remained to be explored by the dragonman youngster.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord put the Automatic Blood Bottle aside and took out the largest round object.

Before he could speak, the dragonman youngster said, “I know this equipment. It's the Ice Heart Headband.”

The main material of the Ice Heart Headband was the Ice Heart.

One of the goals of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' visit to the Ice Sculpture Kingdom was to purchase this alchemy treasure.

After wearing the Ice Heart Headband, magicians would remain calm at all times, their mental power would increase, their spell casting speed would be accelerated, and their personal will would be protected.

The last point was the most important to the survivors.

The Ice Heart Ring could protect their will!

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord nodded, "I saw your battle with Xiong Ju, and he suppressed you with his Bear Roar battle skill. You need to strengthen your mental defense, especially since Teng Dong Lang has mastered the relevant battle skills."

“Is that so?” The dragonman youngster keenly grasped this new information, which the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had not collected.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord: "I'll tell you about Teng Dong Lang later. Next, I want to cover this set of equipment."

“You heard right.”

“This is a set!”

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord pointed to the remaining large and small rings in the small bag, his tone proud.

"Each of these rings is gold level. But what makes them truly powerful is that they can form a complete set."

“The set is called Diamond Protection.”

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord first took out a small orange-gold ring.

The ring was about the size of an adult's index finger bent at the knuckle, with the fingertip touching the tip of the thumb to form a circle.

The round equipment was full-bodied and gave a heavy visual impression.

"This is one of the components of the set, the orange steel nose ring. It can enhance your sense of smell, but at the same time help you resist attacks such as poison gas and foul odors, acting as a filter."

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord picked up a pair of smaller rings with his other hand.

The rings were only half the size of the orange steel nose ring, relatively thin, and shimmered with a crimson metallic luster.

“This pair is the crimson steel earrings, which can enhance your hearing, but at the same time help you resist attacks such as sound waves.”

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord continued his introduction.

“This pair of green steel bracelets can speed up your hand's actions, while also providing some protection for your forearms and palms.”

“Finally, the ink steel ankle rings allow you to move faster, kick harder, and provide excellent protection for your lower limbs.”

“After wearing all the components, the entire Diamond Protection Set will have a spell effect called Diamond Body.”

"Diamond Body makes your surroundings incredibly hard; even your internal organs are somewhat enhanced. You can think of it as an enhanced and optimized version of the battle skill Black Armor, or a comprehensive version of the battle skill Silver Armor."

“The most important thing is that the Diamond Body is just a thin layer of light that does not affect any of your movements but provides good defense against swords and magic.”

“Great!” After listening to the introduction, the dragonman youngster's eyes lit up, and he immediately praised it.

All the components were gold level, and when combined, the defensive effect of the Diamond Body was unimaginable!

The dragonman youngster himself had amazing defenses.

With this set of equipment, even when facing gold level opponents, the dragonman youngster might still maintain a relative advantage.

Under the guidance of the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord, the dragonman youngster tried on all the equipment one by one.

After last night's battle, both sides had established a foundation of mutual trust. Coupled with the constraints of the contract, the dragonman youngster felt at ease and boldly tried them all out.

Before he could carefully understand them, the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord said, “Commander Long Fu, you can slowly familiarize yourself with this equipment later.”

“I'll show you two videos, both of Teng Dong Lang's battles.”

The dragonman youngster raised his eyebrows slightly and instantly felt the gap in intelligence capabilities between them.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had spent a large amount of money but had not been able to gather any intelligence on Teng Dong Lang's combat capabilities. Not only had the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord collected it, but he had also obtained two videos!

“The first video shows Teng Dong Lang's combat performance during his military service.” The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord placed the alchemy device on the floor and turned it on.

The dragonman youngster glanced at it and saw a group of snow elves being attacked by a large pack of wolves in a snowstorm.

Chapter 538: Radiance Ring

In the magic image, there were very few snow elves, but their defense line was very solid.

The dragonman youngster quickly spotted Teng Dong Lang among the crowd.

He was in the middle and seemed unable to move. At his feet, several long whips of ice and snow were rooted in the ground, lashing around.

The whips were extremely accurate and powerful.

Each lash hit the gaps in the snow elf formation in front of him without accidentally injuring his allies.

Each lash also took the lives of one or two ice plain giant wolves.

“This is the battle skill Cold Ice Whip,” explained the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord, "This gold level battle skill has undergone a qualitative change and can almost be considered a small-sized magic spell.”

The dragonman youngster nodded: “Yes, it can be condensed into shape independently, making the combat methods of gold level battle qi cultivators quite rich, no longer comparable to battle qi cultivators silver level and below.”

A large number of ice plain snow wolves lost their lives on the front line, which angered the king of the wolf pack.

A slender ice plain wolf king as big as a small elephant rushed towards the snow elves.

Teng Dong Lang was the highest-ranked member of this snow elf team.

Seeing this, he immediately shouted, “Get down!”

The tacit understanding and trust that had been cultivated over a long period of time allowed the snow elves fighting on the front lines to immediately obey his orders and lie down on the ground.

Teng Dong Lang took a deep breath and pushed his battle qi to the limit.

He stretched out his left arm and aimed his left fist straight ahead.

The king of the wolf pack rushed forward, fierce and powerful, accompanied by a strong wind and blizzard.

In the next moment, the crystal ring on Teng Dong Lang's left index finger suddenly shot out a blue light.

Streams of blue light were fired one after another, striking the wolf king repeatedly within just a few seconds.

With each hit from the blue light, the wolf king's speed decreased slightly.

By the time it reached the front lines, it had frozen into an ice block, posing no threat.

The wolf king was bound by the ice block, and the morale of the remaining ice plain wolves plummeted, causing them to flee in disarray.

The video ended there.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord said, "The ring used by Teng Dong Lang is a silver level Radiance Ring. This was equipment used by the Ice Sculpture Kingdom's army, specifically for officers. Teng Dong Lang has made many contributions to the army, and he exchanged his military merits for this ring."

“The Radiance Ring is very practical. Its specific effect is to transform battle skills into rays of light and shoot them out in a focused manner.”

“At the end of this battle, Teng Dong Lang used the battle skill of extreme cold air.”

“The original effect of this battle skill was that after taking a deep breath, he would open his mouth and spit out a large amount of cold air. It was a range-type battle skill that combined offense and defense.”

“But after the transformation of the Radiance Ring, the attack method became a series of rays.”

“More than a dozen frozen rays hit the wolf king, who was completely engulfed by the extreme cold air and frozen into an ice lump.”

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord paused here and then explained.

“Teng Dong Lang in this image had just become gold level not long ago.”

"This image is from the military archives. Back then, Teng Dong Lang used this magic image as evidence, submitted it to the military department for review, and earned a third-class merit."

The dragonman youngster finally understood the source of this image.

At the same time, he gained a deeper understanding of the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's connections.

At the very least, the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord had considerable connections in the military of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom.

“Let's look at the second image. This image is more valuable than the previous one.” After saying that, the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord played the second image.

The dragonman youngster watched intently and saw that it was deep into the night, and on a deserted island in the sea, two gold level beings were fighting.

One was Teng Dong Lang, and the other was wearing a silver fox mask and a white robe with colorful flowers blooming on it.

“A silver fox mask... Could it be Hua Tang?” The dragonman youngster's pupils suddenly contracted.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord said, “This is valuable information obtained by my subordinates during a night patrol of the sea.”

“Commander Long Fu, look closely. These two are not fighting to the death, but sparring.”

The dragonman youngster watched for a while and immediately confirmed the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's statement: “They are indeed sparring.”

Neither Teng Dong Lang nor the silver fox mask individual used any consumable combat items, such as potions, scrolls, and so on.

Furthermore, the dragonman youngster observed that there were many times when the two actively let opportunities to attack pass.

The two seemed to be enjoying the pleasure of battle, taking no risks and not being aggressive.

Even their battle skills were used very infrequently. Most of the time, they were simply sparring using their fists and feet, amplifying their bodies with battle qi.

The silver fox masked individual moved gracefully, as agile as a butterfly in a flower garden.

Teng Dong Lang's movements were also very fast, only slightly slower than the silver fox mask individual.

He wielded a long whip, using it to attack and defend, firmly maintaining the distance between himself and the silver fox mask individual.

During the battle, the two used their respective battle skills.

For both of them, every battle skill they used was extremely familiar, as if it had become an instinctive part of their bodies.

The dragonman youngster watched the entire battle, his expression and mood equally heavy.

In terms of fighting skills alone, Teng Dong Lang had a distinct military style. But this fighting style was different from Zong Ge's military style.

“The long whip in his hand is as flexible as his arm.”

“He's constantly controlling the distance between himself and his opponent.”

"His fighting performance at medium range is the best. At long range, he is on par with the silver fox mask individual. At short range, he is weaker."

In the video, there were several times when the silver fox mask individual seized an opening and rushed in front of Teng Dong Lang.

Teng Dong Lang had no choice but to use his battle skills to try to create distance.

Most of the battle skills he used in this fight were for medium range.

When the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord saw the magic image being played back for the third time, he couldn't hold back any longer and spoke up, “It is precisely because of this information that I suspect the relationship between Teng Dong Lang and Hua Tang. There must be some inside information between the two that outsiders don't know about!”

"Unfortunately, I can't use this information publicly. Because I don't have direct evidence to prove that the person wearing the silver fox mask is Hua Tang."

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord then proceeded to introduce the dragonman youngster to the battle skills and equipment that Teng Dong Lang had displayed in this battle.

"Commander Long Fu, please recover from your injuries and start competing with Teng Dong Lang as soon as possible!" Snow Bird Harbor City Lord urged.

“If you use this equipment well, it will definitely cause huge trouble for your opponent.”

“I came here secretly this time, so please restrain your subordinates and do not easily reveal my movements, lest the Bayonet Gang become alert.”

The dragonman youngster nodded and replied on the spot, "I understand. In the next few days, I will publicly challenge the next leader of the Bayonet Gang."

“Very good.” The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord got up to leave.

Just then, the dragonman youngster received a report from his communication device—

Bu Quan was visiting.

Chapter 539: Bu Quan Gives Treasures

“Why did she come to visit?” The dragonman youngster's first reaction was surprise.

He knew Bu Quan.

The latter was an excellent alchemist who had an alchemy workshop and property in Snow Bird Harbor and was one of the leading figures in the city.

The dragonman youngster and Zi Di had discussed her when they were talking.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was in dire need of alchemists, and the dragonman youngster had thought of recruiting her, but he also knew that it was difficult for a mercenary corps of their size to recruit such talented people.

“She had sent an invitation letter before, saying that there was a mercenary mission.”

“Could it be related to this mercenary mission?”

The dragonman youngster speculated in his heart.

He had wanted to take advantage of the invitation letter to visit Bu Quan alone and establish preliminary contact. But then a mysterious assassin sneaked in and committed a crime, disrupting the dragonman youngster's plans.

Now, Bu Quan had taken the initiative to visit the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

This was indeed strange!

After the dragonman youngster had publicly accused the Bayonet Gang and intervened in a heroic duel, the conflict between him and the gang had escalated to a very intense level.

The major forces that had originally sent invitation letters to the dragonman youngster were now sitting on the sidelines and suspending their contact with the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

They had all realized that it was almost impossible for the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps and the Bayonet Gang to coexist in Snow Bird Harbor.

They were waiting to see who the ultimate victor would be after the conflict ended.

Compared to these large forces, although Bu Quan had the status of a transcendent alchemy great master, her overall strength was relatively weak.

According to common sense, she should also wait quietly and be a safe bystander.

“But why did she suddenly visit me?” the dragonman youngster muttered to himself.

Seeing the look of confusion on Long Fu's face, the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord immediately smiled and said, “Perhaps I know the reason.”

“Bu Quan and the Bayonet Gang have a deep conflict.”

“Even before she had fully matured, she had already shown exceptional alchemy talent, which aroused the Bayonet Gang's greed.”

“The Bayonet Gang used both force and persuasion to try to force Bu Quan onto their side.”

“Their strong-arm tactics provoked Bu Quan's aversion, and she refused to comply.”

“During her escape, the nanny who had raised her was killed by the Bayonet Gang.”

“Bu Quan had been raised by her nanny since she was a child and had a deep affection for her, like a daughter for her mother.”

“She would definitely want to avenge the murder of her mother!”

“After Bu Quan escaped from Snow Bird Harbor, she took the initiative to go to the Alchemy Guild, where she displayed her talent and ability and successfully obtained the protection of the Alchemy Guild.”

“She thus escaped the pursuit of the Bayonet Gang and cleverly concealed her feelings for her nanny.”

“She gradually grew up and became a recognized alchemy genius.”

"During this period, the Bayonet Gang realized her influence and took the initiative to make peace. Bu Quan was wise. She pretended to accept the Bayonet Gang's apology and put an end to their past feud.

Once she had earned enough contribution to fully repay the Alchemy Guild, she gained her freedom.

She returned to Snow Bird Harbor and opened several alchemy workshops.

She had been secretly searching for and collecting evidence of the Bayonet Gang's crimes, which was also my job. Many times, I deliberately designed and took the initiative to leave some evidence of their crimes for her.

"Because of this, I realized her true purpose, investigated it, and understood her motive for revenge.

"The main reason she took the initiative to visit this time was because you, Long Fu, clearly accused the Bayonet Gang, and the conflict between you and the Bayonet Gang is irreconcilable. This gained her approval to the greatest extent. She came to help her allies!"

“So that's how it is.” The dragonman youngster suddenly understood and couldn't help but show his joy.

On second thought, the dragonman youngster looked at the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord again: “Since that's the case, I might as well introduce her to you, City Lord.”

“We are all fighting against the Bayonet Gang, so comrades-in-arms must naturally unite.”

But the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord shook his head.

“I don't recommend that, Commander Long Fu.”

"Our plan has already been agreed upon. You just need to engage in a heroic duel with Teng Dong Lang and keep him occupied in the dueling arena."

“Trust me, I've been preparing for so many years, taking down the Bayonet Gang is not a problem!”

"Before the heroic duel, we must declare war and delay time. Therefore, even though I have a lot of evidence of the Bayonet Gang's crimes in my hands, I haven't brought it out."

"Don't provoke the Bayonet Gang too much. Let them think that although the situation is bad, it is still under their control.

"I need time to make the final arrangements. And you, Commander Long Fu, need to heal your internal injuries, don't you?"

At this point, Snow Bird Harbor City Lord paused and said, “To be honest, after realizing Bu Quan's true attitude towards the Bayonet Gang, I once wanted to put her up as our banner's symbol.”

“She is very influential, has had a tragic experience, and has achieved success through her own efforts, so once her past is exposed, she will inevitably gain a lot of sympathy and recognition.”

“The more recognition and sympathy the public has, the more disgust and hatred they will have for the Bayonet Gang.”

“But in the end, I chose you.”

"Because the crime of desecrating a heroic duel is far more serious than the crimes the Bayonet Gang committed against Bu Quan. Even gangs in other cities would despise and loathe the Bayonet Gang after this."

“There is another reason, which is profit.”

“Bu Quan is a snow elf and an alchemy master with connections and background in the Alchemy Guild.”

“If I cooperate with her, when I win and eliminate the Bayonet Gang, she will take a large share of my gains, including political influence.”

“In comparison, Commander Long Fu, you are the one I am more willing to cooperate with.”

These words moved the dragonman youngster on the spot.

“Thank you for your honesty. I admire your sincerity and wisdom!” The dragonman youngster bowed slightly to the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord.

He had learned something!

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's choices, actions, and considerations allowed the dragonman youngster to see the political side of a mature struggle.

This was something he had never encountered in his past experiences.

Struggle was never just about fighting and killing.

The dragonman youngster was eager to learn and good at reflection.

His communication with the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord allowed him to learn a lot of things he was lacking.

These words showed him the way forward—he could become a more mature leader going forward.

“It's great that you understand.” The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord patted the dragonman youngster on the shoulder to show his closeness.

Of course, he wouldn't usually speak so plainly.

But he knew even more clearly that the trust he had worked so hard to build with the dragonman youngster could not be destroyed by anything.

This trust had not come easily; he had earned it by slaying a gold level opponent.

It was also a golden opportunity that he had been waiting for many years!

“Go on,” said the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord, "Bu Quan has come to visit you on her own initiative to reinforce you. She will bring you some equipment or medicine. Perhaps there will also be evidence of the Bayonet Gang's crimes. But that's unlikely, since you don't have a deep trust between you."

"The most important point is that this evidence alone won't be enough to bring down the Bayonet Gang. If you defeat Teng Dong Lang, then maybe it'll be a different story."

“Speaking of which, Bu Quan is quite skilled at making alchemy equipment for reconnaissance.”

"When she was studying and growing up in the Alchemy Guild, she created an alchemy blueprint. It was a telescope that could uniquely mark targets within its field of view based on threat level—a highly creative design."

“The first such telescope she personally crafted earned her seventh place in the Warm Snow Cup Alchemy Competition.”

"Don't underestimate seventh place—the Warm Snow Cup is the highest-tier alchemy competition in our kingdom. The top ten in each competition are extremely valuable!"

The dragonman youngster temporarily bid farewell to the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord, left the cabin, came to the ship's rail, and greeted Bu Quan.

Bu Quan had the beautiful appearance of a snow elf.

But what was even more memorable was that she always wore a pair of black-rimmed glasses.

“Commander Long Fu, please forgive me for my presumptuous visit.” When Bu Quan saw the dragonman youngster, she was very polite and did not act like an alchemy master.

Other places are another matter, but in Snow Bird Harbor, she is considered the best in the field of alchemy.

The dragonman youngster hurriedly said, "Master Bu Quan, your visit is a pleasant surprise. Though we have only just met, I have long admired your reputation."

“To be honest, after receiving your invitation letter, I had planned to visit you.”

“Unfortunately, that plan was disrupted.”

Bu Quan comforted him, "Commander Long Fu, you are a believer in the Justice God. I believe that under your hands, justice will prevail."

“I admire your courage.”

“The Bayonet Gang has committed countless crimes, and you are the first person to stand up and publicly accuse them!”

"I admire you as a person, and at the same time, I have a deep grudge and hatred towards the Bayonet Gang. So, don't be surprised by my visit. I am here to help you!"

Bu Quan was also very frank and directly stated her purpose.

The dragonman youngster hurriedly thanked her, then led her into the captain's cabin at the stern of the flagship to entertain her.

The first item she took out was a pair of ski goggles.

The lenses were large, covering half of the dragonman youngster's face.

“I modified these for you overnight, Commander Long Fu.”

“These goggles can automatically mark targets based on threat level, with colors ranging from white, green, yellow, red, to purple.”

“White indicates no threat.”

“Green means the threat is minimal but should not be ignored.”

“Yellow indicates the target poses a certain threat and requires attention.”

“Red signifies a severe threat and demands extreme caution.”

"Purple is the highest level of threat, typically exceeding your combat strength. Withdrawing temporarily is the wise choice."

Finally, Bu Quan said with a touch of pride, “This equipment is my original creation and has not been disclosed yet.”

“Even the Alchemy Guild has not received any information about it.”

“Commander Long Fu, I am gifting it to you, and I believe it will be of great assistance to you.”

Chapter 540: Bing Xiao Makes Amends

The dragonman youngster took the goggles, examined them, put them on his face, and found that they fit perfectly, as if they had been tailor-made for him.

Bu Quan was also satisfied with the result. She pinched the frame of her glasses with her thumb and index finger and raised them slightly: “My assessment of people has always been very accurate.”

Next, she took out the second alchemy artifact.

It was a small bag.

The bag was semi-transparent blue.

From the outside, one could see that it contained small cubic ice cubes.

“This is the Cold Heart Ice Bag,” Bu Quan explained, "Its effect is to absorb external ice-attribute battle qi and automatically convert it into a defensive force. This force can help the user temporarily increase their spirit power and clear their mind, thereby protecting them from spiritual attacks."

After listening to this introduction, the dragonman youngster's first impression was that this was an alchemy item made to target Teng Dong Lang.

This was because the battle qi and battle skills that Teng Dong Lang practiced were all ice-based.

This equipment was particularly suitable for the dragonman youngster.

He could even deliberately not dodge in battle and let Teng Dong Lang hit him so that he could absorb the external ice-type battle qi and help himself increase his spirit power.

“No, to be more precise, Bu Quan imagined a battle situation like this...”

“After suffering a mental shock, I'll be temporarily unable to fight back and suffer from Teng Dong Lang's frenzied attacks.”

"At that time, the cold bag will take effect, absorbing Teng Dong Lang's ice-based battle qi, which weakens the enemy's offensive on the one hand and reduces my healing pressure on the other. Most importantly, it will also increase my spirit power. It will clear my mind and restore my combat strength."

The dragonman youngster had suffered greatly from his lack of willpower defense. Therefore, any items and treasures that could help with spirit defense were welcome to him.

The third item taken out by Bu Quan was a spell scroll.

This spell scroll exuded a gold level aura.

After Bu Quan introduced it, the dragonman youngster was secretly startled.

Because the content recorded on this spell scroll was none other than the spell—Lava Hell!

Spells were divided into tactical-class, battlefield-class, and strategic-class.

The spell Lava Hell was battlefield-class, surpassing tactical-class spells.

Once used, it could transform the surrounding area into a hellish scene of constantly erupting dragon rocks.

The dragonman youngster was puzzled: "Battlefield-class spells are very difficult to find on the market. This is because such spells cause considerable destruction. Many nations have explicitly banned the trade of such spells and enforced strict regulations."

"If I were to use such a spell in the dueling arena, would that be permissible? Would it violate your nation's laws?"

“As far as I know, the Ice Sculpture Kingdom has strict regulations and imposes a strict ban on such spells.”

Bu Quan shook her head with a smile and explained, “Don't worry about that, Commander Long Fu.”

“Although this is a Lava Hell spell, it has been deliberately weakened.”

“The spell's range has been greatly reduced, now only about a third of the original version.”

“Using this spell scroll during the heroic duel is completely fine.”

“I believe that when the entire Snow Bird Dueling Arena is turned into a lava hell, it will greatly weaken Teng Dong Lang.”

“Not only that, but the scorching battlefield environment will surely be more suitable for you, Commander Long Fu.”

The dragonman youngster nodded and gracefully admitted this point.

He carried the bloodline of the Flame Dragon King, a secret that could not be deduced from his appearance.

However, people could infer from the dragonman youngster's bright red dragon scales, burly physique, amber dragon eyes, and other characteristics that his bloodline was inclined towards fire.

After giving the dragonman youngster three treasures, Bu Quan exchanged a few words with him and then took her leave.

Her quick and decisive behavior made the dragonman youngster like her even more.

The dragonman youngster accompanied her all the way to the pier.

At the moment of parting, Bu Quan blessed the dragonman youngster and expressed her hope that he would defeat Teng Dong Lang.

The dragonman youngster said that he would deeply remember Bu Quan's help and looked forward to deeper cooperation between the two sides in the future.

After returning to the ship, the dragonman youngster continued to gaze at the carriage Bu Quan had left in.

“Compared to these three treasures, I would rather have you, Bu Quan,” the dragonman youngster sighed secretly.

He did not reveal his intention because he also understood that this was not the right time to recruit Bu Quan.

He also understood that this was not the right time to recruit Bu Quan.

The current progress was already very good.

Having a common enemy allowed two strangers meeting for the first time to get off to a very good start.

Shortly after Bu Quan left, another visitor arrived at the pier.

“Bing Xiao?” The dragonman youngster heard his subordinate's report and found it hard to believe at first.

Bing Xiao was the former head of the Bayonet Gang.

How could he come at a time like this?

This was completely unexpected by the dragonman youngster.

"Commander, are you going to see him? Or should we just kick him out?" asked the subordinate.

The dragonman youngster quickly thought about it and said, “Let him wait outside first.”

The dragonman youngster turned and went down into the cabin to meet with the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord again.

He briefly told the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord about the three items that Bu Quan had sent and then mentioned Bing Xiao.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord was secretly satisfied with the dragonman youngster's behavior.

The dragonman youngster could have met with Bing Xiao first and then told him the results.

But he didn't do that.

Instead, before acting, he came down specifically to communicate with the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord and ask for his opinion.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord knew that Commander Long Fu was a very opinionated person, and the main purpose of doing so was to express his sincerity of cooperation to him!

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord said, “I do understand the personalities of Bing Xiao and Teng Dong Lang very well.”

“He must have discussed this with Teng Dong Lang beforehand before coming here this time.”

“The main purpose is to show goodwill and weakness, to paralyze you, to try their best to undermine your fighting spirit, so as to buy time for themselves.”

The dragonman youngster shook his head, his eyes determined: “No matter what price they pay, the city Lord and I have already reached an alliance, and we will never waver.”

But the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord shook his head: “No, what I mean is, Commander Long Fu, please try your best to be tactful and play along with their plan.”

“With the condition of not signing any contract with Bing Xiao, try your best to soften your attitude and lull them into a false sense of security.”

“That way, my subsequent actions will have a higher success rate!”

“I understand.” The dragonman youngster agreed, frowning.

Because for him, this was a difficult challenge.

It was hard for him to control the situation.

After temporarily bidding farewell to the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord, the dragonman youngster returned to the captain's cabin.

Here, he received Bing Xiao.

When Bing Xiao saw the dragonman youngster, he first bowed deeply and said in a sincere tone, “Commander Long Fu, thank you for forgiving me and giving me this opportunity to meet with you.”

“I am here to apologize to you.”

“Regarding the conflict over the giant-type intelligence potion, I was arrogant and did not recognize your strength.”

“Ah!” Bing Xiao sighed deeply, his face filled with regret, "If I had known that the minor conflict from back then would escalate to this extent, I would never have acted that way.”

“Giving up a portion of the giant-type intelligence potion to gain a friend like you, Commander Long Fu, is truly a worthwhile trade.”

"I was blind!"

When the dragonman youngster heard his apology, he did not feel any relief but instead felt a strong chill.

Bing Xiao's attitude was definitely not a sincere apology, but rather an attempt to lull the dragonman youngster into a false sense of security.

He was a dignified former gang leader, yet he did something so humiliating without any regard for his own reputation.

He was truly a remarkable person that was able to put down his pride.

“The lower he can stoop today, the stronger his revenge against me will be when he has the chance in the future.”

“This kind of person is very dangerous!”

The dragonman youngster understood this clearly, and said in a low voice, full of emotion, “Lord Bing Xiao, I never thought it would come to this.”

“I admit that the conflict with you was the natural result of me going with the flow.”

“When I found out your identity, I regretted it.”

"But there was no way out, so we had to go along with it. Although I took the initiative to provoke a heroic duel, my original plan was to impress Teng Dong Lang, show my strength, and promote peace through war."

"I never expected the Bayonet Gang to be so domineering. After I defeated Xiong Ju, not only did you use your connections to make things difficult for us, but you also sent a gold level assassin to steal and assassinate, causing us heavy losses..."

Bing Xiao raised his hand and hurriedly said, "Commander Long Fu, please forgive me for interrupting you. Our side definitely did not send any assassin to infiltrate and assassinate."

“The gold level battle qi cultivator that Snow Bird Harbor City Lord killed was definitely not one of our people.”

“This is a huge misunderstanding!”

“The main purpose of my visit this time is to clarify this matter!”

The dragonman youngster's face darkened, and he snorted coldly, “Since you've done it, you should face up to it.”

“No, no, no, we definitely didn't do that.” Bing Xiao waved his hands repeatedly.

The dragonman youngster sneered, "I understand. Your explanation is not for me, but for the people of Snow Bird Harbor."

“If I were you, even if the assassination failed, I would never admit that I was the mastermind behind it.”

Bing Xiao sighed deeply, “Commander Long Fu, you have too many prejudices against our gang.”

“Our gang has never had any intention of assassinating your gang members, let alone taking action.”

“The second purpose of my visit is to make up with your mercenary corps.”

“The compensation on this list is a token of our sincerity. Please take a look, Commander.”

With that, Bing Xiao handed the dragonman youngster a piece of paper.

The dragonman youngster took it and looked at it, his breath pausing slightly.

In order to paralyze the dragonman youngster, the Bayonet Gang had come up with a list of apologies worth a fortune!

There were eight gold level items on this list alone.

In addition, some fixed assets, such as shops in excellent locations, were to be compensated to the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps free of charge.

There were even two alchemy workshops among these fixed assets!

Chapter 541: Giant Nurturing Insights

Bing Xiao was old and cunning. Seeing the slight change in the dragonman youngster's expression, he took the opportunity to hand him another scroll.

It was a contract scroll.

“As long as you, Commander Long Fu, sign this contract, then all the assets on the list will be yours!”

The dragonman youngster put down the asset list in his hand, and his expression returned to normal.

He did not take the contract scroll but looked at Bing Xiao: “Let me guess—the contract’s terms aren’t about making me abandon the heroic duel, are they?”

“That’s not the case,” Bing Xiao smiled, "We have fully considered your circumstances, Commander Long Fu.”

“The heroic duel was something you initiated voluntarily.”

“If, under the current circumstances, you suddenly take the initiative to give up your challenge to our gang leader, Teng Dong Lang, how will the outside world view you?”

“You will be misunderstood as cowardly, weak, and a loser who dares to talk but not act.”

“This will cause too much damage to your reputation!”

“I came here to sincerely seek cooperation with your group, so of course I won't do that.”

The dragonman youngster sneered, “If I accuse the Bayonet Gang of crimes and then suddenly announce that I am giving up the heroic duel, I'm afraid the outside world will guess that you threatened me.”

Bing Xiao hurriedly waved his hands and denied, “I have no intention of threatening you, Commander, during this visit!”

He continued, "We only need you to publicly clarify that the previous accusation was a misunderstanding. Our gang has never done anything to desecrate the heroic duel."

The dragonman youngster smiled and leaned back in his chair: “Are there any other requests?”

Bing Xiao hesitated for a moment before saying, “If you are willing to lose to our gang leader in the heroic duel, then I personally will give you a generous reward!”

The dragonman youngster looked strange: "Bribing your opponent to stage a fake fight—is that not a desecration of the honorable duel?"

Bing Xiao immediately replied, "Of course not! This is a mutually beneficial arrangement between both parties—how could it be considered a desecration?"

The Bayonet Gang's demand hit the nail on the head.

Without this accusation, the Bayonet Gang would be out of danger, with no longer any threat to the gang's survival.

Even if Teng Dong Lang was careless and lost to the dragonman youngster, it would not cause serious damage to the Bayonet Gang.

But how could the dragonman youngster agree to that?

This was the opportunity that the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord had been waiting for many years!

He had already made a contract with the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord, and based on the dragonman youngster's beliefs and values, he would not conspire with the Bayonet Gang.

However, the dragonman youngster remembered the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's request to let him play along and paralyze the enemy as much as possible.

So, after thinking about it for a while, the dragonman youngster said carefully, “You want me to explain that this is a misunderstanding?”

“That request sounds easy, but it's actually very unreasonable!”

“You did interfere with the heroic duel and used the devil's eye outside the arena.”

Seeing that Bing Xiao wanted to speak, the dragonman youngster immediately waved his hand: “Don't deny it, because if you do, I definitely won't believe you.”

“If you do something wrong, you have to be punished!”

“The assets on the list alone are not enough, far from enough to compensate for the damage to my reputation.”

Hearing this, Bing Xiao suddenly felt a flash of hope.

Before he came, he had discussed it with Teng Dong Lang and felt that the dragonman youngster did not have any relevant evidence.

If there was sufficient evidence, the dragonman youngster would have brought it out immediately to nail down the blasphemy of the Bayonet Gang, which would be in the best interests of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

Therefore, it was highly likely that the dragonman youngster had no evidence.

But in case he really did have it, considering that he did not bring it out and announce it directly, Bing Xiao naturally wondered: Was the dragonman youngster worried about the Bayonet Gang's desperate retaliation, so he deliberately left room for reconciliation in the end?

After all, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was far weaker than the Bayonet Gang.

Or there was another possibility.

The dragonman youngster was holding onto this evidence, waiting for the Bayonet Gang to negotiate, so that he could get what he wanted from the negotiating table.

Now, the dragonman youngster's answer confirmed Bing Xiao's previous guess.

There was hope!

Bing Xiao smiled and said, “Commander Long Fu, whatever you want, you can ask for it, and we can discuss it.”

These words sounded familiar to the dragonman youngster.

Not long ago, the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord had said something similar to him.

So, the dragonman youngster made an exorbitant demand, asking for a price so high that it made Bing Xiao's face turn solemn.

Bing Xiao's eyes twitched, and he took a deep breath before managing to compose himself: "Commander Long Fu, your demand is rather unrealistic. I doubt that all of our Bayonet Gang's assets would be enough to meet your asking price!"

This time, it was the dragonman youngster's turn to smile: “Didn't you just say that we could discuss it?”

Bing Xiao was taken aback for a moment, and the look in his eyes changed slightly as he looked at the dragonman youngster.

I didn't expect you, with your honest-looking thick eyebrows and big eyes, to be such a cunning businessman!

"Come, have some coffee." The dragonman youngster took the initiative to make coffee for Bing Xiao.

"Do you have tea? I'm getting old, so I prefer to drink that."

“Of course.”

The dragonman youngster and Bing Xiao then engaged in a difficult negotiation.

The best outcome of Bing Xiao's trip would be to get the dragonman youngster to sign a contract and publicly state that the previous accusation was a misunderstanding.

Therefore, Bing Xiao was prepared to make a big sacrifice.

He already had a plan.

“Even if Long Fu demands a high price, I can agree to it for the time being.”

“As long as he signs the contract, we can stabilize him and have him clarify that his previous accusation was a misunderstanding, and the Bayonet Gang will be safe.”

"After that, how we deal with him and the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps will depend on our mood, right? "

“Even if these assets fall into his hands, there will always be countless ways to get them back.”

"But we have to be careful with the gold coins, potions, equipment, and so on that can be taken away. I have to minimize the scale of such transactions to prevent the difficulty of recovering the losses in the future."

Although Bing Xiao's plan was very good, the dragonman youngster didn't want to reach an agreement with him!

The dragonman youngster deliberately made things difficult, even though Bing Xiao repeatedly said that the Bayonet Gang really didn't have that many assets. He spoke with sincerity, almost to the point of tears.

The dragonman youngster relented a little, but only very slightly.

Bing Xiao gradually felt despair at the deadlock in the negotiations, which were difficult to move forward.

Suddenly, he had a flash of inspiration: “Commander Long Fu, I have something that you will definitely be interested in.”

"Oh? Tell me about it," said the dragonman youngster.

Bing Xiao's smile was a little forced, and his face showed a look of pain: “As everyone knows, I have experience in training giant slaves.”

“When I was in the Bayonet Gang, I trained a gold level giant slave.”

“After I retired, I had more time to study and refine my skills, and I summarized a set of unique training techniques.”

“With these techniques, I am about to train my second gold level giant slave.”

“Commander Long Fu, since you are pursuing giant-type intelligence potions, and your group also has a giant... this knowledge will definitely be of great help to you!”

“Commander Long Fu, since you are pursuing giant-type intelligence potions, and your group also has a giant... this knowledge will definitely be of great help to you!”

The dragonman youngster instantly thought of the big guy.

He was moved.

Although the big guy's bloodline had not been tested for the final results, it was at least legendary.

This represented his bright future!

But to grow up, a lot of investment was needed.

If there was a unique insight into cultivating giants, it would allow the dragonman youngster to have a higher return on his subsequent investment in the big guy.

Not to mention anything else, just the time saved was enough to tempt the dragonman youngster.

The survivors' time was very tight.

What they needed was to continuously and rapidly strengthen their own power, communicate with the upper echelons of the empire, and successfully atone for their sins.

The future of the survivors was also uncertain.

As their leader, the dragonman youngster had to consider the consequences of failing to atone for their sins.

That was why he had risked a minor conflict with Bing Xiao in order to obtain the giant-type intelligence potion.

As for the conflict escalating to its current state, it was not a normal development and was beyond the dragonman youngster's expectations.

If the dragonman youngster had known at the time that such a small conflict would lead to a life-and-death struggle between the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps and the Bayonet Gang, he would not have done it.

Even if he knew about the help of the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord.

It was an unnecessary risk.

It had nothing to do with their main purpose.

But since it had already happened, the dragonman youngster naturally had to go with the flow.

Time would not give him a chance to regret it!

Similarly, since the giant cultivation insight had been shown to the dragonman youngster by Bing Xiao in such a strange way, he naturally had to get his hands on it.

So, the dragonman youngster said bluntly, “Then tell me, how can I cultivate a gold level giant slave?”

“Commander Long Fu, I can't go into detail about that,” Bing Xiao smiled.

He mentioned a few minor points that were relatively unimportant.

But even these points were very useful to the dragonman youngster, who eagerly memorized them.

Seeing that the dragonman youngster was interested, Bing Xiao took advantage of the moment and tempted him, “Commander, I heard that your group has a giant member who seems to be of mixed race.”

“You can use my unique knowledge and insight.”

“The bloodline of giants is very excellent, comparable to that of dragons in terms of bloodline grade.”

"The prerequisite for cultivating a gold level giant is to find a giant with gold level talent. This is much easier than finding a snow elf or human with gold level talent."

“The most important point is, once we have cultivated a gold level giant, how do we control him

“The bloodline of giants is excellent. To make a gold level giant obey us, to keep the slave contract in effect, and to make him listen to our every word, it requires our spirit power to be much higher than that of the giant slave.”

"The core of my insights is to skillfully suppress the spirit of the giant slave so that we can firmly control him. Even if we are only at the silver level!"

Chapter 542: This Dragonman is Very Greedy

The dragonman youngster and Bing Xiao tugged at each other for a long time before the latter finally took his leave.

The dragonman youngster remained in the captain's cabin, looking at a piece of paper in his hand.

It was a thick piece of parchment from Bing Xiao.

The dragonman youngster continuously infused it with battle qi, and the text on the surface of the paper continuously evolved, as if a book had been compressed into a piece of paper.

This was the alchemy technique of the endless paper.

The content on the paper was Bing Xiao's insights on cultivating giant slaves.

Of course, it was a simplified version.

The most important core content was missing—how to control higher level giant slaves as a lower level life form.

The dragonman youngster read it intently.

Although the core was missing, he didn't actually need it.

He had never thought of using a slave contract to control the big guy.

They were companions, comrades-in-arms who had experienced life and death together, not tools.

Bing Xiao was not easy to deal with.

A straightforward refusal was not in line with the strategies of the dragonman youngster and the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord.

This time, in order to paralyze the enemy and ease the situation, the dragonman youngster still signed a contract with Bing Xiao.

However, it was not the magic contract that Bing Xiao carried with him, but a temporary draft.

The dragonman youngster would not change his mind and say that the previous accusation was a pure misunderstanding.

This was the core reason for the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord to launch an offensive against the Bayonet Gang, and it was the best opportunity to strike the Bayonet Gang.

According to the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord, he had been waiting for this opportunity for many years!

But the dragonman youngster promised Bing Xiao that the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps would not take any further action against the Bayonet Gang unless the Bayonet Gang attacked first and violated the agreement.

This promise was beneficial to the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

It was to prevent the Bayonet Gang from sensing something was wrong and becoming evil and daring, carrying out a second assassination attempt on the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, or even more despicable, insidious, and vicious ambushes!

The final agreement between the two sides was that the dragonman youngster said he would sign it after the end of his heroic duel with Teng Dong Lang.

He made it clear to Bing Xiao that this heroic duel was his initiative, and he needed to fulfill it. He also needed to personally gauge the strength of the leader of the Bayonet Gang!

When he said this, the dragonman youngster stared intently at Bing Xiao, his eyes burning like flames.

Under such a gaze, Bing Xiao felt the dragonman youngster's fighting spirit.

When Bing Xiao left the captain's cabin, the expression on his face was somewhat complicated.

“Commander Long Fu really wants to have a duel with Teng Dong Lang!”

“Judging from his previous performance, I'm afraid he really has a way to deal with gold level opponents.”

“Otherwise, with his silver level, he wouldn't be so confident as to challenge a gold level opponent to a duel.”

“He is the commander of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, and his every move is not just his personal will.”

Thinking of this, a look of concern flashed across Bing Xiao's face.

“What is his trump card?”

“What makes him confident enough to challenge someone above his level?”

"Teng Dong Lang's previous caution was right. Unfortunately, his arrangements didn't seem to work. Even Xiong Ju couldn't force him to reveal his trump card!"

“The other three ringleaders can't do it either.”

“Given the current situation, perhaps Long Fu's trump card can't be revealed lightly.”

“Once the opponent is on guard, the threat will plummet!”

“The primary objective of this operation has not been achieved.”

A fleeting glimpse of coldness flashed in Bing Xiao's eyes.

“Long Fu is holding out for a better offer.”

“I presented such a tempting list, but he didn't buy it!”

“He wants more.”

“This dragonman is greedy, very greedy.”

“He wants to use his trump card to defeat Teng Dong Lang and embarrass the leader of the Bayonet Gang in front of everyone.”

“This will put him in a more advantageous position in the upcoming negotiations.”

“He wants to take more from us!”

"Hmph, this dragonman is really bold and reckless. Teng Dong Lang should indeed deal with this kind of thing and restore the reputation of the Bayonet Gang!"

“Let the whole of Snow Bird Harbor know what kind of existence the Bayonet Gang is.”

Bing Xiao was not satisfied with the results of his trip, but it wasn't as if he had nothing to show for it.

At least, after negotiating with Long Fu, he had softened the other party's attitude and greatly reduced the intensity of the conflict.

Next, it was time to watch the heroic duel between Teng Dong Lang and Long Fu.

“The Bayonet Gang needs a decisive victory with a huge advantage!”

“Long Fu needs to be taught a harsh lesson, preferably by losing his life in the arena under the gaze of everyone.”

“Even if his trump card works and Teng Dong Lang is humiliated, we can still compensate the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps after the duel.”

“It doesn't matter!”

“They are just outsiders. If they want to settle in Snow Bird Harbor in the future, we will have plenty of opportunities and time to deal with them.”

“There are many ways to do this.”

“We'll make them spit out the meat they've swallowed, though this alone won't be enough.”

“If we don't make them pay back tenfold, how can we live up to the reputation of the Bayonet Gang?”

As the saying went, a true man could lower his head when necessary. Bing Xiao understood this well.

We'll see how it goes!

The dragonman youngster returned to the cabin with the endless paper and met with the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord.

He concisely described his negotiations with Bing Xiao and the preliminary results.

Upon hearing about the magic contract between the dragonman youngster and Bing Xiao, he shook his head slightly, unconcerned: “This contract is not a problem.”

“According to our previous plan, Long Fu, you only need to accuse the Bayonet Gang of desecrating the heroic duel and fulfill the heroic duel with Teng Dong Lang.”

“I will take care of the Bayonet Gang.”

“You are just a battle banner. Wave the battle banner to attract the main attention of the Bayonet Gang. You don't need to follow up with a charge into battle.”

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord said this and gave the dragonman youngster a meaningful look.

He knew that the dragonman youngster had deliberately made this contract with Bing Xiao in order to counterbalance him, the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord.

In the event that it came to a full-scale battle, if the city Lord wanted to force the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps to help out on the grounds that the situation required it, the dragonman youngster could use this magic contract to block him.

The dragonman youngster's move once again made the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord feel the dragonman's tactics. The latter was definitely not a partner who could be manipulated at will.

The dragonman youngster noticed the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's gaze and smiled slightly: “Right, this is the giant training insight from Bing Xiao.”

The dragonman youngster said, and then directly handed over the parchment.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord was slightly taken aback, but then smiled: “To be honest, I am indeed very interested.”

He took it and quickly glanced through it, then pondered, “There is some content, but the most core secrets are not there.”

“The most powerful aspect of Bing Xiao's training insight lies in the fact that this method can be used to make weak slaves strong.”

“The gold level giant slaves he trained always obeyed his every command, even if it meant dying on the battlefield, they were willing to do so.”

The dragonman youngster looked slightly stern. After hearing this, he realized that he had underestimated Bing Xiao's cultivation insight.

Sacrificial fighters were always troublesome.

Gold level sacrificial fighters were very dangerous.

This was because those who reached the gold level were basically city Lords. Even if they were not, their social status was not low.

But Bing Xiao was able to get a city Lord-equivalent expert to fight to the death for him!

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord said with emotion, “Gold level giant slaves can really play a role on large battlefields.”

“Unfortunately, they ended up in the hands of a local gang like the Bayonet Gang.”

“The most common battle scenes are street fights in alleys, which are mere skirmishes. For giant slaves, it's like being tied up.”

“Bing Xiao cannot truly unleash the value of giant slaves!”

“To be honest with you, I've always been interested in Bing Xiao's method and have been secretly using means to probe into the specifics.”

“The things you have in your hands are indeed substantial, but I've already collected them.”

"Much of the content is actually available in major factions, libraries, or academies. As long as you're willing to pay the price in time, money, and so on, you can collect it."

"The information I have collected is even more comprehensive than yours. When I return, I will have someone send it to you."

“Like yours, we are missing the most core secrets!”

The dragonman youngster thought to himself, “Actually, I don't need it. This information alone is enough for me to train the big guy.”

Of course, he did not say what he was thinking.

In fact, the dragonman youngster changed his mind and thought that if he could really collect such secrets, it wouldn't be bad.

What if it came in handy in the future?

Seeing the reaction of the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord, the dragonman youngster knew that he had done the right thing by taking the initiative to share the insights this time.

Since the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord wanted to deal with the Bayonet Gang, it was impossible for him not to consider Bing Xiao and his gold level giant slave.

Before leaving, the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord patted the dragonman youngster on the shoulder to show his closeness.

He was satisfied with the dragonman youngster's sharing.

This showed that the dragonman youngster did not want to hide anything from him and was happy to give back to his allies.

The dragonman youngster had previously made the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord express his sincerity in cooperation in an almost calculating manner, so the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord was naturally uncomfortable in his heart.

But now, after cooperating, the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord felt much better.

Because all signs indicated that although the dragonman youngster was difficult to deal with, he was quite reliable as an ally.

“You've gained a lot of good things today.”

“Familiarize yourself with this equipment as soon as possible, you don't have much time left.”

“Also, hurry up and heal your injuries.”

“The sooner you engage in a heroic duel with Teng Dong Lang, the less likely we are to be exposed.”

The dragonman youngster nodded.

He didn't have any internal injuries, so he didn't feel any psychological pressure in this regard.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord quietly left.

He didn't want to expose his whereabouts and let the Bayonet Gang guess his true relationship with the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

The dragonman youngster began to inspect all the equipment he had obtained today.

These alchemy treasures were very valuable, but since they came from outside, he could only feel a little more at ease using them after testing them.

After Zi Di finished her inspection, she said with emotion, “Compared to the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord, Bu Quan is much more generous by giving them away directly.”

She then asked the dragonman youngster, “When will the alchemists we want arrive?”

The dragonman youngster shook his head slightly and said, “I asked, but the city Lord said that it would take time to make arrangements. There should be two alchemists.”

Zi Di said, "I understand. We will only get these agreed-upon rewards after we complete the duel."

Chapter 543: Greed God’s Bone

On the calm sea surface, a faint gray-white glow suddenly flickered.

Massive magic fluctuations intensified the waves, causing the surrounding seawater to surge into towering waves.

A dense negative energy rapidly expanded outward in all directions.

Amidst the surging waves, the outline of a ship gradually emerged and solidified into a tangible form within mere seconds.

This was no ordinary vessel.

From its appearance alone, it was hard to believe such a dilapidated ship could still sail.

The ship's sails were covered with holes of various sizes, and the planks were like rotten wood that had been decaying for many years, covered with moss, shells, and other debris.

The ship was filled with a gloomy atmosphere, and from time to time, ghosts would float out and then dive back into the cabin.

This was a ghost ship, a gold level ghost ship.

The cabin door opened, and several necromancers filed out and walked onto the deck.

They were Jian Gui, Cang Xu, and the others.

“The Main world, we're finally back!” The little skeleton Xiao Wang Zi with a big head and small frame raised his arms and shouted.

The zombie ronin Ku Feng looked at the blue sky and sea and exhaled a cloud of smoke: “Now, we are finally safe.”

After Doctor Sui Shi left, they had originally planned to leave the Ghost World as soon as possible and return to the main world.

But unexpectedly, they encountered trouble on their way back.

The main channel of the Underworld River was teeming with powerful undead beings.

Jian Gui and the others struggled desperately and narrowly escaped with their lives from a life-threatening crisis.

Even now, thinking back on it, everyone still felt a chill run down their spines.

“Even a gold level ghost ship is not safe sailing on the Underworld River.” Cang Xu was deeply impressed by his first trip to the Ghost World.

He was no longer just a head.

He had a body again.

This silver level zombie body was a gift from Jian Gui.

After Cang Xu awoke, the first thing he did was remove the zombie's head and replace it with his own.

Jian Gui was the leader of these people. Upon returning to the main world, he immediately detected his location and the time in the main world.

After hearing Cang Xu's reflections, he explained while casting spells: “This is why I don't often use the Ghost World Transversal spell.”

“Before, there was no choice but to risk fleeing to the Ghost World.”

“Traveling the Underworld River is extremely dangerous; one must at least reach the saint level to truly qualify for exploration.”

At this point, he paused.

“Alright.”

"Our current coordinates have been confirmed. The good news is we're less than five nautical miles from the nearest safe house."

“The bad news is: eight days have passed in the main world since we last used the Ghost World Transversal.”

“Eight days?” Cang Xu narrowed his eyes slightly.

In his perception, it had only been three days.

The flow of time in the Ghost World was different from that in the main world.

Furthermore, when traveling between spatial planes, it was possible to encounter time disturbances, causing a portion of time to be lost for no apparent reason.

As a magician, Cang Xu was no stranger to this kind of knowledge, he just didn't have any relevant experience.

“Eight days have passed, I wonder how the mercenary group is doing.”

“They should have reached the Ice Sculpture Kingdom by now. I wonder if they've entered the capital city.”

Cang Xu silently thought about his companions.

His primary objective on this journey was to approach Jian Gui, gain his trust, and obtain higher level knowledge.

After experiencing battles in the Underworld River voyage, he had made some progress but had lost his body.

Only his head remained intact.

After a brief rest, the necromancers boarded the ghost ship and set sail for their next destination.

Necromancers usually set up multiple safe houses.

Their situation around the world was dire, as they were being hunted down by almost all forces.

Without two or three safe houses as a fallback, the necromancers would not feel at ease.

Jian Gui's safe house was established on an uninhabited small island.

According to his rough description: the island was very low-lying. During the evening high tide, it would be completely submerged. Only between midnight and 3 PM would the island be exposed.

However, as they neared their destination, an unexpected incident occurred.

A scene of relocation came into view through their magic surveillance.

“Lord Jian Gui, is this the safe house you mentioned?” Xiao Wang Zi asked in an odd tone.

Jian Gui was also at a loss for words.

At that moment, three pirate ships were already anchored on the island.

A group of pirates were loading items onto the ships.

It was clear that the supplies Jian Gui had hidden in the safe house had been discovered by the pirates for some reason.

Now the pirates were loading the supplies onto the ships.

Ku Feng and the others looked at Jian Gui, silently asking him what to do next.

Based on the current reconnaissance results, this group of pirates was weak, not even reaching the silver level, with only three iron level pirates.

But Jian Gui was in no hurry to take action.

He first used magic to raise a thick fog to hide the entire ghost ship.

Then he asked, “They shouldn't have discovered us yet. Do you know which pirate group this is?”

Jian Gui had a certain understanding and grasp of the pirate groups operating in the surrounding area.

But the origin of the group in front of him was beyond the scope of his intelligence.

The flags of the three pirate ships were all the same.

On the white background, a large number of black lines intertwined, weaving a pattern of crossed swords.

This was not the flag of any famous pirate group.

The necromancers shook their heads, indicating that they did not know.

Qing Xin pondered, "Once the Pirate Throne appeared, every day a large number of sailors became pirates. Even inlanders who aren't sailors are willing to sell everything they own to buy ships and set sail as pirates. These people are probably of that kind."

Qing Xin did not participate in the battle of the Underworld River.

After the battle, Qing Xin awoke from her coma and learned of everything that had happened.

As the only living person on the ghost ship, her situation was awkward, and she had been marginalized.

Speaking up now would increase her sense of presence.

Xiao Wang Zi said, "That's probably how it is. Let's act quickly and eliminate these pirates! They dare to take our supplies!"

He was already active along several merchant ship routes, focusing on protecting his country's ships and severely cracking down on pirates.

He had no sympathy for pirates.

Jian Gui nodded slightly: “Then let's do it together, and make sure not to let a single one get away.”

Cang Xu added: "First, let's sneak up on them and destroy their communication devices as much as possible. Try to keep a few alive to gather information. At the very least, we need to know how they were able to dig up the safe house."

Jian Gui looked at Cang Xu with appreciation and agreed: “That's right.”

Fog began to gather around the pirates.

“It's getting cold!” one pirate sneezed.

More people looked up at the sky, noticing the sunlight had grown much weaker, with mist rising constantly. They cursed, “This damn weather—could a storm be coming?”

The three captains, stationed on their respective pirate ships, sensed something was amiss and urged their men to hurry.

What they didn't know was that Ku Feng and Cang Xu had quietly broken through the bottom of the ship and sneaked into the cabin.

The ghosts controlled by Jian Gui took the lead in completing the reconnaissance.

Ku Feng and Cang Xu easily destroyed the ship's long-distance communication equipment according to the intelligence.

“Attack!” Seeing that everything was ready, Jian Gui immediately ordered the general attack.

Xiao Wang Zi was already prepared and cast a spell.

Hundreds of white bone spears appeared out of thin air, descending from midair and covering the densest area of pirates.

In an instant, the pirates were killed in large numbers by the bone spears.

“Enemy attack!”

“No, my first mate...”

"What is this thing?

"Damn it, they're necromancers!"

The pirates were caught off guard by the surprise attack and immediately fell into chaos.

Ku Feng swung a sharp slash from the lower deck, striking the pirate captain standing above in a single blow.

Cang Xu charged at the gunners.

His hands transformed into claws, powerful and heavy, each strike effortlessly sending pirates flying into the air.

The pirate gunners were all broken-boned and died on the spot.

Seeing the dire situation, a pirate captain hurriedly ordered, "Sail away, sail away! Escape from here quickly."

The next moment, several wind blades flew toward them, precisely cutting down the pirate ship's sails.

“No!” The pirate captain fell into despair.

Ku Feng jumped over from the ship next to him, stabbed several times into the pirates, and then killed the captain with one stab, showing his fierce fighter spirit.

The necromancers' surprise attack was successful, and they immediately took control of the battlefield.

The pirate captain was quickly killed, and the first mate and others also perished, unable to organize any decent resistance.

Soon after, the battle was over.

Only a few pirates remained, and they were deliberately left alive by the necromancers.

It was not difficult to interrogate the pirate captives.

These people were completely terrified.

They revealed their identities—they were members of the Rope Pirates.

As Qing Xin had guessed, the Rope Pirates were a pirate gang that had recently been formed.

Jian Gui was more concerned about why this pirate gang was able to find his safe house.

“It was the Greed God's Bone,” Jian Gui sighed.

These were the remains of the fourth generation pirate king, scattered all over the world.

As long as intelligent beings obtained these bone fragments, the greed divinity inside would take effect. These bone fragments will point to the location of the deepest desires within the user's heart.

The deputy leader of the Rope Pirates possesses a fragment of the Greed God's Bone. When passing through this area, the bone fragment reacted, leading her to discover this safe house.

The Rope Pirates' leader and deputy leader collaborated to breach the safe house's defenses, entered it, and took some supplies.

They were in a hurry to do other things, so they left three ships behind and ordered them to move all the supplies here, while they set off with the main force.

"Magician Huo Sheng and Archer Chuan Yun. The two of them joined forces to form the Rope Pirates." After learning the name of the leader, Jian Gui and Xiao Wang Zi both remembered.

They were two famous bounty hunters.

Previously, they made a living by accepting missions and hunting down criminals.

But recently, the two of them joined forces, gave up their former lives, and became pirates.

“The sea is becoming more and more chaotic,” Jian Gui sighed, "Hurry up and clean up the battlefield, we have to leave as soon as possible. It's no longer safe here."

Chapter 544: Head Creation Spell

The fog did not dissipate with the end of the battle but instead grew thicker and thicker.

A powerful magic fluctuation suddenly erupted from the ghost ship.

The fluctuation swept across the battlefield, quickly covering every corner and causing the fog to roll and churn.

The spell fluctuation came quickly and disappeared just as quickly.
