A few seconds later, the thick fog returned to calm.

From the large number of pirate corpses, ghosts began to emerge one after another.

A gold level spell—Intermediate Soul Summoning Spell!

Cang Xu, Xiao Wang Zi, and the others did not intervene, but floated above the battlefield, quietly watching the ghost ship cast the spell.

This was the ghost ship casting Intermediate Soul Summoning Spell.

More accurately, it was Jian Gui casting the spell.

When Jian Gui turned into a complete ghost form and merged with the ghost ship, any spell he cast would be twice as powerful!

“In a sense, this ghost ship is Jian Gui's spellcasting tool,” Cang Xu thought to himself.

Over the past few days, he had been traveling on the ghost ship along the Underworld River and had gained a deep understanding of this unique vessel.

Strictly speaking, the ghost ship was not like the Deep Sea Monster Fish, Iron Lump, or White Bandage ships.

The latter were magic ships. They were crafted using alchemy technology. There were various alchemy components on the ships that made the magic ships powerful and versatile.

Ghost ships were similar to undead creatures such as ghosts, zombies, and skeletons. It was just that its life form looked like a ship from the outside.

Cang Xu once asked Jian Gui how to build a ghost ship.

Jian Gui replied that it was a higher level soul aspect spell that was not taught to outsiders. Only after passing a deeper test and signing a contract could it be taught to others.

Jian Gui also gave a rough introduction to this necromancy spell.

Cang Xu thus learned that to cast this spell, the caster had to be at least gold level. Although the spell had a high success rate, the materials had to be carefully selected—it had to be the wreckage of a ship with spirituality.

The ghost ship that Jian Gui possessed was not made by him.

It was looted from the Underworld River by his teacher while exploring it.

The Underworld River was the main road of the Ghost World, possessing the strongest negative energy, and countless undead creatures of all kinds lived there.

His teacher had given this ghost ship to Jian Gui.

Therefore, when Jian Gui used the ghost ship to cast the world travelling spell, he returned to the Underworld River.

This was because the ghost ship came from the Underworld River and was closely related to it.

Under the effect of intermediate Soul Summoning Spell, a large number of ghosts floated in midair, enveloped in thick fog.

Although Xiao Wang Zi was a magician of the bone aspect, he still knew a lot about the soul aspect.

When he saw this scene, he couldn't help but praise it.

“Basically every corpse has summoned a ghost.”

"The success rate is close to 100%. As expected of Lord Jian Gui!"

Cang Xu added, “Not only is the success rate extremely high, but look at the spirits that have been converted. Their quality is very high, basically the same as the life level of the corpses when they were alive.”

The effect of necromancy was to use the souls in the corpses as material to create ghosts.

If Cang Xu were to cast the spell, there would never be such a high success rate and efficiency.

“Such an intermediate Soul Summoning Spell is already a battlefield-class spell.”

“The combination of Jian Gui and the ghost ship is very powerful. This spell alone can overturn the outcome of a battle.”

At the same time, Cang Xu was secretly paying attention to the thick fog around him.

This was the spell—Soul-Binding Mist.

The effect of this spell was to temporarily detain and conceal the souls of living beings, preventing them from being detected and taken by the deities they worshipped in life, nor being drawn into the abyss or hell. Of course, they would not dissipate into thin air either.

It was precisely because of this dense fog that the souls within these corpses were largely preserved.

Otherwise, when Jian Gui cast his Soul Summoning Spell, at least half of the souls in the pirates' corpses would have disappeared.

Jian Gui reappeared on the deck.

He divided the ghosts into several groups, throwing most of them into the ghost ship and assigning the rest to the necromancers present.

The standard for distribution was the magicians' merits in this surprise attack.

The more military merits one had, the more one would be assigned.

The humanoid ghosts let out shrill screams, their bodies quickly twisting and distorting, constantly condensing.

In just a few seconds, the essence of the humanoid ghosts was poured into the ghost ship, and the remaining soul debris turned into specks of gray light, quickly dissipating on the deck.

“This is the Soul Healing Spell,” Cang Xu realized.

He also mastered this spell, but only at a basic level. Jian Gui was clearly not using such a low level spell.

He consumed the ghosts he had just obtained to heal the ghost ship.

The ghost ship was not essentially a magic ship, but an undead life form, so it could be healed by the Soul Healing Spell.

Jian Gui quickly consumed all the ghosts he had just obtained.

“There's still a long way to go.”

"To completely restore the ghost ship to its prime, at least 1,000 ordinary humanoid ghosts are needed. Of course, if they are transcendents, the number required will be greatly reduced."

“I hope we can encounter such pirates next.”

“If that doesn't work, we'll have to catch fish in the sea, but that will increase the demand even more.”

Jian Gui took the initiative to explain the ghost ship's damage.

"The ghost ship is very important, so I think we should try our best to repair it. I am a bone aspect necromancer, and these ghosts are of little use to me. Jian Gui, I'll give these to you." Xiao Wang Zi took the initiative to contribute his ghosts.

Ku Feng said, “I'm just a ronin, I don't need these ghosts either.”

Cang Xu obtained a total of seven ghosts this time. He thought for a moment, kept three transcendent ghosts, and gave away the rest of the ordinary ghosts.

“Let's make a deal.” Although Jian Gui accepted these ghosts, he did not take them for free but gave money corresponding to the market price.

“Finally, I have money,” Cang Xu said emotionally.

He was truly penniless.

Even his current body was given to him by someone else.

Next, it was time to distribute the pirates' bodies.

Xiao Wang Zi expressed interest in their bones, but upon seeing Cang Xu, he asked, “Do you need their heads?”

Although Cang Xu’s body had been taken by Doctor Sui Shi, he wasn’t entirely without gain.

Doctor Sui Shi had directly imparted his unique Flying Head Spell to Cang Xu.

Cang Xu nodded, “I only need the heads of transcendents, at least five of them.”

Jian Gui said, “We are all curious about the Flying Head Spell, so you can take all of these heads and use them.”

Xiao Wang Zi nodded, “That's right.”

The Flying Head Spell was the original creation of Doctor Sui Shi, but its value was difficult to assess.

This was because such original creations usually required a long period of practical testing before their true value could be determined.

In history, many great magicians' original creations had a mediocre response, while some minor figures' creations had a widespread impact. There were also many unconscious innovations that had created enormous value, or by-products of innovation that had overshadowed the original.

“Okay,” Cang Xu nodded, “I do need fresh heads to try the Flying Head Spell.”

“The extra resources will have to be considered a debt for now.”

Cang Xu thanked the others.

He was also eager to see the actual effects of the Flying Head Spell.

After distributing the spoils, Cang Xu retreated into his cabin and began casting spells on the pirate heads.

The necromancy school has evolved into three branches: Bone, Soul, and Corpse.

Doctor Sui Shi was a master of the corpse aspect, and his uniquely created Flying Head Spell naturally belonged to it.

However, after studying it, Cang Xu discovered that while the Flying Head Spell was a necromancy spell, it involved a significant amount of alchemy knowledge.

It was not just a single spell but a combination of multiple spells.

The most important Head Creation Spell could transform the head of a normal living being into a special undead creature—a death head.

Death heads had the ability to float and move, so flying was one of their characteristics.

Just as Jian Gui controlled ghosts and Xiao Wang Zi manipulated skeletons, Cang Xu could use the Head Creation Spell to refine a large number of death heads for his command.

“Ghosts can be transformed into water ghosts, frozen ghosts, substitute ghosts, ghost horses, and so on.”

“Death heads can also be transformed.”

"Necromancers can even transform their own heads into death heads. In this way, the main part of their life is just a flying head, and their bodies can be replaced at will."

Cang Xu thought of himself.

His situation was: his original body was not only taken away but also refined into something else.

He was left with only his head.

“Doctor Sui Shi entrusted me with the Flying Head Spell, ostensibly as payment, but in reality, he wanted to see me practice the spell and observe the practical application of his innovation.”

“And I had no choice but to follow his wishes!”

“How infuriating...”

But Cang Xu had no practical experience, so he dared not experiment with his own head first.

He had only this head left; if the spell failed, losing his head would mean he could only transform himself into a ghost.

Without a body, Cang Xu cherished his own head all the more.

The Head Creation Spell was quickly released by him.

He was very familiar with the spell.

In recent days, he had been studying the contents of these spells and practicing them.

Under the effect of the spell, the pirate's head quickly shriveled, with almost all the moisture drained from its flesh, leaving only a small amount of blood, which had turned blue.

The skin color changed from its normal wheat-like hue to a pale white.

The pirate's head, which had been contorted in agony before death, now appeared calm and serene.

Suddenly, his eyes opened.

His pupils had turned blue, filled with icy coldness and cruelty.

Cang Xu released his grip, and the skull slowly floated into the air, moving slowly and easily becoming a target for arrows.

One by one, the skulls, after being subjected to the Head Creation Spell, became death heads, floating in the air within the ship's cabin like balloons.

They drifted slowly, occasionally colliding gently before bouncing apart.

Negative energy filled the ship's hold, creating a spine-chilling scene.

Cang Xu's face betrayed a hint of surprise.

In practice, he found that the success rate of the Head Creation Spell was very high.

As long as he cast the spell successfully, he could basically create a death head. This success rate far exceeded that of the spells that summoned souls, skeletons, or zombies.

This alone gave the Flying Head Spell a unique advantage.

“Next, I will use deep mental communication to fully control these heads.”

“Then, I will study their characteristics and perform targeted modifications and enhancements.”

With a solid start, Cang Xu proceeded methodically.

Chapter 545: Tycoon Long Fu Enters the Dueling Arena

Snow Bird Harbor.

“Have you heard? The Snow Pearl theft case from back then was the work of the Bayonet Gang!”

“The fire at the Nightingale Dress Shop was also their doing, and the victims have already filed a complaint with the city government palace.”

“The previous city Lord had planned to reduce taxes, but the Bayonet Gang bribed officials to secretly resist it.”

“How many evil deeds have these damn guys done?!”

“They are the termites of Snow Bird Harbor.”

“It's no wonder that the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was attacked before. It must have been their doing.”

"In my opinion, there's not a single good thing about gangs. I wish Commander Long Fu could give Teng Dong Lang a good beating!"

“I wish that too, but Commander Long Fu is only a silver level fighter.”

"Even if he loses, I still support him and the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps! Because he is the first fighter in recent years to openly oppose the Bayonet Gang!"

“That's right.”

“That's so true!”

Similar comments were heard constantly in the streets and alleys of Snow Bird Harbor.

While the dragonman youngster was secretly training under the pretext of recovering from his injuries, the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord was not idle either. He secretly distributed a lot of evidence of crimes and manipulated public opinion.

For the Bayonet Gang, public opinion in Snow Bird Harbor suddenly turned against them.

The upper echelons, including Bing Xiao and Teng Dong Lang, sensed that something was wrong and actively investigated, but saw no results in the short term.

The former and current gang leaders discussed the matter.

“Could it be Bu Quan's doing?”

"Before I went to negotiate with the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, she publicly boarded the Love Triangle. She must have given Long Fu a lot of evidence."

“Hasn't Long Fu already reached a preliminary agreement with us?”

“So, these rumors and frame-ups are more likely to have been secretly spread by Bu Quan herself?”

“I’ve always said that that snow elf is trouble. She’s been secretly investigating us and collecting evidence of our crimes. We should find a way to eliminate her!”

“I’ve tried, but she’s an alchemy great master and a member of the Alchemy Guild. That operation failed, and we received a severe warning.”

“Talking about this now is pointless. We can’t act against Bu Quan either. The whole of Snow Bird Harbor is watching us. I spoke with the city Lord not long ago, and he also warned me not to act rashly."

When the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord was mentioned, Bing Xiao's face darkened.

“This guy really has a big appetite. We paid so much to get him to back down and stay out of this.”

"Be patient. His term is almost over, and he's about to leave. Overall, he's not bad, unlike his predecessor who had a lot of opinions about our Bayonet Gang. All he wants now is peace and quiet. He doesn't want any blemishes on his record during the last days of his term."

“Let's just take note of this incident for now. Once the commotion is over, we'll settle the score with Bu Quan!”

After deliberation, the two gang leaders decided to hold their horses.

They didn't make any big moves, and the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord was happy to wait quietly for the right moment.

The surface of Snow Bird Harbor was calm, but in reality, there were turbulent undercurrents.

Two days later, the dragonman youngster suddenly announced that his injuries had healed.

The Bayonet Gang immediately responded, and the remaining three ringleaders simultaneously announced their defeat.

When the public heard this news, they were mostly excited.

The duel between the dragonman youngster and Teng Dong Lang was just around the corner.

As a result, after learning of the Bayonet Gang's response, the dragonman youngster publicly announced that afternoon that he was willing to fight Teng Dong Lang as soon as possible.

Teng Dong Lang immediately proposed, “Why don't we fight tonight?”

The Bayonet Gang's situation was getting worse and worse, so Teng Dong Lang was eager to resolve the predicament.

After hearing this proposal, the dragonman youngster immediately agreed.

Just like that, before the crowd could even react, the two parties reached an agreement, making this heroic duel, which was the focus of everyone's attention, come much sooner than anyone expected.

The location of the duel was still the Snow Bird Dueling Arena.

This was the largest arena in Snow Bird Harbor, with comprehensive facilities and the largest capacity for spectators.

It was the second time the dragonman youngster had been here.

He found that the arena was completely full. Not only that, but even the aisles were crowded with people.

In fact, there were still many spectators who could only gather outside the arena.

There, there was also a live broadcast of the event via magic images.

It was the first time in more than 20 years that Snow Bird Harbor had seen such a spectacular duel.

“Come on, Commander Long Fu!”

“Even if you can't win, make Teng Dong Lang suffer!”

“Let him taste your iron fist, Commander Long Fu.”

When the dragonman youngster walked into the arena wearing a hooded robe, the audience in the stands cheered and shouted.

Even the audience outside the arena was noisy.

As the local tyrant, Teng Dong Lang also had the support of the Bayonet Gang members. But even though the latter waved their flags and shouted, their momentum was far inferior to the crowd cheering for the dragonman youngster.

“The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps has only been here for a few days, and they already have such momentum. If they continue to develop, what room will there be for our Bayonet Gang?” Bing Xiao, in the VIP viewing room, had a gloomy look on his face.

At this moment, he felt a huge threat from the dragonman youngster.

Also in the room were leaders such as Xiong Ju, Ban Lan Gen, and Spring Boxer.

Their eyes were all focused on the dragonman youngster.

When the dragonman youngster walked to the center of the arena and removed the hooded robe covering his entire body, the entire arena suddenly erupted with a buzz.

“Look at his equipment!” Seeing the dragonman youngster in the magic image, the human ringleader cried out in surprise.

The dragonman youngster was wearing silver level leather armor and boots, and silver level arm guards.

The Ice Sculpture Kingdom had a ban on armor, which targeted gold level and above armor, but silver level was not included in the ban.

This equipment was not difficult to obtain.

Just like in the last duel, the dragonman youngster also brought two silver level crossbows. They were spoils of war obtained by the survivors on Mystifying Monster Island.

Compared to these, the other equipment on the dragonman youngster was even more eye-catching.

He wore the Ice Heart Headband, huge ski goggles over his eyes, an orange-gold nose ring, and pink metal earrings on both ears. His forearms and calves each had bracelets and anklets.

On his chest was a Dragon's Reverse Scale badge.

A blue ice pack hung from his belt.

On the dragonman youngster's back, there was also a longbow and a pair of short spears.

All of the above equipment exuded a gold level aura.

"More than ten pieces of gold level equipment?!" Bing Xiao couldn't sit still and stood up immediately.

“I... I...” Ban Lan Gen stammered, wanting to curse.

Compared to the battle with Xiong Ju earlier, the dragonman youngster was fully equipped from head to toe, transforming from a commoner to a tycoon. The difference was huge and out of everyone's expectation.

Chapter 546: Teng Dong Lang Does Not Feel Satisfied

Seeing the dragonman youngster fully armed, the entire audience was shocked.

In the VIP viewing room, Bing Xiao couldn't sit still and stood up immediately: "Quick, quickly find out what equipment Long Fu is wearing! We need to figure out their functions as soon as possible and inform the gang leader!"

Heroic duels strictly prohibit outsiders from intervening, but reminders from outside the arena were permitted.

Spring Boxer spoke first: “I recognize the badge worn by Long Fu. It is the Dragon's Reverse Scale."

"Everyone knows it's the Dragon's Reverse Scale badge, but there are many variations in this series. Which exactly is it?" Bing Xiao frowned.

Spring Boxer shook his head, indicating that he didn't know.

Ban Lan Gen pointed to the dragonman youngster's belt: “The blue bag hanging from his waist is a Cold Heart Ice Bag.”

“It can absorb ice-type battle qi and convert it into mental and spiritual defense.”

“Add to that the Ice Heart Headband worn by the dragonman youngster, and this time, it will be very difficult to achieve anything in terms of mental impact.”

“I found it!” The human ringleader suddenly said, "His badge is the Sword Return Reverse Scale. It can absorb external blows, accumulate them, and eventually burst forth with a torrent of sword qi. If the user has sufficient ability, they can repeatedly draw on this power, releasing wave after wave of long-range sword qi."

Bing Xiao's expression darkened: "This equipment is a perfect match for Long Fu! His defense is already formidable; with this equipment, he can extend his defensive advantage into his attacks."

“I've also found out something,” the demon hybrid ringleader exclaimed, “and it's bad news!”

"Do you know what? The nose ring, earrings, nose ring, and anklets he is wearing are actually a gold level defensive set!"

“What?” Everyone was shocked.

On the market, defensive equipment was more valuable than offensive weapons.

Defensive equipment that could reach the gold level was rare on the market and in short supply. This was especially true in the Ice Sculpture Kingdom, where armor was banned.

As a result, the dragonman youngster not only wore defensive equipment, but it was gold level, and what's more, it was a set!

The demon hybrid ringleader continued, “Fortunately, this set only has one spell, called Diamond Body.”

“The Diamond Body provides comprehensive protection inside and outside the body. When used, it is only a faint golden halo, so it does not affect the battle qi cultivator's movements in the slightest.”

Bing Xiao slowly shook his head, his heart heavy: “Only one spell is precisely the worst situation.”

"This shows that the alchemist who created this set of armor put all his energy into defense. One can imagine the defensive capabilities of the Diamond Body."

Upon hearing this, Xiong Ju couldn't help but turn pale.

He recalled his battle with the dragonman youngster, and he had truly witnessed the dragonman youngster's strong defense.

The dragonman youngster's defense was once comparable to the gold level.

Now he had obtained a gold level defensive set!

It was really hard to imagine how outstanding his defense would be.

Xiong Ju couldn't help but feel relieved that he had already fought Long Fu. He wouldn't have to face that thick turtle shell again!

The Bayonet Gang was the local tyrant of Snow Bird Harbor, and its high-ranking members were well prepared.

Soon, they had dug up most of the information about the new equipment on the dragonman youngster.

However, the goggles he wore on his face was Bu Quan's original creation, and today's battle was its first appearance, so the upper echelons of the Bayonet Gang were unable to obtain relevant information.

Not only the goggles, but also the Automatic Blood Bottle that the dragonman youngster had swallowed in advance and the spell scrolls he had hidden on his person were not exposed for the time being.

The members of the Bayonet Gang shouted loudly, informing Teng Dong Lang in the arena of the situation.

Not only the Bayonet Gang, but also many transcendents and alchemist magicians among the spectators discussed and exchanged information with each other, and they also figured out most of the dragonman youngster's equipment.

Everyone was talking about it.

"Commander Long Fu really knows how to surprise people. In the last battle, he was almost empty-handed. In today's battle, he's completely blown me away with his wealth!"

"This set of equipment suits him well. I just wonder how effective it will be in actual combat?"

“How did he suddenly get so much luxurious equipment?”

"Hehehe, don't forget their identities. Behind the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps is a mysterious arms dealer."

At this moment, Bu Quan was also in a VIP viewing room.

She looked at the dragonman youngster in the magic image with a smile and anticipation on her face.

She had also thought of many ways to take revenge.

Challenging the important high-ranking members of the Bayonet Gang to a heroic duel was something she had thought of long ago.

Unfortunately, this method was not suitable for her at all.

Although she was a magician, she specialized in alchemy, and her combat strength was very weak.

If she rashly challenged them to a duel, she would be putting herself in danger and fighting with a disadvantage, which would be very foolish.

The dragonman youngster stared intently at Teng Dong Lang, who was not far away.

When he lifted his robe and made his official appearance, the entire Snow Bird Dueling Arena was filled with discussions about his equipment.

But in fact, he had two other important pieces of equipment that were well hidden, even though they were in plain sight.

Those were the Grand Arena Medal and the Golden Silk Chainmail.

“If these pieces of equipment were mine, covering them with the Anti-investigation and Prophecy spell and Deceptive Disguise spell would be more advantageous to me.” The dragonman youngster felt pity in his heart.

Once these items were revealed, it meant that the information had been leaked.

But there was nothing he could do about it.

In fact, the Automatic Blood Bottle he had swallowed, and the spell scrolls he had hidden were also giving off an aura.

As time passed, they would inevitably be exposed one after another.

Unlike the dragonman youngster, Teng Dong Lang did not have any new equipment.

He had a Small Thunder God Flying Hammer hanging from his waist and wore the full body Flowing Wave Armor.

He did not take Xiong Ju's Gold Flower Hacksaw. This was wise, as this gold level attack weapon was very different from Teng Dong Lang's fighting style.

Although he did not see the Battle Qi Syringe, the dragonman youngster knew that this extremely practical tool must have been brought along.

Other than that, Teng Dong Lang was wearing silver level leather armor.

Due to the armor ban, even the leader of the Bayonet Gang did not have any gold level armor.

However, he did have gold level protective gear.

That was the boots on his feet.

The boots were made of deer skin and had exquisite green vine patterns on the surface.

This was something that was not mentioned in the intelligence report.

It seemed that Teng Dong Lang was wearing it for the first time.

The dragonman youngster looked at it several times and secretly guessed its function.

Like the Bayonet Gang, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps also had personnel accompanying them to watch the battle.

They were also actively searching for and finding information about Teng Dong Lang's equipment, then informing the dragonman youngster.

In fact, the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord was also secretly and actively providing all kinds of information.

“We can't find the origin of those boots!”

“But we've detected that their aura contains ice and wood elements.”

“Combined with Teng Dong Lang's Holly bloodline, they should be his trump card.”

The dragonman youngster's ears twitched slightly as he heard his companions' shouts.

The information was not worth much.

Teng Dong Lang also listened attentively and learned a lot of information about the dragonman youngster's equipment, which was quite a gain.

“Commander Long Fu, if your only reliance is on your equipment, then you will definitely lose today!” Teng Dong Lang spoke first, his tone flat and his expression calm.

As a gold level, he was very arrogant when facing a silver level like the dragonman youngster.

He was putting pressure on the dragonman youngster in this way.

The dragonman youngster smiled slightly and immediately retorted, “Even if I lose this battle, so what?”

“Teng Dong Lang, your problem is not this duel, but my testimony and whether I will change my statement in the future.”

Teng Dong Lang's face darkened. From these words, he felt a strong threat from the dragonman youngster.

This was indeed his greatest concern.

Teng Dong Lang said directly, “Our side has never desecrated the heroic duel. Commander Long Fu, perhaps you have misunderstood, or perhaps you are deliberately slandering us.”

“But none of that matters now.”

“Tonight's competition is the best way to resolve the dispute.”

“I hope you can still be so tough when you beg for mercy soon!”

Teng Dong Lang's heart was filled with a fierce killing intent.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps threatened the survival of the Bayonet Gang, and Teng Dong Lang was determined to kill the dragonman youngster.

But given the current situation, the most helpful outcome for the Bayonet Gang was to defeat Long Fu on the spot and force him to “clarify” that he had misunderstood the Bayonet Gang. All of his previous accusations were fabricated.

Thinking of the Bayonet Gang, Teng Dong Lang could only take the initiative to restrain his murderous intent.

This made him feel very dissatisfied.

Chapter 547: Then Let’s fight to Our Heart’s Content!

There was nothing else to say.

After the dragonman youngster exchanged a few words with Teng Dong Lang, he jumped back.

Unlike the previous duel, this time, the dragonman youngster chose to start the battle with long-range shots.

In the battle with Xiong Ju, the dragonman youngster could fight with his fists and feet. But when fighting Teng Dong Lang, the enemy was strong and he was weak, so the dragonman youngster chose a conservative and safe tactic, intending to first adapt to the rhythm of the battle with the gold level opponent, and then implement different tactics depending on the specific situation.

The crossbow was already loaded with alchemy arrows.

The arrows flew through the air, leaving trails of sparks, frost, and lightning—various elemental effects.

Teng Dong Lang snorted coldly and slashed with one hand.

Suddenly, a golden battle qi, like a huge sword beam, slashed forward with great momentum.

Wherever the battle qi sword beam went, the arrows were either shattered or changed direction and were unable to threaten Teng Dong Lang at all.

Teng Dong Lang snorted coldly, his voice not loud, but it spread throughout the venue: “Commander Long Fu, your attack isn't even enough to tickle me.”

“This kind of meaningless test is fruitless.”

"Come on, face me head-on. I'm standing right here."

Teng Dong Lang's words were full of provocation.

The dragonman youngster raised his eyebrows slightly and continued to use his alchemy crossbow to shoot arrows.

It had no effect.

Most of the various types of alchemy arrows were destroyed by Teng Dong Lang in advance, and even if they were shot in front of him, they were blocked by a gold level battle qi shield that condensed out of thin air.

At the gold level, because battle qi could condense out of thin air, the battle qi cultivator's combat methods became very rich.

Teng Dong Lang did not use any battle skills at all, relying solely on his battle qi to steadily maintain his combat power and easily block the dragonman youngster's barrage of arrows.

In the audience seats outside the arena.

Lan Zao showed a hint of joy on his face: “The commander can use alchemy arrows to consume a lot of Teng Dong Lang's battle qi, which is advantageous for us.”

However, Men Shi frowned: “Don't forget that the Bayonet Gang has the Battle Qi Syringe.”

The dwarf had a very deep impression of this equipment.

It was because of this equipment that he lost to Ban Lan Gen.

"Although I haven't seen the Battle Qi Syringe, Teng Dong Lang must have it on him. That's why he's wasting his golden battle qi like this."

“No, to be precise, he can't be said to be wasting it.” Tripleblade had a different opinion, "The quality of gold level battle qi far exceeds the silver level alchemy arrows of the commander. Therefore, Teng Dong Lang only needs to consume a small amount of gold battle qi to deal with the commander's attack. The consumption of his battle qi is much less than we imagined."

Tripleblade understood this, and the dragonman youngster was naturally very clear about it as well

“If I don't pose a threat to Teng Dong Lang, then there's no question of draining his fighting power!”

After firing the arrows from his crossbow again, the dragonman youngster threw them both into the air, then took out the gold level longbow from behind his back.

He drew his bow, infused it with battle qi, and in a second, fired an arrow.

The arrow was powerful and made a sound as it broke through the air.

It shot in front of Teng Dong Lang, violently collided with the shield formed by golden battle qi, and caused a violent explosion.

The arrow completely sacrificed itself and turned into nothingness.

The floating golden battle qi shield was badly damaged, but it quickly returned to its original state due to the newly filled battle qi.

This arrow excited all the spectators.

“Gold level weapons are really powerful.”

“If he shoots a volley of arrows, Teng Dong Lang's shield will be completely destroyed and there won't be time to repair it.”

But in the next moment, the dragonman youngster put away his golden longbow again. Just then, the crossbow he had thrown into the air fell back down, and he caught it with his now free hands.

Both crossbows had an automatic reloading function.

They would automatically absorb the alchemy arrows from the quiver behind the dragonman youngster. The arrows were automatically loaded into the quiver, saving the dragonman youngster a lot of time.

When the dragonman youngster took the crossbow, both weapons were already fully loaded.

He continued to fire the crossbow at Teng Dong Lang.

Lightning, flames, and poisonous mist exploded.

All kinds of arrows created a dazzling scene of bombardment around Teng Dong Lang.

But after the smoke and dust cleared, Teng Dong Lang was still standing in the same place, unscathed.

However, the dragonman youngster did not waver in the slightest. Every time he fired the crossbow, he would draw his golden longbow and shoot an arrow that threatened Teng Dong Lang.

The spectators discussed this.

"Long Fu's barrage is so fierce. The alchemy crossbow he uses is really practical."

“If the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps sells them, I want to buy one too.”

“If the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps sells them, I want to buy one too.”

“That's right. Although Long Fu used a gold level longbow, he didn't seem to have matching gold level long arrows. Moreover, he is not a qualified archer, and I didn't see him use any archery battle skills at all."

"If it were me, I would do the same. Long Fu challenged Teng Dong Lang, which was essentially a weak fighting a strong opponent. Only by not letting go of any opportunity to wear down his opponent and fully grasping and utilizing it would Long Fu have a better chance."

"Teng Dong Lang is still standing motionless... I can understand that he wants to fully demonstrate his strength and contempt for his weak enemy. But isn't he being too arrogant? His opponent is not a simple silver level."

Just as everyone was discussing this, the battle suddenly changed.

The dragonman youngster completely threw the crossbow and golden longbow to the side of the field, and like an arrow released from a bow, he rushed towards Teng Dong Lang.

This scene surprised almost everyone.

Even the members of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps were no exception.

“What's going on?”

“30%”

“The commander brought a lot of alchemy arrows, and he's so skilled, he hasn't even used up 30% of them yet.”

It was very strange.

Just as many people were praising the dragonman youngster for resolutely executing the correct tactic, the latter suddenly abandoned his persistence and “recklessly” rushed towards Teng Dong Lang.

However, when the dragonman youngster rushed forward and was 50 meters away from his opponent, something unexpected happened.

A translucent ice vine suddenly shot out from the ground.

The dragonman youngster used his battle skill, causing a small explosion on the side of a ring mound, pushing him to the other side and successfully dodging the ice vine's sneak attack.

But immediately, several more ice vines burst out of the ground, some shooting out violently, some sweeping across, and some even attempting to entangle the dragonman youngster.

The ring mounds around the dragonman youngster exploded, causing him to move repeatedly.

He successfully dodged all the ice vine attacks but was forced back and did not rush to Teng Dong Lang's front.

The dragonman youngster's face darkened as he looked at Teng Dong Lang surrounded and protected by the ice vines. He realized that he had fallen into a trap!

Teng Dong Lang stood with his hands behind his back, calm and composed, looking at the dragonman youngster with interest: “You're quite sharp. You can see through my tactics, which proves that you are fighting with your head.”

“That's right, when I use the battle skill ‘Base’, I can't move.”

“My weakest moment is right after I use this battle skill.”

“Unfortunately, by now, my base has spread to a radius of 50 meters.”

“Can you stop me?”

“If you can't destroy my battle skill, my advantage will continue to grow until ‘Base’ covers the entire battlefield.”

“No, to be precise, when my ‘Base’ expands to a radius of 300 meters, you will have no chance of winning.”

When the dragonman youngster adopted a long-range attrition tactic, Teng Dong Lang took advantage of the situation and used his battle skill, 'Base'.

He spoke, playing the role of a powerful figure looking down on others, to cover up his own tactics.

The dragonman youngster was executing an attrition tactic, and Teng Dong Lang was secretly executing his own tactics!

Obviously, the dragonman youngster's tactics were not very effective, while Teng Dong Lang had established his own advantage.

As time went on, his advantage would grow larger and larger, until he reaped the fruits of victory!

The spectators were in an uproar.

“That treacherous, despicable bastard!” Bu Quan gritted her teeth; her eyes filled with hatred.

“Another rooting tactic—how shameless!” Men Shi clenched his fists in anger.

Not long ago, his opponent, Ban Lan Gen, used this tactic to establish an advantage from the start of the battle, maintain it, and expand it until he won.

Men Shi had a bad experience.

Even though he tried his best, he couldn't see any hope!

Now, Teng Dong Lang was also using this tactic.

Moreover, compared to Ban Lan Gen, Teng Dong Lang's execution of the tactic was undoubtedly more covert and faster.

“Heroes think alike!” When Ban Lan Gen saw the tactic Teng Dong Lang had chosen, he laughed loudly, smugly.

Xiong Ju glared at Ban Lan Gen: "Stop laughing, it's noisy! The battle has just begun, and Long Fu won't give up easily."

Spring Boxer said, "Commander Long Fu is indeed better than me, I couldn't sense anything at all. How did he find out?"

How did the dragonman youngster find out that something was wrong?

The biggest contributor was still the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord.

In the first piece of magic image he provided, Teng Dong Lang was standing in place, summoning ice vines to deal with the ice wolf pack.

This gave him a big clue.

“The discovery was quite timely!”

“50”

"It's already difficult to break into a 50-meter radius. If this radius expands, I will have no chance of winning."

“But I still have to stay calm!”

“Teng Dong Lang's words seemed honest, but in fact, he was putting pressure on me, trying to make me feel overwhelmed and lose my composure.”

This was the first time the dragonman youngster was fighting a gold level opponent.

During the battle at Two Eyes Island, although there were fights of this level, the dragonman youngster only had a taste of it.

This time, he could fight to his heart's content!

Bloodcore Mutation — Spurting Smoke Dragon Transformation.

Gold level Diamond Protection set — Diamond Body!

Dragon's Reverse Scale, activate!

The Cold Heart Ice Bag, Ice Heart Headband, and Grand Arena Medal had already taken effect.

Battle skill—Surge!

Bam!

With a loud explosion, the dragonman youngster suddenly disappeared from where he stood.

He seemed to have transformed into a crimson cannonball, charging at Teng Dong Lang with all his might.

Chapter 548: Three Segment Whip

Even though he was his enemy, Teng Dong Lang couldn't help but admire the dragonman youngster's decisiveness as he charged towards him.

As soon as the dragonman youngster discovered the truth, he immediately changed his tactics, completely and decisively.

His eyes burned with fighting spirit, but they still shone with the light of thought.

Not long after the fight began, the youngster already felt that the enemy in front of him was completely different from Xiong Ju.

Xiong Ju was a fierce fighter whose instincts prevailed, while Teng Dong Lang was clear-headed and used his wisdom to fight.

Such an enemy was much more difficult to deal with than Xiong Ju. In addition, he was a gold level battle qi cultivator, which made him even stronger.

After changing his tactics, the dragonman youngster exploded with all his strength, holding nothing back.

Because he had no way to hold back.

Facing such a strong enemy, he had to give it his all. To hold back would be extremely foolish!

“He's charging!” In the VIP viewing room, Ban Lan Gen was a little restless.

Seeing the dragonman youngster's charge, he couldn't help but recall the time he was knocked out by his fist.

Even though he knew that the gang leader Teng Dong Lang was on a different level than himself, he still couldn't help but feel subconsciously worried.

The Snow Bird Harbor Dueling Arena erupted with surprised cheers.

The situation took a sudden turn, and the two sides were about to engage in close combat.

The duel suddenly became exciting!

Hiss.

With a soft sound, the ground was broken open, and an ice vine stabbed at the dragonman youngster's ankle.

Teng Dong Lang had deliberately said that his battle skill 'Base' had expanded to 50 meters.

This was just a smokescreen.

The truth was that it had already reached 60 meters!

Bu Quan cried out in surprise.

The dragonman youngster was clearly at least 60 meters away from Teng Dong Lang, yet there were ice vines lying in wait underground.

Bam!

The dragonman youngster used his Surge battle skill to dodge.

He snorted coldly in his heart, as he had already been mentally prepared for this.

When he realized that Teng Dong Lang was a powerful enemy skilled in strategy, he understood how untrustworthy the latter's words were.

The dragonman youngster continued to sprint.

Ice vines burst out of the ground and stabbed straight at him, like a group of spears.

The dragonman youngster did not dodge or evade but charged straight through.

The ice vines broke into pieces.

The dragonman youngster's momentum was astonishing, destroying everything in its path!

But the ice vines were not without effect. They offset the dragonman youngster's momentum, and the cold air wrapped around his body, inevitably slowing him down.

Teng Dong Lang stood still, not moving.

The dragonman youngster was so brave, but he remained calm.

However, the way he manipulated the ice vines in battle had changed.

The ice vines no longer stabbed straight but instead wrapped around and whipped.

Soft tactics could overcome hardness.

For the dragonman youngster, the ice vines posed an even greater threat.

“He can't lose his speed, otherwise the commander will be surrounded by ice vines,” said Tripleblade.

The members of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps were all nervous.

The power of each ice vine was comparable to the attack of a silver level battle qi cultivator. If any of them faced it, there would be no way out, and they would die a miserable death on the spot.

Of course, that didn't include Zong Ge.

Zong Ge still didn't come to watch the battle this time.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' ships needed a reliable person to stay behind.

The dragonman youngster also responded to this.

He no longer charged headlong but focused on dodging.

He fought with all his might and made remarkable progress in breaking through. While dodging, he kept getting closer to Teng Dong Lang.

Teng Dong Lang suddenly smiled.

Swish, swish, swish.

A large number of ice vines suddenly shot out from the ground, trapping the dragonman youngster who had just dodged and arrived at the spot!

Teng Dong Lang had secretly planted a large number of ice vines as a trap. Relying on other ice vines, he forced the dragonman youngster to move and successfully drove him into the trap.

Hundreds of ice vines densely covered the dragonman youngster and attacked him from all directions.

At this critical moment, the dragonman youngster used his Machine Gun Fist battle skill.

Bang, bang, bang...

A series of explosions resounded.

One by one, the ice vines exploded into countless pieces under his fists.

Ice shards flew everywhere.

This battle skill had proven itself in the battle against Xiong Ju, and it was still effective this time.

The dragonman youngster attacked instead of defending, defeated the ice vines, and rushed out.

He finally faced Teng Dong Lang!

The next moment, Teng Dong Lang gently swung his right arm.

A thin whip wrapped around his forearm stretched out with momentum.

A gold level long whip!

Teng Dong Lang infused it with battle qi and swung the long whip.

The speed of the whip suddenly increased.

Battle skill—Fast Whip.

The whip lashed out fiercely.

The dragonman youngster quickly raised his arms to shield his face.

The next moment, the tip of the whip struck his forearm.

The light golden aura of his Diamond Body trembled violently, and the blue steel armbands on his arms emitted a buzzing sound.

Relying on the powerful defense of his gold level armor, the dragonman youngster blocked the attack.

The youngster was about to continue his charge when suddenly his pupils contracted slightly.

The long whip had just finished striking, but suddenly recoiled, completely defying the laws of physics, and struck him again.

Battle skill—Additional Whip!

The dragonman youngster was caught off guard and was struck on the forearm.

His forearm swung open, leaving him wide open.

Gold level battle qi filled the long whip, which suddenly gushed out and turned into a knife beam.

Battle skill—Blade Beam.

The gold level battle qi was like a machete with powerful cutting properties, slashing the dragonman youngster's chest.

The faint halo of the Diamond Body trembled violently again, blocking this attack.

The dragonman youngster was completely knocked out of his momentum by this blow.

The long whip, which had lost its gold battle qi a moment ago, suddenly rose like a python raising its head.

Battle skill—Wild Whip.

The gold level battle qi expanded at the end of the whip, forming a round hammer.

The round hammer collided with the dragonman youngster, fiercely.

The dragonman youngster hurriedly retreated backwards.

Boom!

With a loud bang, the curled end of the whip crashed into the ground, forming a small pit.

As the dislodged soil flew, a large number of ice whips rose up.

Whoosh, whoosh, whoosh.

They were like a group of snakes launched by a trap, giving the dragonman youngster no chance to catch his breath.

The dragonman youngster no longer had any intention of approaching Teng Dong Lang.

At this moment, he only wanted to retreat with all his might!

The spurting smoke ring mounds appeared on his knees, palms, chest, and other places.

Bang, bang, bang...

The Surge battle skill caused a series of mini explosions in the smoke ring mounds, propelling the dragonman youngster's body and causing him to continuously retreat.

A large number of ice vines missed their target.

Finally, the dragonman youngster rolled awkwardly and retreated to a relatively safe distance.

Looking closely, he was already a hundred meters away from Teng Dong Lang.

His first full-force attack ended in failure!

The entire venue fell silent for a few seconds.

Immediately after, the audience began to discuss heatedly.

“Teng Dong Lang is truly a gold level battle qi cultivator. This is how he really fights.”

“When Long Fu charged, I thought for a moment that Teng Dong Lang would be forced to move, but I didn't expect him to easily defuse the attack with a series of strikes.”

“Very impressive, Teng Dong Lang is very impressive!” Some discerning spectators were shocked.

“Teng Dong Lang just used several battle skills in succession, but in fact, the Fast Whip was not the first battle skill he used, but the second.”

"The first battle skill he used was the Triple Whip! The final battle skill was also the Triple Whip."

"When he used the Triple Whip, he also used the Fast Whip, the Additional Whip, and the Wild Whip in succession. He even had enough energy left in between to use a gold level Knife Beam!"

“His whip skills are really profound.”

In the viewing room where Bing Xiao was located, the senior members of the Bayonet Gang were also very excited.

Ban Lan Gen sat down and clenched his fists: "My worries were completely unnecessary. Lord Teng Dong Lang's strength far exceeds mine. I was worrying needlessly."

Bing Xiao recalled a scene from the past.

At that time, Teng Dong Lang was much younger than he was now.

It was by using his whip skills that Teng Dong Lang defeated Bing Xiao's gold level slave in front of everyone and became the leader of the gang in one fell swoop.

As the memory faded, Bing Xiao stared at the magic image in front of him.

Teng Dong Lang's once young face was gradually replaced by a more aged one.

This was the result of being burdened by gang affairs and working hard.

It was also the price of power.

“Although I rarely see you fight, there is no doubt that you are now stronger than before, Teng Dong Lang.”

"Based on the Triple Whip, you combine different battle skills, giving the ordinary Triple Whip special effects with each whip. It's difficult for enemies to defend against this extremely targeted attack!"

“The complicated gang affairs and the power of the gang leader have not made you slack off in training yourself.”

“It was the right decision to hand over the position of gang leader to you!”

Once he used his main weapon, Teng Dong Lang easily defeated the dragonman youngster.

Remember that the dragonman youngster was fighting with all his might!

The dragonman youngster was panting heavily, but his attention was focused on the ski goggles in front of him.

His field of vision was different from that of normal people.

The ski goggles given to him by Bu Quan could mark different levels of threat.

Just now, when Teng Dong Lang used various battle skills, his gold level whip produced different colors.

“When gold level battle qi is infused into gold level equipment, it turns red, representing a strong threat.”

“Every time a battle skill is used, the threat level of the whip skyrockets.”

“The threat level of the first battle skill was relatively low, but by the time he used the final whip, the color had turned deep purple!”

It was precisely because of this that the dragonman youngster instantly realized that if he resisted, he would suffer serious damage.

Even though he had gold level protective gear.

A gold level battle qi cultivator used gold level equipment and performed gold level battle skills.

The combined power of the three far exceeded that of the gold level artillery and gold level shells.

The Wild Whip missed and hit the ground, only making a small, yet deep pit

But don't forget, this was the largest dueling arena in Snow Bird Harbor, with high-end and complete facilities.

To be able to create such a deep pit, the attack power was already extraordinary.

The dragonman youngster was only silver level, and although he had a large amount of gold level equipment, without the corresponding level of battle qi, he was unable to unleash its full power.

“This Teng Dong Lang is probably the strongest gold level I've ever seen.”

“Perhaps Hun Tong can compete with him.”

“But these goggles are really convenient.”

“Even though it's my first time fighting Teng Dong Lang, with these goggles, I can see through every move he makes and make accurate judgments.”

Under normal circumstances, the dragonman youngster could only deeply understand Teng Dong Lang's methods through continuous battles, learning from pain and injuries, and paying the price of blood, in order to make such accurate judgments.

It was just like when he fought Xiong Ju before and was unable to guard against the Bear Roar battle skill.

But after fighting him once, the dragonman youngster kept reviewing the battle, and now if they fought again, he would be able to judge Xiong Ju's Bear Roar battle skill in advance based on details such as Xiong Ju's Adam's apple.

“Teng Dong Lang is the leader of a gang, and his strength is not just ordinary gold level.”

“When he uses his long whip, that's when he really gets going.”

“Long Fu was nearly knocked down by a few lashes.”

"Silver, gold... the former is lower-tier, while the latter is already mid-tier. The gap between the two is not easily bridged."

“Wait, look, Long Fu has drawn his short spear.”

“This is the first time I've seen him use a melee weapon.”

“This stance... seems unusual.”

Chapter 549: Root Boots

The dragonman youngster held two antique bronze short spears in his hands.

These were weapons that he had specifically requested, and which the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord had acquired for him with all his might.

Over the past few days, the dragonman youngster had studied short spear battle skills under Zong Ge.

Thanks to his fighter talent and the Grand Arena Medal, he had successfully mastered the short spear and made it his main weapon.

The enemy used their main weapon, but this time, the dragonman youngster also had his weapon.

Teng Dong Lang couldn't help but show a hint of caution.

This was the first time the dragonman youngster had used a melee weapon, and just by looking at his posture, it was clear that his skill with the short spear far exceeded that of the bow and crossbow.

Ice vines broke through the ground and stabbed at him.

The dragonman youngster did not dodge the ice vines that stabbed straight at him, but collided with them directly, using this to recharge his Sword Return Reverse Scale.

He mainly dodged the ice vines that entangled and whipped at him.

If dodging was too troublesome, he would use his short spear to fight back.

The short spear was a gold level weapon, and its quality alone was superior to most ice vines.

The quality of these ice vines varied, with some having gold level combat power, some silver level, and many bronze level and iron level.

Relying on the advantage of his short spear, the dragonman youngster once again broke through the ice vine organization's defense line and rushed to Teng Dong Lang's front.

Smack, smack, smack.

Teng Dong Lang swung his long whip.

Both sides simultaneously used their battle skills.

The long whip and short spear collided, and the dragonman youngster's short spear almost slipped out of his hand.

The dragonman youngster's offensive was blocked, while Teng Dong Lang's whip shadows continued unabated.

The dragonman youngster was forced to retreat again.

The battle reached a stalemate.

The dragonman youngster continued to charge forward, and no matter how many ice vines there were, they couldn't withstand him using his main weapon for the first time.

However, when he faced Teng Dong Lang, his attacks were always repelled.

Teng Dong Lang's long whip was both soft and hard, and he was very good at defusing the dragonman youngster's horizontal charges.

“This can't go on!” Bu Quan gritted his teeth, the worry on his face growing heavier and heavier.“ Commander Long Fu has been unable to attack for a long time, while Teng Dong Lang continues to accumulate advantages.”

The spectators could clearly see that the range of Ice Vine's attacks was getting wider and wider.

After several failed charges by the dragonman youngster, the radius of the Base battle skills had expanded to more than 80 meters.

In the VIP viewing room.

Xiong Ju said with emotion, "I didn't expect Long Fu to be so good with a short spear. But why didn't he use it in his duel with me?"

Xiong Ju couldn't imagine that the dragonman youngster had gained some insight after his duel with him.

Moreover, in the short period of a few days after that, his intensive training yielded such fruitful results.

The demon hybrid ringleader sneered, “He must have underestimated you, Xiong Ju, and nearly lost to you as a result.”

"Since he initiated the heroic duel against the gang leader, these short spears should be left for the gang leader. That must have been his plan."

Spring Boxer praised, but also felt puzzled, “It's hard to imagine that Commander Long Fu is a young man, yet his fist technique, footwork and weapon skills are so outstanding.”

The human ringleader shook his head slightly and sneered, “He's taking shortcuts.”

"Notice how he uses the short spears in combat—it's the same as how he throws punches. Even if there are differences, they're very minor."

"So, when he trains his fist and foot techniques, he's also training his spear skills. Similarly, training with short spears also helps his basic combat skills. There are many overlapping areas between the two."

Spring Boxer finally understood: “So that's how it is.”

"Hmm? That's a good idea. Does that mean I should also train with short spears too?"

The situation was favorable for the Bayonet Gang, so the ringleaders felt relaxed and engaged in casual conversation.

“Even if he had kept the short spears for this battle, it is still useless.” Bing Xiao analyzed calmly, “The gap between the silver level and the gold level is quite huge.”

“If Long Fu was gold level, he wouldn't have been forced to retreat repeatedly.”

“In fact, in the same circumstances, it is difficult for a hard weapon such as the short spear to be forcibly repelled by a soft weapon such as a long whip.”

At the same time.

Among the comrades and friends, Tripleblade sighed regretfully, “If Commander Long Fu had chosen swords, it would have been more advantageous, right?”

He knew about the dragonman youngster's assault training.

Zong Ge had tutored the dragonman youngster, and he had advised the dragonman youngster not to publicize his fighter talent.

But for the survivors, this was not something worth keeping secret

On the contrary, the dragonman youngster, Zi Di, and others were more inclined to announce it in order to maintain the dragonman youngster's leadership influence.

The dragonman youngster, who was in the midst of a fierce battle, had a similar realization to Tripleblade.

If it were a sword, it would be more convenient to chop ice vines and long whips.

Short spears were a little awkward to use.

Although the spear tip could still be used for cutting and slashing attacks, it was indeed difficult to compare with swords.

“But there's nothing we can do about it!”

“If I use swords, then it will be different from my fighting style.”

“Even with the Grand Arena Medal and Zong Ge's guidance, I couldn't achieve my current level of proficiency.”

“The learning cost is at least twice as much as a short spear, and I don't have enough time.”

Training with swords and other weapons would have to wait until later.

With the Grand Arena Medal and his own fighter talent, it would not be difficult for the dragonman youngster to master other weapons.

After a series of failed advances, the dragonman youngster deeply felt the gap between himself and his opponent.

There was a qualitative difference in their battle qi.

The dragonman youngster's weapons and armor were not bad, but it was difficult to fully utilize their effects with silver battle qi.

The battle between the two sides had been going on for nearly ten minutes, but the dragonman youngster still couldn't shake Teng Dong Lang, and couldn't even force him to leave his position.

Teng Dong Lang did not forget to use words to put pressure on the dragonman youngster.

“I can see that you have worked hard on your short spears, Commander Long Fu.”

“What a pity.”

“If this is all you've got, you will definitely lose today!”

Just then, a shout came from the audience area.

He said, “Commander, it's the boots!”

“The gold level deer skin boots on Teng Dong Lang's feet are called the Root Boots.”

"They can be used with almost all wood-based bloodlines and give a powerful bonus when using related battle skills. In particular, they give the strongest boost to the battle skill, Base, up to 80%!"

The dragonman youngster heard this new information.

While he was fighting, the other members of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps were not idle either.

Of course, the dragonman youngster also knew that the source of the information should be the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord.

Before this battle, the latter had agreed to use the magician tower to remotely investigate Teng Dong Lang's equipment. Through alchemy technology that calculated aura, he could get to the bottom of things and find out the details of his opponent's equipment.

Nearly ten minutes had passed since the start of the battle.

The results of the magician tower's reconnaissance finally reached the dragonman youngster's ears.

“Root Boots...” The dragonman youngster sighed in his heart.

An 80% increase was really too strong.

It was almost equivalent to having two Teng Dong Lang working together to use the Base battle skill.

No wonder the range of his Base battle skill spread so quickly.

The dragonman youngster's equipment was luxurious, but as the leader of the Bayonet Gang, Teng Dong Lang was naturally no slouch either.

The Root Boots on his feet were perfect for him!

The battle continued.

Every charge by the dragonman youngster brought a wave of hope and expectation.

Then, as he was forced back by Teng Dong Lang, the hope and expectation that had just risen in the hearts of the audience was dashed.

“The range of Teng Dong Lang's Base battle skill is already close to 100 meters.”

“Commander Long Fu has no hope!”

"It's not his fault. A silver level battle qi cultivator who can actively challenge a gold level battle qi cultivator is already a brave man. Unfortunately, the gap in strength cannot be made up for by courage alone."

“If Teng Dong Lang were a brute like Xiong Ju, Commander Long Fu might still have a chance.”

“No, he still has a chance.” Bu Quan gritted her teeth and stubbornly held on to her hopes. She really wanted to see the dragonman youngster win.

In the VIP viewing room.

Ban Lan Gen laughed loudly, “We're going to win!”

“No, there are still some uncertainties.” Bing Xiao was not blindly optimistic, "Long Fu still has unknown gold level items. Why haven't our alchemists calculated the specific results yet?"

The human ringleader agreed, "Even if we exclude the unknown items, Commander Long Fu still has the Sword Return Reverse Scale. I've carefully counted, and there have been many times when the Dragon Robe deliberately took hits and charged toward the ice vines. He's charging up the dragon scale."

“Once it's charged up enough, he'll definitely launch an explosive attack.”

“That's probably his biggest advantage!”

Many people saw this.

In fact, this was precisely the dragonman youngster's tactic.

He attacked with all his might and full firepower, but he was still no match for Teng Dong Lang, who wielded a long whip.

So, the only way to break the deadlock was to rely on even more powerful means of attack.

From his previous battle with Xiong Ju, he understood that he lacked powerful means of attack. Now, he felt this even more deeply.

It wasn't that the dragonman youngster hadn't thought about making up for this weakness.

But no matter what battle skill it was, it took time to master.

He had already made full use of every second of these past few days. He simply didn't have the time or energy to start practicing a powerful battle skill from scratch.

Fortunately, before this battle, the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord had taken the initiative to lend the dragonman youngster the Sword Return Reverse Scale.

This equipment brought the dragonman youngster the greatest hope.

Of course, the dragonman youngster had also thought about using his bloodline spell, Life Scorching Dragon Breath.

This was a legendary bloodline talent that originated from the Flame Dragon King.

However, casting this spell required chanting, which would leave him very vulnerable.

Even if Teng Dong Lang stood still, the dragonman youngster would not have had any chance to do so.

Don't forget that since the fight began, Teng Dong Lang had not yet used the Small Thunder God Flying Hammer hanging from his waist.

It was obvious that Teng Dong Lang still had a lot of energy left!

The only gold level equipment he used were the long whip and the boots.

Chapter 550: Thin Ice Wall Shield

There were at least two more unknown gold level auras on Teng Dong Lang.

Like the members of the Bayonet Gang, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps and the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord were actively using aura calculations and other methods to investigate the specific appearance of these gold level items.

“No matter how many gold level items he has, he most likely has defensive gold level equipment!” The dragonman youngster had already guessed this.

But until now, his aura was surging, his physical strength and battle qi were greatly depleted, and he still hadn't forced Teng Dong Lang to use his gold level defensive equipment.

The dragonman youngster charged again.

Ice vines grew densely in front of the dragonman youngster.

Battle skill—Charge.

The dragonman youngster raised his double spears in front of him, stretched out his arms, and sprinted straight forward in a second, charging more than ten meters away.

Wherever he went, the ice vines disintegrated. Even gold level ice vines couldn't stop him.

The dragonman youngster had already seen that Teng Dong Lang was still unable to use the ice vines scattered all over the ground to perform whip-type battle skills.

Otherwise, his combat power would be terrifying.

“Since the start of the battle, he has been maintaining his Base battle skill.”

"When I changed tactics and kept charging, he was forced to use the gold level whip. At most, he used three battle skills at the same time."

“Even if this isn't his limit, he's not far from it.”

As the number of exchanges increased, the dragonman youngster gathered more and more relevant information and gained a lot of understanding about Teng Dong Lang.

The Charge battle skill lasted only one second.

The dragonman youngster's momentum was exhausted, and he immediately jumped up.

The next moment, several ice vines attacked him from behind, catching him off guard.

A second earlier, the dragonman youngster had used his battle skill to destroy the ice vines along the way. But the next second, ice vines grew out of the ground again.

This was the power of the battle skill, Base.

Within the domain of Base, the battle qi spread, receiving support from all directions and responding immediately. As long as the source was not depleted, the battle qi would not run out.

The dragonman youngster's situation became increasingly difficult.

Because the ice vines could be quickly replenished, every time he failed to sprint forward, even retreating was fraught with danger.

The dragonman youngster leaped into the air, but he didn't go very high.

He was deliberately restraining himself.

Sure enough, in the next moment, three or four ice vines broke through the ground and shot straight at him.

One of them was surging with energy and was actually gold level.

The gold level ice vine was mainly controlled by Teng Dong Lang. The whip’s head was as agile as a snake. After drawing a smooth arc in the air, it wrapped around the dragonman youngster's waist.

Two large ring mounds appeared behind the dragonman youngster.

He raised his arms behind him, and two small ring mounds appeared at his elbow joints.

Crack!

Four ring mounds burst into yellow flames.

The Surge battle skill worked again, pushing the dragonman youngster forward.

The dragonman youngster used this to avoid the entanglement of the gold level ice vines.

Although he was only silver level and did not have flying battle skills, because of his clever combination, he was still able to forcefully move in midair.

The ice vines grew on the ground, and each one had a limited range of attack.

The dragonman youngster had just escaped from the gold level ice vines, only to find a row of ice vines standing in front of him.

The ice vines were like a city wall, blocking his path.

There was no time to examine them closely.

But through his perception of their aura, the dragonman youngster understood that at least seven of the ice vines were silver level.

The dragonman youngster let out a low cry and swung the short spears in his hand.

The short spears shot out a dazzling light.

Battle skill—Horizontal Cut.

The dragonman youngster used the sharp blade of the spear tip to cut off the row of ice vines at the root.

After the ice vines broke, Teng Dong Lang suddenly lost control.

The dragonman youngster took the opportunity to roll over and cross this obstacle.

Behind him, the broken ice vines were transformed back into elements and battle qi

The elements dissipated, and the battle qi flowed back underground.

This was also one of the effects of the Base battle skill.

It could help the cultivator recover battle qi, effectively reducing its consumption.

The dragonman youngster rushed forward another ten meters or so and once again found himself in danger. Three gold level ice vines attacked the dragonman youngster's head, waist, and calves from three directions, simultaneously striking the upper, middle, and lower areas.

The closer he got to Teng Dong Lang, the higher the level of the ice vines growing from the ground.

Battle skill—Pointed Spear!

The dragonman youngster took a deep breath and stabbed the short spears in his hand three times in succession.

Each stab accurately hit one of the three ice vines.

The ice vines were pierced by the spear tip and shattered almost simultaneously.

After overcoming this obstacle, the dragonman youngster rushed forward again.

After this scene passed, the spectators let out low cries and looked surprised.

The dragonman youngster's move was beautiful and eye-catching.

"In an instant, he used his Pointed Spear battle skill and successfully repelled three gold level ice vines. It seems that Commander Long Fu has practiced this battle skill many times in his daily training."

“Without solid basic skills, it would be almost impossible to use it so perfectly in such a short time.”

“The ice vines are very fast and can strike accurately instead of slashing, which shows that Long Fu's dynamic vision is excellent!”

Just as people were discussing this, they all fell silent.

This was because the dragonman youngster had fallen into a trap once again.

A large number of ice vines surrounded him.

This time, the dragonman youngster did not dodge away. First, his speed was slowed down, affecting his rhythm, and then his body was entangled by the ice vines.

The ice vines became more and more entangled, gradually covering his whole body.

A large number of ice vines followed one after another, like a swarm of snakes spreading out, enveloping the dragonman youngster and forming a hemispherical tomb.

“It's over!” The members of the Bayonet Gang shouted excitedly.

“The gang leader is mighty!” Teng Dong Lang's supporters cheered and jumped for joy.

“Commander Long Fu...” Bu Quan's face turned pale at this moment.

"It's already very difficult to hold on until now. After all, he's only at the silver level..." More people shook their heads and sighed.

Suddenly, the dragonman youngster's low grunt rang out from among the countless ice vine balls.

Battle skill—Close Spiral Spear!

The dragonman youngster stretched his arms above his head, clutching two short spears in his hands, the tips of which were tightly pressed together.

His whole body was like a sharp arrow, constantly spinning in a spiral, shooting through the layers of ice vines and darting out.

Wherever he went, even the gold level ice vines crumbled.

The ice vines that had originally entangled him were all torn apart.

With the boost from the Close Spiral Spear battle skill, the dragonman youngster shot forward more than ten meters.

When his battle skill was over, he rushed to Teng Dong Lang's front again!

Such a turn of events reignited hope in the disappointed audience.

“How many times has he charged toward the powerful enemy?” Bu Quan's face flushed again, her eyes slightly red, revealing a look of emotion.

As an observer, she couldn't count exactly how many times.

Ten times, or twenty times?

At least a dozen times.

Every time the dragonman youngster charged, he ended in failure.

But he was not discouraged in the slightest, his fighting spirit was fierce, and his fighting spirit was unyielding.

He kept persevering!

“No matter how many times, it's futile.” Teng Dong Lang swung his long whip.

The gold level long whip was different from those ice vines.

The two were worlds apart in terms of quality!

Even with his short spear and battle skills, the dragonman youngster couldn't defeat the whip.

Every time he reached this stage, the dragonman youngster would return empty-handed.

But this time, it was different!

The badge on the dragonman youngster's chest suddenly burst into an intimidating glow.

“Sword Return Dragon Scale!” Many spectators screamed.

They had been waiting for this moment.

Swoosh!

As soon as the gold level whip was swung out, the whip immediately arched, causing the whip head to swing back defensively.

Battle skill—Reverse Whip.

The long whip swung back, circling around Teng Dong Lang like a thin python flying and circling.

Teng Dong Lang was already prepared for the Sword Return Reverse Scale.

He knew the dragonman youngster's tactics very well!

In the next moment, a dazzling sword light suddenly shot out, aiming at Teng Dong Lang's face.

The whip moved and blocked the sword light.

Teng Dong Lang frowned slightly, surprised in his heart: “Only one?”

This did not match the sword qi he had expected.

If the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord had been there to watch the battle and saw this scene, he would have been very pleased.

This was because the dragonman youngster had followed his advice and practiced his skills as much as possible, achieving precise control of the Sword Return Dragon Scale in just a few short days.

Only with precise control could he shoot out the sword light again and again.

Rough control would only cause all his accumulated energy to be released at once.

The gold level whip actively retreated to defend, leaving space.

The dragonman youngster exchanged his chance to charge forward with just one sword light.

He firmly grasped the opportunity he had created and finally fought his way to Teng Dong Lang!

This was the closest he had ever been to Teng Dong Lang.

The distance between the two was less than 5 meters!

The whip flying around Teng Dong Lang's body was like an angry python, breaking through the air.

Battle skill—Multiple Whip Shadows.

The long whip swung, throwing out a series of black whip shadows.

The whip shadows were dense and intertwined, instantly forming a fishing net curtain that covered the dragonman youngster.

The dragonman youngster let out a low cry and suddenly threw the short spear in his hand.

Battle skill—Spear Throw!

Bam!

An explosive sound echoed as the short spear pierced through the layers of whip shadows.

Spear Throw involved throwing a close-range short spear directly, serving as a decisive move in critical moments, a desperate gamble in dire straits.

Its power was immense; the whip shadows shattered, and even the whip itself was struck, causing the whip body to soften and reveal a defensive weakness.

The whip itself is a soft weapon, and under the dragonman youngster's forceful attack, it still showed the characteristics of a soft weapon.

The dragonman youngster took advantage of this opportunity to jump over the long whip and attack Teng Dong Lang directly.

At this moment, the distance between them was less than 1 meter!

Battle skill—Demolition Fist!

The dragonman youngster used his free hand to perform his most proficient battle skill.

Boom!

There was a loud bang.

His long-planned punch was blocked by an ice shield.

The ice shield was thin, translucent, 1.8 meters high, and covered with cracks.

At the top of the shield was an uneven shape, like the battlements on a city wall.

It exuded a gold level aura and firmly blocked the dragonman youngster's punch.

At the critical moment, Teng Dong Lang took out his defensive shield and successfully stopped his opponent's powerful attack.

“Thin Ice Wall Shield!”

“This should be a shield used by the kingdom's regular army, so how did it end up in Teng Dong Lang's hands?”
